*Eshwar Sai Kandimalla,Sravan Chowdary Kankanala,Sumana Bhimineni,Hem Sundhar Korukunda,Vivek Yelleti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时空图与双层级混沌融合技术的方法，通过估计预测区间中心与宽度、引入波动感知门控机制，在动态市场环境下生成高质量的不确定性量化预测，在NSE 43家公司数据上显著优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融市场预测方法多为点预测，无法量化不确定性；而生成预测区间的方法往往忽略资产间关系，或无法在动态市场环境下同时保证区间校准性和锐度。

Method: 提出时空图+双层级混沌融合方法：使用独立非线性变换分别估计区间中心与宽度；引入波动感知门控机制使预测依赖市场状态；通过图嵌入和序列建模捕捉时间依赖；采用LUBE目标函数进行训练。

Result: 在2016-2026年NSE 8个行业43家公司的数据上，Winkler分数0.0778、PIAW=0.1407、PICP=96.6%，均显著优于LSTM、GRU、GCN、HGNN等基线（Diebold-Mariano检验p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在预测区间校准性、锐度和覆盖率上均达到最优，验证了融合时空图结构与混沌技术的有效性。

Abstract: Financial market forecasting is inherently uncertain, yet most deep learning approaches rely on point predictions that provide only single-value estimates without quantifying uncertainty. Such predictions are insufficient for risk-aware decision-making, as they fail to capture the range of possible outcomes and the associated confidence of forecasts.The problem can be solved using prediction intervals, which allow obtaining an upper and lower bound for the prediction, thus enabling uncertainty representation in the model. Yet, the current methods tend to disregard relationships between assets or cannot simultaneously ensure good calibration and sharpness of the resulting intervals in dynamically changing market regimes. In our work, we propose a spatio-temporal graph-based approach with a bi-level chaotic fusion technique to solve this problem. Our model uses separate nonlinear transformation functions to estimate the interval center and width. Additionally, a volatility-aware gating mechanism is used to make predictions dependent on the regime in which the market operates. Temporal dependencies are considered by embedding graph structures and sequentially modeling them. Training is conducted according to a Lower-Upper Bound Estimation (LUBE) objective. Our experimental results show significant improvements compared to existing baselines (LSTM, GRU, GCN, HGNN) when applied to data from 2016 to 2026 with 43 leading companies in eight sectors of the NSE. It provides the lowest Winkler score (0.0778), tightest prediction intervals (PIAW = 0.1407), and highest coverage (PICP = 96.6%), with all differences statistically significant (p < 0.001) according to the Diebold-Mariano test.

</details>


### [10] [Orth-Dion: Eliminating Geometric Mismatch in Distributed Low-Rank Spectral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16341)
*Tatsuhiro Nakamori,Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez,Ganesh Talluri,Ansh Tiwari,Hideyuki Kawashima,Ioannis Mitliagkas,Guillaume Rabusseau,Hiroki Naganuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dion方法使用列归一化导致低秩梯度压缩与谱优化器之间存在几何不匹配，收敛速度比全秩方法慢√r倍；本文提出的Orth-Dion通过QR正交化替代列归一化，消除了该差距，在相同通信成本下匹配全秩谱方法的收敛速率O(√(L_r/T))，实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩梯度压缩方法Dion通过列归一化近似Muon谱优化器，但收敛速度显著慢于全秩谱方法；作者旨在揭示这一性能差距的根本原因并提供解决方案。

Method: 提出Orth-Dion，将Dion中的列归一化替换为QR正交化；理论分析上采用自洽不动点论证（去除有界漂移假设）和时间平均收缩技术（仅要求误差序列平均收缩而非每步收缩）。

Result: Orth-Dion收敛率为O(√(L_r/T))，匹配全秩谱方法；实验上验证了√r的缩放规律，在大规模语言模型预训练中以Dion的通信成本达到了Muon的收敛性能。

Conclusion: 低秩梯度压缩与谱优化器结合时，正确的正交化（QR）比简单列归一化更关键；Orth-Dion在理论上和实验上都证明可以在不增加通信成本的前提下弥合与全秩方法的收敛差距。

Abstract: Low-rank gradient compression reduces communication in distributed training by representing updates with rank-$r$ factors. Dion is a recent method that approximates Muon, a spectral optimizer that orthogonalizes momentum, using one step of power iteration followed by column normalization (rescaling each column of the right factor to unit length). This makes it compatible with fully sharded data parallel training, but it converges more slowly than full-rank spectral methods. We show that this gap is geometric: column normalization does not yield the rank-$r$ polar factor that Muon implicitly targets, so the resulting direction violates the dual-norm constraint of the low-rank spectral geometry, and the rate picks up an extra factor of $\sqrt{r}$ even though the low-rank approximation of the gradient itself is accurate. The same mismatch enters the smoothness term and the error-feedback recursion in the analysis, which has a knock-on effect on empirical performance. We propose Orth-Dion, which replaces column normalization with QR orthogonalization of the right factor. Under non-Euclidean smoothness, with $L_r$ the curvature constant along rank-$r$ directions, Orth-Dion attains rate $O(\sqrt{L_r/T})$, matching exact spectral methods at the same per-step communication cost as Dion. The proof removes the bounded-drift assumption common in prior error-feedback analyses via a self-consistent fixed-point argument, and uses a time-averaged contraction that only requires the error sequence to contract on average rather than at every step. Experiments on large-scale language model pre-training validate the predicted $\sqrt{r}$ scaling and show that Orth-Dion closes the convergence gap to Muon at Dion's communication cost.

</details>


### [11] [LoopQ: Quantization for Recursive Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16343)
*Rui Fang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究循环语言模型（LoopLM）在后训练量化（PTQ）中的挑战，提出循环感知量化框架LoopQ，通过激活缩放、选择性变换、跨循环状态对齐和轨迹优化等策略，在W4A4量化下相比最强静态PTQ基线将下游准确率平均提升68.8%，困惑度降低87.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型通过递归复用Transformer块提升参数效率，但在后训练量化下表现脆弱，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 提出LoopQ框架，包含激活缩放、选择性变换、跨循环状态对齐和轨迹感知优化，在保持共享量化骨干的同时引入轻量级适配。

Result: 在七个基准上，W4A4量化下相比最强静态PTQ基线，下游准确率平均提升68.8%，困惑度平均降低87.7%。

Conclusion: LoopQ有效解决了LoopLM量化中的分布偏移、状态复用和递归误差累积三大挑战，显著提升了量化后的性能。

Abstract: Looped language models (LoopLMs) improve parameter efficiency by recursively reusing Transformer blocks, enabling deeper computation under a fixed model size. However, this reuse makes LoopLMs more fragile under post-training quantization (PTQ). We present the first systematic study of quantization in LoopLMs and identify three challenges: distribution shift across roles, state reuse across loop transitions, and recursive error accumulation. To address these challenges, we propose LoopQ, a loop-aware PTQ framework that preserves a shared quantized backbone while introducing lightweight adaptations. LoopQ combines activation scaling, selective transformation, cross-loop state alignment, and trajectory-aware optimization to reduce distributional mismatch within loops and error accumulation across loops. Experiments across seven benchmarks show that, under W4A4 quantization, LoopQ improves average downstream accuracy by 68.8% and reduces average perplexity by 87.7% compared with the strongest static PTQ baseline.

</details>


### [12] [Goal-Conditioned Supervised Learning for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16345)
*Shijun Li,Kaiwen Dong,Xiang Gao,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种称为目标条件监督学习（GCSL）的离线微调框架，通过将反馈信号直接作为显式目标，并引入基于质量阈值的渐进式目标设计，使模型能够通过纯监督学习实现超越标准离线方法（SFT、DPO）的对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法分为在线和离线两类：在线方法（如RL）效果好但成本高、部署困难；离线方法（SFT、DPO）效率高但存在局限——SFT将分级反馈压缩为二元监督，DPO依赖难以获取的成对偏好数据。因此需要一种既高效又能充分利用分级反馈的离线对齐方法。

Method: 提出GCSL框架：1) 将反馈信号直接作为显式目标，通过纯监督学习训练模型生成达到该目标的响应；2) 引入新的目标定义——学习持续追求超过目标质量阈值的结果，而非模仿高质量子集样本，从而缓解SFT和经典GCSL的边界学习效应；3) 提出自然语言目标表示，更好地利用LLM的语义理解和推理能力。

Result: 在无毒文本生成、代码生成和推荐系统三个任务上，该方法一致优于标准离线微调基线（SFT、DPO等），同时保留了监督学习的高效率、可扩展性和简单的数据需求。

Conclusion: GCSL作为一种纯监督学习的离线微调框架，通过巧妙的"以目标为条件"设计，有效克服了SFT和DPO的固有局限，在不牺牲效率的前提下实现了更优的对齐效果，为LLM对齐提供了一种实用且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models often require fine-tuning to better align their behavior with user intent at deployment. Existing approaches are commonly divided into online and offline paradigms. Online methods, such as RL-based alignment, can directly optimize outcome quality but typically rely on external reward models and iterative rollouts, making them costly and difficult to deploy in many cases. Offline methods are more efficient, but prevailing approaches such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and direct preference optimization (DPO) remain limited: SFT typically collapses graded feedback into binary supervision, while DPO depends on paired preference data that is often unavailable or expensive to construct.
  In this paper, we propose goal-conditioned supervised learning (GCSL) as an offline fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our core idea is to treat feedback signals directly as an explicit goal and train the model, purely through supervised learning, to generate responses that achieve that goal. To better exploit graded feedback, we further introduce a novel goal formulation that defines learning as consistently pursuing outcomes above a target quality threshold, rather than imitating samples from a selected high-quality subset. This design mitigates the bounded-learning effect of SFT and classic GCSL by explicitly guiding the model to learn the directional progression of quality. We also propose natural-language goal representations to better leverage the semantic understanding and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. We evaluate our method on three tasks: non-toxic generation, code generation, and LLM for recommendation. Results show that our approach consistently outperforms standard offline fine-tuning baselines while retaining the efficiency, scalability, and simple data requirements of supervised learning.

</details>


### [13] [PropGuard: Safeguarding LLM-MAS via Propagation-Aware Exploration and Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16346)
*Bingyu Yan,Xiaoming Zhang,Jinyu Hou,Chaozhuo Li,Ziyi Zhou,Xiaozhe Zhang,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PropGuard，一个面向LLM多智能体系统的传播感知防御框架，通过双视角时空图构建、GE-GRPO驱动的异常传播子图挖掘、子图感知诊断和源头导向修复，在多种攻击设置下有效降低攻击成功率并维持高任务防御成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-MAS安全防御方法主要依赖局部过滤或基于图的异常检测，无法追踪细粒度的恶意指令传播路径，也无法在不破坏良性协作的前提下修复被污染的状态，亟需一种能够感知传播过程并实现精准修复的防御框架。

Method: 1) 构建双视角时空图：结合响应中心的风险估计与全状态证据保存；2) 采用GE-GRPO训练的顺序探索器，利用风险先验在全状态图中挖掘紧凑的可疑传播子图；3) 通过子图感知诊断验证有害传播；4) 实施源头引导修复，纠正上游污染并重放受影响的下游交互。

Result: 在四种通信架构和五种攻击设置下的实验表明，PropGuard持续降低了攻击成功率，同时保持高任务级防御成功率，实现了效果与效率之间的良好权衡。

Conclusion: PropGuard通过传播感知的防御机制有效解决了LLM-MAS中恶意指令传播的安全问题，验证了基于子图挖掘与源头修复策略的优越性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) have become a promising paradigm for solving complex tasks through role specialization, tool use, memory, and collaborative reasoning. However, these interactions create new security risks that malicious instructions injected through messages, tools, or memories can propagate across agents and rounds, causing system-level compromise. Existing defenses largely rely on local filtering or graph-based anomaly detection, but they often fail to trace fine-grained propagation paths or remediate contaminated states without disrupting benign collaboration. We propose PropGuard, a propagation-aware framework for safeguarding LLM-MAS. PropGuard constructs a dual-view spatio-temporal graph that combines response-centric risk estimation with full-state evidence preservation. Guided by these risk priors, a GE-GRPO trained inspector sequentially explores the full-state graph to recover compact suspicious propagation subgraphs. PropGuard then verifies harmful propagation through subgraph-aware diagnosis and applies source-guided remediation to correct upstream contamination and replay affected downstream interactions. Experiments across four communication architectures and five attack settings demonstrate that PropGuard consistently lowers attack success while maintaining high task-level defense success, achieving a favorable effectiveness--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [14] [HPC-LLM: Practical Domain Adaptation and Retrieval-Augmented Generation for HPC Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16347)
*Nourin Shahin,Izzat Alsmadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HPC-LLM，一个面向高性能计算工作流的检索增强与领域自适应助手。通过对Llama 3.1 8B进行QLoRA微调（使用约9k-24k条HPC领域数据），在JetStream2集群上评估，该8B模型在性能上接近Qwen 2.5 14B，但GPU内存需求和推理延迟显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研人员在使用HPC基础设施（集群环境、任务调度器、GPU资源、并行计算框架）时面临显著的操作障碍，而通用大语言模型缺乏HPC领域特定的运维知识，无法提供可靠的高性能计算支持。

Method: 提出HPC-LLM框架，集成自动化文档摄取、稠密检索、基于QLoRA的轻量级领域自适应、以及本地推理的模块化编排管道。构建了包含约9,000-24,000条HPC训练样本的语料库（涵盖作业调度、GPU计算、分布式训练、存储系统和集群管理），基于Llama 3.1 8B进行QLoRA微调。

Result: 在JetStream2基础设施上的检索增强设置下，适配后的8B模型性能与Qwen 2.5 14B相当，但GPU内存需求和推理延迟显著更低。

Conclusion: 领域自适应的轻量级模型（8B参数）可以在HPC运维任务中达到与更大型通用模型（14B参数）相近的表现，同时大幅降低计算资源开销，验证了检索增强与领域微调结合方案的有效性。

Abstract: Modern scientific research increasingly depends on High-Performance Computing (HPC) infrastructures, yet many researchers face significant operational barriers when interacting with cluster environments, job schedulers, GPU resources, and parallel computing frameworks. General-purpose large language models (LLMs) provide useful coding assistance but often lack the domain-specific operational knowledge required for reliable HPC support. This paper presents HPC-LLM, a retrieval augmented and domain-adapted assistant designed to support common HPC workflows including Slurm scheduling, MPI execution, GPU utilization, filesystem management, and cluster troubleshooting. The proposed framework integrates automated documentation ingestion, dense retrieval, lightweight domain adaptation using QLoRA, and local inference within a modular orchestration pipeline. To support domain adaptation, we construct an HPC-oriented corpus from publicly available university HPC documentation, curated operational examples, and synthetic instruction-answer pairs generated from retrieved HPC content. The resulting dataset contains approximately 9,000 to 24,000 HPC-focused training examples spanning job scheduling, GPU computing, distributed training, storage systems, and cluster administration topics. We fine-tune Llama 3.1 8B using QLoRA and evaluate the resulting model against several open weight baselines under retrieval-augmented settings on JetStream2 infrastructure. Experimental results indicate that the adapted 8B model achieves performance comparable to substantially larger general-purpose models while operating under significantly lower GPU memory requirements and inference latency. In particular, the adapted model approaches the performance of Qwen 2.5 14B while requiring substantially fewer computational resources.

</details>


### [15] [Flow-Direct: Feedback-Efficient and Reusable Guidance for Flow Models via Non-Parametric Guidance Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16348)
*Kim Yong Tan,Yueming Lyu,Ivor Tsang,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flow-Direct提出了一种通过持久指导场引导生成过程的框架，该指导场基于所有累积的奖励评估样本构建，实现了高反馈效率和可重用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练自由引导方法反馈效率低，因为奖励反馈仅用于局部的梯度近似或离散搜索决策，之后便被丢弃，造成了奖励信息的浪费。

Method: Flow-Direct通过一个持久指导场引导生成，理论上该指导场由基分布与奖励加权目标分布之间的对数密度比解析推导而来；实践中通过非参数估计器从所有累积的奖励评估样本中构建，随着样本增多而不断精确。

Result: 该方法具有两大优势：1) 高反馈效率，每个评估样本都用于优化全局指导场，无奖励信息浪费；2) 自然可重用，优化完成后数据集可用于生成新样本而无需额外奖励评估，不同指导场可组合以满足多目标需求。

Conclusion: Flow-Direct通过持久化的奖励利用机制显著提升了训练自由引导的反馈效率和可重用性。

Abstract: Training-free guidance enables pre-trained diffusion and flow models to optimize application-specific objectives using feedback from external black-box reward functions. However, existing methods are feedback-inefficient because reward feedback is used only transiently to inform a localized gradient approximation or a discrete search decision, and is subsequently discarded. To address this limitation, we propose Flow-Direct, a framework that guides the generation process via a persistent guidance field. Theoretically, this guidance field is analytically derived from the log-density ratio between the base and reward-weighted target distributions; it transports the pre-trained distribution to the target distribution. In practice, the field is implemented as a non-parametric estimator constructed from all accumulated reward-evaluated samples. As more samples are collected during optimization, this empirical guidance field becomes increasingly accurate. This persistent formulation yields two major advantages. First, Flow-Direct is highly feedback-efficient: because every evaluated sample is used to refine the global guidance field, no reward information is wasted. Second, the framework is naturally reusable: once optimization is complete, the collected dataset defines a reusable guidance field for generating novel target samples without additional reward evaluations, and distinct guidance fields can be combined to generate samples that simultaneously satisfy multiple objectives.

</details>


### [16] [Geometric Asymmetry in MoE Specialization: Functional Decorrelation and Representational Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16349)
*Feilong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann统一分析框架，发现MoE层中专家功能解耦与表示子空间重叠并存的不对称几何结构，且路由稀疏性是塑造这种几何形态的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构通过稀疏路由实现可扩展容量，但专家专业化的几何结构仍然未被充分理解，缺乏统一的分析框架来刻画专家在函数空间和表示空间中的行为。

Method: 提出Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann统一框架，在函数空间和表示空间两个层次分析MoE层。在Mistral和Qwen等预训练MoE Transformer上进行实验，并通过控制路由稀疏度进行对比实验。

Result: 发现结构不对称性：专家间Jacobian对齐度持续偏低（功能强解耦），但路由表示占据不同且部分重叠的子空间。Top-k路由导致更尖锐的功能分离和更大的子空间发散，而全软路由导致更纠缠的专家结构。

Conclusion: MoE层可被理解为在共享表示流形上重叠子流形上实现局部解耦算子的几何结构，该框架为研究现代Transformer中的条件计算提供了通用诊断工具。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures achieve scalable capacity through sparse routing, yet the geometric structure of expert specialization remains poorly understood. We introduce a unified Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann framework for analyzing MoE layers in both function space and representation space. Across pretrained MoE Transformers (Mistral, Qwen), we find a consistent structural asymmetry: experts exhibit strong functional decorrelation (consistently low, near-zero cross-expert Jacobian alignment) while their routed representations occupy distinct but partially overlapping subspaces. This indicates that functional decorrelation and representation overlap coexist rather than coincide in MoE specialization. Controlled routing experiments further indicate that routing sparsity appears to be a key factor shaping this geometry: top-k routing induces sharper functional separation and larger subspace divergence, whereas fully soft routing yields more entangled expert structure. Together, these results suggest a geometric interpretation in which MoE layers may be viewed as implementing locally decorrelated operators over overlapping submanifolds on a shared representation manifold, and provide a general diagnostic framework for studying conditional computation in modern Transformer architectures.

</details>


### [17] [Federated Nested Learning: Collaborative Training of Self-Referential Memories for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16350)
*Hong Chen,Pengcheng Wu,Yuanguo Lin,Peilin Zhao,Xiuze Zhou,Fan Lin,Han Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦嵌套学习（FedNL），将联邦学习重新构想为三层嵌套优化系统，通过引入Titans-based线性注意力机制，使客户端能够利用增量规则作为在线梯度步骤进行轻量级零样本测试时自适应，在非独立同分布MMLU和长上下文基准测试中取得了竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的联邦学习仅关注学习静态模型，难以有效处理客户端非独立同分布数据；本文从嵌套学习视角重新审视联邦学习，核心挑战在于如何协作学习优化规则而非仅静态模型。

Method: 提出FedNL框架，将联邦学习重构为三层嵌套优化系统；嵌入Titans-based线性注意力机制，使客户端能够通过将增量规则视为在线梯度步骤，实现轻量级零样本测试时自适应。

Result: 在非独立同分布MMLU和长上下文基准测试中，FedNL在短上下文推理上达到竞争性性能，在长上下文检索和流式交叉熵上性能提升，并保持恒定的推理内存。

Conclusion: FedNL通过嵌套优化视角和线性注意力机制，有效解决了非独立同分布数据下的联邦学习挑战，同时兼顾了长上下文处理能力和内存效率。

Abstract: We rethink Federated Learning (FL) from a nested learning perspective, framing the core challenge as how to collaboratively learn optimization rules, not just static models, to tackle Non-IID client data. To address this, we propose Federated Nested Learning (FedNL), a novel framework that reformulates FL as a three-level nested optimization system. FedNL embeds Titans-based linear attention into FL, enabling clients to perform lightweight, zero-shot test-time adaptation by treating a delta rule as an online gradient step. Experiments on Non-IID MMLU and long-context benchmarks show that FedNL achieves competitive performance in short-context reasoning, enhances the performance of long-context retrieval and streaming Cross-Entropy, and maintains constant inference memory.

</details>


### [18] [Augmenting Human Evaluation with LLM Judges: How Many Human Reviews Do You Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16354)
*Jane Paik Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM评估者角色从替代人类转变为辅助人类，通过两阶段抽样设计（LLM评估全部样本，人类评估部分子样本）并采用双重稳健估计量来系统化LLM-as-a-judge范式，同时提出基于渐近方差进行样本量确定和效能分析的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM作为评估者的应用缺乏严谨的形式化基础，主要依赖人类与LLM评分间的一致性指标来证明替代人类评分的合理性，缺乏研究设计的正式依据。

Method: (1) 将LLM评估者的角色从替代性(substitutive)转变为辅助性(auxiliary)；(2) 将LLM-as-a-judge范式形式化为两阶段抽样设计——第一阶段对所有观测值进行LLM评估，第二阶段对子样本进行人类评分；(3) 采用缺失数据文献中的双重稳健估计量，利用其对抗预测模型错误设定的稳健性；(4) 基于估计量的渐近方差提出样本量确定和效能分析方法。

Result: 提出了基于双重稳健估计量的LLM辅助人类评估框架，给出了样本量分配策略——当LLM评分可预测性不高时应分配更多人类评分，以及如何在目标效能水平下确定人类和LLM评分的样本量。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM评估者的使用提供了正式的形式化基础，填补了基准验证中人类监督应保留多少的指导空白，有望提高AI系统评估的严谨性和效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as automated evaluators of AI systems, including in high-stakes applications. In this role, LLMs are used to generate judgments about the quality, appropriateness, or even safety of model outputs. This approach is motivated by practical constraints. Expert human ratings are costly and difficult to scale, whereas LLM ratings can be produced quickly at low cost. However, current approaches to deploying LLM evaluators are ad hoc, typically limited to reporting agreement metrics between human and LLM judges as a justification for substitution of human ratings, and lack a formal basis for study design. This paper (1) shifts the role of the LLM judge from substitutive to auxiliary, and (2) formulates the LLM-as-a-judge paradigm as one of augmenting human evaluation through a two-stage sampling design, where LLM evaluations are measured for all observations at the first stage and human ratings are partially observed for a subsample at the second stage. We propose to use a doubly robust estimator from the missing data literature, which takes advantage of the robustness property against the prediction model, since the missingness model is known by design. Using the asymptotic variance of this estimator, we propose how sample sizes of human and LLM ratings can be determined to achieve a targeted level of power. We also show that a study can be efficiently designed by allocating more human ratings for types of evaluations where the predictability of LLM ratings is not high. To the best of our knowledge, there is very little guidance on how much human oversight should be retained when validating benchmarks.

</details>


### [19] [TailedTS: Benchmark Dataset for Heavy-Tailed Time Series Prediction and Periodicity Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16361)
*Xinyu Chen,HanQin Cai,Lijun Ding,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailedTS是一个基于2024年维基百科每小时页面浏览量构建的大规模基准数据集，包含约246.9亿数据点，专门用于测试时间序列预测模型在重尾、零膨胀和非高斯条件下的鲁棒性。实验发现高频页面周期性结构显著弱于低频页面，且标准高斯估计器在高流量类别上性能显著下降，而鲁棒替代方法在所有流量尺度上均有一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准数据集（如M4、M5、UCI电力数据集）缺乏或未充分体现真实世界时间序列中常见的重尾分布、零膨胀和非高斯特性，导致模型在这些条件下的鲁棒性评估不足。维基百科流量遵循幂律分布（约5%页面贡献超70%流量），为测试模型对极端波动的鲁棒性提供了自然且严苛的实验环境。

Method: 数据集源自2024年维基百科每小时页面浏览量，涵盖每月约300万个独立页面，总计约246.9亿数据点，采用高效Apache Parquet格式存储。引入基于稀疏自回归（含稀疏性和非负性约束）的周期性量化框架。预测基准测试采用非高斯损失函数套件（ℓ1范数、Huber、分位数、ℓp范数损失）进行评估。

Result: 高频访问页面相比低频页面表现出显著更弱的周期性结构。标准高斯损失函数在高流量页面类别上性能大幅下降，而鲁棒损失函数在所有流量规模上均有一致性能提升。

Conclusion: TailedTS为时间序列预测模型在非理想条件下的鲁棒性评估提供了标准化基准，揭示了周期性随流量规模变化的规律，并证明了鲁棒损失函数在实际大规模流量预测中的有效性。数据集已公开在Zenodo平台。

Abstract: We present TailedTS, a large-scale benchmark dataset derived from Wikipedia hourly page view observations throughout 2024, specifically designed to test time series forecasting models under heavy-tailed, zero-inflated, and non-Gaussian conditions. The dataset comprises approximately 24.69 billion data points spanning roughly 3 million unique Wikipedia pages per month, stored in high-efficiency Apache Parquet format. Wikipedia traffic follows a pronounced power-law distribution where roughly 5% of pages account for over 70% of total page views, creating a natural and rigorous testbed for model robustness against extreme volatility that are absent from or underrepresented in existing benchmarks such as M4, M5, and UCI electricity datasets. TailedTS enables several research tasks. First, we introduce a periodicity quantification framework based on sparse autoregression with sparsity and non-negativity constraints, revealing that frequently-viewed pages exhibit significantly weaker periodic structure than their less-viewed counterparts, showing direct implications for server allocation and traffic forecasting on large digital platforms. Second, we provide standardized prediction benchmarks evaluated under a suite of non-Gaussian loss functions, including $\ell_1$-norm, Huber, quantile, and $\ell_p$-norm losses, demonstrating that standard Gaussian-based estimators degrade substantially on high-volume page categories, while robust alternatives provide consistent gains across all traffic scales. TailedTS is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.17070469.

</details>


### [20] [When Is Rank-1 Steering Cheap? Geometry, Granularity, and Budgeted Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16362)
*John T. Robertson,Jianing Zhu,Haris Vikalo,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出激活向量干预的有效性差异主要源于搜索困难而非概念不可表示，通过几何引导搜索可将恢复最优效用所需的试验次数平均减少39.8%，并引入"概念粒度"指标解释剩余搜索困难，最终构建GRACE框架将激活几何从描述性工具转化为可操作的先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活干预方法的有效性在不同概念间差异显著，先前工作将此归因于许多概念无法被单一方向捕获。本文认为这种差异实际上反映了搜索困难——有用的秩1干预通常存在但难以高效找到。

Method: (1) 将秩1干预形式化为预算约束的优化问题（干预层和系数）；(2) 发现提示边界方向对齐性可预测有效干预位置，实现几何引导搜索；(3) 引入"概念粒度"度量跨对比上下文的向量方向异质性；(4) 构建GRACE框架，利用激活几何诊断干预困难来源、选择适当补救措施并高效分配优化资源。

Result: 几何引导搜索在三个模型家族上平均减少39.8%的试验次数即可恢复最优效用的95%；概念粒度与收敛速度（r=0.44, p<0.001）和最佳效用（r=-0.46, p<0.001）显著相关。

Conclusion: 本文转变了研究视角，从"秩1干预何时失败"转向"秩1干预何时廉价且稳定"，将激活几何从描述性工具转化为可操作的控制先验。

Abstract: Activation steering offers a lightweight way to control LLMs without retraining, but its effectiveness varies sharply across concepts. Prior work often reads this variability as evidence that many concepts are not captured by a single steering direction. We argue instead that much of it reflects search difficulty: a useful rank-1 intervention often exists, but finding it can be expensive.
  We formalize rank-1 steering as a budget-constrained optimization over intervention layer and coefficient. Across concepts and model families, prompt-boundary directional alignment predicts where effective interventions occur, enabling geometry-guided search that reaches high utility with substantially fewer evaluations, reducing the trials needed to recover 95\% of best-found utility by 39.8\% on average across three model families. To explain why some concepts remain expensive even under better search, we introduce \emph{concept granularity}, a measure of directional heterogeneity across contrastive contexts. Granularity distinguishes concepts whose difference vectors share a stable global direction from those where prompts agree locally within each input but the utility-maximizing direction rotates systematically across inputs. Higher granularity is associated with slower convergence and lower best-found performance (Pearson $r{=}0.44$ with trials-to-95\%, $r{=}{-}0.46$ with best-found utility, both $p<0.001$).
  We present \textit{GRACE}, a Granularity- and Representation-Aware Concept Engineering framework that uses activation geometry to diagnose the dominant source of steering difficulty, select the appropriate remedy, and allocate optimization effort efficiently. Our results shift the frame from ``\textit{when does rank-1 fail?}'' to ``\textit{when is rank-1 cheap and stable?}'', turning activation geometry from a descriptive tool into an actionable prior for LLM control.

</details>


### [21] [ORACLE: Anticipating Scams from Partial Trajectories in Streaming App Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16363)
*Wenbo Gao,Songbai Tan,Zhongan Wang,Fei Shen,Gang Xu,Huiping Zhuang,Yunyun Yang,Ming Li,Xiaofeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ORACLE框架，首个用于从流式应用使用轨迹中早期预警诈骗的智能体框架，通过自演进上下文管理器和在线自蒸馏方案，在部分观测下实现跨时间证据重建与早期信号感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机诈骗日益普遍且呈现多阶段、跨应用的特点，意图逐步显现。有效的干预需要在意图明确之前预警诈骗，但现有方法难以处理部分轨迹中时间分布的证据碎片化问题。

Method: 提出ORACLE框架：(1) 自演进上下文管理器，自适应整合实体为中心的跨时间交互；(2) 在线自蒸馏方案，教师模型基于反诈骗反思和线索指导学生模型，无需教师模型访问这些反思，从而蒸馏证据信息知识并提升对部分轨迹中新兴欺诈模式的识别能力。

Result: 实验表明ORACLE在早期诈骗预警方面持续改进，在真实流式场景中提供及时警告并减少误报。

Conclusion: ORACLE框架有效解决了流式应用使用轨迹中早期诈骗预警的挑战，通过跨时间证据重建和早期信号感知实现了高效干预。

Abstract: Smartphone scams are increasingly prevalent and typically manifest as multi-stage, cross-application processes with gradually emerging intent. Effective intervention thus requires anticipating scams before the intent becomes explicit. This is inherently challenging, as decisions must rely on partial trajectories with temporally distributed evidence. In this paper, we propose \textbf{ORACLE} Online Reasoning for Anticipating Cross-temporal Latent thrEats, the first agentic framework for early scam anticipation from \textit{streaming app-usage} trajectories. To support this setting, we curate a real-world long-horizon benchmark of streaming app-usage trajectories, covering 12 scam types, spanning extended periods (15 days on average), involving diverse applications (95 apps), and interleaving normal and scam behaviors. To address fragmented evidence, we introduce a self-evolving context manager that adaptively consolidates entity-centric interactions over time, enabling more effective reconstruction of cross-temporal evidence from partial observations. To enhance sensitivity to latent early-stage signals, we propose an on-policy self-distillation scheme in which a teacher model, conditioned on summarized anti-scam reflections and clues by skills, supervises a student model without access to such reflections. This scheme thereby distills evidence-informed knowledge and improves recognition of emerging fraud patterns from partial trajectories. Experiments show that \method{} consistently improves early scam anticipation, yielding timely warnings while reducing false alerts in realistic streaming scenarios.

</details>


### [22] [Machine Learning-Based Pre-Test Risk Stratification for PCR-Confirmed Chlamydia Using Patient-Reported Data and Urine Biomarkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16365)
*Mehrab Mahdian,Marko Lehes,Katrin Krolov,Tamas Pardy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于机器学习模型的衣原体感染检测前风险分层（PTRS）的可行性，利用患者报告数据和尿液生物标志物等常规非侵入性临床信息。结果显示，患者报告数据模型具有中等区分能力（AUC最高0.72），尿液生物标志物模型表现更稳健，特征整合可提升模型鲁棒性。该工作支持将非侵入性信息整合到筛查流程中以优化检测优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期识别衣原体感染高风险个体可在资源有限的筛查中优化分子检测的使用，本研究旨在探索利用常规可用、非侵入性临床数据进行检测前风险分层的可行性。

Method: 使用93份尿液样本（PCR参考标签）的 curated 数据集，分析三组特征（患者报告病史与症状、尿液生物标志物、两者组合），采用五种监督分类器，通过分层5折交叉验证和袋外概率估计进行评估，性能指标包括AUC及阈值相关指标，不确定性通过自助法置信区间量化。

Result: 仅使用患者报告数据的模型AUC最高达0.72，尿液生物标志物模型区分能力略低但更稳健，集成方法表现最佳；组合特征组小幅提升峰值AUC并减少性能变异，增强了模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 尿液生物标志物提供了与患者报告信息互补的可靠预测信号，特征整合增强了鲁棒性。该研究支持将非侵入性常规信息整合到筛查流程中（包括分散或家庭PCR检测场景）以优化检测优先级。

Abstract: Early identification of individuals at elevated risk of Chlamydia trachomatis infection may enable optimal use of molecular testing in resource-aware screening. We evaluate the feasibility of pre-test risk stratification (PTRS) using machine-learning models trained on routinely available, non-invasive clinical data.
  A curated dataset of 93 urine samples with PCR reference labels was analyzed using three feature groups: patient-reported history and symptoms, urine biomarkers from standard urinalysis, and their combination. Five supervised classifiers were evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation with out-of-fold probability estimates. Performance was assessed using area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) and threshold-dependent metrics, with uncertainty quantified via bootstrap confidence intervals.
  Models using only patient-reported data showed moderate discrimination (AUC up to 0.72). Urine biomarker-based models demonstrated slightly lower peak discrimination but more consistent performance, with ensemble methods yielding the strongest results. Combining feature groups marginally increased the peak AUC and reduced performance variability across models, indicating improved robustness.
  Findings indicate that urine biomarkers provide a reliable predictive signal for PTRS that is complementary to patient-reported information, while feature integration enhances robustness. This work supports the integration of non-invasive, routinely available information for PTRS into screening workflows, including decentralized or home-based PCR contexts, to optimize testing prioritization.

</details>


### [23] [M$^2$FedAQI: Multimodal Federated Learning for Air Quality Prediction on Heterogeneous Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16375)
*Manjil Nepal,Kimsie Phan,Tamoghna Ojha,Aritra Dutta,M Krishna Siva Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出M²FedAQI，一种面向异构边缘设备的轻量级多模态联邦学习框架，通过特征调制融合机制整合视觉和表格模态数据，用于分布式空气质量指数预测。实验表明其在分类和回归任务上显著优于现有方法，并在资源利用和通信安全方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空气质量预测方法多依赖集中式学习，面临扩展性差、隐私风险高和通信开销大等问题；同时，现有联邦学习方法仅使用单模态数据，难以捕捉复杂环境模式。

Method: 提出M²FedAQI轻量级多模态联邦框架，通过特征调制融合机制实现视觉与表格模态的高效跨模态交互，并集成TLS认证保障通信安全，无需修改底层联邦学习协议。

Result: 在PM25Vision和TRAQID数据集上，分类任务Accuracy提升最高11.0%，AUC提升3.53%，F1-score提升12.2%；回归任务R²提升18.0%，MAE和RMSE分别降低25.4%和20.4%。在异构边缘设备上实现高效的通信、内存和计算资源利用。

Conclusion: M²FedAQI是一种高效、安全且资源友好的多模态联邦学习方法，为分布式IoT环境下的空气质量预测提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate air quality prediction is essential for public health, environmental monitoring, and industrial safety. However, most existing approaches rely on centralized learning paradigms, which introduce challenges related to scalability, privacy preservation, and communication overhead in distributed Internet of Things (IoT) environments. Moreover, current federated learning (FL) based solutions predominantly utilize unimodal data, limiting their capability to capture complex environmental patterns. To address these limitations, we propose M$^2$FedAQI, a lightweight multimodal federated framework for decentralized Air Quality Index (AQI) prediction across heterogeneous edge devices. The proposed framework integrates visual and tabular modalities through a feature modulation based fusion mechanism that enables efficient cross-modal interaction while maintaining low computational overhead. M$^2$FedAQI is evaluated on two benchmark datasets, PM25Vision and TRAQID, for both classification and regression tasks under centralized and federated settings. Experimental results demonstrate that M$^2$FedAQI consistently outperforms existing approaches, achieving improvements of up to 11.0\% in Accuracy, 3.53\% in AUC, 12.2\% in F1-score, and 18.0\% in $R^2$, while reducing MAE and RMSE by up to 25.4\% and 20.4\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baselines. Furthermore, deployment on heterogeneous edge devices demonstrates efficient resource utilization in terms of communication overhead, memory footprint, and computational cost. To enhance communication security, TLS-based authentication is incorporated to ensure secure client participation and protect the FL communication channel from unauthorized third-party access without modifying the underlying FL protocol.

</details>


### [24] [Mixing Times of Glauber Dynamics on Masked Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16378)
*Suvadip Sana,Sami Wolf,Neer Mehta,Alina Shah,Aitzaz Shaikh,Janna Goodman,Lionel Levine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了掩码语言模型（MLM）的条件分布不兼容性，将其迭代生成过程建模为Glauber动力学马尔可夫链，理论分析了高低温下的混合行为，发现了温度依赖的相变现象及语义盆地中的亚稳态特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLM定义的是局部条件分布，但不保证对应任何一致的全局联合分布。这引发了一个根本性问题：当迭代使用这些条件分布进行生成时，会诱导出怎样的全局分布行为？

Method: 将迭代掩码标记重采样建模为离散标记序列空间上的Glauber动力学马尔可夫链；提出矩形测试来验证MLM条件分布的不兼容性；在交叉标记影响有界条件下建立高温收缩结果（O(n log n)混合时间）；在均匀局部边界条件下证明低温指数缓慢逃逸的亚稳态；通过温度与序列长度的相变实验验证理论预测。

Result: 发现MLM条件分布存在内在不兼容性，且在现代MLM中普遍存在；高温下链在O(n log n)时间内混合，低温下表现出亚稳态且指数慢逃逸；观测到温度依赖的相变现象；通过语义轨迹识别出长期陷阱和循环语义盆地等持久结构。

Conclusion: MLM的迭代生成行为具有温度依赖的相变特性：高温下快速混合接近均衡，低温下陷入语义盆地呈现亚稳态；这种动力学行为可通过Glauber动力学框架有效地刻画和理解。

Abstract: Masked language models (MLMs) define local conditional distributions over tokens but do not, in general, correspond to any consistent joint distribution over sequences. This raises a fundamental question: what global distributional behavior is induced when such conditionals are used iteratively for generation? We address this question by modeling iterative masked-token resampling as a Glauber dynamics Markov chain on the discrete space of token sequences. We first show that MLM conditionals are intrinsically incompatible: we introduce a rectangle test that certifies this incompatibility and empirically verify its prevalence across modern MLMs. We then provide a theoretical analysis of the induced Markov chain. Under bounded cross-token influence, we establish a high-temperature contraction result implying $O(n\log n)$ mixing time where $n$ is the sequence length. In contrast, we prove that under a uniform local margin condition, the chain exhibits metastability, with exponentially slow escape from semantic basins at low temperatures. Empirically, we demonstrate a phase transition in mixing behavior as a function of temperature and sequence length, consistent with the theoretical predictions. We further characterize the induced stationary behavior through semantic trajectories, identifying persistent structures such as long-lived traps and recurrent semantic basins, with political content serving as a measurable case study.

</details>


### [25] [An Information-Theoretic Criterion for Efficient Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16379)
*Hanyu Li,Zhengqi Sun,Xiaotie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度解释合成数据训练大模型的效果不一致现象：只有当生成-训练循环是信息开放（依赖外部信号注入任务相关信息）时才有提升，信息封闭循环（仅依赖模型自身输出）会导致信息减少和崩溃。信息开放管线的效率与泛化取决于监督的元层级——粗粒度信号（如二元正确性）因不绑定特定领域而自然跨任务泛化。学习偏好收敛于信息效率最高的信号成分，这既加速学习也可能导致奖励黑客行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在LLM训练中效果高度不一致，缺乏统一理论解释。作者希望从信息论角度为这一现象提供严谨的理论框架，解释何时合成数据有效、何时导致崩溃，并揭示学习过程中的偏好收敛机制。

Method: 采用信息论分析，核心运用数据处理不等式（Data Processing Inequality）证明信息封闭循环中任务相关信息必然递减；通过信息开放与信息封闭的对比分析，结合监督信号粒度（二元正确性 vs 细粒度反馈）的元层级差异，提出"学习收敛于信息效率最高信号成分"的指导性论点。

Result: 信息开放循环有效、信息封闭循环必然崩溃；粗粒度外部信号（如二元正确性）因不绑定特定表面形式而具有更强的跨任务泛化能力；学习倾向于收敛到信息效率最高的信号成分，这既是加速学习的原因，也是奖励黑客行为的根源。

Conclusion: 合成数据训练LLM的有效性取决于生成-训练循环是否信息开放，以及监督信号的元层级设计。该理论框架统一解释了合成数据的成功与失败案例，并为设计更可靠的合成数据训练管线提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Synthetic data becomes crucial for large language model training, but its effectiveness is highly inconsistent. We provide an information-theoretic account of this inconsistency: synthetic data improves a model only when the generation-training loop is information-open, i.e., shaped by external signals (verifiers, environments, or rubrics) that inject task-relevant information beyond the model's current distribution. When the loop is information-closed (relying on the model's own outputs without such signals), the data processing inequality ensures that task-relevant information can only decrease, making collapse a predicted outcome. Among information-open pipelines, both efficiency and generalization hinge on the meta-level of supervision: a coarser signal such as binary correctness treats all acceptable outputs as equivalent, so the behavior it teaches is not tied to any particular domain or surface form and generalizes naturally across tasks and domains. These observations lead to a guiding thesis: learning preferentially converges to the most information-efficient signal component available, which accelerates learning when that component is the intended one, but causes reward hacking when a spurious pattern happens to be simpler.

</details>


### [26] [ReTAMamba: Reliability-Aware Temporal Aggregation with Mamba for Irregular Clinical Time Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16380)
*Jinwoong Kim,Sangjin Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReTAMamba模型，通过可靠性感知的时间聚合、时间变量标记序列、时间顺序编织和有预算标记路由，有效处理临床时间序列中的不规则采样、缺失值和异构观测模式。在MIMIC-IV、eICU和PhysioNet 2012上分别提升AUPRC 7.51%、7.80%和10.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用观测掩码和时间间隔信息，但未能持续捕捉过去观测的衰减可靠性，也未能在聚合过程中将多分辨率信息组织到连贯的时间上下文中。

Method: 1) 将临床时间序列重构为时间变量标记序列；2) 从缺失性和经过时间估计观测可靠性；3) 使用统计描述符增强区间摘要；4) 通过时间顺序编织整合短期和长期时间信息；5) 使用有预算标记路由器约束序列长度同时保留信息摘要。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、eICU和PhysioNet 2012上AUPRC相对提升分别为7.51%、7.80%和10.15%；eICU上动态信号（心率、血压）的学习平均衰减比静态信号（实验室检验变量）大24.3%。

Conclusion: 有效的不规则临床时间序列预测不仅要建模测量了什么，还要建模何时以及如何观测，包括信息新鲜度和观测及时性。

Abstract: Clinical time-series data are difficult to model with methods designed for regular sequences because they exhibit irregular sampling, frequent missing values, and heterogeneous observation patterns across variables. Existing approaches commonly use observation masks and time-gap information, but they do not continuously capture the decaying reliability of past observations or consistently organize multi-resolution information within a coherent temporal context during aggregation. To address these limitations, we propose Reliability-aware Temporal Aggregation with Mamba (ReTAMamba), which reconstructs clinical time series as time-variable token sequences, estimates observation reliability from missingness and elapsed time, and augments interval summaries with statistical descriptors. Chronological Weaving is used to integrate short- and long-term temporal information within a coherent temporal context, and a budgeted token router is applied to constrain sequence length while preserving informative summaries. Experiments on MIMIC-IV, eICU, and PhysioNet 2012 show that ReTAMamba consistently improves AUPRC over strong baselines, with average relative gains of 7.51%, 7.80%, and 10.15%, respectively. Cohort-level and patient-level analyses on eICU further showed that the learned mean decay for more dynamic signals, such as heart rate and blood pressure, was 24.3% larger than that for relatively static signals, such as laboratory test variables. These findings suggest that effective prediction in irregular clinical time series requires modeling not only what was measured, but also when and how it was observed, including information freshness and observation timeliness.

</details>


### [27] [QuantFPFlow: Quantum Amplitude Estimation for Fokker--Planck Policy Optimisation in Continuous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16429)
*Abraham Itzhak Weinberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QuantFPFlow框架，将量子振幅估计集成到Fokker-Planck随机策略优化中，在连续空间强化学习中实现配分函数估计的二次加速（从O(1/ε²)降至O(1/ε)），并利用FP扩散匹配驱动的探索奖励机制防止策略过早收敛，在连续控制任务上相比SAC更频繁地发现全局最优解（33.9% vs 30.7%），同时保持策略熵稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典连续空间RL估计FP配分函数Z=∫e^(-V(x)/D)dx的代价为O(1/ε²)，计算效率低下；此外，标准RL方法（如SAC）在多模态奖励景观中容易陷入局部最优，策略熵迅速坍塌导致过早收敛。因此需要一种更高效的估计方法和更鲁棒的探索机制。

Method: 1) 将量子振幅估计（Grover放大）集成到FP公式中替代经典配分函数估计，实现O(1/ε)的二次加速；2) 利用估计的稳态分布ρ*设计探索奖励Raug = Renv + α log(1/ρ*(s))，引导智能体探索全局最优区域；3) 通过FP扩散匹配约束策略方差，防止策略熵坍塌。

Result: 1) 连续控制任务上，QuantFPFlow平均奖励1295.7±423.2 vs SAC的1284.0±474.0；2) 全局最优发现频率33.9% vs 30.7%，提升10.4%；3) 策略熵保持~6.5 nats，SAC降至1.5 nats；4) 维度实验显示计算缩放O(d^0.35) vs 经典FP的O(d^0.76)。

Conclusion: QuantFPFlow通过量子振幅估计实现了配分函数估计的理论二次加速，其量子启发式经典模拟已展现出O(1/ε)的算法结构。FP扩散匹配机制有效防止策略过早收敛，在连续控制任务上优于SAC。尽管完全量子加速需要容错硬件，但该框架为量子增强RL提供了理论可行的路径。

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{QuantFPFlow}, a reinforcement learning framework that integrates quantum amplitude estimation into the Fokker--Planck~(FP) formulation of stochastic policy optimisation. Classical continuous-space RL agents must estimate the FP partition function $Z = \int e^{-V(\mathbf{x})/D}\,d\mathbf{x}$ at cost $\calO(1/\varepsilon^{2})$; QuantFPFlow replaces this with a Grover-amplified amplitude estimator achieving $\calO(1/\varepsilon)$ -- a provable quadratic speedup. While the full quantum acceleration requires fault-tolerant hardware, the quantum-inspired classical simulation demonstrated here already exhibits the $\calO(1/\varepsilon)$ algorithmic structure.
  The estimated stationary distribution $\rhostar$ drives a theoretically grounded exploration bonus $\Raug = \Renv + α\log(1/\rhostar(s))$. This bonus steers the agent toward globally optimal regions of multimodal reward landscapes while simultaneously constraining policy variance through FP diffusion matching.
  On a continuous-control task specifically designed to expose local-optima failure, QuantFPFlow achieves mean reward $1{,}295.7 \pm 423.2$ versus $1{,}284.0 \pm 474.0$ for Soft Actor-Critic~(SAC), while discovering the global optimum \textbf{10.4\,\% more frequently} (33.9\,\% vs.\ 30.7\,\%). Policy entropy remains near $H(π)\approx 6.5$\,nats throughout training, whereas SAC collapses to $1.5$\,nats, confirming that FP diffusion matching actively prevents premature convergence. Dimensionality experiments further show computational scaling of $\calO(d^{0.35})$ for QuantFPFlow versus $\calO(d^{0.76})$ for classical FP estimation.

</details>


### [28] [A Theory of Training Profit-Optimal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16430)
*Sophie Hao,William Merrill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文结合缩放定律与微观经济学理论，构建了LLM训练厂商的经济模型，分析在计算受限和数据受限两种情境下，模型质量提升如何转化为收入以及利润最优的训练策略。研究发现：计算受限时最优模型规模与硬件效率近线性相关，训练成本亚平方增长；数据受限时利润最优训练支出与数据量平方成正比、与硬件效率成反比。当前行业趋势符合计算受限下的宽松模型假设，但在数据受限或硬件进步停滞时并非利润最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已知扩大LLM规模能可靠提升模型质量（损失或下游评估），但尚不清楚质量提升如何转化为潜在收入，以及收入增长是否能抵消更大规模训练和推理的成本。作者试图填补这一空白，建立可量化分析利润最优训练决策的理论框架。

Method: 将缩放定律（scaling laws）与微观经济学理论结合，构建包含消费者质量阈值采纳行为、训练与推理成本结构的厂商利润最大化模型。分别在计算受限（compute-bound）和数据受限（data-bound）两种资源约束下进行理论分析，推导最优模型参数量、token预算和训练支出的解析表达式。

Result: 计算受限时：最优模型大小和token预算与硬件效率E（FLOPs/$）近线性正相关，训练总成本随E亚平方增长，数据效率提升会激励更大模型和更多训练支出。数据受限时：利润最优训练支出与数据量D的平方成正比、与硬件效率E成反比。当前行业训练支出趋势与计算受限下的宽松模型变体一致，但在数据受限或硬件进步停滞情境下并非利润最优。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解利润最优的LLM训练提供了理论框架，有助于批判性地审视行业关于训练投入的论述，并为长期经济决策提供支撑。

Abstract: Scaling LLMs requires tremendous computational resources, and recent advances in AI have gone hand in hand with massive amounts of capital expenditure. While it is established that scaling up LLMs reliably increases model quality (quantified in terms of loss or downstream evaluations), it is unclear how these quality improvements translate to potential revenue, and whether revenue increases would offset costs of larger-scale training and inference. In this work, we develop an economic model for characterizing the rational behavior of an LLM training firm by combining scaling laws with microeconomic theory. Under our model of firm behavior, LLM quality can be increased with more parameters and training tokens, leading to more potential adoption by consumers, who each have a quality threshold for using the LLM. On the other hand, additional parameters and training tokens both incur additional costs. We analyze the profit maximization problem for this model under compute-bound and data-bound regimes. In the compute-bound regime, optimal model size and token budget track hardware efficiency $E$ (FLOPs/\$) at a near-linear rate; total training cost then scales sub-quadratically in $E$. Data efficiency improvements incentivize larger models and training expenditure. When we are limited to $D$ data, profit-optimal training expenditure scales as $D^2/E$, i.e, increase with data and decreases with hardware efficiency (as well as data efficiency). Finally, we analyze practical trends in training expenditure: current trends are consistent with our most permissive model variants in the compute-bound regime, but are not profit-optimal in the data-bound regime or assuming hardware advances will stall. Overall, our results provide a theory of profit-optimal LLM training, providing a foundation for engaging critically with industry statements and supporting long-term economic decision making.

</details>


### [29] [Edge-AI-Driven Learning-to-Rank for Decentralized Task Allocation in Circular Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16433)
*Mohammadhossein Ghahramani,Yan Qiao,Mengchu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于排序感知协商的边缘AI驱动去中心化任务分配框架，通过将轻量级决策智能嵌入机器层级，实现高负载和紧迫截止时间场景下延迟、截止时间违规、吞吐量和能耗的优化，验证了学习目标与去中心化决策结构对齐的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造系统中的任务分配面临去中心化决策、动态工作负载和共享资源约束的挑战，特别是在循环制造场景中还需平衡运营效率与资源能源可持续性。现有学习方法多聚焦于预测绝对性能指标，但去中心化分配本质上由候选机器的相对排序决定，这些指标未必能转化为实际的分配改进。

Method: 框架分三阶段渐进式构建：(1) 资源感知启发式建立去中心化投标结构；(2) Edge-AI回归模型提供学习的局部投标近似；(3) 排序感知公式重塑学习目标以对齐胜选者选择的排序本质。每台机器利用本地信息（处理能力、队列状态、资源争用）评估传入任务。

Result: 在高负载和紧迫截止时间场景下进行离散事件仿真评估，结果显示框架在高负载下改善了延迟和截止时间遵守率，在更紧约束下提升了能效，实现了更符合循环制造目标的资源高效运行。

Conclusion: 将学习目标与去中心化决策结构对齐对于有效的协商驱动任务分配至关重要。

Abstract: Task allocation in smart manufacturing systems needs to operate under decentralized decision-making, dynamic workloads, and shared resource constraints. In circular manufacturing settings, these challenges are further intensified by the need to balance operational efficiency with resource and energy sustainability. While learning-based approaches have been explored, many focus on predicting absolute performance metrics that do not necessarily translate into improved allocation outcomes, since decentralized assignment is governed by the relative ordering of candidate machines. This work proposes an Edge-AI-driven decentralized task allocation framework based on ranking-aware negotiation, where lightweight decision intelligence is embedded at the machine level to enable low-latency coordination without centralized control. The framework is developed progressively: a resource-aware heuristic first establishes the decentralized bidding structure, an Edge-AI-based regression model then provides learned local bid approximation, and a ranking-aware formulation finally reshapes the learning objective to align with the ordering-based nature of winner selection. Each machine evaluates incoming tasks using local information, including processing capability, queue state, and resource contention. The framework is evaluated via discrete-event simulation under high-load and tight-deadline scenarios using delay, deadline violations, throughput, and energy consumption. Results show improved delay and deadline adherence under high load, and enhanced energy efficiency under tighter constraints, leading to more resource-efficient operation aligned with circular manufacturing objectives. These findings demonstrate that aligning learning objectives with decentralized decision structures is critical for effective negotiation-driven task allocation.

</details>


### [30] [GPU-Accelerated Deep Learning for Heatwave Prediction and Urban Heat Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16435)
*Adis Alihodžić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于GPU的深度学习框架，利用MODIS地表温度数据和Open-Meteo气象预报数据，对萨拉热窝进行次日城市热状况预测和热风险评估。ConvLSTM结合混合损失函数取得最佳性能（MAE=0.2293, RMSE=0.3089, R2=0.8877），并可通过更长时序和更多气象变量进一步提升效果。该框架可在GPU上混合精度训练，并能够结合暴露度和脆弱性数据生成城市热风险地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市热浪是一个重要问题，而气候变化使这一问题更加严峻，因此需要有效的城市热状况预测和热风险评估工具。

Method: 使用MODIS地表温度数据和Open-Meteo预报数据，在萨拉热窝进行实验，测试了卷积模型和时空模型（包括ConvLSTM），采用混合损失函数，并在GPU上以混合精度进行训练。

Result: ConvLSTM模型表现最佳：MAE=0.2293, RMSE=0.3089, R2=0.8877；更长时序和更多气象变量可提升效果；GPU混合精度训练减少了执行时间。

Conclusion: 该框架可结合热危害、暴露度和脆弱性数据生成城市热风险地图，作为城市热分析的实际基础。

Abstract: Heatwaves are an important problem in cities, and climate change makes this problem more difficult. In this paper, we present a GPU-based deep learning framework for next-day prediction of urban thermal conditions and for heat risk assessment. The study was carried out in Sarajevo by using MODIS land surface temperature data and Open-Meteo forecast data. We tested several models, including convolutional models and spatiotemporal models. Among them, ConvLSTM with a mixed loss function gave the best results. The obtained values were MAE = 0.2293, RMSE = 0.3089, and R2 = 0.8877. The experiments also showed that results can be improved by using longer temporal series and additional meteorological variables. Since the framework was implemented on a GPU and trained with mixed precision, the execution time was reduced. Based on the predicted temperature fields, it was also possible to combine hazard information with exposure and vulnerability data in order to generate city heat risk maps. The proposed framework can be used as a practical basis for city heat analysis.

</details>


### [31] [Byzantine-Resilient Federated Learning via QUBO-Based Client Selection on Quantum Annealers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16438)
*Andras Ferenczi,Sutapa Samanta,Dagen Wang,Jason Qizhe Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子退火的QUBO方法进行拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习客户端选择，通过将选择问题转化为二次无约束二元优化问题，利用量子退火器求解全局最优子集；针对大规模场景设计MultiSignal集成机制，在100客户端MNIST任务上达到95.3%检测准确率，比经典MultiKrum提升3.5个百分点，尤其对复杂拜占庭攻击效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在大规模场景下易受恶意客户端拜占庭攻击，现有鲁棒聚合方法（如MultiKrum）采用贪心逐客户端评分策略，无法有效检测那些保留统计特性的复杂恶意更新，亟需一种能全局联合优化的客户端选择方法。

Method: 将客户端选择建模为QUBO问题，编码成对距离为代价函数，由量子退火器求解全局最优的m个客户端子集；针对大规模场景提出MultiSignal集成框架，使用基于欧氏距离和余弦距离Krum分数差距的双特征路由门将攻击分为四类，对规避攻击采用嫌疑惩罚型QUBO加一致性投票处理。

Result: 15客户端场景下，QUBO对Advanced LIE攻击检测准确率在MNIST达95.11%（MultiKrum 81.33%），CIFAR-10达97.78%（MultiKrum 75.56%）；100客户端MNIST上MultiSignal平均检测准确率95.3%（MultiKrum 91.8%），Sparse Lie提升23.2个百分点，Advanced Lie提升4.8个百分点。

Conclusion: QUBO量子退火方法结合MultiSignal是一种有原则且可扩展的防御策略，能有效应对联邦学习中最具挑战性的拜占庭攻击，且与MultiKrum在简单攻击场景上具有互补性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) trains a global model across decentralized clients while preserving data privacy, but at scale it is vulnerable to malicious updates. Byzantine-resilient aggregation methods such as MultiKrum score gradients against their nearest neighbors and can miss malicious updates that preserve the statistical properties of honest ones. We propose a quantum annealing approach that reformulates client selection as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) problem, encoding pairwise distances into a cost function solved by quantum annealers (QA). Unlike MultiKrum's greedy per-client scoring, the QUBO formulation jointly optimizes over all subsets to find the mutually closest group of $m$ clients. At small scale (15 clients), QUBO outperforms MultiKrum on the most challenging Byzantine attacks: e.g., Advanced LIE is detected with 95.11% accuracy versus 81.33% on MNIST and 97.78% versus 75.56% on CIFAR-10. QUBO fares poorly on simpler attacks where MultiKrum excels, so the two methods are complementary. QUBO quality also degrades as the number of clients grows. To address this, we introduce a MultiSignal ensemble that uses a dual-feature routing gate based on Euclidean and cosine Krum score gaps to classify attacks into four regimes and routes evasion attacks to a suspicion-penalized QUBO with agreement voting. At 100 clients on MNIST, MultiSignal achieves 95.3% average detection accuracy versus 91.8% for classical MultiKrum, with the largest gains on Sparse Lie (72.0% to 95.2%, +23.2 points) and Advanced Lie (80.4% to 85.2%, +4.8 points). These results show that QUBO-based quantum annealing with MultiSignal is a principled and scalable defense against the most challenging Byzantine strategies in federated learning.

</details>


### [32] [DeepArrhythmia: Segment-Contextualized ECG Arrhythmia Classification via Selective Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16441)
*Jiahui Li,Ruili Fang,Zishuai Liu,WenZhan Song,Jin Lu,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepArrhythmia是一个基于工具的多模态框架，通过结合原始ECG信号和波形图像，利用智能体机制在节拍级别进行心律失常分类，并动态选择最小或丰富的生理证据来优化决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG心律失常检测系统将每个心跳视为孤立的局部实例，忽略了心律失常标签往往依赖于多心跳的节律上下文（包括时序、代偿性间歇和心跳间形态一致性），导致分类不准确。

Method: 该方法构建了一个工具接地（tool-grounded）的多模态框架：1) 输入多心跳ECG段，同时使用原始ECG信号和渲染波形图像；2) 利用专用工具进行R峰定位、节律-形态数值提取和形态学文本分析；3) 基于段级置信度在最小证据和丰富证据状态之间动态路由，实现选择性证据获取。

Result: 提出了一种将生理测量与证据集成解耦的智能体设计，能够整合节律上下文、显式生理基础并选择性获取证据进行决策。

Conclusion: 通过智能体化的多模态设计，DeepArrhythmia能更好地利用节律上下文信息进行节拍级心律失常分类，并且并非所有情况下都需要丰富的生理证据，动态路由策略可以提高效率。

Abstract: Beat-level Electrocardiography (ECG) arrhythmia detection aims to assign an arrhythmia class to each beat in a recording, yet many existing systems treat beats as isolated local instances. This is limiting because beat labels often depend on multi-beat rhythm context, including timing, compensatory pauses, and beat-to-beat morphological consistency. We present DeepArrhythmia, a tool-grounded multimodal framework for segment-contextualized beat-level ECG arrhythmia classification. Given a multi-beat ECG segment, DeepArrhythmia combines the raw ECG signal and a rendered waveform image, localizes R peaks to identify beat instances, and produces structured beat-level predictions. The framework decouples physiological measurement from evidence integration using specialized tools for beat localization, numerical rhythm--morphology extraction, and morphology-focused textual analysis. DeepArrhythmia uses segment-level confidence to route between minimal and rich evidence states, since richer physiological evidence is not uniformly useful. This agentic design integrates rhythm context, explicit physiological grounding, and selective evidence acquisition for decision making.

</details>


### [33] [Avoiding Structural Failure Modes in Tabular Fair SSL: Online Primal-Dual Allocation under Confidence Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16446)
*Hangchun Liang,Changchun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在线原始-对偶分配（OPDA）方法，通过动态调度公平性与熵稳定性惩罚，解决了表格数据公平半监督学习中静态权重导致的掩码坍缩和琐碎饱和两种失效模式，无需每数据集调参即可获得非退化工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的表格应用（医疗、信贷、再犯预测）需要统计公平性保证，但现有半监督学习在结合公平性正则化时存在结构冲突——基于置信度门控的伪标签机制与矩匹配公平性正则器相互作用，会触发掩码坍缩（公平性侵蚀置信度，致使伪标签匮乏）和琐碎饱和（漂移至常数预测器）两种失效模式，且静态公平性权重需针对每个数据集手动调整。

Method: 提出在线原始-对偶分配（OPDA）控制器，利用违规度、风险、伪标签健康度等多信号动态调度公平性与熵基稳定性惩罚；在诊断应激测试框架下与静态权重及简单单信号自适应基线进行对比评估。

Result: 在Adult、ACSIncome、COMPAS三个表格基准数据集上，OPDA缓解了静态权重和简单基线下的退化状态；在Adult和COMPAS上达到与静态λ经验前沿竞争的非退化工作点；在ACSIncome上以更宽的公平性-效用权衡范围保持效用；完整版相比轻量版主要在ACSIncome上向更高效用偏移。

Conclusion: OPDA是一种无需校准的控制器，能在表格公平半监督学习中无需每数据集调参即可获得非退化工作点。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) enables prediction with limited labels, but high-stakes tabular applications (medical, credit, recidivism) require statistical fairness guarantees. We identify a structural conflict in tabular fair SSL through a diagnostic stress test: under confidence-gated pseudo-labeling, moment-matching fairness regularizers can trigger two failure modes -- Masking Collapse (fairness erodes confidence, starving pseudo-labels) and Trivial Saturation (drift to constant predictors). We propose Online Primal-Dual Allocation (OPDA), an online controller that schedules fairness and entropy-based stability penalties using violation, risk, and pseudo-label health signals, avoiding per-dataset selection of a fixed fairness weight within this diagnostic regime. On the evaluated tabular benchmarks (Adult, ACSIncome, COMPAS), OPDA mitigates the degenerate regimes observed under static weighting and simple single-signal adaptive baselines. On Adult and COMPAS, it yields non-degenerate operating points competitive with the empirical static-$λ$ frontier; on ACSIncome, it preserves utility with a wider fairness-utility spread. Relative to OPDA-lite, the full controller mainly shifts the operating point toward higher utility on ACSIncome, while Adult highlights the fairness-utility trade-off between the two variants. These results position OPDA as a calibration-free controller for non-degenerate operating points in tabular fair SSL without per-dataset tuning.

</details>


### [34] [Nested Spatio-Temporal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16447)
*Yinghao Ai,Yukai Zhou,Ruoxi Jiang,Junyi An,Chao Qu,Zhijian Zhou,Shiyu Wang,Fenglei Cao,Zenglin Xu,Furao Shen,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种嵌套式时空预测框架，通过谱聚类构建语义一致区域提取宏观未来趋势，以粗到细方式引导微观预测，在高维数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空预测方法依赖静态历史空间先验，无法捕捉动态时序相关且易受系统性误差影响，亟需一种能利用未来宏观趋势指导细粒度预测的方法。

Method: 提出嵌套预测框架：(1) 基于谱聚类的语义一致区域构建，理论+经验证明可滤除噪声并保留趋势；(2) 渐进式粗到细预测器，将宏观区域趋势整合到微观推理中，使模型能提前预判动态异常（如周期性偏移）。

Result: 在多个高维数据集上，该方法一致性地优于当前最先进的基线模型，验证了未来宏观引导的嵌套预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 将未来宏观区域趋势作为自上而下的引导信号，与传统仅依赖历史局部信息的范式互补，为时空预测提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal forecasting is critical for real-world applications like traffic management, yet capturing reliable interactions remains challenging under noisy and non-stationary conditions. Existing methods primarily rely on historical spatial priors, often failing to account for evolving temporal correlations and suffering from systematic errors. In this work, we propose a nested forecasting framework that couples future macro-level regional trends with micro-level historical observations, enabling top-down guidance from abstract future representations for fine-grained forecasting. Specifically, we employ a spectral clustering-based approach to construct semantically coherent regions, providing both theoretical and empirical evidence that this representation effectively filters systematic noise while preserving essential trends. Building on this, we develop a progressive coarse-to-fine predictor to integrate these representative features into the inference process. This enables the model to leverage trend predictions to anticipate dynamic anomalies, such as periodic offsets, in advance. Furthermore, extensive experiments on multiple high-dimensional datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating the effectiveness of future macro-guided nested forecasting.

</details>


### [35] [PESD-TSF: A Period-Aware and Explicit Structured Decomposition Framework for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16449)
*Hua Wang,Xianhao Jiao,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PESD-TSF框架，通过乘法周期门控、多尺度结构化编码器和跨尺度协作注意力机制，解决深度预测模型中周期感知衰减、趋势噪声纠缠及变量间依赖破坏问题，在多元时序预测上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度预测模型存在三个关键问题：(1) 深度增加导致周期感知衰减和趋势-噪声表征纠缠；(2) 通道独立范式破坏变量间的动态协调关系，难以建模跨变量一致性。

Method: 提出PESD-TSF框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) 乘法周期门控(MPG)：引入连续时间先验动态调制信号幅度，保持深层周期结构；(2) 多尺度结构化编码器：结合去趋势注意力与层次采样，显式解耦长期趋势与高频变化；(3) 跨尺度协作注意力(CSCA)+RLC正则化：重建深层特征空间中的全局变量间拓扑，通过正交性与一致性约束强制执行物理一致协作。

Result: 在多个领域基准数据集上持续取得SOTA性能，在涉及复杂变量耦合的多元预测任务上表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: PESD-TSF通过物理启发的结构化分解框架，同时提升了可解释性和预测精度，展现了优越的结构建模能力和泛化性。

Abstract: Deep forecasting models often suffer from attenuated periodic perception and entangled trend-noise representations as network depth increases. Moreover, the widely adopted channel-independent paradigm, while improving training stability, disrupts intrinsic dynamic coordination among variables, hindering the modeling of cross-variable consistency in multivariate time series. To address these issues, we propose PESD-TSF, a physics-inspired structured decomposition framework for long-term time series forecasting that jointly emphasizes interpretability and predictive accuracy. PESD-TSF introduces three key designs. First, a Multiplicative Periodic Gating mechanism incorporates continuous-time priors to dynamically modulate signal amplitudes, preserving periodic structures across deep layers. Second, a multi-scale structured encoder integrates detrended attention with hierarchical sampling to explicitly decouple long-term trends from high-frequency variations while retaining fine-grained temporal semantics. Third, to recover disrupted inter-variable dependencies, we propose Cross-Scale Collaborative Attention (CSCA) together with an RLC regularization scheme, which reconstructs global inter-variable topology in deep feature spaces and enforces physically consistent collaboration through orthogonality and consistency constraints. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets from multiple domains demonstrate that PESD-TSF consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly strong gains on multivariate forecasting tasks involving complex inter-variable coupling, highlighting its superior structural modeling capability and generalization.

</details>


### [36] [Physics-Guided Geometric Diffusion for Macro Placement Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16451)
*Jongho Yoon,Jinsung Jeon,Seokhyeong Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MacroDiff+ 是一个基于物理引导的几何扩散框架，用于VLSI宏单元布局。它通过双域去噪架构（异质GNN编码拓扑连接 + Transformer建模全局几何上下文）和物理引导采样策略，在ISPD2005 MMS基准上实现了6.1-6.2%的线长缩减，并在大规模设计中展现出优越的稳定性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动布局方法难以处理序列依赖关系，且无法平衡拓扑连接与物理约束之间的矛盾。

Method: 提出MacroDiff+物理引导几何扩散框架，包含：(1) 双域去噪架构，耦合异质GNN编码的拓扑连接与Transformer建模的全局几何上下文；(2) 物理引导采样推理策略，利用显式梯度引导生成，确保统计合理性与物理有效性。

Result: 在ISPD2005 MMS基准上，MacroDiff+相较最先进基线方法实现了6.1-6.2%的线长缩减，并在先前方法无法收敛的大规模设计中展现出优越的稳定性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: MacroDiff+通过物理引导的扩散框架有效解决了宏单元布局中拓扑与物理约束的平衡问题，显著提升了布局质量、稳定性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Macro placement is a pivotal stage in VLSI physical design, fundamentally determining the overall chip performance. Recent data-driven placement methods have demonstrated significant potential, yet they often struggle to handle sequential dependencies and to balance topological connectivity with physical constraints. To bridge this gap, we propose MacroDiff+, a physics-guided geometric diffusion framework. Specifically, we design a dual-domain denoising architecture that couples topological connectivity encoded by heterogeneous GNNs with global geometric context modeled by a Transformer. Furthermore, we introduce Physics-Guided Sampling, an inference strategy that actively steers the generation using explicit gradients to ensure both statistical plausibility and physical validity. On the ISPD2005 MMS benchmarks, MacroDiff+ outperforms state-of-the-art baselines with a 6.1-6.2% reduction in wirelength. Notably, it exhibits superior stability and scalability on large-scale designs where prior methods fail to converge. The source code is available at https://github.com/jhy00n/MacroDiff-plus.

</details>


### [37] [Peak-Detector: Explainable Peak Detection via Instruction-Tuned Large Language Models in Physiological Sign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16452)
*Jiahui Li,Yida Zhang,Zixuan Zeng,Jiayu Chen,Yingjian Song,Yin Xiao,Nishan Dong,Junjie Lu,Younghoon Kwon,Xiang Zhang,Jin Lu,Wenzhan Song,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Peak-Detector框架，利用指令微调的大语言模型实现跨模态（ECG/PPG/BCG/BSG）可解释的心跳峰值检测，通过峰值表征技术将时序数据压缩为事件级表征，结合监督微调与强化学习优化，在7个数据集上达到最优或持平性能，并具备自解释能力支持错误分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统峰值检测算法依赖专家知识、局限于单一信号模态，而深度学习方法缺乏可解释性，限制了专家验证和人机交互。亟需一种跨模态、鲁棒且可解释的峰值检测框架。

Method: 提出"峰值表征"技术，将时序数据压缩为保留关键事件信息的紧凑格式；采用两阶段优化：监督微调（SFT）→ 强化学习（RL）多目标奖励函数；构建Peak-Explanation数据集微调模型自解释能力；利用指令微调LLM进行跨模态推理。

Result: 在ECG、PPG、BCG、BSG四种模态共7个数据集（6个公开基准+1个真实世界队列）上，在临床相关时间容差下达到最优或持平性能，且生成的推理过程能够揭示失败模式并支持验证和错误分析。

Conclusion: 指令微调LLM结合峰值表征技术可有效实现跨模态、可解释的峰值检测，在保持高精度的同时提供透明推理，为专家-计算机交互提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurate peak detection across diverse cardiac physiological signals, including the Electrocardiogram (ECG), Photoplethysmogram (PPG), Ballistocardiogram (BCG), and Bodyseismography (BSG), is fundamental for cardiovascular monitoring but is often hindered by artifacts and signal variability. Conventional algorithms are typically engineered with expert knowledge for a single signal modality, limiting their generalizability. Conversely, deep learning-based methods often lack interpretability, limiting transparency for expert verification and hindering expert-computer interaction. To address these limitations, we introduce Peak-Detector, a novel framework that leverages instruction-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) for robust, cross-modal, and explainable peak detection. A core innovation of our framework is a "peak-representation" technique that transforms time-series data into a condensed format, preserving critical event information while significantly reducing signal length. This representation provides a crucial inductive bias, guiding the LLM to reason over physiologically meaningful events rather than raw, noisy data. The model is optimized through a two-stage process: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning (RL) with a multi-objective reward function. The model's self-explanation capabilities are cultivated by fine-tuning on a custom-built Peak-Explanation dataset. Across four modalities-ECG, PPG, BCG, and BSG-spanning seven datasets (six public benchmarks plus one real-world cohort), Peak-Detector demonstrates strong cross-modal performance, achieving best or tied-best detection under clinically relevant temporal tolerance. Beyond accuracy, the generated rationales surface failure modes and support verification and error analysis.

</details>


### [38] [Identifiable Token Correspondence for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16457)
*Youngin Kim,Ray Sun,Inho Kim,Bumsoo Park,Hyun Oh Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于Transformer的世界模型在长程推演中存在的时间不一致性问题（物体重复、消失、变形），本文将下一帧预测公式化为具有潜在token对应变量的结构化概率推断问题，提出每个下一帧token要么从上一帧复制，要么生成新token的模型，在4个基准上达到SOTA，Craftax-classic基准上回报率72.5%、得分35.6%，显著超越之前最优的67.4%和27.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的视觉强化学习世界模型在长程推演中常出现时间不一致性问题（物体重复、消失、变形），根本原因在于现有方法将下一帧预测纯粹视为token生成问题，没有显式建模跨时间步的token对应关系。

Method: 将下一帧预测公式化为具有潜在token对应变量的结构化概率推断问题，每个下一帧token通过两种方式生成：从上一帧复制已有的token，或生成全新的token。

Result: 在4个具有挑战性的基准上达到SOTA性能。在Craftax-classic基准上，回报率达到72.5%，得分为35.6%，显著超越之前最优的67.4%和27.9%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模跨时间步的token对应关系，有效解决了基于Transformer的世界模型在长程推演中的时间不一致性问题，显著提升了性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based world models have shown strong performance in visual reinforcement learning, but often suffer from temporal inconsistency in long-horizon rollouts, including object duplication, disappearance, and transmutation. A key reason is that most existing approaches treat next-frame prediction purely as a token generation problem, without explicitly modeling correspondence between tokens across time. We formulate next-frame prediction as a structured probabilistic inference problem with latent token correspondence variables, deriving a model in which each next-frame token is explained either by copying a token from the previous frame or by generating a new token. Our experiments show state-of-the-art performance on 4 challenging benchmarks. The proposed method achieves a return of 72.5% and a score of 35.6% on the Craftax-classic benchmark, significantly surpassing the previous best of 67.4% and 27.9%. We release our source code on https://github.com/snu-mllab/Identifiable-Token-Correspondence.

</details>


### [39] [Seeking the Unfamiliar but Memorable: Conceptual Creativity as Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16477)
*Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出创造力是生成对自适应观察者最初陌生但能快速学习的新概念，通过Creator-Appraiser框架实现：Creator生成候选，Appraiser通过少量内循环学习步适应，将Appraiser的提升作为Creator优化的奖励。使用冻结的扩散模型作为Creator，在MNIST上用自编码器、在自然图像上用带低秩适配器的CLIP作为Appraiser，元学习梯度足以产生基础模型无法单独生成的风格变化和概念组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨如何创造新概念而非仅仅检索熟悉概念，为机器创造力提供形式化定义和可计算方法。

Method: 提出Creator-Appraiser框架：Creator（扩散模型）生成候选样本，Appraiser（自编码器或带LoRA的CLIP）通过少量内循环学习步适应新样本，将Appraiser的损失下降作为奖励信号反向优化Creator。扩散模型保持冻结，无需额外语言条件，仅通过元学习梯度驱动。

Result: 该方法能产生基础扩散模型无法单独生成的风格变化和概念组合，证明了元学习梯度足以驱动创造性生成。

Conclusion: 机器创造力可形式化为Creator-Appraiser的元学习过程，生成对观察者陌生但可快速学习的新概念，为创造性AI提供了可操作的理论框架。

Abstract: What does it mean to create a new concept, rather than retrieve a familiar one? Repeatedly sampling a generative model at the same prompt produces variations with similar styles and typical content. We propose that creativity is the production of stimuli that are unfamiliar to an adaptive observer at first sight, but quickly learnable from a few exposures. We formalize this as a Creator-Appraiser pair: a Creator generates a candidate, an Appraiser adapts to it for a few inner-loop learning steps, and the Appraiser's improvement becomes the reward the Creator optimizes through. We instantiate the framework with diffusion as the Creator, an autoencoder Appraiser on MNIST, and a CLIP Appraiser with a low-rank adapter for natural images. The diffusion model remains frozen with no additional language conditioning; the meta-learning gradient is enough to produce both stylistic variations and concept compositions that the base model does not generate on its own.

</details>


### [40] [Global Convergence of Sampling-Based Nonconvex Optimization through Diffusion-Style Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16520)
*Zeji Yi,Chaoyi Pan,Guanya Shi,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过平滑化视角建立了采样优化(SBO)的非渐近收敛分析，揭示了平滑化带来的覆盖-最优性权衡，并提出了一种可证明收敛到全局最优的退火SBO算法DIDA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样优化方法（如交叉熵方法、进化算法）在无梯度非凸问题中成效显著，但其收敛性理论理解不足，亟需建立严谨的收敛分析框架。

Method: 将SBO重新表述为平滑目标上的梯度下降（类似扩散模型的噪声条件得分上升），进行了平滑目标的地形分析，并提出了扩散启发式双退火算法DIDA。

Result: 揭示了平滑化的覆盖-最优性权衡（扩大凸区域但引入最优性间隙），建立了SBO到全局最优邻域的非渐近收敛保证，证明DIDA可收敛到全局最优。

Conclusion: 平滑化视角统一了SBO与扩散模型的理论，DIDA在无梯度优化中表现优越，并为扩散模型提供了理论启示。

Abstract: Sampling-based optimization (SBO), like cross-entropy method and evolutionary algorithms, has achieved many successes in solving non-convex problems without gradients, yet its convergence is poorly understood. In this paper, we establish a non-asymptotic convergence analysis for SBO through the lens of smoothing. Specifically, we recast SBO as gradient descent on a smoothed objective, mirroring noise-conditioned score ascent in diffusion models. Our first contribution is a landscape analysis of the smoothed objective, demonstrating how smoothing helps escape local minima and uncovering a fundamental coverage-optimality trade-off: smoothing renders the landscape more benign by enlarging the locally convex region around the global minimizer, but at the cost of introducing an optimality gap. Building on this insight, we establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for SBO algorithms to a neighborhood of the global minimizer. Furthermore, we propose an annealed SBO algorithm, Diffusion-Inspired Dual-Annealing (DIDA), which is provably convergent to the global optimum. We conduct extensive numerical experiments to verify our landscape results and also demonstrate the compelling performance of DIDA compared to other gradient-free optimization methods. Lastly, we discuss implications of our results for diffusion models.

</details>

*Kyrie Zhao,Zehong Wang,Tianyi Ma,Fang Wu,Xiangru Tang,Pietro Lio,Sheng Wang,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出超图模式机（HGPM），从消息传递范式转向学习子集的组合模式，通过标记化组合子集、构建包含DAG并训练包含感知Transformer，在十项超图基准测试中达到或超越SOTA，并能识别现有方法无法区分的特征相同但作用不同的药物组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图学习方法仅通过消息传递在观测超边上传播信息，未能建模高阶关系中的组合信号（组合性、涌现性、抑制性），导致在药物联合用药等实际场景中无法区分特征相同但作用截然不同的药物组合，存在安全风险。

Method: HGPM将组合子集进行标记化（tokenization），构建包含偏序关系的DAG（有向无环图），并训练一个包含感知的Transformer，通过掩码重建目标来学习子集间的组合模式。

Result: 在十个超图基准数据集上达到或超越现有最先进方法；在真实不良事件预测任务中，成功识别出特征完全相同的候选药物中哪一个添加后能抑制副作用，这是现有方法无法做到的。

Conclusion: HGPM通过显式建模高阶关系的组合性信号，在超图学习任务上取得了更优性能，尤其在需要区分组合效应的关键应用（如药物安全）中展现了独特优势。

Abstract: Hypergraphs model higher-order relations that drive real-world decisions, from drug prescriptions to recommendations. A central structural signal in such data, beyond what pairwise relations can express, is interaction compositionality: whether a higher-order relation is compositional, emergent, or inhibitory with respect to its observed or unobserved sets. In polypharmacy, the regime decides whether a drug should be dropped, kept, or excluded: a compositional drug triple can be safely simplified, an emergent triple requires all drugs jointly, and an inhibitory triple flags a drug that disrupts an existing interaction. However, existing hypergraph learning methods, which merely propagate messages over observed hyperedges, leave this compositional signal unmodeled, allowing dangerous drug combinations to slip through and be misclassified. To this end, we propose the Hypergraph Pattern Machine (HGPM), shifting the paradigm from message passing to learning the compositional pattern of subsets. It tokenizes compositional subsets, organizes them in an inclusion DAG, and trains an inclusion-aware Transformer under masked reconstruction. On ten hypergraph benchmarks, HGPM matches or exceeds state-of-the-art methods. Notably, in a real adverse-event prediction case, HGPM correctly identifies the drug addition that inhibits the side effect among feature-identical candidates, a discrimination existing methods cannot make. The code and data are in https://github.com/KryieZhao/HGPM.git.

</details>

*Qiangwei Peng,Lezhi Chen,Peijie Zhou*

TL;DR: MUST-FM是一个利用层次化数据结构和迁移先验知识来加速非平衡最优传输（UOT）计算的模拟无关框架，能够在降低计算开销的同时实现大规模单细胞数据集上稳健且生物学有意义的轨迹推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡最优传输（UOT）虽然为单细胞动态建模提供了理论基础，但其高计算成本限制了在大规模数据集上的应用。现有UOT近似方法很少利用单细胞数据中普遍存在的层次化标注和已知迁移先验信息。

Conclusion: MUST-FM通过利用多尺度结构和监督先验有效解决了UOT在大规模单细胞数据中的可扩展性问题，为大规模动态建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Unbalanced optimal transport (UOT) provides a principled framework for modeling single-cell transitions and birth-death dynamics, but its high computational cost limits scalability to large-scale datasets. Although single-cell data often contain hierarchical annotations and known transition priors, existing UOT approximations rarely exploit this multiscale structure or prior knowledge. We introduce Multiscale Supervised Unbalanced Optimal Transport Flow Matching (MUST-FM), a simulation-free framework that scales UOT by leveraging hierarchical data structure. MUST-FM further supports an optional supervised formulation that incorporates transition priors, such as cell lineages, to guide the learning of displacement fields and mass variations. Experiments show that MUST-FM reduces computational overhead while achieving robust and biologically meaningful trajectory inference, enabling dynamic modeling of atlas-scale single-cell datasets.

</details>

*Mehrzad Khosravi,Max Kleiman-Weiner,Hema Yoganarasimhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分层实验室任务研究消费者在序贯选择中的跨情境知识迁移（元学习），发现参与者能在不同航线间迁移品牌经验，且一种有限理性的元动态规划模型（BRMDP(1)）最能拟合人类行为，表明消费者以粗略方式迁移品牌规律，而非完全整合的贝叶斯学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统消费者决策模型假设学习在每次新情境中重新开始，但在现实中，消费者会将先前品牌经验迁移到后续相关决策中。本文旨在研究这种跨情境知识迁移（元学习）在序贯选择中的机制和特征。

Method: 设计分层实验室任务，参与者多次在不同航线中选择航空公司，观察含噪二元结果。对比人类选择与无迁移基准及完全整合贝叶斯元学习基准的表现，引入有限理性元动态规划策略族（BRMDP(D)），通过超后验分布采样数量D来近似完全整合。

Result: 参与者不仅在同一航线内表现提升，在不同航区间也表现出迁移效应。低D的有限理性元学习（尤其是BRMDP(1)）比无迁移和完全整合贝叶斯迁移更好地拟合参与者行为。

Conclusion: 消费者确实进行跨情境的品牌规律迁移，但方式较为粗略（使用较少的超后验分布采样）。消费者学习模型应考虑近似跨情境迁移，基于无迁移或完全整合学习的管理反事实分析可能产生误导。

Abstract: Many consumer decisions are repeated choices under uncertainty. Standard models capture these decisions using Bayesian learning and dynamic programming: consumers update beliefs from feedback and use those beliefs to guide future choices. In many markets, however, learning does not restart when consumers enter a new context: prior experience with a brand, product, or provider can shape beliefs in later, related decisions. We study this cross-context knowledge transfer, or meta-learning, in sequential choice. We design a hierarchical laboratory task in which participants repeatedly choose among airlines across routes and observe noisy binary outcomes. Reduced-form evidence shows that participants improve not only within routes, but also across routes: they choose better airlines earlier in later routes and reduce pseudo-regret. To identify the mechanism behind this transfer, we compare human choices to a no-transfer benchmark and a fully integrated Bayesian meta-learning benchmark. In particular, we introduce a class of boundedly rational meta dynamic programming policies, BRMDP(D), that approximate full integration using a limited number of hyper-posterior draws, denoted by D. Trial-by-trial likelihood comparisons show that low-D boundedly rational meta-learning, especially BRMDP(1), fits participant behavior better than both no transfer and fully integrated Bayesian transfer. Consumers, therefore, transfer brand-level regularities across contexts, but through coarse representations of prior uncertainty. The findings imply that models of consumer learning should allow for approximate cross-context transfer, and that managerial counterfactuals based on either no-transfer or fully integrated learning can be misleading.

</details>
