*Arne Nix,Robert James,Lasse Borgholt,Anna B. Ekner,Lana Krumm,Julius Severin,Dan Engel,Lars Maaløe,Jakob Havtorn*

TL;DR: 本文提出Symphony for Speech-to-Text，一个面向医疗场景的医学级语音识别系统，通过将转录过程分解为识别、格式化和上下文校正等专门组件，在临床基准测试中显著超越现有最先进系统，同时保持通用领域的竞争力，并发布了临床基准数据集和生产级API。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗语音识别面临专业术语、上下文歧义、临床缩写等挑战，现有系统要么针对通用转录优化，要么局限于狭义的听写流程，在安全关键场景中可靠性不足，难以适应更广泛的临床工作流。

Method: Symphony将转录过程分解为识别、格式化和上下文校正三个专门组件，分别优化医学术语召回、生成临床结构化文本，并支持实时流式处理和批量文件处理，从而在不同用例间自适应。

Result: 在公开基准和医疗语音数据集上的评估表明，Symphony在临床场景中大幅优于现有最先进系统，在通用领域达到或超越其性能，展现出鲁棒的泛化能力而非过拟合。

Conclusion: Symphony作为医疗级语音识别系统，通过模块化解耦设计在专业医疗场景和通用场景均取得优异表现，并提供了临床基准数据集和生产级API，有望推动医疗语音识别的可靠验证与进一步发展。

Abstract: After decades of use in dictation and, more recently, ambient documentation, speech is emerging as a primary modality for interacting with technology and AI in healthcare. Yet medical speech recognition remains difficult: systems must capture specialized terminology, resolve contextual ambiguity, and render measurements, abbreviations, and clinical shorthand precisely. Existing solutions are typically optimized either for general-purpose transcription or narrow dictation workflows, limiting their reliability in safety-critical settings and their usefulness for broader clinical workflows. We introduce Symphony for Speech-to-Text, a medical-grade speech recognition system for real-time streaming and batch file-based clinical use. Symphony decomposes the transcription process into specialized components for recognition, formatting, and contextual correction to optimize medical term recall while producing clinically structured text in real time and adapting across use cases. Evaluations on public benchmark and medical speech datasets show that Symphony substantially outperforms state-of-the-art systems in clinical settings while matching or exceeding them in general-domain settings, suggesting robust generalization rather than overfitting. We release a clinical benchmark dataset to support reliable validation and further progress in medical speech recognition. Symphony is available through a production-grade API for live dictation, conversational transcription, and batch audio file processing.

</details>


### [45] [World Model-Enabled Causal Digital Twins for Semantic Communications in Physical AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16547)
*Lingyi Wang,Tingyu Shui,Walid Saad,Pascal Adjakple*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向物理AI闭环系统的因果信息价值（CIV）度量与基于世界模型的因果数字孪生（WM-CDT）框架，将语义通信问题建模为长周期每比特回报最大化问题，通过反事实推理和演员-评论家策略实现语义传输与控制的高效协同，在无人机导航仿真中显著提升了每千比特回报和导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信方案大多针对单次任务并优化瞬时性能，无法支持具有闭环感知-通信-推理-控制的物理AI系统，其中传输的语义不仅影响当前推理结果，还会影响未来控制动作、状态演化及长期任务性能。

Method: 将问题建模为比特预算约束下的长期每比特回报最大化；提出因果信息价值（CIV）度量以评估每个语义token对期望长期回报的边际贡献；提出基于世界模型的因果数字孪生（WM-CDT）框架，通过反事实推理进行长时域想象展开训练演员-评论家策略和语义token选择器（基于CIV每比特评估）。

Result: 在AirSim-Sionna无人机导航仿真平台上，WM-CDT框架在每千比特回报和导航成功率上显著优于现有强化学习方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的CIV度量和WM-CDT框架有效弥合了语义通信与闭环物理AI系统之间的鸿沟，实现了通信效率与控制效率的联合优化。

Abstract: Semantic communication has emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling goal-oriented networking. However, most existing semantic communication solutions are tailored to one-shot tasks and optimize instantaneous performance. Hence, they cannot be used to support closed-loop dynamic systems with physical artificial intelligence (AI), in which the transmitted semantics affect not only the current inference outcome but also future control actions, state evolution, and ultimately long-horizon task performance. To address this gap, this paper investigates goal-oriented semantic communications for physical AI systems with closed-loop sensing-communication-inference-control. In particular, the problem of semantic communications is formulated as a long-term return-per-bit maximization under wireless bit-budget constraints while capturing both control efficiency and communication efficiency. To solve this problem, a novel causal information value (CIV) metric is introduced to evaluate the marginal contribution of each semantic token to the expected long-term return by transmission interventions. Then, a world-model-enabled causal digital twin (WM-CDT) framework is proposed to capture the dynamics of closed-loop physical AI systems and enable counterfactual reasoning for long-horizon imagined rollouts. Based on these imagined rollouts, an actor-critic policy is trained for long-horizon agent control with high data efficiency, while the semantic token selector is trained through CIV-per-bit evaluation. Extensive simulations on an AirSim-Sionna-based unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) navigation simulator show that the proposed WM-CDT framework achieves significant improvement in return-per-kbit and navigation success rate compared to existing reinforcement learning solutions.

</details>


### [46] [Wavelet Flow Matching for Multi-Scale Physics Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16573)
*Gabriele Accarino,Juan Nathaniel,Carla Roesch,Pierre Gentine,Sara Shamekh,Duncan Watson-Parris,Viviana Acquaviva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Wavelet Flow Matching (WFM)，一种在小波空间中结合最优传输的生成式仿真器，无需预训练自编码器，在混沌流体动力学系统上实现长期稳定性和频谱一致性优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式仿真器在捕捉精细结构方面优于确定性仿真器，但存在计算成本高的问题；潜空间生成模型需额外预训练自编码器。WFM旨在打破精度与成本之间的权衡。

Method: 利用U-Net的分层结构，在小波空间中直接预测最优传输速度，无需学习潜在压缩表示；将最优传输与小波多尺度表示相结合。

Result: 在三个混沌流体动力学系统上，WFM在长期自回归稳定性、精度和频谱一致性方面均优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: 小波空间是一种无需训练的有效表示空间，适用于复杂物理动力学的生成式仿真。

Abstract: Accurate emulation of multi-scale physical systems governed by PDEs demands models that remain stable over long autoregressive rollouts while preserving fine-scale structures. Deterministic emulators produce overly-smoothed predictions, while generative approaches better capture details but are costly. Latent-space generative models have emerged as a compromise but with the additional cost of separately pre-trained autoencoders. We propose Wavelet Flow Matching (WFM), a novel generative emulator that overcomes current trade-offs between cost and skill by performing optimal-transport directly in the multi-scale wavelet space. Rather than learning a latent compression, WFM leverages the hierarchical structure of a U-Net to jointly predict transport velocities of a prescribed wavelet representation. On three challenging systems of chaotic fluid dynamics, WFM achieves superior long-horizon stability, accuracy and spectral coherence compared to state-of-the-art models. Our results clearly position the wavelet space as an effective training-free representation for generative emulation of complex physical dynamics.

</details>


### [47] [Structure-Aware Masking for Protein Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16581)
*Thomas Walton,Ayan Goel,Amirali Aghazadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构感知的掩码策略——Bucket Masking，通过优先掩盖三维空间中结构耦合的残基，代替传统的随机掩码，在蛋白质适应度预测任务中提升了最高14%的性能，并证明改进源自掩码位置而非跨度大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MLM在蛋白质语言模型训练中随机掩盖单个残基，忽略了蛋白质序列中三维结构依赖和长程残基接触所导致的非局部耦合，无法有效建模对蛋白质功能至关重要的长程相互作用。

Method: 提出Bucket Masking策略，根据残基在三维空间中的距离选择残基组，优先掩盖结构耦合区域，通过将掩码分布条件化于残基接触信息，使学习目标偏向于建模长程相互作用。

Conclusion: 通过控制消融实验证明，性能提升源于掩码放置的位置（位置归纳偏置）而非跨度大小，确立了掩码策略作为一种位置归纳偏置的有效性。

Abstract: Masked language modeling (MLM) is the standard objective for training protein language models, typically implemented by randomly masking individual residues at a fixed rate (e.g., 15%). This practice implicitly assumes that all sequence positions contribute equally to representation learning. In downstream fitness prediction tasks, however, protein sequences are governed by three-dimensional structural dependencies and long-range residue contacts that induce strong nonlocal couplings between residues. We introduce Bucket Masking, a structure-aware masking strategy that selects groups of residues based on their proximity in three-dimensional space, preferentially masking structurally coupled regions during training. By conditioning the masking distribution on residue contacts, Bucket Masking shifts the learning objective toward modeling long-range interactions that are critical for protein function. Across four downstream protein fitness prediction tasks, Bucket Masking enables up to a 14% improvement over standard random masking, excelling at predicting higher-order mutational interactions. Through controlled ablations, we show that these improvements arise from mask placement rather than span size, establishing masking as a positional inductive bias.

</details>


### [48] [How Few-Shot Examples Add Up: A Causal Decomposition of Function Vectors in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16591)
*Entang Wang,Yiwei Wang,Aleksandra Bakalova,Michael Hahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了上下文学习（ICL）中少样本提示的机制：n-shot函数向量（FV）可近似为单个示例子FV的线性组合，且模型会根据上下文动态调整各示例的注意力权重，使更信息量、更少歧义的示例主导FV；因果分解表明，查询-键对齐（尤其在歧义场景下）是FV质量的主要贡献来源，而值更新效应更具异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏对少样本提示如何塑造模型的函数向量（FV）——一种驱动ICL查询任务行为的因果激活方向——的机制性解释。

Method: 通过线性近似验证n-shot FV可分解为示例级子FV的线性组合；分析注意力权重在上下文中的自适应重分配；采用因果分解方法将查询-键路由与值更新分离，分别评估其对FV质量的贡献。

Result: n-shot FV可近似为示例级子FV的线性组合（加性和可组合性）；模型根据上下文自适应地重新加权示例的贡献，注意力转向更信息量、更少歧义的示例；查询-键对齐（尤其在歧义场景下）对FV质量贡献最一致，值更新效应更具异质性。

Conclusion: 将加性叠加与上下文依赖的注意力重新加权统一为一种可测试的机制性解释，阐明了少样本提示如何实现任务执行。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) excels at new tasks from minimal examples, yet we still lack a mechanistic explanation of how few-shot prompts shape a model's function vector (FV)--a causal activation direction that drives task behavior on the ICL query. Across tasks and models, an $n$-shot FV is well-approximated by a linear combination of example-level sub-FVs, suggesting additive and composable contributions from individual demonstrations. Beyond additivity, we show that models contextualize individual examples' representations based on prior examples to adaptively reweight which demonstrations dominate the FV: attention shifts toward examples that are more informative and less ambiguous under the context. Finally, a causal decomposition separates Query-Key routing from Value updates, finding that contextualization's most consistent contributions to FV quality arise from Query-Key alignment--particularly in ambiguous settings--while Value-mediated effects are more heterogeneous. Together, these results unify additive superposition with context-dependent attention reweighting into a mechanistic, testable account of how few-shot prompts implement tasks.

</details>


### [49] [Where Pretraining writes and Alignment reads: the asymmetry of Transformer weight space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16600)
*Valeria Ruscio,Eli-Shaoul Khedouri,Keiran Thompson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过相对子空间分数探针揭示了交叉熵预训练与偏好对齐在Transformer权重中留下的几何痕迹差异：对齐更新集中在读通路（W_Q, W_K）的注意力输入激活主方向上，而在写通路（W_O, W_2）中保持近各向同性。这一模式由各向异性梯度累积解释——读通路的输入激活协方差呈尖峰结构导致目标无关的集中，写通路的梯度各向异性取决于损失函数（交叉熵提供尖锐信号，对齐目标贡献有限）。通过检查点轨迹、对比目标控制和秩-1干预提供了因果证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究交叉熵预训练与偏好对齐这两种训练范式在相同Transformer权重上留下的几何痕迹差异，理解其背后的机制原理。

Method: 提出相对子空间分数探针，跟踪权重增量与残差流激活子空间及解嵌入预测子空间的对齐程度；通过各向异性梯度累积理论解释模式；使用检查点轨迹、对比目标控制实验和秩-1干预进行因果验证。

Result: 对齐更新集中在读通路（W_Q, W_K）沿注意力输入激活主方向，写通路（W_O, W_2）在预测子空间中保持近各向同性；读通路由输入激活的尖峰协方差驱动，写通路的各向异性取决于损失函数的梯度结构。

Conclusion: 交叉熵提供尖锐的逐样本梯度信号，在预训练中塑造写通路的预测几何；对齐目标在写通路侧添加的集中度有限，导致权重空间中的非对称几何痕迹。该发现通过因果干预得到验证。

Abstract: Cross-entropy pretraining and preference alignment update the same transformer weights, but leave geometrically distinct traces. We characterise this asymmetry with a relative-subspace-fraction probe that tracks how weight deltas align with residual-stream activation subspaces and with the prediction subspace defined by the unembedding. Alignment deltas concentrate in the read pathway ($W_Q$, $W_K$), along principal directions of attention-input activations, while remaining near-isotropic in the write pathway ($W_O$, $W_2$) relative to the prediction subspace. We explain this pattern through anisotropic gradient accumulation: updates to a matrix $W$ are sums of outer products $δ_t a_t^\top$, and inherit directional structure from whichever side has concentrated covariance. For read-pathway matrices, this side is the input activation $a_t$, whose covariance is spiked in trained transformers and therefore produces objective-agnostic concentration. For write-pathway matrices, the relevant side is the upstream gradient $δ_t$, whose anisotropy depends on the loss. Cross-entropy supplies the canonical sharp per-sample signal, inducing write-pathway prediction geometry during pretraining; alignment objectives typically add little further write-side concentration. We support this explanation with a within-checkpoint trajectory, a graded contrastive-objective control, and a closed-form rank-1 intervention with matched direction controls, providing causal evidence for the proposed weight-space geometry.

</details>


### [50] [To MRL or not to MRL: Text Embeddings are Robust to Truncation Without Matryoshka Embeddings, Except In Heavy Truncation Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16608)
*Sotaro Takeshita,Yurina Takeshita,Simone Paolo Ponzetto,Daniel Ruffinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）与随机截断在文本嵌入压缩中的效果对比，发现除非进行重度截断（减少至少80%），非MRL模型的截断嵌入在性能上与MRL模型相当甚至更优，表明截断鲁棒性并非来自MRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究指出随机截断文本嵌入对下游性能影响很小（除非减少70%以上），但没有工作系统比较随机截断与MRL这两种嵌入压缩方法的效果，因此需要明确二者作为有效嵌入缩减方法的优劣。

Method: 将MRL使用的相同截断策略应用于有/无MRL训练的多个模型，在多个下游任务上对比性能。

Result: 除非重度截断（减少至少80%），非MRL模型的截断嵌入与MRL模型性能相当，甚至更优。

Conclusion: 截断鲁棒性不一定来自MRL训练；是否采用MRL取决于是否需要重度截断。

Abstract: Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) is a widely adopted approach for training text encoders so they provide useful text representations at various sizes, available by simply truncating the resulting vectors at sizes pre-determined at training time. Recent works have shown that randomly truncating text embeddings has minimal impact in downstream performance unless vectors are reduced in size by at least 70%, suggesting that embeddings are already robust to truncation without the use of MRL. However, no prior work has compared random truncation to MRL, so it is unclear how the two methods compare as effective embedding reduction methods. In this paper, we study this by applying the same truncation used by MRL to models trained with and without MRL. Our results across several models and downstream tasks show that, unless heavily truncating embeddings (i.e. reducing their size by at least 80%), truncated embeddings of non-MRL models are competitive with, and often outperform models trained with MRL. This suggests that truncation robustness may not necessarily come from MRL, and that the choice of spending the additional training cost of MRL depends on whether heavy truncation is desired.

</details>


### [51] [Learning What Evaluators Value: A Reliable Approach to Modeling Evaluator Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16615)
*Madeline Celi Kitch,Nihar B. Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从多准则评估中学习人类和LLM评估者偏好的基本问题，在仅假设偏好函数是坐标单调非递减的最小假设下，理论刻画了模型失配的严重性，并提出了一种鲁棒的学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对评估者偏好做了过多强假设，这些假设在现实世界中可能被严重违反，导致学习效果不佳。作者希望在最小假设下学习偏好函数，并解决模型失配带来的问题。

Method: 采用坐标单调非递减作为唯一假设，理论分析了常见假设下模型失配的严重性；提出了一种鲁棒的学习算法，理论上证明该算法能在线性假设成立时无性能损失地学习任意偏好函数。

Result: 理论分析表明模型失配会导致显著问题；合成模拟和真实数据实验验证了算法能鲁棒地学习偏好，并揭示了LLM和人类偏好的关键特征。

Conclusion: 本文提出的鲁棒学习算法在最小假设下有效，能在线性假设成立时保持性能，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: In many applications, human and LLM evaluators use assessments of relevant criteria to create an overall evaluation for an item or individual. For example, in admissions, committees assess candidates on attributes such as test scores, GPA, and research experience to evaluate their overall fit for the program. Another example arises in medical care where clinicians use patient reports of symptoms to consider preliminary diagnoses and assess risks. Each setting involves mapping multiple criteria to an overall evaluation -- a process that reflects the evaluator's underlying preferences. We focus on the fundamental question of learning these preferences.
  Many applications of this problem make specific modeling assumptions on evaluator preferences that may be substantially violated in the real world. We make the minimal assumption that the preference function is coordinate-wise non-decreasing, which is reasonable in a large number of evaluation settings. We theoretically characterize the severity of model mismatch for many common assumptions and show that it can lead to significant issues for learning evaluator preferences and other important downstream tasks. We then present an algorithm for learning evaluators' preferences that is robust to model mismatch. We prove theoretically that our algorithm can learn any preference function without sacrificing performance when the linearity assumption holds. Evaluations of our algorithm with synthetic simulations and real-world data confirm its ability to learn preferences robustly and illustrate key aspects of LLM and human preferences.

</details>


### [52] [MLReplicate: Benchmarking Autonomous Research Systems for Machine Learning Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16616)
*Sasi Kiran Gaddipati,Diyana Muhammed,Farhana Keya,Gollam Rabby,Sören Auer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MLReplicate 基准，用于评估自主科研系统在机器学习可重复性方面的能力。基于 ICML 2025 杰出论文构建标准化输入规范，评估 6 种前沿系统（产出 45 篇手稿，3 次失败），采用自动化会议评审+专家人工评审双协议评估。发现 59% 的自动化评审通过稿件包含伪造或无法支持的结论，最廉价系统在人工评审中反而优于最昂贵系统（尽管输入 token 相差 38 倍），证明工作流设计比计算规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主科研系统发展迅速，但缺乏稳健且现实主义的评估框架，难以衡量其生成完整科学手稿的真实能力与科学严谨性。

Method: 构建 MLReplicate 基准：基于 ICML 2025 杰出论文标准化为输入规范；评估 6 种自主科研系统（AI SCIENTIST-V1/V2、AGENT LABORATORY、CYCLERESEARCHER、AI RESEARCHER、TINY SCIENTIST），产出 45 篇手稿；采用双协议评估（自动化会议评审+结构化专家人工评审），同时追踪计算成本、运行时间和人工干预量。

Result: 自动化会议评审接受了 37 篇有效提交中的 10 篇，另有 8 篇因未达页数门槛被 desk reject；人工评审发现所有系统均存在方法缺陷、幻觉实验结果和可重复性失败问题，59% 的自动化评审通过稿件包含伪造或无法支持的结论；最廉价系统在人工评估中优于最昂贵系统（输入 token 相差 38 倍）。

Conclusion: 自主科研系统的工作流设计比计算规模更重要；当前系统与真正的科学严谨性之间存在显著差距；MLReplicate 提供了一个实用且可扩展的评估框架，推动面向可信赖 AI 驱动科学发现的系统性进展。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems capable of generating complete scientific manuscripts have advanced rapidly, yet robust and realistic evaluation frameworks have failed to keep pace. To bridge this gap, we introduce MLReplicate, an end-to-end benchmark evaluating autonomous research systems on machine learning reproducibility. The benchmark was constructed from ICML 2025 outstanding papers reformulated into standardized input specifications and evaluated across 6 state-of-the-art research systems: AI SCIENTIST-V1, AI SCIENTIST-V2, AGENT LABORATORY, CYCLERESEARCHER, AI RESEARCHER, and TINY SCIENTIST, yielding 45 generated manuscripts, with 3 failed experiments. Outputs are assessed using a dual-protocol approach that combines automated conference-style review and structured expert human evaluation, while tracking computational cost, runtime, and the amount of required human intervention. The automated conference-style review accepted 10 out of 37 valid submissions. An additional 8 submissions were desk-rejected before review for failing to meet the minimum page threshold. In contrast to automated reviews, human reviewers consistently identified methodological flaws, hallucinated experimental results, and reproducibility failures across all systems, and 59% of accepted automated reviews contained fabricated or unsupported claims. We further find that neither token budget nor computational cost predicts output quality: the cheapest system outperforms the most resource-intensive system in human evaluation, despite a 38-fold difference in input tokens. We thus demonstrate that autonomous research workflow design matters more than the scale of compute. MLReplicate exposes a substantial gap between current autonomous research systems and genuine scientific rigor, and establishes a practical, extensible evaluation framework for systematic progress toward trustworthy AI-driven scientific discovery.

</details>


### [53] [SCOUT: Cyclic Causal Discovery Under Soft Interventions with Unknown Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16620)
*Alpar Turkoglu,Muralikrishnna G. Sethuraman,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个基于归一化流的新型因果发现框架，能够从未知目标的软干预数据中学习非线性循环因果关系，在合成和真实数据实验中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现算法大多依赖无环、高斯噪声和已知干预目标等假设，这些假设在真实系统中常被违反，而现有解决这些局限的方法要么假设线性模型，要么限于有限的干预设置。

Method: 提出SCOUT框架，通过最大化数据对数似然来恢复图结构，采用两种归一化流架构：收缩残差流和神经样条流。

Result: 在合成和真实数据实验中，SCOUT在因果图恢复和未知目标恢复方面均优于现有最先进方法，适用于多种干预和噪声设置。

Conclusion: SCOUT有效解决了非线性循环因果发现中干预目标未知的挑战，为更贴近实际应用场景的因果发现提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Learning causal relationships between variables from data is a fundamental research area with many applications across disciplines. Most existing causal discovery algorithms rely on the assumptions that (i) the underlying system is acyclic, (ii) the exogenous noise variables are Gaussian, and (iii) the intervention targets for the data-generating experiments are known. While these assumptions simplify the analysis, they are violated in real-life systems. Most existing methods that address these issues either assume the underlying model is linear or are constrained to operate in limited interventional settings. To that end, we propose SCOUT, a novel causal discovery framework for learning nonlinear cyclic causal relationships from soft interventional data with unknown targets. Our approach maximizes the data log-likelihood to recover the graph structure, using two normalizing-flow architectures: contractive residual flows and neural spline flows. Through experiments on synthetic and real-world data, we show that SCOUT outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both causal graph recovery and unknown target recovery across various interventional and noise settings.

</details>


### [54] [Does Weight Decay Enhance Training Stability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16622)
*Marius Saether,Amir Kolic,Tomaso Poggio,Pierfrancesco Beneventano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了权重衰减（weight decay）对深度学习训练动态稳定性的影响，发现其通过减缓渐进锐化（progressive sharpening）和引发架构相关的相变（CNN中阻尼振荡，MLP中锐度稳定低于理论阈值）来稳定训练。机制上，参数向量与锐度梯度的全局对齐驱动了相变，且这些现象可映射到函数空间（NTK）的稳定性。此外，凸/二次启发式得到的曲率阈值在正则化下不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究权重衰减是否以及如何稳定训练动态——其现代作用已偏离经典的正则化惩罚角色，但稳定性机制尚未被清晰理解。

Method: 在边缘稳定性（EoS）框架下分析权重衰减对参数空间动力学和损失锐度的影响；开发数学模型以准确建模现象；识别参数向量与锐度梯度的全局对齐作为相变的机制驱动因素。

Result: 1) 权重衰减稳健地减缓渐进锐化；2) CNN中阻尼EoS振荡，MLP中引发相变使锐度稳定在低于2/η理论边界的阈值；3) 相变由参数向量与锐度梯度的全局对齐驱动；4) 现象可转化为函数空间（NTK）的稳定性。

Conclusion: 权重衰减通过多种机制（减缓锐化、架构依赖相变）稳定训练动态，但基于凸/二次近似的曲率阈值在正则化下不是可靠的稳定性诊断。

Abstract: In modern deep learning, weight decay is often credited with "stabilizing" training dynamics, diverging from its classical role as a static regularization penalty. We investigate a fundamental question: *does weight decay stabilize training dynamics, and if so, through which mechanism?* Indeed, training stability is understood through different but related notions in the literature. We consider how weight decay affects the parameter-space dynamics and loss sharpness by analyzing its effects at the \emph{Edge of Stability} (EoS). We show that weight decay robustly slows *progressive sharpening}. Furthermore, we uncover a striking architecture-dependent phase transition. In CNNs, weight decay dampens the oscillations at the EoS, while in MLPs, increasing weight decay causes a phase transition in which the sharpness stabilizes at a threshold significantly below the theoretical $\frac{2}η$ boundary. We develop a mathematical framework that accurately models these phenomena and identify the global alignment of the parameter vector and the sharpness gradient as the mechanistic driver of the phase transition. Importantly, we show that these phenomena translate into stability in terms of search in function-space (NTK). Last, this shows that curvature thresholds obtained from convex/quadratic heuristics may not be reliable stability diagnostics under regularization.

</details>


### [55] [Learning How to Cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16632)
*Ferhat Erata,Sam Kouteili,Thanos Typaldos,Timos Antonopoulos,Robert B. Jones,Byron Cook,Ruzica Piskac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号后训练框架，通过MCTS数据筛选管道和两阶段后训练（SFT+DPO），使4B参数Transformer模型在SAT竞赛基准上达到与最佳符号启发式方法相当的立方分割性能（pass@5=53），首次证明Transformer可学习有效的立方启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cube-and-Conquer（C&C）是解决挑战性SAT问题的有效方法，但此前没有工作证明基于Transformer的模型能够学习有效的立方启发式（cubing heuristics），该研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 设计了一个基于MCTS的数据筛选管道，利用符号启发式探索SAT竞赛公式的分割决策，生成基于求解器统计数据的偏好数据，并用教师模型的推理轨迹进行增强。采用两阶段后训练：先进行监督微调（SFT），再进行直接偏好优化（DPO）。

Result: 4B参数模型在100个SAT竞赛基准上pass@5得分达到53，超越Claude-Sonnet-4（50）并与最佳符号启发式方法（53）持平。消融实验显示SFT将pass@5从46提升至51，DPO额外增加2个基准；熵/一致性消融表明SFT贡献了根级决策多样性，从而产生互补的覆盖效果。

Conclusion: Transformer模型可以通过训练在传统由符号方法主导的领域中做出有效的立方分割决策，证明了神经符号方法在SAT求解中的潜力。

Abstract: Despite the effectiveness of Cube-and-Conquer (C&C) for solving challenging Boolean Satisfiability (SAT) problems, no prior work has shown that transformer-based models can learn effective cubing heuristics. We introduce a neuro-symbolic post-training framework for this task. We design an MCTS-based data curation pipeline that uses symbolic heuristics to explore splitting decisions over SAT competition formulas, producing preference data grounded in solver statistics and augmented with reasoning traces from a teacher model. Our two-stage post-training, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by direct preference optimization (DPO), enables a 4B-parameter model to achieve a pass@5 score of 53 on 100 SAT competition benchmarks, surpassing frontier LLMs such as Claude-Sonnet-4 (50) and matching the best symbolic heuristic (53). Ablations show that SFT alone improves pass@5 from 46 to 51, with DPO adding 2 additional benchmarks; an entropy/agreement ablation on realized first-cube decisions further shows that SFT, not DPO, accounts for the root-level decision diversity that produces complementary per-run coverage over deterministic symbolic methods. This demonstrates that transformers can be trained to make effective cubing decisions in a domain traditionally dominated by symbolic methods.

</details>


### [56] [MedMIX: Modality-Internal Expert Fusion for Multimodal Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16639)
*Seungik Cho,Anqi Li,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MedMIX是一个多模态临床预测框架，通过模态内专家融合、模态间学习融合和训练时大-小模型协作，有效应对多种基础模型互补性、模态缺失和样本特异性挑战，在三个异构基准和跨队列迁移中保持强健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床预测面临三大挑战：不同模态有各自专长的多种基础模型、训练和测试时普遍存在的模态缺失、以及不同样本间模态贡献的差异。现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: MedMIX框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 模态内专家融合——聚合同一模态内多个小专家模型的互补嵌入；(2) 模态间学习融合——对可用模态进行可学习的加权融合；(3) 训练时大-小模型协作——利用大型教师模型提升部署表示质量，不增加推理成本。

Result: 在OpenI、MIMIC-IV-MM和MMIST-ccRCC三个异构基准上，MedMIX实现了一致的强性能，在受控的模态缺失扰动下保持鲁棒，并在MIMIC-III跨队列迁移中展现了持续的稳定性。

Conclusion: MedMIX是一个实用的统一框架，融合了模态内专家协作、样本特异性跨模态融合和高效的大-小模型协作，同时对不完整模态保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical prediction faces three challenges: multiple foundation models (FMs) with complementary strengths per modality, pervasive missing modalities at training and test time, and sample-specific variation in modality contributions. We introduce MedMIX, a multimodal framework that combines intra-modality expert fusion, learned inter-modality fusion, and training-only large--small model collaboration for robust medical prediction under incomplete modalities. Within each modality, MedMIX aggregates complementary embeddings from multiple small expert models; across modalities, it performs learned fusion over available modalities; and during training, it leverages large teacher models to improve deployed representations without additional inference cost. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks (OpenI, MIMIC-IV-MM, and MMIST-ccRCC), MedMIX achieves consistently strong performance while remaining robust under controlled missing-modality perturbations, and further demonstrates sustained robustness under cross-cohort shift on MIMIC-III. These results highlight MedMIX as a practical framework that unifies within-modality expert collaboration, sample-specific cross-modality fusion, and efficient large--small model collaboration while remaining robust to incomplete modalities.

</details>


### [57] [Provably Shorter Scratchpads in Hybrid DeltaNet-Attention Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16640)
*Tomasz Steifer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合循环-注意力解码器（如Qwen3-Next中使用的架构）的表达能力，证明了在奇偶条件检索任务中，Qwen风格的混合架构（Gated DeltaNet + Gated Attention）能够以常数scratchpad解决，而纯Gated DeltaNet无法解决，纯Gated Attention需要至少多项式规模的scratchpad。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究混合循环-注意力架构（如Qwen系列采用的Gated Attention heads + Gated DeltaNet heads组合）在模型表达能力和效率上是否存在形式化的优势。

Method: 定义了一个奇偶条件检索任务（parity-conditioned retrieval task），在常数精度假设下，比较三种架构（纯Gated DeltaNet、纯Gated Attention、混合架构）解决该任务所需的scratchpad规模（即CoT步数）。

Result: 混合架构（Qwen风格）可以用常数scratchpad（O(1) CoT步数）解决该任务；纯Gated DeltaNet不存在类似解法；纯Gated Attention至少需要多项式规模的scratchpad。

Conclusion: 混合循环-注意力架构在表达效率上具有严格的形式化优势，证明了这种混合设计不仅是工程实践的选择，更存在理论上的必要性。

Abstract: We investigate the expressive power of hybrid recurrent-attention decoders, a class of architectures used in recent open-source language models such as Qwen3-Next and its successors. These models combine Gated Attention heads with recurrent Gated DeltaNet heads. Is there a formal advantage, in terms of model expressivity or efficiency, to such a hybrid architecture? We show that there is. We define parity-conditioned retrieval task and show that under constant-precision assumption, a Qwen-style hybrid of Gated DeltaNet and Gated Attention solves this task with a constant scratchpad, or equivalently $O(1)$ chain-of-thought steps. In contrast, no similar solution exists for pure Gated DeltaNet models, while pure Gated Attention requires at least a polynomial scratchpad.

</details>


### [58] [In-context learning enables continental-scale subsurface temperature prediction from sparse local observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16665)
*Daniel O'Malley,Christopher W. Johnson,Javier E. Santos,Pablo Lara,Sandro Malusà,Bharat Srikishan,John Kath,Arnab Mazumder,Mohamed Mehana,David Coblentz,Nathan DeBardeleben,Earl Lawrence,Hari Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了In-Context Earth，一种基于Transformer的上下文学习模型，利用稀疏钻孔观测数据预测大陆尺度连续温度-深度场，在美国本土达到4.7°C的MAE，无需微调即可迁移至其他地质区域，且模型内部自发学习了未显式训练的岩石物理性质表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大陆尺度地下温度场知识受限于钻孔测量的高昂成本和稀疏分布，而温度场对地热资源评估和浅层地壳热传输理解至关重要。现有方法（如传统插值或物理模型）难以捕捉由岩性、地壳结构、放射性产热和对流流体相互作用产生的尖锐热异常。

Method: 提出In-Context Earth，一种基于Transformer的上下文学习模型，以稀疏局部钻孔观测作为地质上下文（context），预测连续的温度-深度场，并输出标定的不确定性。无需微调，仅需20个局部观测即可在推理时适应新地质区域。

Result: 在美国本土MAE为4.7°C，优于Stanford Thermal Model、AlphaEarth、Transparent Earth和泛克里金法；KS统计量2.5%，不确定性标定良好；迁移至阿尔伯塔（MAE 2.2°C）、澳大利亚（6.2°C）和英国（5.4°C）。可解释性分析显示模型内部学习了地震波速、地球化学和地壳结构等未显式训练的表征。

Conclusion: 上下文学习能够利用稀疏钻孔观测实现大陆尺度地下表征，无需密集测量或区域特定重新训练，为地热资源评估和浅层地壳热传输研究提供了高效、可迁移的深度学习方法。

Abstract: Continental-scale knowledge of subsurface temperature is limited by the cost and sparsity of borehole measurements, but such information is essential for geothermal resource assessment and for understanding heat transport in the shallow crust. The thermal field reflects the interaction between lithology, crustal structure, radiogenic heat production, and advective fluid flow, sometimes producing sharp anomalies that are smoothed by conventional interpolation or difficult to capture with physical models. Here we introduce In-Context Earth, a transformer-based model that uses sparse local borehole observations as geological context to predict continuous temperature-at-depth fields with calibrated uncertainty. In the contiguous United States, the model achieves a mean absolute error of 4.7 °C, outperforming the physics-informed Stanford Thermal Model, a model based on AlphaEarth embeddings, the multimodal Transparent Earth model, and universal kriging, while resolving sharper thermal gradients in geothermal provinces. Its uncertainty estimates are well calibrated, with a Kolmogorov-Smirnov statistic of 2.5%. Without finetuning, the model adapts to Alberta, Australia, and the United Kingdom (UK) using only 20 local observations at inference time, maintaining high accuracy in geologically distinct test regions with a mean absolute error of 2.2 °C in Alberta, 6.2 °C in Australia, and 5.4 °C in the UK. Interpretability analyses show that the model learns internal representations of subsurface properties it never observes during training, including seismic velocities, geochemistry, and crustal structure, and uses these representations in physically consistent ways. More broadly, this work shows that in-context learning can use sparse borehole observations for continental-scale subsurface characterization, without requiring dense measurements or region-specific retraining.

</details>


### [59] [Scalable Knowledge Editing for Mixture-of-Experts LLMs via Tensor-Structured Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16686)
*Roman Maksimov,Vladimir Aletov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Daniil Medyakov,Vladimir Solodkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对MoE架构大语言模型的知识编辑框架，通过利用MoE层的张量结构和Woodbury矩阵恒等式，实现了无需密集矩阵求逆的高效编辑，在保持编辑质量的同时将编辑速度提升最高6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法主要针对密集前馈层设计，而现代LLM越来越多采用MoE架构，导致大量生产模型缺乏原则性的编辑工具。

Method: 提出类似MEMIT的知识编辑框架，利用MoE层的张量结构在每专家级别构建编辑目标，并应用Woodbury矩阵恒等式避免对专家权重的完整堆叠矩阵进行实例化或求逆，将更新简化为固定低秩矩阵的求逆操作。

Result: 在主要知识编辑指标上与强基线方法匹配，同时由于批量MEMIT风格公式和Woodbury恒等式实现的低维求逆，编辑速度提升最高达6倍。

Conclusion: 闭式、参数修改型知识编辑可以高效地从密集层扩展到稀疏MoE架构，为现代稀疏LLM中的可扩展知识编辑开辟了路径。

Abstract: Knowledge editing (KE) provides a lightweight alternative to repeated fine-tuning of LLMs. However, most existing KE methods target dense feed-forward layers, while modern LLMs increasingly adopt Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures for their superior memory footprint and inference efficiency. This mismatch leaves a growing class of production models without principled editing tools. We propose a MEMIT-like framework for knowledge editing in MoE-based LLMs. Our method exploits the tensor structure of MoE layers to formulate the editing objective faithfully at the per expert level, and applies the Woodbury matrix identity to avoid materializing or inverting the full stacked matrix of expert weights. The resulting update reduces to inversions of fixed low-rank matrices and requires no additional backward passes. Empirically, our approach matches the editing quality of strong baselines on the main KE metrics while accelerating the editing procedure by up to 6x, owing to the batched MEMIT-style formulation and the low-dimensional inversions enabled by the Woodbury identity. These results show that closed-form, parameter-modifying KE can be extended efficiently beyond dense layers, opening a path toward scalable knowledge editing in modern sparse LLM architectures.

</details>


### [60] [Your SaaS Is an Insurance Product: A Modeling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16699)
*Caio Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了按使用量封顶的SaaS产品（如LLM订阅、云平台等）在运营结构上与保险产品同构，并提出基于精算科学的定价框架，将频率-严重度分解、保费计算原理和蒙特卡洛准备金充足性应用于此类产品定价，而非传统的单位经济学方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有cs.LG/stat.ML领域缺乏处理封顶使用量SaaS产品定价的正式工具和术语，而这类产品在结构上与保险产品共享相同特征（固定保费、随机需求、不可转让的定期重置上限、组合级尾部风险），保险精算科学已有成熟方法论可资借鉴。

Method: 构建基于频率-严重度分解、保费计算原理和蒙特卡洛准备金充足性检验的精算定价框架，通过LLM服务和云平台两个领域的公开订阅层数据进行映射，并以经典健康保险经济学文献（Arrow 1963; Pauly 1968; Manning et al. 1987; Brot-Goldberg et al. 2017）作为理论基础。

Result: 通过对工作示例的分析，展示了该精算框架与传统单位经济学方法在定价上的显著偏离，表明精算视角能更准确反映封顶使用量SaaS产品的风险结构。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是操作性的而非理论性的——不是提出新定理，而是为cs.LG/stat.ML社区引入精算科学中已有的词汇和工具，以改进封顶使用量SaaS产品的定价实践。

Abstract: Capped-usage SaaS products -- LLM subscriptions such as Claude Code and ChatGPT, cloud platforms such as Vercel and Cloudflare Workers, corporate benefit platforms, identity-verification services with liability transfer -- share a structural signature with insurance products: a fixed premium decoupled from realized consumption, stochastic per-user demand with heavy-tailed severity, a non-fungible cap that resets on a fixed schedule, and a portfolio-level exposure that requires reserve adequacy under tail risk. We argue that this is not an analogy. It is the same operational problem actuarial science has been tooled for decades to address, restated with new dependent variables (tokens, bandwidth bytes, function-invocations, gym check-ins) in place of medical claims. This paper proposes a modeling framework for capped-usage SaaS pricing built from frequency-severity decomposition, premium calculation principles, and Monte Carlo reserve adequacy. We map the framework to publicly observable subscription tiers in two domains (LLM services and cloud platforms), ground it in canonical health-insurance economics (Arrow 1963; Pauly 1968; Manning et al. 1987; Brot-Goldberg et al. 2017), and demonstrate divergence from traditional unit economics through a worked example. The contribution is operational rather than theoretical: not a new theorem, but vocabulary and tools currently absent from cs.LG/stat.ML practice.

</details>


### [61] [Convex Dataset Valuation for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16704)
*Siqi Zeng,Christopher Jung,Rui Li,Zhe Kang,Ming Li,Nima Noorshams,Zhigang Wang,Fuchun Peng,Han Zhao,Xue Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型后训练阶段的数据集估值问题，提出了基于梯度空间核均值匹配（KMM）的可扩展凸数据集估值方法，该方法同时考虑与目标任务的对齐和数据集间的冗余性，在预算约束下显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM后训练中，开发者面临计算、标注和许可成本约束，无法使用所有可用数据。随着数据集市场的兴起，数据获取受预算和谈判制约，因此需要一种 principled 的数据集层面选择方法。

Method: 提出基于梯度空间核均值匹配（Kernel Mean Matching, KMM）的凸数据集估值方法。该方法在梯度空间中同时建模数据集与目标任务的alignment以及数据集之间的redundancy，形成一个可扩展的凸优化问题。

Result: 在多种后训练设置和任务上的实验表明，该方法一致优于现有估值基线，在低计算开销下实现更强的目标任务性能。

Conclusion: 数据集估值可以作为市场约束下LLM后训练数据选择的实用决策工具。

Abstract: Improving LLM performance on downstream tasks sometimes requires leveraging auxiliary datasets during post-training. In practice, however, developers face constraints on compute, labeling, and licensing costs that preclude using all available data, necessitating principled dataset-level selection. These constraints are increasingly shaped by dataset marketplaces, where data acquisition is governed by budgets and negotiation. We study dataset valuation as a subset selection problem during LLM post-training. Our goal is to identify and weight auxiliary datasets so as to maximize target task performance given constrained budgets. We first show that commonly used gradient alignment scores provide a reasonable yet incomplete valuation signal, as they ignore redundancy among datasets. To address this, we propose a scalable convex dataset-level valuation method based on kernel mean matching (KMM) in gradient space, which jointly accounts for alignment with the target task and redundancy across auxiliary datasets. Through extensive experiments across diverse post-training settings and tasks, we show that our approach consistently outperforms existing valuation baselines, achieving stronger performance with low computational overhead. Our results position dataset valuation as a practical decision tool for post-training data selection in market-constrained large language model settings. The code is available at https://github.com/uiuctml/convex_data_valuation.

</details>


### [62] [EmoMind: Decoding Affective Captions from Human Brain fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16739)
*Bilal A. Mohammed,Lin Gu,Ruogo Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EmoMind是首个从fMRI信号端到端解码情感描述词的流水线：先通过脑解码视觉特征检索中性场景描述，再用同一fMRI记录的34维连续情感向量重写描述，通过无分类器引导在内容保留与情感表达间插值。在三个验证轴（主体特异性、结构几何、因果控制）及合成脑替换测试上，显著优于GPT-4离散标签基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑到文本系统仅恢复语义内容而丢弃情感信息；情感文本生成依赖离散类别标签，丢失了丰富的个体间情感变异性。缺乏从脑活动直接解码连续、个性化的情感描述的端到端方法。

Method: 1) 从fMRI解码的视觉特征中检索语义中立的场景描述；2) 从同一fMRI记录解码34维连续情感向量，用于重写描述；3) 采用无分类器引导训练重写器，通过身份保持的空分支实现语义保真度与情感表达性之间的平滑插值；4) 构建三轴验证框架（主体特异性、结构几何、因果控制）和合成脑替换测试。

Result: 在两个独立情感fMRI数据集上，EmoMind在主体特异性、结构几何和因果控制三个验证轴上均显著优于GPT-4离散标签基线，尤其在需要个体特异性情感结构而非群体水平情感聚合的指标上提升最大。

Conclusion: 连续脑解码情感可作为个性化情感描述生成的可行控制信号，为研究个体情感脑组织开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Decoding visual experience from brain activity has advanced substantially, but cur- rent brain-to-text systems largely recover semantic content while discarding affect. Additionally, language models can generate emotional text when prompted with categorical labels, but such labels collapse rich inter-subject variability into coarse discrete bins. We present EmoMind, the first end-to-end pipeline for decoding affective captions directly from fMRI signals. EmoMind first retrieves a semanti- cally grounded neutral scene description from brain-decoded visual features, then rewrites it using a continuous 34-dimensional emotion vector decoded from the same fMRI recording. To control the balance between content preservation and affective expression, we train the rewriter with classifier-free guidance against an identity-preserving null branch, enabling smooth interpolation between semantic fidelity and affective expressivity. We evaluate affective caption generation with a three-axis validation framework spanning subject-specificity, structural geometry, and causal control. We further augment this framework with a synthetic-brain substitution test that probes robustness to the measurement apparatus, and we benchmark each axis against GPT-4 prompted with brain-decoded top-5 emotion labels as a strong discrete baseline. Across two independent emotion fMRI datasets, EmoMind significantly outperforms label-prompted GPT-4 on all three axes, with the largest gains on metrics that require person-specific affective structure rather than population-level emotion aggregation. These results establish continuous brain-decoded affect as a viable control signal for individualized affective cap- tion generation and open new directions for studying individual affective brain organisation.

</details>


### [63] [Propagation of Chaos in Contextual Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16747)
*Shi Chen,Zhengjiang Lin,Kaizhao Liu,Philippe Rigollet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了上下文流图（CFMs）这一抽象框架，用于在大上下文场景下定量分析Transformer的统计行为。通过将有限上下文模型近似为无限上下文系统（上下文度量被总体分布替代），利用McKean-Vlasov动力学和混沌传播理论，建立了有限与无限上下文CFM之间的前向偏差界（深度均匀）和反向训练轨迹偏差界（在线梯度下降迭代均匀）。两者分别达到最优Wasserstein率$n^{-1/d}$和参数化率$n^{-1/2}$（含Transformer特例）。核心工具是新的欧拉伴随损失梯度公式及前向-伴随系统的稳定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大上下文场景下，Transformer的统计行为缺乏严格的定量理论分析。现有研究多关注架构设计或经验性能，未从统计力学角度解释上下文长度作为统计资源如何影响模型逼近误差和训练动态。

Method: 1. 引入上下文流图（CFMs）抽象，将Transformer注意力块堆栈建模为在上下文测度存在下演化特殊token的动力系统。2. 将有限上下文模型视为对无限上下文系统（上下文测度替换为总体分布）的近似，利用McKean-Vlasov结构建立混沌传播框架。3. 推导前向偏差界（控制有限与无限CFM逐层差异）和反向偏差界（控制在线梯度下降迭代中训练轨迹差异）。4. 关键创新：提出欧拉伴随公式计算损失梯度，并建立前向-伴随系统的稳定性估计。

Result: 前向偏差界达到最优Wasserstein率$n^{-1/d}$（$n$为上下文长度，$d$为数据维度）；反向偏差界达到参数化率$n^{-1/2}$（针对包括Transformer在内的受限CFM类）。两个界均沿深度和训练迭代均匀成立。

Conclusion: 建立了Transformer在大上下文场景下统计行为的严格理论框架，揭示了上下文长度作为统计资源的核心作用，证明了有限上下文模型对无限上下文系统的最优逼近速率，为理解Transformer的上下文学习能力提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a quantitative statistical theory of transformers in the large-context regime by adopting the abstraction of contextual flow maps (CFMs): dynamical systems that evolve a distinguished token in the presence of a contextual measure across a stack of attention blocks. Within this framework, the finite-context model approximates an idealized infinite-context system in which the contextual measure is replaced by its underlying population, so that the context length $n$ becomes a statistical resource. Exploiting the McKean--Vlasov structure of the dynamics and the classical machinery of propagation of chaos, we establish a forward bound controlling the deviation between the finite- and infinite-context CFMs uniformly along depth, and a backward bound controlling the deviation between the corresponding training trajectories uniformly across iterations of online gradient descent. Both bounds achieve the optimal Wasserstein rate $n^{-1/d}$ for general CFMs and parametric rate $n^{-1/2}$ for a restricted class of CFMs that includes transformers as a special case. The analysis rests on a new Eulerian adjoint formulation of the loss gradient and stability estimates for the resulting forward--adjoint system, both of which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [64] [Learning Unbiased Permutations via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16755)
*Yimeng Min,Carla P. Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PermFlow是一个基于条件流匹配的框架，直接在具有单位行和列和的矩阵仿射子空间上操作，通过封闭形式的切空间投影器保持约束，并使用最近目标耦合将不同噪声初始化路由到不同有效排列，从而捕获多模态排列分布，解决Sinkhorn方法在歧义下塌陷的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于熵正则化Sinkhorn的可微排列方法只能产生单一软化解，在歧义情况下（如多个有效排列同时存在）会塌陷到单一模式，无法捕捉多模态排列分布。

Method: 提出PermFlow框架：(1) 在行和与列和均为1的矩阵仿射子空间上直接操作；(2) 使用封闭形式的切空间投影器，通过构造而非迭代校正精确保持约束；(3) 采用最近目标耦合策略，将不同的噪声初始化路由到不同的有效排列。

Result: 在视觉排序任务和对称线性分配问题上，PermFlow对无歧义输入达到高准确率，在歧义情况下能恢复所有有效排列，而Sinkhorn基线方法在结构上失效。

Conclusion: PermFlow能够学习排列的多模态分布，在歧义场景下有效保留多个有效排列解，克服了传统可微排列方法的局限性。

Abstract: Learning permutations is fundamental to sorting, ranking, and matching, but existing differentiable methods based on entropy-regularized Sinkhorn produce a single softened solution and collapse under ambiguity. We present PermFlow, a conditional flow matching framework that operates directly on the affine subspace of matrices with unit row and column sums. A closed-form tangent-space projector preserves these constraints exactly along every trajectory, by construction rather than through iterative correction, and a nearest-target coupling routes distinct noisy initializations toward distinct valid permutations. The result is a model that captures multimodal permutation distributions rather than collapsing them to a single mode. On a visual sorting task with blended-digit ambiguity and a symmetric linear assignment problem, PermFlow achieves high accuracy on unambiguous inputs and recovers both valid permutations under ambiguity, where Sinkhorn-based baselines structurally fail.

</details>


### [65] [Distinguishable Deletion: Unifying Knowledge Erasure and Refusal for Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16776)
*Puning Yang,Junchi Yu,Qizhou Wang,Philip Torr,Bo Han,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Distinguishable Deletion (D²)的新范式，通过能量指数在潜在表示层面区分已遗忘和保留知识，并基于此设计了Energy-based Unlearning Alignment (EUA)方法，在训练和推理阶段同时实现知识擦除和拒绝机制，实验表明优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM去安全方法存在两类问题：知识删除（KD）范式因压制特定token序列作为完整知识删除的替代，导致有偏删除；可区分拒绝（DR）范式因底层知识保持完整，存在有害知识重新涌现的风险。亟需一种既能彻底擦除知识又能安全处理遗忘输入的范式。

Method: 提出D²范式，在潜在表示层而非token层面限制响应分布以擦除不期望知识，同时与保留知识区分开。引入能量指数量化知识存在性及遗忘/保留内容的分离度，据此设计Energy-based Unlearning Alignment (EUA)：训练时强制执行能量边界遗忘，推理时应用基于能量的拒绝机制。

Result: 数学和实证分析表明能量指标既准确又高效。大量实验表明EUA显著优于此前方法，证明了D²范式的优越性。

Conclusion: D²范式通过在潜在表示层面进行可区分的知识删除，有效解决了KD的有偏删除和DR的知识重涌现问题，为LLM安全部署提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Mitigating sensitive and harmful outputs is fundamental to ensuring safe deployment of LLMs. Existing approaches typically follow two paradigms: Knowledge Deletion (KD), which erases undesirable information during training, and Distinguishable Refusal (DR), which steers models away from using sensitive knowledge during inference. Despite rapid progress, KD-based unlearning struggles with biased deletion due to suppressing specific token sequences as a substitute for complete knowledge removal, whereas DR-based unlearning risks the re-emergence of harmful knowledge because the underlying knowledge remains intact. To address these issues, we propose Distinguishable Deletion ($\mathrm{D^2}$), a paradigm that restricts the response distribution in the latent representation rather than specific tokens to erase undesirable knowledge, while distinguishing it from retained knowledge, enabling a refusal mechanism to handle unlearned inputs safely and coherently. To implement $\mathrm{D^2}$, we introduce an energy index that quantifies the presence of knowledge and the separation between unlearned and retained content. Mathematical and empirical analyses show that energy is both accurate and efficient, enabling Energy-based Unlearning Alignment (EUA) to enforce energy-boundary unlearning during training and apply an energy-based refusal mechanism at inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EUA significantly outperforms previous methods, indicating the superiority of $\mathrm{D^2}$. Our code is available at https://github.com/Puning97/EUA-for-LLM-Unlearning.

</details>


### [66] [Lever: Speculative LLM Inference on Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16786)
*Tuowei Wang,Fengzu Li,Yanfan Sun,Wei Gao,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lever是一个端到端系统，通过联合优化推测解码的三个阶段（草稿生成、验证和执行），在智能手机上实现高效的闪存支持LLM推理，相比基线闪存卸载推理和传统推测解码，分别平均降低2.93倍和1.50倍的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在交互式移动应用中需求日益增长，但高质量模型超出智能手机有限的DRAM容量。虽然闪存可以存储更大模型，但基于闪存的推理速度慢，因为自回归解码频繁调用目标模型导致昂贵的I/O开销。现有推测解码方法假设服务器级加速器，未能考虑移动场景下的长I/O延迟、有限的计算并行性和不规则的推测执行。

Method: Lever联合优化推测解码的三个阶段：1) 草稿阶段：使用I/O和计算感知的增益-成本目标构建token树；2) 验证阶段：通过早停预测剪枝低价值分支，减少目标模型计算量；3) 执行阶段：在移动CPU-NPU异构硬件上高效映射推测执行以提高利用率。

Result: 相比基线闪存卸载推理，推理延迟平均降低2.93倍；相比传统推测解码，推理延迟平均降低1.50倍，缩小了闪存支持与内存驻留LLM推理之间的延迟差距。

Conclusion: Lever通过针对移动约束的联合优化，有效解决了闪存支持LLM推理在智能手机上的延迟问题，显著缩小了与内存驻留推理的性能差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly needed for interactive mobile applications, but high-quality models exceed the limited DRAM available on smartphones. Flash storage can hold larger models, yet flash-backed inference is slow because autoregressive decoding repeatedly invokes the target model and incurs costly I/O. We observe that speculative decoding is a natural fit for this setting: a small draft model can remain in DRAM, while a larger flash-resident target model verifies multiple candidate tokens per invocation. However, existing methods assume server-class accelerators and fail to account for prolonged I/O latency, limited computation parallelism, and irregular speculation execution.
  We present Lever, an end-to-end system for efficient flash-backed LLM inference on smartphones. Lever jointly optimizes the three stages of speculative decoding under mobile constraints. For drafting, it builds token trees using an I/O- and compute-aware gain-cost objective. For verification, it prunes low-value branches through early-exit prediction to reduce target-model computation. For execution, it maps speculation efficiently across mobile CPU-NPU hardware to improve utilization. Comprehensive evaluations show that Lever reduces inference latency by an average of 2.93x over baseline flash-offloaded inference and 1.50x over conventional speculative decoding, narrowing the latency gap between flash-backed and memory-resident LLM inference.

</details>


### [67] [The Unlearnability Phenomenon in RLVR for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16787)
*Yulin Chen,He He,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了RLVR训练中一个反直觉现象：在模型最初难以处理的困难样本中，相当一部分即使存在正确rollout也无法被学习。研究通过跨样本梯度分析表明，这些“不可学习”样本存在表征缺陷，表现为梯度相似性低且推理模式不可泛化，且数据增强也无法缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR已被证明能有效提升LLM的推理能力，但其学习动力学尚未被充分探索。作者希望揭示RLVR训练中存在的未被注意到的学习困难现象及其根本原因。

Method: 1) 通过实验发现困难样本中存在不可学习的子集；2) 验证现有优化和采样技术无法解决该问题；3) 采用跨样本梯度分析方法，计算样本间梯度相似性，揭示不可学习样本的表征缺陷；4) 测试数据增强对梯度相似性的影响。

Result: 不可学习样本具有低梯度相似性和不可泛化的推理模式；现有优化和采样技术无法解决；数据增强不能改善梯度相似性，表明RL中难以缓解表征缺陷。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统刻画了RLVR训练中的不可学习数据，揭示了当前RL方法在推理任务中的根本局限性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) has proven effective in improving Large Language Model's (LLM) reasoning ability. However, the learning dynamics of RLVR remain underexplored. In this paper, we reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon: among hard examples that the model initially struggles with, a substantial subset remains unlearnable even when correct rollouts are present. To understand the phenomenon, we first demonstrate that existing optimization and sampling techniques fail to resolve unlearnability. With cross-example gradient analysis, we show that unlearnable examples have fundamental representation issue, characterized by low gradient similarity with the rest of the examples and ungeneralizable reasoning patterns. We further show that representation flaws are difficult to mitigate in RL, as data augmentation does not improve gradient similarity. Our study provides the first systematic characterization of unlearnable data in RLVR training and reveals fundamental limitations in current RL approaches for reasoning tasks. Code and data are available at \url{https://github.com/yulinchen99/unlearnability-rlvr}.

</details>


### [68] [TIER: Trajectory-Invariant Execution Rewards for Multi-Step Tool Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16790)
*Anay Kulkarni,ChiaEn Lu,Dheeraj Mekala,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TIER（轨迹不变执行奖励）框架，通过函数架构和运行时执行提供密集奖励信号，在组合工具使用任务中实现>90%准确率，超越轨迹监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在多步组合工具使用场景中难以扩展，结果奖励稀疏，轨迹监督奖励依赖标注参考解且惩罚有效替代路径，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: TIER框架将奖励分解为格式有效性、架构遵循性、执行成功性和答案正确性四个层次，从函数架构和运行时执行中获取监督信号，而非参考轨迹，支持任意有效执行路径。

Result: 在DepthBench（1-6步）上，TIER在各步骤准确率>90%，轨迹监督方法在步骤4后崩溃；在BFCL v3和NestFUL上也取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 基于执行而非轨迹的密集奖励框架能有效支持组合推理，所有奖励组件均不可或缺，多层级监督对组合推理至关重要。

Abstract: Tool use enables large language models to solve complex tasks through sequences of API calls, yet existing reinforcement learning approaches fail to scale to multi-step composition settings. Outcome-based rewards provide only sparse feedback, while trajectory-supervised rewards depend on annotated reference solutions, penalizing valid alternatives and limiting scalability. We propose TIER: Trajectory-Invariant Execution Rewards, a reward framework that derives supervision directly from function schemas and runtime execution, rather than from reference trajectories. The reward decomposes into format validity, schema adherence, execution success, and answer correctness, providing dense, interpretable sequence-level feedback derived from fine-grained verification of individual steps of tool use. This design allows any valid execution path to receive credit, naturally supporting multiple solution strategies and adapting to evolving tool interfaces. On DepthBench, a compositional benchmark stratified by depth (1 to 6 steps), TIER achieves >90% accuracy across steps, where trajectory-supervised rewards collapse beyond step-4. We further demonstrate consistent gains on benchmarks like BFCL v3 and NestFUL. Ablation studies confirm that all reward components are necessary, highlighting the importance of multi-level supervision for compositional reasoning.

</details>


### [69] [PULSE: Generative Phase Evolution for Non-Stationary Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16793)
*Yangyou Liu,Zezhi Shao,Xinyu Chen,Hu Chen,Fei Wang,Yuankai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PULSE框架，通过物理启发的解缠-演化-模拟范式解决非平稳时间序列预测中的相位遗忘问题，在12个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非平稳时间序列预测方法隐式依赖静态历史假设，导致"相位遗忘"（Phase Amnesia）——模型无法捕捉不断演化的全局上下文，这是根本性的失败模式。

Method: 基于Wold分解、动力学相位演化和异方差流形生成三个物理假说，提出PULSE框架：(1) 相位锚定解缠解决优化干扰；(2) 相位路由器主动生成未来轨迹；(3) 统计感知混合（SAM）增强对分布外波动的鲁棒性。

Result: PULSE使简单的MLP主干在12个真实世界基准上达到SOTA或极具竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 正确的物理启发的归纳偏置远比原始架构复杂性对非平稳预测更为关键。

Abstract: Time series forecasting under non-stationarity faces a fundamental tension between capturing stable representations and adapting to distribution shifts. Existing methods implicitly rely on static historical assumptions, leading to a critical failure mode we term Phase Amnesia, where models become blind to the evolving global context. To resolve this, we formalize non-stationary dynamics through three physical hypotheses: wold decomposition, dynamical phase evolution, and heteroscedastic manifold generation. These principles inspire PULSE, a physics-informed, plug-and-play framework adopting a Disentangle--Evolve--Simulate design philosophy. Specifically, PULSE utilizes phase-anchored disentanglement to resolve optimization interference caused by dominant trends, employs a Phase Router to actively generate future trajectories, and introduces Statistic-Aware Mixup (SAM) to ensure robustness against out-of-distribution volatility. Empirically, PULSE enables a simple MLP backbone to achieve state-of-the-art or highly competitive performance across 12 real-world benchmarks. This validates that a correct physics-informed inductive bias is far more critical than raw architectural complexity for non-stationary forecasting. The code is available at: https://github.com/Gemost/PULSE.

</details>


### [70] [Cross-Domain Molecular Relational Learning: Leveraging Chemical Structure-Activity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16799)
*Peiliang Zhang,Jingling Yuan,Shiqing Wu,Mengqing Hu,Chao Che,Yongjun Zhu,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DisTrans框架，通过域对抗训练和结构-语义迁移差异优化跨域分子表示，在跨域分子关系学习中超越16种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子关系学习方法局限于域内建模，存在域封闭效应，难以解释跨域交互机制，亟需跨域分子关系学习。

Method: 1) 基于子结构拓扑差异的梯度反转策略，学习域可分离的结构表示；2) 跨域表示引导机制，对齐源域和目标域的官能团语义信息。

Result: 在两种典型跨域策略下，DisTrans优于16种基线方法，且在域间差异显著时仍保持良好性能。

Conclusion: DisTrans有效解决了跨域分子关系学习中的域适应问题，通过结构-语义双维度对齐实现了鲁棒的跨域分子表示。

Abstract: Recent advances in molecular representation integrates molecular topological and visual modalities, opening new avenues for precise Molecular Relational Learning (MRL). Existing MRL methods focus on intra-domain modeling, and their inherent domain-closed effect limits applicability to molecular science, particularly in elucidating cross-domain interaction mechanisms. Consequently, the imperative for Cross-Domain Molecular Relational Learning has become increasingly pressing. Benefiting from structure-activity analysis, we propose the Domain Adversarial Training Network with Structural-Semantic Transfer Discrepancy (DisTrans) to optimize cross-domain adaptive representation for molecular structures and visual images. 1) We employ the gradient reversal strategy based on substructure topological discrepancies between domains to learn the domain dependence of molecular structures. This strategy guides the model to adapt to the structural adjacency patterns in the target domain, generating domain-separable structural representations. 2) We apply the cross-domain representation guidance mechanism to align the functional-group semantic information between the source and target domains, learning cross-domain consistency information. The experimental results in two typical cross-domain strategies demonstrate that DisTrans outperforms 16 baseline methods, maintaining satisfactory performance even under pronounced inter-domain discrepancy.

</details>


### [71] [Cross-modal Affinity-aligned Multimodal Learning Analytics for Predicting Student Collaboration Satisfaction in Game-Based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16806)
*Wen-Hsin Tsai,Chia-Ming Lee,Yuk-Ying Tung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AAMLA框架，通过跨模态亲和力引导的模态对齐（CAMA）模块显式建模模态间关系，利用对比学习实现跨模态一致性，在50名中学生的协作学习实验中表现出对模态退化条件的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作游戏学习环境中自动预测学生协作满意度面临模态退化问题——单一模态（如眼动）在不同学生群体中信息量不一致，导致隐式注意力融合产生脆弱的跨模态表征。

Method: 提出AAMLA框架，核心为CAMA模块：通过亲和矩阵显式建模跨模态关系，利用对比学习强制跨模态一致性，自适应抑制信息量低的模态而非丢弃；采用模态特定投影层将面部动作单元、头部姿态、眼动和交互日志特征映射到统一语义空间。

Result: 在50名中学生的EcoJourneys协作学习实验中，AAMLA在标准和模态退化条件下均优于单模态基线和先前的交叉注意力方法；SHAP和t-SNE分析证实CAMA生成鲁棒且可解释的跨模态表征。

Conclusion: AAMLA框架通过显式跨模态对齐有效应对模态退化问题，为协作学习分析提供了更稳健的多模态融合方案。

Abstract: Collaborative game-based learning environments offer rich opportunities for small-group knowledge construction, yet automatically predicting student collaboration satisfaction remains challenging. A critical barrier is modality degradation: in educational deployments, individual modalities such as eye gaze exhibit inconsistent informativeness across student cohorts, causing implicit attention-based fusion to produce brittle multimodal representations. We propose the Affinity-Aligned Multimodal Learning Analytics (AAMLA) framework, whose core contribution is the Cross-modal Affinity-guided Modality Alignment (CAMA) module, which explicitly models inter-modal relationships via affinity matrices and enforces cross-modal consistency through contrastive learning, enabling adaptive suppression of uninformative modalities without discarding them. AAMLA further applies modality-specific projection layers to map heterogeneous features, including facial action units, head pose, eye gaze, and interaction trace logs, into a unified semantic space prior to alignment. Experiments on 50 middle school students in the EcoJourneys collaborative learning environment demonstrate consistent improvements over unimodal baselines and prior cross-attention approaches under standard and modality degradation conditions, with SHAP and t-SNE analyses confirming that CAMA produces robust, interpretable cross-modal representations for student collaboration modeling.

</details>


### [72] [Informative Graph Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16809)
*Shen Han,Zhiyao Zhou,Jiawei Chen,Sheng Zhou,Canghong Jin,Hai Lin,Da Zhong Li,Bingde Hu,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出InGSL方法，通过互信息引导的边构建策略同时考虑相似性和多样性，在减少边数量的同时提升图结构学习性能，可作为插件集成到现有GSL框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图结构学习方法（GSL）虽然能优化节点连接，但通常以大幅增加边数为代价，导致巨大的存储和计算开销。作者发现问题根源在于基于相似性的边构建策略倾向于连接高度相似的邻居，引入了大量结构冗余。

Method: 提出InGSL（Informative Graph Structure Learning），采用互信息引导的学习策略，在边构建过程中同时考虑节点间的相似性和多样性，从而减少冗余边并保留信息量丰富的结构。

Result: 在六个代表性GSL方法上的实验表明，InGSL在减少边数量的同时实现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: InGSL作为一种即插即用模块，可无缝集成到现有GSL框架中，有效解决了GSL中边数量膨胀的问题，为图结构学习提供了更高效、更信息丰富的解决方案。

Abstract: The quality of graph-structured data is fundamental to the success of modern graph analysis techniques such as Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). However, real-world graph data is often suboptimal, suffering from issues such as noise and incomplete connections. Graph Structure Learning (GSL) has emerged as a promising technique that adaptively optimizes node connections. However, we observe that the effectiveness of GSL often comes at the cost of a dramatic expansion in edge count, resulting in significant storage and computational overhead.
  In this work, we reveal that this limitation stems from the prevalent use of similarity-based edge construction, which predominantly connects highly similar neighbors based on their embeddings, introducing substantial structure redundancy. To address this, we propose a novel Informative Graph Structure Learning method (InGSL), which jointly considers both similarity and diversity in edge construction by incorporating a mutual-information-guided learning strategy. Notably, InGSL serves as a plug-in module that can be seamlessly integrated into existing GSL frameworks. Through extensive experiments on six representative GSL methods, we demonstrate that InGSL achieves significant performance improvements at a reduced number of edges.

</details>


### [73] [Jacobian-Guided Anisotropic Noise Reshaping for Enhancing Representation Utility under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16812)
*Youngmok Ha,Viktor Schlegel,Yidan Sun,Anil Anthony Bharath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于雅可比矩阵的局部差分隐私噪声重塑方法，通过识别任务关键子空间并异向调制噪声分布，在保持隐私预算不变的前提下显著提升数据效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LDP机制任务无关地均匀注入噪声，导致数据效用严重下降；需要在不增加隐私预算的前提下减少对任务关键维度的噪声影响。

Method: 利用公开下游模型的雅可比矩阵识别任务关键子空间，沿关键维度选择性衰减噪声，将标准LDP的各向同性噪声重塑为各向异性分布，实现统一隐私预算下不同维度的异质性噪声调制。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C（亮度损坏，严重等级5）上，集成该方法使PrivUnit2和PrivUnitG在ε=7.5时效用提升约20%。

Conclusion: 该方法能推广至线性和非线性模型，可无缝集成现有LDP机制，有效提升数据效用。

Abstract: While Local Differential Privacy (LDP) serves as a foundational primitive for distributed data collection, its stringent noise injection requirement often leads to severe degradation in data utility. This degradation stems from the task-agnostic nature of conventional LDP mechanisms, which inject noise uniformly across all dimensions regardless of their relative importance to the downstream objective. To address this issue, we propose a novel approach that mitigates noise in task-relevant subspaces of the data representation. Our method identifies task-critical subspaces via the Jacobian matrix of the public downstream model, selectively attenuates noise along those dimensions, and reshapes the isotropic noise of standard LDP into an anisotropic distribution. This method preserves the uniform per-dimension privacy budget while heterogeneously modulating noise impact across dimensions, thereby substantially enhancing data utility. Furthermore, our approach generalizes to both linear and non-linear models and integrates seamlessly with existing mechanisms. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10-C (Brightness corruption at the highest severity level 5) demonstrate that integrating our approach improves the utility of PrivUnit2 and PrivUnitG by approximately 20\% at $ε=7.5$. The source code is available at \url{https://github.com/ymha/jacobian-anr-ldp}.

</details>


### [74] [Decoupling KL and Trajectories: A Unified Perspective for SFT, DAgger, Offline RL, and OPD in LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16826)
*Anhao Zhao,Haoran Xin,Yingqi Fan,Junlong Tong,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统解耦了知识蒸馏中的两个正交维度（前缀来源和token级KL方向），提出了四种有效目标函数，并在数学推理任务上进行了大规模对照研究，揭示了精度-熵、质量-计算、精度-稳定性三种权衡，进而提出KL混合和熵门控长度课程方法，显著提升了推理性能并降低生成长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM后训练中知识蒸馏的设计空间尚未得到充分理解，尤其是在与强化学习结合时。现有范式（离策略蒸馏和同策略蒸馏）隐含地将前缀来源与token级KL方向耦合，但这种耦合并非内在必然，有必要解耦这两个维度以系统探索蒸馏目标的设计空间。

Method: 1) 通过分解序列级KL散度，证明前向KL配对教师前缀与token级前向KL，反向KL配对Student前缀与token级反向KL，从而解耦提出四种目标；2) 建立梯度级恒等式，将前向KL关联到SFT风格的交叉熵匹配，反向KL关联到RL风格策略梯度目标；3) 在数学推理任务上开展大规模对照实验，评估四种目标作为独立方法及RL初始化的效果；4) 提出KL混合（平衡前向/反向KL权重）和熵门控长度课程（基于熵阈值控制训练序列长度）两种实用方法。

Result: 1) 揭示了三种权衡：KL方向导致精度-熵权衡，前缀来源导致质量-计算权衡，训练长度导致精度-稳定性权衡；2) KL混合表明长序列蒸馏需要足够的前向KL权重以防止熵崩溃和长度膨胀；3) 熵门控长度课程在Avg@k和Pass@k上分别提升3.6和最高5.8个点，同时将平均响应长度缩减约3倍。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个理解推理蒸馏目标设计的概念框架和实用方法，能够在精度、多样性、计算成本和强化学习行为之间实现有效平衡。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is central to LLM post-training, yet its design space remains poorly understood, especially alongside reinforcement learning (RL). We show that the prevailing paradigms, off-policy distillation and on-policy distillation (OPD), implicitly couple two orthogonal choices: prefix source and token-level KL direction. This follows from decomposing sequence-level KL over autoregressive response distributions: forward KL pairs teacher prefixes with token-level forward KL, and reverse KL pairs student prefixes with token-level reverse KL. We argue this coupling is not intrinsic: decoupling the two axes yields four valid objectives. We establish gradient-level identities showing forward KL gives SFT-style cross-entropy matching with teacher soft targets, whereas reverse KL gives an RL-style policy-gradient objective with a dense teacher-student log-ratio reward, connecting them to off-policy SFT, DAgger-style on-policy SFT, offline-RL-style distillation, and OPD. We conduct an extensive controlled study on math reasoning, evaluating the four objectives both as standalone methods and as initializations for subsequent RL. The results reveal three tradeoffs: KL direction induces an accuracy-entropy tradeoff, prefix source a quality-compute tradeoff, and training length an accuracy-stability tradeoff. Motivated by these findings, we propose KL mixing and an entropy-gated length curriculum. KL mixing shows long-sequence distillation requires substantial forward-KL weight to prevent entropy collapse and length inflation without sacrificing accuracy. The entropy-gated length curriculum improves Avg@k and Pass@k by 3.6 and up to 5.8 points, and cuts average response length by roughly 3x versus fixed long-horizon training. Our results provide a framework and practical methods for designing reasoning distillation objectives that balance accuracy, diversity, compute, and RL behavior.

</details>


### [75] [PhysioSeq2Seq: A Hybrid Physiological Digital Twin and Sequence-to-Sequence LSTM for Long-Horizon Glucose Forecasting in Type 1 Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16860)
*Phat Tran,Neville Mehta,Clara Mosquera-Lopez,Robert H. Dodier,Lizhong Chen,Peter G. Jacobs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhysioSeq2Seq混合架构，结合患者特定生理建模与Seq2Seq LSTM，通过数字孪生匹配注入ODE状态变量，消除递归误差累积，在240分钟血糖预测中MAE达39.28 mg/dL，偏差较递归LSTM降低13.89 mg/dL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归LSTM在长时域存在系统性负偏置误差累积，而纯机理ODE模型在群体层面参数化后无法跨个体泛化，因此需要一种结合两者优势的方法实现准确的长期血糖预测。

Method: 使用双胞胎匹配从300个参数化数字孪生中为每个血糖段选出最佳生理匹配，将匹配孪生的10个内部ODE状态变量作为外生协变量注入Seq2Seq LSTM的编码器和解码器，采用48步同步预测（而非递归）以消除误差累积。

Result: 在T1DEXI数据集（348训练/74测试）上，240分钟时MAE为39.28 mg/dL，ME为-10.62 mg/dL，偏差较递归LSTM降低13.89 mg/dL，MAE较ODE数字孪生降低28.62 mg/dL。

Conclusion: 消除架构反馈并注入患者匹配的生理状态是1型糖尿病长期血糖预测的有效且临床有意义的策略。

Abstract: Accurate long-horizon glucose forecasting is critical for automated insulin delivery systems, which help people with type 1 diabetes (T1D) manage their glucose and avoid dangerous hypoglycemia. However, standard recursive long short-term memory (LSTM) networks suffer from systematic negative bias at longer horizons due to error compounding, while purely mechanistic ordinary differential equation (ODE) models fail to generalize across individuals when parameterized at the population level. We propose PhysioSeq2Seq, a hybrid architecture that combines patient-specific physiological modeling with a sequence-to-sequence (Seq2Seq) LSTM. For each glucose segment, twin matching searches a population of 300 parameterized digital twins to identify the best-fitting physiological match from a 3-hour continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) history. The 10 internal ODE state variables of the matched twin are injected as exogenous covariates into both the encoder and decoder of the Seq2Seq LSTM. This simultaneous 48-step prediction strategy eliminates recursive error compounding, while the ODE features provide a physics-grounded constraint that bounds long-horizon drift within physiologically plausible ranges. PhysioSeq2Seq was trained on CGM and insulin data from 348 participants in the Type 1 Diabetes Exercise Initiative (T1DEXI) dataset and evaluated on 74 held-out participants. At the 240-minute horizon, PhysioSeq2Seq achieves a mean absolute error of 39.28 mg/dL and a mean error of -10.62 mg/dL, reducing bias by 13.89 mg/dL over the recursive LSTM and reducing mean absolute error by 28.62 mg/dL over the ODE-based digital twin. These results show that eliminating architectural feedback and injecting patient-matched physiological states is an effective and clinically meaningful strategy for long-horizon glucose forecasting in T1D.

</details>


### [76] [SE-GA: Memory-Augmented Self-Evolution for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16883)
*Shilong Jin,Lanjun Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SE-GA框架，通过分层记忆结构（TTME）实现动态长程规划，并结合迭代自改进机制（MASE）持续优化基础策略，在多个GUI智能体基准测试中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体受限于有限上下文窗口和静态策略，难以适应动态环境中的多步任务，亟需一种能够动态扩展上下文并持续自我改进的解决方案。

Method: 提出SE-GA框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) TTME（测试时记忆扩展）——在推理期间动态检索情节记忆、语义记忆和经验记忆，提供相关上下文以支持长程规划；(2) MASE（记忆增强自进化）——利用TTME收集的数据进行训练，以稳定和增强智能体的基础策略。

Result: ScreenSpot上89.0%成功率，AndroidControl-High数据集上75.8%成功率，在AndroidWorld基准上展现了优越的动态环境泛化能力。

Conclusion: 分层记忆结构与自进化机制的结合能有效突破GUI智能体的上下文长度限制和策略僵化问题，显著提升多步任务表现和动态适应能力。

Abstract: Autonomous Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents often struggle with multi-step tasks due to constrained context windows and static policies that fail to adapt to dynamic environments. To address these limitations, this work proposes the Self-Evolving GUI Agent (SE-GA), a novel framework that integrates hierarchical memory structures with an iterative self-improvement mechanism. At the core of our approach is Test-Time Memory Extension (TTME), which facilitates long-term planning by dynamically retrieving episodic, semantic, and experiential memories to provide salient contexts during inference. To ensure continuous learning, we introduce Memory-Augmented Self-Evolution (MASE), which is a training pipeline that adopts the data collected by TTME to stabilize and enhance the agent's foundational policy. Extensive evaluations across both offline and online benchmarks demonstrate SE-GA achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching success rates of 89.0\% on ScreenSpot and 75.8\% on the challenging AndroidControl-High dataset. Furthermore, significant improvements on the AndroidWorld benchmark highlight the superior generalization to dynamic environments. Open source code: https://github.com/jinshilong-dev/SE-GA

</details>


### [77] [AIM: Adversarial Information Masking for Faithfulness Evaluation of Saliency Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16905)
*Chia-Ying Hsieh,Hsin-Yuan Fang,Chun-Shu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗信息掩蔽（AIM）框架，通过对抗特征替换来评估显著性图的忠实性和掩蔽算子的可靠性，在图像、音频和EEG任务中减少了掩蔽偏差，并揭示了模态依赖的归因差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事后显著性方法的忠实性评估存在缺陷：零掩蔽会产生分布外伪影，插值掩蔽会保留残差预测信息，导致性能下降被掩蔽算子混淆。

Method: 提出AIM框架，用输入的对抗样本值替换被选特征，比较互补掩蔽顺序下的性能下降；通过随机归因偏差和解释方法忠实性排名的稳定性来评估掩蔽算子可靠性。

Result: 在图像、音频和EEG任务上，AIM相比零掩蔽和插值掩蔽减少了掩蔽引起的偏差，并揭示了有符号与无符号归因之间的模态依赖差异。

Conclusion: AIM提供了更可靠的显著性方法忠实性评估方式，且不同模态下归因方法的选择需要根据数据类型进行调整。

Abstract: Post-hoc saliency methods are widely used to interpret deep neural networks, but their faithfulness is difficult to evaluate reliably. Existing evaluations mask features according to saliency-induced feature ordering and measure performance degradation, but this degradation can be confounded by the masking operator: zero masking may create out-of-distribution artifacts, while interpolation-based masking may preserve residual predictive information. We propose Adversarial Information Masking (AIM), a saliency-guided adversarial feature replacement framework for evaluating both saliency-map faithfulness and masking-operator reliability. AIM replaces selected features with values from an adversarial counterpart of the input and compares degradation under complementary masking orders. We assess reliability using random-attribution bias and stability of explanation-method faithfulness rankings. Experiments on image, audio, and EEG tasks suggest that AIM reduces masking-induced bias compared with zero and interpolation-based masking, while revealing modality-dependent differences between signed and unsigned attributions.

</details>


### [78] [Decision-Aware Proximal Bridge Learning for Optimal Treatment Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16989)
*Tomàs Garriga,Alejandro Almodóvar,Axel Brando,Gerard Sanz,Eduard Serrahima de Cambra,Juan Parras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向决策的加权桥梁损失函数，用于在存在隐混淆的连续治疗选择中优化个体化治疗决策，区别于传统均匀估计全局因果响应面的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续动作空间下的个体化治疗选择方法通常全局均匀估计因果响应面，这在决策相关区域上分配了次优的建模资源；而近端因果推断中的决策感知方法尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出策略目标加权桥梁损失（policy-targeted weighted bridge loss），强调决策相关治疗区域同时保持全局稳定性；证明了加权不适应常数控制治疗选择遗憾的界限；在多种近端桥梁求解器中实例化为决策感知变体，交替进行加权桥梁估计、响应面投影、策略更新和权重细化。

Result: 实验表明，决策感知加权在多个桥梁求解器上降低了治疗选择的遗憾，改善了近端设置下的治疗选择效果。

Conclusion: 决策感知加权方法能有效提升近端因果推断中的个体化治疗选择质量，弥补了该领域在最优决策方面的研究空白。

Abstract: Individualized treatment selection with continuous actions requires accurate causal response estimation in decision-relevant regions, rather than uniformly over the entire action space. Estimating a global causal response surface and then choosing the treatment that maximizes it can therefore be suboptimal, since standard estimation objectives allocate modeling effort according to the observed treatment distribution rather than the regions that determine the optimal decision. While decision-aware approaches have been studied in unconfounded settings, this problem remains underexplored in proximal causal inference, where proxy variables and bridge functions enable identification under suitable assumptions even in the presence of hidden confounding. Despite recent progress, proximal methods have primarily focused on treatment-effect and potential-outcome estimation rather than treatment selection and optimal decision-making. To bridge this gap, we introduce a policy-targeted weighted bridge loss that emphasizes decision-relevant treatment regions while retaining global stabilization. We prove a regret bound showing that the proposed weighted bridge loss controls treatment-selection regret through a weighted ill-posedness constant. We instantiate the framework in decision-aware variants of several proximal bridge solvers, yielding practical algorithms that alternate between weighted bridge estimation, response-surface projection, policy update, and weight refinement. Empirically, we find that decision-aware weighting reduces regret across several bridge solvers, suggesting improved treatment selection in proximal settings.

</details>


### [79] [When Dynamics Shift, Robust Task Inference Wins: Offline Imitation Learning with Behavior Foundation Models Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17017)
*Rishabh Agrawal,Rahul Jain,Ashutosh Nayyar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个在任务推理阶段实现动力学鲁棒性的行为基础模型框架，通过鲁棒极小极大优化，仅使用单一标称环境的离线数据即可适应现实世界中的动力学变化（如摩擦、驱动、传感器噪声），显著优于标准BFM和鲁棒离线IL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为基础模型（BFMs）假设环境动力学固定不变，无法应对现实世界中摩擦、驱动或传感器噪声等动力学变化，限制了其在动态环境中的鲁棒性。

Method: 将BFM任务推理形式化为鲁棒极小极大优化问题，在不修改预训练过程的前提下，通过对抗最坏情况下的动力学扰动来实现任务推理阶段的适应性。

Result: 在动力学变化场景下，该方法显著优于标准BFM和鲁棒离线IL基线方法。

Conclusion: 鲁棒策略可以完全在任务推理阶段实现，无需修改预训练或依赖多样化的环境数据，从而提升了BFM在动态环境中的实用性。

Abstract: Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) enable scalable imitation learning (IL) by pretraining task-agnostic representations that can be rapidly adapted to new tasks. However, existing BFMs assume fixed environment dynamics, limiting their robustness under real-world shifts such as changes in friction, actuation, or sensor noise. We address this by formulating BFM task-inference as a robust minimax optimization problem, enabling adaptation to worst-case dynamics perturbations without modifying pretraining. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first BFM-based framework that achieves robustness to dynamics shifts while relying solely on offline data from a single nominal environment. Our approach significantly outperforms standard BFM and robust offline IL baselines under dynamics shifts. These results demonstrate that robust policy can be achieved entirely at task-inference time, improving the practicality of BFMs in dynamic settings.

</details>


### [80] [Why Do Reasoning Models Lose Coverage? The Role of Data and Forks in the Road](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17026)
*Ngoc-Hieu Nguyen,Parshin Shojaee,Phuc Minh Nguyen,Nan Zhang,Chandan K Reddy,Khoa D Doan,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于SFT的后训练中推理模型出现的"覆盖收缩"现象（pass@k下降），发现该现象与训练数据中的"决策点"（分叉路口）场景密切相关，并提出了通过目标数据合成和多样性解码机制进行缓解的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在通过SFT后训练后虽然pass@1准确率提升，但出现了pass@k退化的覆盖收缩行为，作者希望探究这一现象产生的根本原因。

Method: 设计受控案例研究模拟决策点场景（包括图分支中的不可判定节点和推理模式），追踪后训练动态，分析收缩现象与训练数据中决策点场景普遍性的相关性。

Result: 发现覆盖收缩现象与训练数据中决策点场景的普遍性紧密相关；通过目标数据合成和多样性鼓励解码机制可部分缓解该行为。

Conclusion: 数据中心的因素是推理模型覆盖收缩的关键驱动因素，多样性感知设计是控制该现象的有效手段。

Abstract: Recent progress in large language models has led to the emergence of reasoning models, which have shown strong performance on complex tasks through specialized fine-tuning procedures. While these methods reliably improve pass@1 accuracy, prior works have observed that they show a coverage shrinkage behavior, where pass@k degrades relative to the base model. In this paper, we investigate the reasoning shrinkage arise under SFT-based post-training. We hypothesize that this behavior is driven by properties of the fine-tuning data, specifically related to decision points or "forks in the road" scenarios where model faces indecipherable patterns with multiple valid reasoning paths. To test this hypothesis, we design controlled case studies that simulate such decision-point settings, spanning indecipherable nodes in graph branching, and reasoning modes.
