Motivation: 差分隐私微调LLM时，梯度裁剪和噪声注入导致显著的效用下降。现有工作通过将DP与LoRA等参数高效微调方法结合来改善这一权衡，但这些方法仅约束了更新的形式（where to update），本文探索互补方向：选择性微调，即约束更新的位置（which parameters to update），以进一步缓解DP带来的效用损失。

Method: 提出DP-SelFT框架，包含三个关键设计：1) 构建轻量级DP合成数据集，选择阶段仅在该合成数据上进行，不产生额外隐私成本；2) 在合成数据上划分训练集和验证集，临时训练候选层子集并评估其性能；3) 临时训练时施加与下游DP微调匹配的扰动（最坏情况扰动与DP噪声同尺度），确保选出的层子集不仅可学习，还对噪声鲁棒。

Result: 在基准任务上的实验表明，DP-SelFT在相同隐私保证下，持续优于现有的DP微调基线方法，有效改善了隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 选择性微调（约束更新位置）是与参数高效微调（约束更新形式）互补的有效方向，DP-SelFT通过精心设计的参数选择机制可显著提升DP微调的效用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are commonly adapted to downstream tasks through fine-tuning, but fine-tuning data often contains sensitive information that may be leaked by the resulting model. Differential privacy (DP) offers formal protection against such leakage, yet DP fine-tuning of LLMs still suffers from substantial utility degradation due to gradient clipping and noise injection. Existing work improves this trade-off by combining DP with parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA, which constrain the form of updates. In this work, we study a complementary direction: selective fine-tuning, which constrains where updates are applied.
  We propose DP-SelFT, a framework for differentially private selective fine-tuning of LLMs. DP-SelFT addresses three DP-specific challenges in parameter selection: avoiding repeated privacy cost, improving stability under noisy estimates, and selecting parameters that remain useful under clipped and noisy updates. It first constructs a lightweight DP synthetic dataset and performs selection only on this synthetic data, so the selection stage incurs no additional privacy cost. It then conducts layer-level selection by temporarily training candidate layer subsets on a synthetic training split and evaluating them on a synthetic validation split. Crucially, this temporary training is performed under a perturbation regime matched to downstream DP fine-tuning, with worst-case perturbations of the same scale as DP noise. This favors layer subsets that are not only learnable but also robust to noisy private updates. Experiments on benchmark tasks show that DP-SelFT consistently improves the privacy--utility trade-off over existing DP fine-tuning baselines under the same privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [117] [ClaHF: A Human Feedback-inspired Reinforcement Learning Framework for Improving Classification Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17458)
*Tianxiang Xu,Xiaoyan Zhu,Xin Lai,Jiayin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ClaHF框架，将人类反馈强化学习引入文本分类，通过偏好建模和RL优化替代传统监督微调，在8个分类任务上提升性能与置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督微调（SFT）本质上是基于实例标签的行为克隆，无法充分捕捉样本间的相对偏好关系，限制了模型塑造决策边界和校准预测置信度的能力。

Method: 构建ClaHF框架：1）构造多个候选预测及其相对排序关系；2）在奖励模型中联合建模Top-1偏好与非最优候选间的排序；3）将标签监督转化为偏好信号用于策略优化，无需额外人工标注。

Result: 在涵盖三类场景的8个分类任务上，ClaHF在不同语言模型上一致提升了分类性能和置信度校准。

Conclusion: 将人类反馈启发下的偏好建模与RL优化引入文本分类是有效的，能弥补SFT在偏好关系建模上的不足。

Abstract: Text classification models are typically trained via supervised fine-tuning (SFT). However, SFT essentially performs behavior cloning from instance-wise labels and thus fails to adequately capture relative preference relations among samples, which limits the model's ability to shape decision boundaries and calibrate predictive confidence. In this paper, we propose ClaHF, a human feedback-inspired reinforcement learning (RL) framework for text classification that integrates preference modeling and RL optimization into the classification pipeline without requiring additional human annotations. Unlike prior work that relies solely on instance-wise supervision, ClaHF constructs multiple candidate predictions together with their relative ranking relations, and jointly models the Top-1 preference and the ordering among non-optimal candidates within a reward model (RM). This design converts conventional label supervision into preference signals that are directly applicable to policy optimization. We conduct systematic evaluations on eight classification tasks spanning three categories of scenarios. Results demonstrate that ClaHF consistently improves both classification performance and confidence calibration across diverse language models (LMs). The data and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ClaHF.

</details>


### [118] [TriOpt: A Scalable Algorithm for Linear Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17465)
*Rafat Ashraf Joy,Elena Zheleva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TriOpt方法，通过将排序估计与连续优化两阶段紧密结合，实现了高维线性因果发现中数量级的加速，同时保持可比或更优的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于排序的方法和连续优化方法在高维场景下均面临严重的可扩展性限制，难以兼顾效率和精度。

Method: TriOpt采用两阶段策略：第一阶段利用Sherman-Morrison秩1下推技术与线性核的加性结构高效恢复拓扑排序；第二阶段在给定排序下将结构学习转化为无环性约束的凸连续优化问题。

Result: 在合成、半合成及真实数据集上，TriOpt在高维场景下相比现有最优方法实现数量级加速，准确率相当或更优。

Conclusion: 理论上证明在真实排序下TriOpt可精确恢复线性DAG；实验验证其在可扩展性与准确性之间的有效权衡。

Abstract: Learning causal relations from observational data is challenging because the graph search space grows super-exponentially with the number of variables. Ordering-based methods reduce this space by first identifying the topological ordering, whereas continuous optimization methods explore most likely regions of the space by casting DAG learning as a differentiable objective with an acyclicity constraint. Despite their conceptual appeal, both paradigms face significant scalability limitations in high-dimensional settings, restricting their practical applicability. In this work, we introduce a new formulation for linear causal discovery that tightly integrates these two paradigms to achieve substantial gains in scalability without sacrificing accuracy. Our approach, TriOpt, decomposes the problem into two efficient stages. First, it recovers the topological ordering by exploiting the Sherman-Morrison rank-1 downdate together with the additive structure of linear kernels, enabling fast and scalable ordering estimation. Second, given this ordering, we reformulate structure learning as a convex continuous optimization problem that entirely avoids the need for enforcing costly acyclicity constraints. We theoretically show that, under the true ordering, TriOpt exactly recovers the underlying linear DAG. Empirically, across synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real-world datasets, TriOpt achieves orders-of-magnitude speedups over state-of-the-art linear causal discovery methods in high-dimensional regimes, while maintaining comparable or superior accuracy.

</details>


### [119] [WinQ: Accelerating Quantization-Aware Training of Language Models Around Saddle Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17471)
*Dongyue Li,Zechun Liu,Kai Yi,Zhenshuo Zhang,Changsheng Zhao,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi,Harshit Khaitan,Hongyang R. Zhang,Steven Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了量化感知训练（QAT）收敛缓慢的根本原因——权重收敛到鞍点附近的平坦区域，Hessian矩阵特征值集中在零附近且幅度减小。提出WinQ算法，通过周期性重置权重到全精度与量化权重的线性插值以及噪声注入梯度正则化，加速QAT达4倍，在子4位量化中提升高达8.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化感知训练（QAT）在低位宽（<4-bit）下存在收敛缓慢和性能早期停滞的问题，尽管已有研究观察到该现象，但其精确原因尚不明确。

Method: (1) 通过估计损失曲面Hessian矩阵的谱来分析QAT收敛性，发现权重收敛到鞍点附近的平坦区域；(2) 提出WinQ算法：周期性将权重重置为全精度权重与量化权重的线性插值，以减小到量化网格的距离并增大特征值幅度；对注入噪声的权重计算梯度以正则化Hessian矩阵。

Result: WinQ在各种量化方法和模型上加速QAT高达4倍；在相同训练成本下，子4位量化性能提升高达8.8%。结果在16种不同语言模型、量化方法和位宽设置中保持一致。

Conclusion: QAT收敛慢的原因是权重陷入鞍点附近的平坦区域（Hessian特征值幅度小且正负共存）。WinQ通过权重重置和噪声注入梯度有效缓解该问题，显著加速QAT收敛并提升低位宽量化性能。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) is widely adopted to quantize language models by training full-precision weights using gradients from the quantized model. The main bottleneck is its slow convergence and early performance plateau, particularly below 4-bit-widths. While this problem has been observed in prior work, its precise cause remains unclear. In this paper, we analyze the convergence of QAT by estimating the spectrum of the loss-surface Hessians. We find that the weights converge to flat regions around saddle points, where a large fraction of the Hessian eigenvalues are both positive and negative. During training, an increasing fraction of Hessian eigenvalues concentrates around zero, whose magnitude decreases. At lower bit-widths, the magnitude of eigenvalues in the Hessian spectrum is significantly smaller. To mitigate these issues, we propose an algorithm called WinQ to accelerate QAT, which involves: (1) periodically resetting weights to the linear interpolation of full-precision and quantized weights, reducing the distance to the quantization grid and increasing eigenvalue magnitude, and (2) computing gradients of noise-injected weights to regularize the Hessian. Extensive experiments show that WinQ accelerates QAT by up to 4 times across various quantization methods and models. Under the same training cost, WinQ improves state-of-the-art sub-4-bit quantization by up to 8.8%. These results are consistent across 16 settings with different language models, quantization methods, and bit widths.

</details>


### [120] [Self-Supervised On-Policy Distillation for Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17497)
*Zhiquan Tan,Yinrong Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自我监督在线策略蒸馏（SSOPD），通过将组内正确/错误回答对比转化为密集过程监督信号，在不依赖外部解决方案轨迹的情况下提升推理模型训练效率。在多个数学基准上，SSOPD优于GRPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO风格的RLVR训练仅通过终止奖励利用多个在线尝试，忽略了组内更丰富的过程信号——正确回答可作为当前策略能解决问题的自生成证据，错误回答则提供需要纠正的在线前缀。

Method: SSOPD从组内最短正确完成中提取教师分布，蒸馏到最长错误完成的前缀上，将组内正确-错误对比转化为密集过程监督。采用最短正确/最长错误规则作为有限组近似，并引入提示级前沿权重聚焦辅助损失。

Result: 在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上，SSOPD在所有9个模型-基准设置中均优于GRPO。在Qwen3-8B上，宏平均Avg@12达到65.6，比GRPO高1.6点，比解决方案条件的OPSD基线高0.8点。

Conclusion: SSOPD通过自生成的过程监督信号有效提升了推理模型性能，验证了组内对比信息的价值，为无外部监督的推理训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: GRPO-style RLVR trains reasoning models from multiple on-policy attempts per prompt, but typically uses these attempts only through terminal rewards. We show that a mixed group contains a richer process signal: a correct completion is a self-generated witness of how the current policy can solve the problem, while a wrong completion provides on-policy prefixes where the policy needs correction. We introduce \emph{Self-Supervised On-Policy Distillation} (SSOPD), which distills a teacher distribution conditioned on the shortest correct completion into prefixes of the longest wrong completion. This converts intra-group correct--wrong contrast into dense process supervision without external solution traces. A stopping-time view motivates the shortest-correct / longest-wrong rule as a finite-group approximation to editing persistent failures toward fast-success actions, and a prompt-level frontier weight concentrates the auxiliary loss where correct and wrong branches coexist. Across AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025, SSOPD improves over GRPO in all nine model-benchmark settings. On Qwen3-8B, it reaches a macro Avg@12 of 65.6, outperforming GRPO by 1.6 points and the solution-conditioned OPSD baseline by 0.8 points. Code will be released at https://github.com/tzq1999/SSOPD.

</details>


### [121] [BESplit: Bias-Compensated Split Federated Learning with Evidential Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17508)
*Yuhan Xie,Chen Lyu,Jingrong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BESplit通过利用分割架构的固有结构，提出证据聚合（EA）、偏差补偿协作（BCC）和双教师蒸馏（DTD）三种机制，在不平衡数据分布下提升分割联邦学习的精度、收敛稳定性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非独立同分布数据下，分割联邦学习存在有偏优化和收敛不稳定问题，现有方案多直接套用传统联邦学习方法，未能利用SFL分割架构的独特优势进行偏差补偿。

Method: 提出BESplit框架，包含三个组件：1) 证据聚合(EA)基于证据不确定性对客户端贡献进行细粒度重加权；2) 偏差补偿协作(BCC)通过配对互补客户端对齐分割层表示；3) 双教师蒸馏(DTD)在解耦的客户端和服务器模型间同步知识，支持独立本地推理。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，BESplit在多种非独立同分布场景下，在准确率、收敛稳定性和计算效率方面均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 利用SFL分割架构的固有结构设计架构感知的偏差补偿方法，能够有效缓解非独立同分布带来的负面影响，为分割联邦学习提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Split Federated Learning (SFL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative training by partitioning models between clients and a server. However, under non-IID data distributions, SFL often suffers from biased optimization and unstable convergence, while existing solutions largely adapt techniques from conventional federated learning. In this work, we observe that the split architecture of SFL inherently alters how client information is represented and coordinated, opening opportunities for bias compensation beyond parameter-level aggregation. Based on this insight, we propose BESplit, an architecture-aware framework that exploits the intrinsic structure of SFL to mitigate non-IID effects. First, to prevent biased local data from dominating global updates, we introduce Evidential Aggregation (EA) to perform fine-grained reweighting of client contributions based on evidential uncertainty. Second, to further reduce distributional skew, we develop Bias-Compensated Collaboration (BCC) to align split-layer representations by pairing complementary clients. Finally, Dual-Teacher Distillation (DTD) is incorporated to synchronize knowledge between decoupled client and server models, enabling independent local inference. Extensive experiments on five benchmark datasets demonstrate that BESplit consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in accuracy, convergence stability, and computational efficiency under diverse non-IID settings.

</details>


### [122] [Coordinate Heterogeneity Governs Binary Quantization: From InfoNCE to Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17524)
*Wenxuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过建立InfoNCE表征的高斯结构与二进制量化质量之间的完整分析框架，揭示了坐标异质性（即各坐标方差非均匀性）是决定BQ性能的关键因素，推导了排序保真度的闭式表达式，并证明随机旋转会破坏一种范式所需的信号同时创造另一种范式所需的各向同性，最终通过13个数据集和6种嵌入族验证了所有预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决二进制量化在对比学习嵌入上表现优异但在其他嵌入上失败这一长期存在的谜题，以及两种主流系统（随机旋转 vs 保持坐标轴）采取截然相反策略却缺乏统一理论解释的问题。

Method: 将InfoNCE训练表征的高斯结构与BQ质量分析框架连接，推导排序保真度的闭式表达式，证明幅度比特携带的信息与异质性成正比，建立两参数缩放定律预测跨模型和维度的保真度。

Result: 在13个数据集和6种嵌入族上的实验验证了所有理论预测，首次提供了二进制量化系统的原则性设计指南。

Conclusion: 坐标异质性是BQ性能的核心决定因素，不同范式对旋转策略的需求差异源于异质性信号的利用方式不同，该框架为实际BQ系统设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Binary quantization (BQ) compresses high-dimensional embeddings into one or two bits per coordinate, enabling nearest neighbor search at extreme speed. Yet a striking puzzle persists: BQ achieves competitive recall on contrastive embeddings but fails on others -- and two leading systems adopt diametrically opposite strategies (random rotation vs. preserving coordinate axes) without a common theory explaining when each is appropriate. We resolve this puzzle by connecting the Gaussian structure recently established for InfoNCE-trained representations to a complete analytical framework for BQ quality. The key insight is that coordinate heterogeneity -- the non-uniformity of per-coordinate variances -- governs the key aspects of BQ performance. We derive closed-form expressions for ranking fidelity, prove that the magnitude bit carries information proportional to heterogeneity, and show that random rotation destroys precisely the signal that one paradigm exploits while creating the isotropy that the other requires. A two-parameter scaling law predicts fidelity across models and dimensions. Experiments on 13 datasets and 6 embedding families validate all predictions and provide the first principled design guide for binary quantization systems.

</details>


### [123] [CasualSynth: Generating Structurally Sound Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17528)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun,Thomas Lukasiewicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalSynth 是一个将因果结构生成与语义实现解耦的框架，通过三阶段流程（SCM生成因果骨架→LLM约束翻译→迭代一致性验证）生成既符合因果机制又语义丰富的合成数据，在三个因果基准上实现了近名义水平的条件独立性保持和96%以上的可实现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的合成数据虽然语义真实，但无法保证其输出遵循目标领域的因果机制，现有方法在因果有效性上存在缺陷。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 基于结构因果模型（SCM）通过祖先采样生成满足全局马尔可夫性质的因果骨架；(2) LLM作为受约束的"实现器"将骨架映射为高维观测数据；(3) 迭代一致性验证模块通过确定性提取检测结构违规并反馈LLM进行闭环修正。同时提出语义后门问题并证明迭代机制可减少选择偏差。

Result: 在ASIA、ALARM和MIMIC-Struct三个因果基准上，条件独立性的假阳性率接近名义水平α=0.05，使用70B参数LLM骨干时可实现率超过96%。

Conclusion: CausalSynth能够生成因果有效且语义丰富的合成数据，支持原则性的干预和反事实生成，并通过迭代验证有效缓解了LLM的语义后门问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) generate realistic synthetic data but offer no guarantee that their outputs respect the causal mechanisms governing the target domain. We introduce CausalSynth, a framework that decouples causal structure generation from semantic realization, yielding synthetic data that is both causally valid and linguistically rich. The framework operates in three phases. First, a Structural Causal Model (SCM) - a tuple of structural equations defined over a directed acyclic graph (DAG) generates causal skeletons, i.e., variable assignments that satisfy the Global Markov Property of the governing DAG, via ancestral sampling. Second, an LLM acts as a constrained \emph{realizer}, a conditional translator that maps each skeleton to a high-dimensional observation such as a clinical note or a transaction log. Third, an Iterative Consistency Verification module detects structural violations through deterministic extraction and feeds targeted corrections back to the LLM, forming a closed-loop refinement process. We identify the Semantic Backdoor problem the systematic tendency of LLMs to override imposed causal facts with pre-training priors -- and prove that our iterative mechanism reduces the resulting selection bias relative to standard rejection sampling. On three causal benchmarks (ASIA, ALARM, and MIMIC-Struct), CausalSynth preserved conditional independencies with false-positive rates near the nominal $α=0.05$ level and achieved realizability rates above 96% with 70B-parameter LLM backbones. The framework additionally supports principled interventional and counterfactual generation through noise retention and graph mutilation.

</details>


### [124] [Q-LocalAdam: Memory-Efficient Client-Side Adaptive Optimization for Edge Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17552)
*Vedant Waykole,Haroon R. Lone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-LocalAdam，通过对联邦Adam优化器中的动量（对称有界）和方差（对数正态分布，跨度8个数量级）采用不同的8位量化策略（动量用块级线性编码，方差用对数空间编码），在非IID边缘设备联邦学习场景下实现3.37倍优化器内存压缩，且在极端数据异构下准确率提升达+5.74个百分点，而均匀量化会退化至随机性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在边缘设备上需应对非IID数据和有限内存的双重挑战。自适应优化器（如Adam）虽能稳定训练，但其全精度动量状态和方差状态会使客户端内存开销增加近两倍，严重限制了资源受限设备上可部署的模型大小和并发联邦任务。

Method: 首先通过实证分析发现联邦Adam中动量与方差具有根本不同的统计特性：动量值对称且有界，而方差跨度达8个数量级且呈对数正态分布。基于此不对称性，提出Q-LocalAdam：对动量采用块级线性8位量化，对方差采用对数空间8位编码，同时模型参数保持全精度。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，不同数据异构程度（α∈{0.1, 0.5, 1.0, IID}）下，Q-LocalAdam实现3.37倍优化器内存压缩；中等异构下无精度损失，极端异构下显著提升（如CIFAR-100 α=0.1时+5.74个百分点）。多种子验证统计显著（p<0.01）。对比实验表明均匀量化退化至随机性能。

Conclusion: 分布感知的量化设计对联邦Adam优化器内存压缩至关重要。Q-LocalAdam在不修改联邦协议的前提下，使内存受限的边缘设备能够部署更大模型和运行更多并发工作负载，为资源高效的联邦学习提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Federated learning on edge devices must cope with non-IID client data and tight memory budgets. Adaptive optimizers like Adam stabilize training under data heterogeneity but require storing full-precision momentum and variance states, often tripling client memory overhead. This limits deployable model sizes and concurrent federated jobs on resource-constrained devices.
  We empirically observe that momentum and variance in federated Adam exhibit fundamentally different statistical properties: momentum values are symmetric and bounded, while variance spans eight orders of magnitude with log-normal structure. Motivated by this asymmetry, we propose \textbf{Q-LocalAdam}, which applies distribution-aware 8-bit quantization block-wise linear encoding for momentum and log-space encoding for variance while keeping model parameters in full precision.
  Across CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 under varying data heterogeneity ($α\in \{0.1, 0.5, 1.0, \text{IID}\}$), Q-LocalAdam achieves $3.37\times$ optimizer memory reduction with no accuracy loss under moderate heterogeneity and significant improvements under extreme heterogeneity (e.g., +5.74pp on CIFAR-100, $α=0.1$). Multi-seed validation confirms statistical significance ($p<0.01$). In contrast, naive uniform quantization degrades to random performance, demonstrating that distribution-aware design is essential. Q-LocalAdam enables larger models and more concurrent workloads on memory-constrained edge devices without modifying the federated protocol.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Accuracy: Robustness, Interpretability and Expressiveness of EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17562)
*Urban Širca,Maryam Alimardani,Stefanos Zafeiriou,Konstantinos Barmpas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地评估了六种EEG基础模型在鲁棒性、可解释性和表达能力三个维度上的表现，发现：无单一模型在所有故障模式下占优；模型虽能关注正确脑区但解码内容受损；预训练表示质量被低估，保留token级嵌入足以支撑下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型的评估主要集中于干净、分布内数据的准确率，对其鲁棒性、可解释性和表征质量缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 在八个数据集上对六种EEG-FM与基线深度学习模型进行基准测试，从三个层面分析：(i)鲁棒性：测试时扰动包括加性噪声、随机/区域通道丢弃及区域特异性噪声注入；(ii)可解释性：首次将Attention-Aware Layer-Wise Relevance Propagation (AttnLRP)应用于EEG-FM；(iii)表达能力：采用分块探针分析各层在微调中的重利用情况。

Result: 无单一模型在所有故障模式下占优；最抗噪声的模型在通道丢弃下最脆弱，且丢弃而非补零可缓解脆弱性；模型注意力集中于任务相关脑区但扰动下预测退化；后期块在微调中被重利用，早期块已包含任务相关信息；头部分类器性能差主要源于池化操作，保留token级嵌入即可展现充分表征能力。

Conclusion: 为EEG基础模型的鲁棒性、可解释性和表达能力提供了首次系统评估，揭示了模型开发中的关键考量因素。

Abstract: EEG foundation models (EEG-FMs) have been evaluated predominantly on clean, in-distribution accuracy, leaving their robustness, interpretability and representational quality largely unexamined. This study addresses these gaps by benchmarking six EEG-FMs against a baseline deep learning model across eight datasets. Beyond clean accuracy, we conduct three layers of analysis: (i) Robustness: we apply test-time perturbations including additive noise, random and region-based channel dropout and region-specific noise injection. Our analyses show that no single model dominates all failure modes. The most noise-robust model is among the most fragile under channel dropout and much of the dropout fragility disappears when channels are removed rather than zero-padded. (ii) Interpretability: we present the first application of Attention-Aware Layer-Wise Relevance Propagation (AttnLRP) to EEG-FMs and show that models broadly concentrate relevance on task-appropriate brain regions consistent with known neurophysiology. However, attribution maps remain spatially stable under perturbation while predictions degrade, suggesting that the models attend to the correct brain regions but decode corrupted content. (iii) Expressiveness: With block-wise probing we show that late blocks are repurposed during fine-tuning, while early blocks already hold task-related information. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the poor head-only performance previously attributed to low-quality pre-trained representations is largely explained by pooling and that EEG-FMs possess sufficient representational capacity when their token-level embeddings are preserved. Together, these findings provide the first systematic assessment of robustness, interpretability and expressiveness for EEG-FMs and highlight critical considerations for their development.

</details>


### [126] [How Off-Policy Can GRPO Be? Mu-GRPO for Efficient LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17570)
*Minghao Tian,Yunfei Xie,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mu-GRPO框架，通过将训练组织为少量大规模序列化的生成-优化阶段，允许更大的rollout陈旧性，在保持或提升性能的同时实现约2倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO采用低陈旧性的近策略训练，系统开销大。研究探索GRPO能容忍多大程度的off-policy陈旧性，以降低切换开销、提升训练效率。

Method: 将训练分为少量（如4个）大规模序列化生成-优化阶段，引入松弛剪辑（保留有用陈旧梯度）和负优势否决（去除不稳定后缀更新）两种稳定策略。

Result: 在5种语言模型和多个数学推理基准上，Mu-GRPO性能匹配或超越标准GRPO，同时实现约2倍的实际训练时间加速。

Conclusion: GRPO算法能容忍比以往假设更大的rollout陈旧性，Mu-GRPO建立了显著更优的LLM强化学习性能-效率权衡。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has been a key driver of recent progress in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for large language models, but it is typically trained in a low-staleness, near-on-policy regime that incurs substantial system overhead. We ask a simple question: How off-policy can GRPO be? We show that GRPO-style algorithms can tolerate substantially larger rollout staleness than previously assumed, and propose Mu-GRPO, an RL training framework that organizes training into a small number (e.g., four) of large sequential generation-optimization stages. This design induces high rollout staleness while greatly reducing rollout-optimization switching overhead. To stabilize learning under stale data, Mu-GRPO combines relaxed clipping, which preserves useful stale-rollout gradients, with negative-advantage veto, which removes destabilizing post-trigger suffix updates in negative-advantage responses. Across five language models and multiple math reasoning benchmarks, Mu-GRPO matches or exceeds the performance of standard GRPO while achieving around 2x speedup in wall-clock training time, establishing a substantially improved performance-efficiency trade-off for LLM reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [127] [Form and Function: Machine Unlearning as a Problem of Misaligned States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17590)
*Kennon Stewart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了在线L-BFGS的机器遗忘问题，将其建模为反事实状态对齐问题，并引入了状态感知评估指标。在凸性假设下推导了反事实状态偏差的递归界，并通过实验表明遗忘不仅是参数校正问题，还需要与可实现的优化器状态对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线L-BFGS优化器在处理数据流时，需要移除已删除样本的影响（即机器遗忘），但现有方法缺乏针对优化器状态（包括参数和内存算子）的专门遗忘评估框架。

Method: 将遗忘建模为反事实状态对齐问题，定义实际事件流与删除编辑后的反事实流之间的状态差异；引入四种状态感知指标（参数误差、内存算子误差、组合状态误差、更新方向误差）；在凸性假设下推导反事实状态偏差的递归界；设计了包括仅内存校正和仅参数校正的消融基准实验。

Result: 遗忘不仅是参数校正问题，还需要对齐可实现的优化器状态；仅校正参数或仅校正内存均不足以实现有效的遗忘。

Conclusion: 在线L-BFGS的机器遗忘需要同时对齐参数和内存状态，单纯参数级别的校正无法达到反事实目标。

Abstract: We formulate machine unlearning for online L-BFGS as a counterfactual state-alignment problem. Given an actual event stream and a deletion-edited counterfactual stream, the target of unlearning is the optimizer state that would have arisen had the deleted samples never been processed. We introduce state-aware metrics that separately measure parameter error, memory-operator error, combined state error, and update-direction error. The memory metric compares the inverse-Hessian actions induced by the o-L-BFGS memory, rather than treating curvature pairs as of finite influence. Under convexity assumptions, we derive a recursive bound on counterfactual state deviation. We then evaluate a state-aware benchmark of deletion interventions, including memory-only and parameter-only corrections, against an counterfactual oracle model. These results show that unlearning for online L-BFGS is not merely a parameter-correction problem: it requires alignment with a realizable counterfactual optimizer state.

</details>


### [128] [Venom: A PyTorch Generative Modeling Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17605)
*Liang Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VENOM是一个基于PyTorch的教育工具包，将多种生成模型（扩散模型、得分匹配、流匹配、VAE、归一化流、GAN、能量模型）统一在MNIST优先的接口下，提供一致的训练/采样API、双语教程和轻量级基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成建模领域范式繁多（扩散模型、得分SDE、流匹配、VAE、归一化流、对抗模型、能量模型等），各自独立实现，导致新研究者难以在统一的代码库中比较不同方法的训练目标、推理过程、采样算法和条件机制。

Method: 开发了VENOM PyTorch工具包，采用统一的MNIST优先接口，整合了扩散/得分模型、流匹配与一步生成器、VAE、归一化流、GAN和能量模型；提供分离的训练/采样脚本、分类器/无分类器引导示例、双语教程笔记本，采用模型家族组织方式。

Result: 实现了涵盖六大类生成模型族的统一代码库，提供可读性强、可复现的入口，以及一致的训练和采样API，支持教学、原型开发和轻量级基准测试。

Conclusion: VENOM通过统一的代码框架降低了生成模型的学习和比较门槛，强调广度与可读性而非大规模性能工程，为教育和研究原型设计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Modern generative modeling has grown into a broad collection of related but often separately implemented paradigms, including denoising diffusion models, score-based stochastic differential equations, flow matching, variational autoencoders, normalizing flows, adversarial models, and energy-based models. For newcomers, this fragmentation makes it difficult to compare training objectives, inference procedures, sampling algorithms, and conditioning mechanisms within a single coherent codebase. We introduce V ENOM, an educational PyTorch toolkit that implements representative generative modeling families under a unified, MNIST-first interface. V ENOM emphasizes breadth, readability, reproducible entry points, and consistent training and sampling APIs rather than large-scale performance engineering. The package currently includes diffusion and score-based models, flow matching and one-step generators, variational autoencoders, normalizing flows, generative adversarial networks, and energy-based models. It provides separate training and sampling scripts, classifier and classifier-free guidance examples, bilingual tutorial notebooks, and a model-family organization that supports teaching, prototyping, and lightweight benchmarking.

</details>


### [129] [Adaptive Generate-Rank-Verify: Inference-Time Search with Costly Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17609)
*Shaddin Dughmi,Mahdi Haghifam,Yusuf Hakan Kalayci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了生成式主动搜索问题，提出了一种自适应生成-排序-验证算法ADAP，在单调性假设下其期望成本在常数因子内接近最优，并通过数学推理和编程竞赛实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理管线常将廉价奖励信号与昂贵验证器（如数学推理中的精确答案检查、代码生成中的隐藏测试执行）结合使用，但缺乏对这一设置的理论形式化和最优策略分析。

Method: 形式化为生成式主动搜索（成本敏感的首正搜索问题）；在分布已知时用动态规划刻画最优策略；在分布未知时提出ADAP（逐轮自适应生成-排序-验证算法），逐步增加采样数和前k验证数，并在单调性假设下证明常数因子近似最优性。

Result: ADAP在理论上达到分布感知最优策略的常数因子近似；实验在数学推理和竞争性编程任务上优于固定非自适应策略和难度自适应基线。

Conclusion: 提出并理论化了生成式主动搜索问题，开发了实用的ADAP算法，验证了自适应策略相较于固定策略的优势，并给出了必要的结构假设下界。

Abstract: Many inference-time language-model pipelines combine a cheap reward signal with an expensive verifier, such as exact answer checking in mathematical reasoning or hidden-test execution in code generation.
  We formalize this setting using a learning-theoretic lens as generative active search: a cost-sensitive first-positive search problem in which a policy adaptively samples candidates from an unknown distribution, observes cheap scores, and pays for verifier labels until it finds a positive example. For a fixed prompt, the generator and reward model induce two unknown objects: a distribution over reward scores and a score-conditioned success function. When these quantities are known, we characterize the distribution-aware optimal policy using a dynamic programming approach. In the realistic and practical setting where both the score distribution and success function are unknown, we propose ADAP, a shellwise adaptive generate-rank-verify algorithm that progressively increases the number of sampled responses and top-ranked verifications. Under the monotonicity assumption that higher reward scores are no less likely to pass verification, we show that ADAP achieves expected cost within a constant factor of the distribution-aware optimum. We complement this result with learning-theoretic lower bounds, based on a centered star number, showing that structural assumptions on the score--label relationship are necessary. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and competitive programming validate the predicted advantage over both fixed non-adaptive policies and difficulty-adaptive baselines.

</details>


### [130] [TabKDE: Simple and Scalable Tabular Data Generation with Kernel Density Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17642)
*Meysam Alishahi,Yan Zheng,Junpeng Wang,Chin-Chia Michael Yeh,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Copula变换和核密度估计的表格数据生成方法（TabKDE），该方法在几乎不需要训练时间的情况下，接近甚至匹配了现有基于VAE和扩散模型等昂贵方法的精度和泄漏避免能力，同时具有极高的可扩展性和更低的存储需求，并首次将核心集（coreset）思想引入生成式建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据生成方法（如VAE、扩散模型）虽然精度高，但计算成本昂贵，且依赖独热编码，难以处理大规模数据集。作者希望提出一种更轻量、可扩展且能避免数据泄漏的替代方案。

Method: 采用Copula变换将多元列依赖关系解耦，然后使用核密度估计（KDE）对变换后的数据分布进行建模。通过核心集（coreset）存储KDE模型，从而大幅减少存储空间。生成新数据时，从KDE中采样并逆Copula变换回原始空间。

Result: 该方法在几乎零训练时间的情况下，精度和泄漏避免能力接近现有最优方法；可扩展性极强，能在普通笔记本电脑上处理比先前方法大数个数量级的数据集；存储需求显著降低，因为模型以核心集形式保存。

Conclusion: 基于Copula变换和核密度估计的轻量级方法可以替代昂贵的深度学习模型用于表格数据生成，在保持生成质量的同时实现极快的训练速度和极高的可扩展性，核心集存储为生成式建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Tabular data generation considers a large table with multiple columns -- each column comprised of numerical, categorical, or sometimes ordinal values. The goal is to produce new rows for the table that replicate the distribution of rows from the original data -- without just copying those initial rows. The last 4 years have seen enormous progress on this problem, mostly using computational expensive methods that employ one-hot encoding, VAEs, and diffusion.
  This paper describes a new approach to the problem of tabular data generation. By employing copula transformations and modeling the distribution as a kernel density estimate we can nearly match the accuracy and leakage-avoidance achievements of the previous methods, but with almost no training time. Our method is very scalable, and can be run on data sets orders of magnitude larger than prior state-of-the-art on a simple laptop. Moreover, because we employ kernel density estimates, we can store the model as a coreset of the original data -- we believe the first for generative modeling -- and as a result, require significantly less space as well. Our code is available here: \url{https://github.com/tabkde/tabkde-main}

</details>


### [131] [Counterfactual Explanations Under Concept Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17651)
*Marcin Kostrzewa,Jerzy Stefanowski,Maciej Zięba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出数据流环境下反事实解释的维护问题，并设计了一种轻量级、模型无关的更新方案，通过局部采样估计有效性和合理性方向来修复已有解释，实验证明该方法能持续维持解释有效性且成本低于重复生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法假设静态框架，但在数据流环境中模型会因概念漂移而持续更新，导致已生成的解释可能悄然失效，这一问题此前未被关注。

Method: 提出一种轻量级、模型无关的更新方案，通过局部采样估计有效性和合理性方向，在保持与原始实例接近的前提下修复已有的反事实解释。

Result: 在合成漂移数据流上的实验表明，初始生成的反事实解释会迅速失效，而经过维护的解释能够持续保持有效性和局部合理性，且成本低于重复生成。

Conclusion: 所提出的维护方案能够在概念漂移环境下以较低成本持续保持反事实解释的有效性，优于重新生成策略。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CFEs) provide actionable recourse, but most methods assume a static framework with fixed data and a trained classifier. This assumption breaks in evolving data environments, such as data streams, where online models are repeatedly updated under concept drift. We identify CFE maintenance in this setting as a previously overlooked problem: explanations that are valid when generated may silently become invalid as the model evolves, including robust CFEs, which are not designed for continuous drift. We propose a lightweight, model-agnostic update scheme that repairs existing CFEs using local sampling to estimate validity and plausibility directions while preserving proximity to the original instance. Experiments on synthetic drifting streams show that initially created CFEs rapidly lose validity, whereas maintained CFEs preserve validity and local plausibility at a lower cost than repeated regeneration.

</details>


### [132] [When a Zero-Shooter Cheats: Improving Age Estimation via Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17658)
*Erik Imgrund,Pia Hanfeld,Klim Kireev,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的年龄估计存在"身份捷径"问题：模型倾向于先识别出人物身份，再根据记忆中的知识推断年龄，而非从视觉特征估计年龄。这导致非名人被误识别为名人时预测严重错误，但名人图像上表现出欺骗性的高鲁棒性。作者提出激活引导方法，通过干预VLM隐藏状态来抑制这一捷径，在多个基准上平均绝对误差降低高达25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有年龄估计方法主要关注模型性能，但忽视了VLM零样本特性带来的"身份捷径"问题。该问题导致模型在非名人图像上表现不佳，同时在名人图像上产生虚假的鲁棒性，亟需解决。

Method: 提出一种激活引导（activation steering）方法，通过干预VLM的隐藏状态来抑制模型利用身份信息推断年龄的倾向，迫使模型从视觉特征出发进行年龄估计。

Result: 该方法在多个流行基准上，针对记忆身份和未见身份均提升了年龄估计准确率，平均绝对误差最高降低25%。

Conclusion: VLM在年龄估计任务中普遍存在身份捷径问题，激活引导是一种有效的缓解手段；同时提醒研究者对基准测试中名人图像的评估结果持谨慎态度。

Abstract: Different age-related regulations have been proposed to protect minors from harmful content and interactions online. Automated age estimation is central to enforcing such regulations, and vision-language models (VLMs) achieve state-of-the-art performance on this task. However, we find that the zero-shot nature of VLM-based age estimation produces an unexpected side effect we call the identity shortcut: Instead of estimating age from visual features, VLMs tend to identify the depicted person and infer their age from memorized knowledge. This phenomenon leads to substantially incorrect predictions when non-celebrities are misidentified as celebrities. It also produces deceptively high robustness to noise and adversarial perturbations on celebrity images, which dominate popular benchmarks. To mitigate this, we propose an activation steering method that suppresses the shortcut by intervening on the hidden states of the VLM. This method improves age estimation accuracy for both memorized and unseen identities, reducing mean absolute error by up to 25% across popular benchmarks.

</details>


### [133] [Fine-tuning Pocket-Aware Diffusion Models via Denoising Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17693)
*Yuan Xue,Daniel Kudenko,Megha Khosla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DEPPA是一种基于去噪扩散策略优化的结构-based 分子优化方法，通过强化学习微调预训练的pocket-aware扩散模型，在CrossDocked2020基准上实现了结合亲和力(Vina Score -8.5 kcal/mol)、类药性和多样性的最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有pocket-aware 3D生成模型主要拟合训练分布，难以同时满足真实药物发现中多个分子属性要求，需要一种能对多个指定分子属性进行细粒度控制的方法。

Method: 将预训练的pocket-aware扩散模型的逆向去噪过程建模为多步马尔可夫决策过程，使用强化学习中的去噪扩散策略优化，以最终生成配体分子的多个属性作为奖励信号，并引入粗粒度去噪调度器提升优化效率。

Result: 在CrossDocked2020基准上，DEPPA在结合亲和力(Vina Score -8.5 kcal/mol)、类药性和多样性方面超越基线，在可合成性上表现出竞争力。

Conclusion: DEPPA通过强化学习微调扩散模型，有效实现了多属性分子优化，验证了该方法在结构-based药物设计中的潜力。

Abstract: Structure-based drug design has been accelerated by pocket-aware 3D generative models, yet most methods primarily fit the training distribution and may fall short of satisfying multiple properties required in real-world therapeutic drug discovery. Recently, increasing attention has focused on structure-based molecule optimization (SBMO), which targets fine-grained control over multiple specified molecular properties. In this paper, we present DEPPA, a novel SBMO approach building upon Denoising Diffusion Policy Optimization for fine-tuning a pre-trained pocket-aware diffusion model via reinforcement learning. DEPPA enables optimization over multiple properties, including binding affinity, drug-likeness, synthesizability and diversity. We formulate the reverse denoising process of the pretrained pocket-aware diffusion model as a multi-step Markov Decision Process, where the desired properties that serve as reward signals are evaluated on the final generated ligand molecules. DEPPA incorporates a coarse denoising scheduler during the RL fine-tuning to achieve efficient and effective molecule optimization. Experimental results on the CrossDocked2020 benchmark demonstrate that DEPPA outperforms baselines in binding affinity (Vina Score -8.5 kcal/mol), drug-likeness and diversity while exhibiting competitive performance in synthesizability. The source code is available at https://github.com/xy9485/DePPA .

</details>


### [134] [L-Drive: Beyond a Single Mapping-Latent Context Drives Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17730)
*Fan Zhang,Shijun Chen,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出L-Drive，一种基于潜在上下文和门控机制的时序预测框架，通过显式建模高维动态变化和改进段内结构建模，有效应对分布偏移和机制转换带来的预测挑战，在准确性和计算效率间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多变量时序预测方法（Direct-Mapping范式）在观测空间学习统一的历史到未来的映射，但真实系统常发生分布偏移和机制转换，统一的映射在转折点附近会出现响应滞后，导致切换窗口内误差累积，降低预测可靠性。

Method: L-Drive框架引入潜在上下文（Latent-Context）显式刻画随时间演进的高维动态，利用门控机制（gating）调制增量表示，提供更及时的变化线索；同时引入基于共享相对位置基函数的分块（patch-shared relative positional basis functions）增强段内结构建模，减少因绝对位置记忆导致的过拟合。

Result: 大量实验验证了L-Drive的有效性，在预测精度和计算效率之间取得了更好的整体权衡。

Conclusion: L-Drive通过显式建模潜在动态变化和改进段内结构，有效解决了传统方法在转折点附近的响应滞后问题，提升了时序预测在非平稳环境下的适应性和可靠性。

Abstract: Mainstream methods for multivariate time-series forecasting largely follow the Direct-Mapping paradigm. They learn a unified mapping from history to the future in the observation space to fit value-level dependencies. However, real-world systems often undergo distribution shifts and regime changes. In such cases, a unified mapping can exhibit response lag around turning points, causing error accumulation within the switching window and reducing forecasting reliability. To address this issue, we propose L-Drive, a change-aware forecasting framework. L-Drive introduces a Latent-Context, to explicitly characterize high-level dynamics evolving over time, and uses gating to modulate increment representations. This provides more timely change cues and improves adaptation to changing segments. In addition, it incorporates patch-shared relative positional basis functions to strengthen intra-segment structural modeling and reduce overfitting caused by absolute-position memorization. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of L-Drive and show a better overall trade-off between forecasting accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [135] [Testable and Actionable Calibration for Full Swap Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17749)
*Konstantina Bairaktari,Lunjia Hu,Huy L. Nguyen,Jonathan Ullman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的校准度量SCDL，同时满足可操作性和可检验性，解决了现有校准度量无法同时满足这两个基本需求的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有校准度量无法同时满足可操作性（能告知决策者将预测视为真实概率时的效用损失，即swap regret）和可检验性（从少量样本中测量校准误差）这两个基本要求，需要一种能同时满足这两种性质的校准度量。

Method: 提出Soft-Binned Calibration Decision Loss (SCDL)度量，通过软分箱技术结合决策损失，理论上证明了其完全可操作性（不弱化swap regret）和近乎最优的检验误差率，同时满足连续性和一致性。

Result: SCDL在理论上实现了完全可操作性和最优可检验性，实验证明SCDL相比其他度量在实际应用中表现更优。

Conclusion: SCDL是首个同时满足完全可操作性和近乎最优可检验性的校准度量，在理论和实践上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: AI generated predictions increasingly inform decision making in critical tasks, and therefore must be trustworthy. One widely used measure of trustworthiness is calibration, which requires that the predictions match the true frequencies and can be treated like real probabilities of a given outcome. However, defining calibration is subtle, and designing good measures of calibration error has been an active topic of recent research. The first goal is to find calibration measures that are actionable, meaning they can inform decision makers about their utility loss when predictions are treated as true probabilities, which is known as swap regret. The second goal is to find calibration measures that are testable, meaning that calibration error can be measured from a small sample of predictions and outcomes. Although these are very basic requirements, there is no existing calibration measure that fully satisfies both properties, and all existing measures relax actionability by bounding a weaker notion of swap regret, or relax testability by having suboptimal estimation error. We introduce a new calibration measure, Soft-Binned Calibration Decision Loss (SCDL), which we prove is fully actionable without weakening either requirement, and testable with nearly optimal error rate. In addition, SCDL satisfies other desired properties such as continuity and consistency. We also provide a set of experiments confirming that the theoretical advantages of SCDL compared to other measures lead to better performance in practice.

</details>


### [136] [OSCAR: Offline Spectral Covariance-Aware Rotation for 2-bit KV Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17757)
*Zhongzhu Zhou,Donglin Zhuang,Jisen Li,Ziyan Chen,Shuaiwen Leon Song,Ben Athiwaratkun,Xiaoxia Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OSCAR提出了一种基于注意力感知协方差结构的INT2 KV缓存量化方法，通过离线估计协方差结构推导固定旋转和裁剪阈值，实现了高精度且可部署的低比特量化，在多种大模型上接近BF16精度，并实现约8倍内存压缩和最高7倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INT2 KV缓存量化对长上下文LLM服务有吸引力，但现有方法（如Hadamard变换）难以同时保证精度和可部署性，简单的旋转操作虽能减少异常值，却因与下游注意力不对齐而导致性能退化。

Method: 离线估计注意力感知的协方差结构，据此推导固定的旋转矩阵和裁剪阈值用于量化，使KV量化与注意力实际消费的协方差结构对齐；同时设计了兼容分页KV缓存和融合核管线的自定义INT2注意力内核，支持SGLang、vLLM等现代推理框架。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B/8B/32B和GLM-4.7（358B参数）上，OSCAR在INT2量化下精度接近BF16（差距仅1.42~3.78点），而简单旋转方法几乎崩溃；长上下文任务（RULER-NIAH 128K）同样鲁棒；系统方面，内存压缩约8倍，大batch吞吐提升最高7倍，单batch解码加速最高3倍。

Conclusion: OSCAR通过注意力感知的协方差结构化量化，在保持INT2极低比特的同时实现了与BF16相当的精度，且具备完整的系统可部署性，为长上下文LLM服务中的KV缓存压缩提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: INT2 KV-cache quantization is attractive for long-context LLM serving, but it remains difficult to make both accurate and deployable. Simple rotations such as Hadamard transforms reduce outliers, but still degrade at INT2 because they are not aligned with downstream attention. We propose OSCAR, an Ultra-low-bit KV Cache quantization method that estimates attention-aware covariance structures offline and uses them to derive fixed rotations and clipping thresholds for quantization. In this way, it aligns KV quantization with the covariance structures that attention actually consumes. More importantly, we not only provide theoretical justification but also develop a fully deployable OSCAR system with a custom INT2 attention kernel that remains compatible with paged KV-cache serving and fused kernel pipelines, enabling seamless integration into modern LLM serving frameworks such as SGLang and vLLM.
  We evaluate our methods on recent reasoning models with reasoning traces of up to 32k tokens across 5 tasks. On Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 and Qwen3-8B, OSCAR reduces the BF16 accuracy gap to 3.78 and 1.42 points, respectively, while naive rotation INT2 collapses to nearly zero. We further scale OSCAR to Qwen3-32B and GLM-4.7 (358B params), where it remains effectively on par with BF16. On long context - RULER-NIAH up to 128K, OSCAR remains robust on both Qwen3 models, while naive rotation INT2 collapses. System-wise, OSCAR reduces KV-cache memory by approximately 8x, improves throughput by up to 7x at large batch sizes under the same memory budget, and accelerates batch-size-1 decoding by up to 3x over BF16 due to reduced memory bandwidth overhead.

</details>


### [137] [Memisis: Orchestrating and Evaluating Synthetic Data for Tabular Health Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17758)
*Nitish Nagesh,Mahdi Bagheri,Arshia Harish Puthran,Pengbao Zhou,Muhjaazee Love,Aadi Sharma,Ian Harris,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memisis是一个利用现有合成数据工具、大语言模型和评估指标来编排和评估合成数据生成的工具，支持用户通过交互式智能体指定目标并控制训练参数，在精神分裂症数据集上验证了CTGAN、TVAE和GaussianCopula在公平性和效用方面的可比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在医疗领域广泛应用以解决隐私问题，但缺乏统一的工具来协调生成与评估流程，且现有方法难以兼顾隐私、效用和公平性三个维度。

Method: 提出Memisis工具，集成多种合成数据生成器（CTGAN、TVAE、GaussianCopula）、大语言模型驱动交互式智能体以及最新评估指标，构建从数据生成、验证到评估的统一工作流，用户可通过自然语言指定目标并控制训练规模、轮次和采样数量。

Result: 在包含种族和性别保护属性的精神分裂症开放数据集上，CTGAN、TVAE和GaussianCopula在公平性和效用指标上表现相当，验证了工作流的灵活性和可控性。

Conclusion: Memisis提供了一种灵活、可控的合成数据生成与评估统一解决方案，通过LLM驱动的交互式智能体降低用户调参门槛，未来可扩展至更多医疗场景。

Abstract: Synthetic data is widely used in healthcare to create datasets that are similar to original data but without the privacy concerns. Generating and evaluating synthetic data across privacy, utility and fairness is crucial for facilitating high quality data availability for downstream prediction tasks and clinical decision making. We present Memisis, a tool that orchestrates and evaluates synthetic data by leveraging existing synthetic data tools, the power of large language models and state-of-the-art evaluation metrics. Our tool creates a unified workflow for data generation, validation and evaluation. Users have control over the training size, training epochs and the number of synthetic rows to sample. Instead of knobs to tune synthetic data, the interactive agent allows users to specify their synthetic data generation goals and the tool will orchestrate the workflow by leveraging existing tools while performing the requisite evaluation. For the demo, we use an open source schizophrenia dataset with protected attributes related to race and gender, three different synthesizers and a local language model to orchestrate the workflow. We observe that CTGAN, TVAE and GaussianCopula have comparable performance across fairness and utility metrics. The workflow allows users flexibility and control over the data generation and evaluation process.

</details>


### [138] [AURORA: Contextual Orthogonalization for Geometric Representation Learning in Healthcare Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17765)
*Yuanyun Zhang,Shi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AURORA框架，通过将医疗表征分解为正交语义子空间并学习子空间内关系一致性目标，实现语义解耦和几何可解释的潜在表示，在多任务中优于现有基线并提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗基础模型通过大规模自监督学习获得强预测性能，但其潜在表示常将生理严重程度、干预强度、观察结构和机构工作流混杂在同一嵌入方向，导致语义不透明且在上下文偏移下不稳定。

Method: AURORA基于上下文潜在几何，将表示分解为正交语义子空间（对应不同上下文因素），并在每个子空间内学习关系一致性目标，实现语义解耦与几何可解释性。

Result: 在多种临床预测和检索任务中，AURORA一致优于重建、对比学习和自蒸馏基线，在上下文解耦、邻域纯度和机构分布偏移鲁棒性方面显著提升。

Conclusion: 潜在几何本身是医疗基础模型设计的重要维度，显式按上下文语义结构化表示空间为传统预测压缩目标提供了补充方向。

Abstract: Recent healthcare foundation models have achieved strong predictive performance through large scale self supervised learning, yet their latent representations frequently entangle physiologic severity, intervention intensity, observational structure, and institutional workflow into shared embedding directions. While effective for downstream prediction, such representations remain semantically opaque and unstable under contextual shift. We introduce AURORA, Adaptive Uncertainty aware Representations through Orthogonalized Relational Alignment, a new framework for healthcare representation learning based on contextual latent geometry. Rather than optimizing a single unified embedding manifold, AURORA decomposes representations into orthogonal semantic subspaces corresponding to distinct contextual factors and learns relational consistency objectives within each subspace. This induces latent spaces that are both semantically disentangled and geometrically interpretable. Across multiple clinical prediction and retrieval tasks, AURORA consistently outperforms reconstruction, contrastive, and self distillation baselines while substantially improving contextual disentanglement, neighborhood purity, and robustness under institutional distribution shift. Our results suggest that latent geometry itself constitutes an important axis of healthcare foundation model design and that explicitly structuring representation space according to contextual semantics provides a complementary direction beyond conventional predictive compression objectives.

</details>


### [139] [Learning Variable-Length Tokenization for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17779)
*Minhao Wang,Bowen Wu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现生成式推荐中存在“流行度-长度悖论”：热门物品用短ID即可，长尾物品需要长编码。为此提出VarLenRec框架，通过双曲残差量化（提升几何容量）和软长度控制器（可微分预测）实现可变长度编码，显著提升推荐精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐对所有物品使用固定长度的语义ID编码，忽略了不同物品（如热门vs长尾）所需的编码容量差异，导致次优性能。

Method: 提出VarLenRec框架，包含三个关键技术：1) 流行度加权信息预算分配（PIBA）从信息论角度推导最优ID长度与流行度的负幂次关系；2) 双曲残差量化（Hyperbolic Residual Quantization）利用庞加莱球的指数级体积增长自然分层编码容量；3) 软长度控制器（Soft Length Controller）通过连续层保留概率实现可微分长度预测。

Result: 在四个数据集上，VarLenRec相比现有最先进方法在推荐准确性和训练/推理效率上均有显著提升，验证了自适应编码容量的重要性。

Conclusion: 变长编码策略能更合理地分配信息预算，热门物品依赖协同信号用短编码，长尾物品借助细粒度内容特征用长编码，是生成式推荐中的重要设计方向。

Abstract: Generative recommendation reformulates recommendation as next-token prediction over discrete semantic identifiers (IDs). A fundamental yet unexplored design choice is that existing methods employ fixed-length tokenization for all items, implicitly assuming uniform encoding capacity regardless of item characteristics. Through systematic experiments across four datasets, we discover the Popularity-Length Paradox: popular items achieve optimal performance with short IDs, while tail items require substantially longer codes to capture discriminative semantics. This reveals a critical mismatch where popular items benefit from abundant collaborative signals and require minimal semantic detail, whereas tail items must rely on fine-grained content features due to sparse interaction data.
  To address this, we propose VarLenRec, a framework for learning variable-length tokenization. We develop Popularity-Weighted Information Budget Allocation (PIBA), an information-theoretic framework proving that optimal ID length should scale as a negative power of popularity. Directly implementing variable-length allocation faces two technical challenges: standard Euclidean residual quantization lacks geometric capacity to support diverse code lengths without distortion, and discrete length decisions are non-differentiable. We address these through Hyperbolic Residual Quantization, which leverages the exponential volume growth of the Poincaré ball to naturally stratify encoding capacity, and a Soft Length Controller, which enables differentiable length prediction via continuous layer retention probabilities regularized by PIBA-derived priors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VarLenRec achieves significant improvements over state-of-the-art methods in recommendation accuracy and training/inference efficiency, revealing the importance of adaptive encoding capacity in generative recommendation.

</details>


### [140] [Revisiting the Adam-SGD Gap in LLM Pre-Training: The Role of Large Effective Learning Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17787)
*Athanasios Glentis,Dawei Li,Chung-Yiu Yau,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现SGD在LLM预训练中表现远逊于Adam的主要原因是SGD无法维持与Adam相当的大学习率。通过分析训练动态，作者发现预训练中梯度范数小、权重-梯度比大，且输出层梯度在不同词类间分布不均并频繁出现梯度尖峰，这些都限制了SGD的学习率。采用简单的裁剪机制稳定大学习率下的SGD后，1B参数LLaMA模型上SGD与Adam的验证损失差距从50%以上缩小至约3.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解SGD在LLM预训练中表现显著差于Adam等自适应优化器的根本原因，弥合两者之间的性能差距。

Method: 通过实证和理论分析LLM预训练动态，识别出梯度范数小、权重-梯度比大、输出层梯度分布不均及梯度尖峰等现象限制了SGD的学习率，并提出简单的梯度裁剪机制来稳定SGD在较大学习率下的训练。

Result: 在1B参数LLaMA模型、1M token批次大小的预训练实验中，大学习率SGD与Adam的验证损失差距从超过50%缩小到仅约3.5%。

Conclusion: SGD在LLM预训练中的劣势主要源于其无法使用像Adam那样大的有效学习率，通过适当的裁剪机制可以大幅缩小两者差距，表明SGD的潜力被低估了。

Abstract: It is widely believed that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) performs significantly worse than adaptive optimizers such as Adam in pre-training Large Language Models (LLMs). Yet the underlying reason for this gap remains unclear. In this work, we attribute a large part of the discrepancy to SGD's inability to sustain learning rates comparable to Adam's much larger effective learning rates. Through empirical and theoretical analysis of LLM pre-training dynamics, we identify that training is characterized by small gradient norms and large weight-to-gradient ratios, an effect that becomes more pronounced with larger batch sizes typical in pre-training, necessitating such large effective learning rates. However, we find that output-layer gradient magnitudes become highly uneven across token classes, and that large gradient spikes frequently occur during training. Together, these effects severely restrict the admissible learning rate of SGD. Guided by this understanding, we show that simple clipping mechanisms that stabilize SGD at large learning rates enable it to recover most of Adam's performance. In our large-scale experiments, the validation loss gap between large-learning-rate SGD and Adam shrinks from more than 50% to only about 3.5% when pre-training a 1B-parameter LLaMA model with a 1M-token batch size.

</details>


### [141] [HydroAgent: Closing the Gap Between Frontier LLMs and Human Experts in Hydrologic Model Calibration via Simulator-Grounded RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17792)
*Zhi Li,Songkun Yan,Jie Cao,Mofan Zhang,Anjiang Wei,Jinwoong Yoo,Yang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了九种前沿大语言模型在分布式水文模型校核中的表现，发现最优模型NSE仅达0.65-0.75，无法超越人类专家。作者指出瓶颈在于领域接地而非参数规模，并提出HYDROAGENT——基于Qwen3-4B微调、结合监督微调与群体相对策略优化（以NSE为可验证奖励）的领域专用智能体，主张基于模拟器反馈的强化学习是地球系统科学更高效、物理保真的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式水文模型校核是水资源管理的关键瓶颈，当前依赖于人类专家逐流域进行高维参数调整，工作流不可迁移。作者试图探究前沿LLM智能体能否替代人类水文建模者，若不能则明确差距所在及弥合路径。

Method: 基准测试9种前沿LLM智能体（Claude、GPT、Gemini系列）在CREST分布式水文模型上的校核性能；提出HYDROAGENT框架，对Qwen3-4B进行监督微调（2576条专家校核轨迹）并结合群体相对策略优化，以NSE为可验证奖励函数，通过在线CREST模拟进行强化学习。

Result: 最佳20轮NSE在4个流域上介于-0.16至0.75，最强模型集中在0.65-0.75区间，仅Opus-4.7在一个流域达到人类专家水平。HYDROAGENT作为小参数领域微调策略，结合模拟器反馈的RL展现出更优的物理保真度和计算效率。

Conclusion: LLM与人类专家在水文校核上的差距本质是领域接地问题而非模型规模问题；对于地球系统科学，小参数域策略结合模拟器内环强化学习比扩展通用前沿模型更高效；地球数据的多模态丰富性（遥感、原位时间序列、预报员叙事）使领域智能体成为AI在物理科学中的杠杆方向。

Abstract: Calibrating distributed hydrologic models is a critical bottleneck across operational water resources management - streamflow prediction, reservoir operation, drought monitoring, infrastructure design, and flood forecasting all depend on it. Each basin demands an expert to translate hydrograph signatures into adjustments of a high-dimensional parameter vector, and the resulting workflow does not transfer between watersheds. We ask: can frontier large language model (LLM) agents replace the human hydrologic modeler, and if not, what would it take? We benchmark nine frontier LLM agents - Claude Opus 4.6/4.7, Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5/5.4/5.4-pro, and Gemini 2.5-pro/3.1-pro/3-flash - on the operational CREST distributed hydrologic model used by the U.S. National Weather Service for flash-flood forecasting. Best-of-twenty-rounds Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency (NSE) across four held-out gauges spanning 329-40,792 km2 ranges from -0.16 (GPT-5.4) to 0.75 (Sonnet 4.6); the ceiling reproduces across all three vendors and capability tiers, with the strongest models concentrating in the 0.65-0.75 band, and no model reaches the human-expert reference except Opus-4.7 on one gauge. We argue this gap is not a parameter-count problem but a domain-grounding problem. We then propose HYDROAGENT, fine-tuning open-weight Qwen3-4B with supervised fine-tuning on 2,576 expert calibration trajectories and Group-Relative Policy Optimization using NSE as a verifiable reward from online CREST simulations - reinforcement learning with simulation feedback (RLSF). For Earth system science, a small domain-tuned policy with simulator-in-the-loop RL is a more compute-efficient and physically faithful path than scaling generic frontier models, and the multi-modal richness of Earth data - remote sensing, in-situ time series, and forecaster narrative - makes domain agents a leveraged direction for AI in physical science.

</details>


### [142] [When Accuracy Is Not Enough: Uncertainty Collapse between Noisy Label Learning and Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17795)
*Ningkang Peng,Jingyang Mao,Runhan Zhou,Peirong Ma,Yanhui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACC-OOD基准，发现噪声标签学习中的“不确定性坍缩”现象：高闭集准确率并不保证OOD可靠性，因为低置信度的错误分类ID样本与OOD输入在特征空间重叠。提出虚拟边界正则化（VMR）作为轻量修复手段，部分缓解了坍缩导致的远OOD失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的噪声标签学习（LNL）方法主要用闭集分类准确率评估，但实际部署中模型需要拒绝分布外（OOD）输入，而闭集准确率高不代表OOD可靠性好，两者之间存在未被关注的鸿沟。

Method: 构建与学习器无关的ACC-OOD基准，冻结LNL检查点，用标准化的近/远OOD路由和后验得分进行评估。提出虚拟边界正则化（VMR），在可信ID批次上合成边界虚拟异常点并扩大能量间隔。

Result: 高准确率的LNL方法在标准OOD得分下会失去ID错误样本与OOD样本之间的可分离性；VMR能部分减少坍缩导致的远OOD失败，且不降低闭集准确率。

Conclusion: LNL评估应同时报告闭集泛化、开放世界可靠性和结构重叠诊断指标。

Abstract: Learning with noisy labels (LNL) is typically benchmarked by closed-set classification accuracy, yet deployment often requires classifiers to reject out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs. We present a learner-agnostic ACC-OOD benchmark that freezes LNL checkpoints and evaluates them with standardized near-/far-OOD routing and post-hoc scores across synthetic and real label noise. The benchmark reveals a recurring failure mode: high closed-set accuracy does not ensure OOD reliability, because low-confidence, misclassified in-distribution samples can overlap the score and feature regions occupied by OOD inputs under noisy training. We term this pathology uncertainty collapse. This structural overlap can make high-accuracy LNL methods lose separability at the ID-error/OOD interface under standard OOD scores. As an intervention, we study Virtual Margin Regularization (VMR), a lightweight repair probe demonstrated mainly with PSSCL that synthesizes boundary virtual outliers on trusted ID batches and widens the energy margin. VMR partially reduces the collapse-induced far-OOD failure without replacing the host objective or sacrificing closed-set accuracy in the tested settings. These results support LNL benchmarks that co-report closed-set generalization, open-world reliability, and structural overlap diagnostics.

</details>


### [143] [GenTS: A Comprehensive Benchmark Library for Generative Time Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17804)
*Chenxi Wang,Xiaorong Wang,Peiyang Li,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenTS是一个针对生成式时间序列模型的综合性基准测试库，提供统一的数据预处理、多样化模型和全景评估指标，支持灵活定制，并基于多种任务进行了基准实验，为模型选择和研究方向提供建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列库主要面向判别式模型设计，对生成式模型（如对抗训练、扩散过程）的覆盖有限，其基于特定任务（如优化均方误差进行预测）的标准化工作流与生成式模型学习数据分布而非直接映射的范式存在根本性不兼容。

Method: 提出GenTS基准库，包含统一数据预处理流水线、多种生成式模型集合、全景评估指标，采用模块化设计支持自定义扩展，并在合成、预测、插补等多种任务上进行基准实验。

Result: 基于GenTS在多项时间序列任务上进行了全面的基准测试实验，为模型选择提供了建议，并指出了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: GenTS填补了生成式时间序列模型系统评估工具的空白，其模块化设计和开放源码有助于推动该领域的研究发展。

Abstract: Generative models have demonstrated remarkable potential in time series analysis tasks, like synthesis, forecasting, imputation, etc. However, offering limited coverage for generative models, existing time series libraries are mainly engineered for discriminative models, with standardized workflows for specific tasks, such as optimizing Mean Squared Errors for time series forecasting. This rigid structure is fundamentally incompatible with the distinct and often complex paradigms of generative models (e.g., adversarial training, diffusion processes), which learn the underlying data distribution rather than a direct input-output mapping. To this end, we proposed GenTS, a comprehensive and extensible benchmark library designed for systematic assessment on generative time series models. GenTS features a unified data preprocessing pipeline, a collection of versatile models, and panoramic evaluation metrics. Its modular design also enables the researchers to flexibly customize beyond our built-in datasets and models. Based on GenTS, we conducted benchmarking experiments under diverse tasks, accordingly offering suggestions for model selection and identifying potential directions for future research. Our codes are open-source at https://github.com/WillWang1113/GenTS. The official tutorials and document are available at https://willwang1113.github.io/GenTS/.

</details>


### [144] [A Unified Framework for Data-Free One-Step Sampling via Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17808)
*Chenguang Wang,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Wasserstein梯度流的统一理论框架，用于从非归一化目标分布进行无数据一步采样。研究表明，标准f-散度目标诱导的速度场具有通用形式 V(x)=w(r(x))β(x)，其中β(x)为共享项，w由散度选择唯一决定。进一步将框架扩展到Log-Variance散度，并给出了基于KDE和归一化流的实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无数据一步采样方法缺乏统一的理论框架来理解不同散度目标函数之间的内在联系和差异，特别是它们在质量传输过程中的修复行为如何随散度选择而变化。

Method: 1) 推导了f-散度目标下速度场的通用分解形式V(x)=w(r(x))β(x)；2) 提出了单步区域响应理论和压缩-弹性恒等式；3) 将框架扩展到Log-Variance散度并分析了参考分布的影响；4) 提出了KDE-based实现和归一化流两种实现路径。

Result: 理论分析表明标准f-散度漂移项共享相同的渐近目标分布p，差异主要在于它们如何重新分配欠覆盖区域的瞬态修复力度。在多模态高斯混合基准上的实验与理论预测一致，展示了有效的单步采样能力。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了多种散度目标函数下的无数据一步采样方法，揭示了散度选择与质量传输几何之间的本质联系，为实际应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We develop a unified theoretical framework for data-free one-step sampling from unnormalized target distributions based on Wasserstein gradient flows. For a broad class of standard f-divergence objectives, we show that the induced velocity field admits the universal form $\mathbf{V}(x)=w(r(x))\,β(x)$, where $β(x)=\nabla \log (p(x)/q(x))$ is shared across objectives and $w$ is determined solely by the choice of divergence. This decomposition shows that standard f-divergence drifts share the same asymptotic target distribution $p$ and differ primarily in how they redistribute transient repair effort across under-covered regions. To formalize this distinction, we derive a one-step regional-response theory for a soft under-coverage functional and obtain a compression--elasticity identity that links divergence choice to the geometry of mass transport into under-covered regions. We further extend the framework beyond the f-divergence family to the Log-Variance (LV) divergence, analyze how the reference distribution alters the resulting drift structure, and motivate a practical LV-inspired surrogate for data-free training. Based on this theory, we instantiate the framework with a KDE-based implementation and describe a complementary normalizing-flow route, enabling one-step inference after training. Experiments on multimodal Gaussian-mixture benchmarks are consistent with the theoretical predictions and demonstrate effective one-step sampling on these targets.

</details>


### [145] [One Model, Two Roles: Emergent Specialization in a Shared Recurrent Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17811)
*Jucheng Shen,Barbara Su,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究共享权重的循环Transformer（AIR架构）能否在没有显式分区的情况下发展出不同的内部角色。实验表明，在两种状态（L和H）的循环更新中，H状态表现为完全承诺的提案状态，L状态保留局部不确定性。这种分工依赖于输入注入不对称性或层级标记，且L更新比H更新更局部化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索共享权重的循环Transformer是否能够自发地在内部形成不同的功能角色分工，而不需要将模型显式划分为独立的模块。

Method: 提出非对称输入循环（AIR）架构，同一Transformer模型在两种更新规则（L和H）中复用，唯一的区别是编码输入仅在L更新时注入。在Sudoku-Extreme和Maze两个任务上，通过解码轨迹、冻结实验、消融研究和注意力分析等方法进行研究。

Result: 1) 解码轨迹显示zH表现为完全承诺的提案状态，zL保留局部不确定性；2) 冻结实验揭示了状态间动态依赖关系；3) 消融实验表明需要状态身份区分信号（输入不对称或层级标记）才能诱导专业化；4) 注意力分析显示L更新比H更新更具局部性。

Conclusion: 在双状态循环设置中，清晰的状态身份信号能够诱导共享参数循环Transformer内部形成稳定且相关的功能角色分工。

Abstract: Can a shared-weight recurrent Transformer develop distinct internal roles without being partitioned into separate modules? We study this in Asymmetric Input Recurrence (AIR), a minimal two-state reasoning architecture in which the same Transformer model is reused for both updates (per literature, L and H) and the only built-in difference in the update rule is that the encoded input is injected during L-updates but not H-updates. Across Sudoku-Extreme and Maze, decoded rollouts reveal a consistent split: $\zH$ behaves like a fully committed proposal state, whereas $\zL$ retains local uncertainty and shifting intermediate structure. Freeze experiments show that this split is, in practice, related to the model's state dynamics: in Sudoku, freezing $\zH$ reduces $\zL$'s content changes whereas freezing $\zL$ increases $\zH$'s, while in Maze, freezing either state increases content changes in the other state. Ablations show that to induce specialization, the shared model needs to be able to tell the two update types apart, either from input injection asymmetry or from a separate level token. Mechanistically, attention analysis shows that L-updates are consistently more local than H-updates in both Sudoku and Maze. Together, these results show that, in a two-state recurrent setting, a clear state-identity signal can induce stable, related functional roles inside a shared-parameter recurrent Transformer. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/juchengshen/air}{\textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/juchengshen/air}}.

</details>


### [146] [Content-Style Identification via Differential Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17827)
*Subash Timilsina,Hoang-Son Nguyen,Sagar Shrestha,Xiao Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出内容-风格微分独立性（CSDI）条件，通过要求内容与风格在数据流形上诱导正交方向来实现可识别性，即使两者存在依赖且雅可比矩阵稠密时仍有效，并设计了基于数值雅可比近似的随机正则化器以支持高维生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要内容与风格之间的（块状）统计独立性或对非线性混合函数的稀疏雅可比假设才能实现可识别性，这些条件在实践中具有局限性。

Method: 引入内容-风格微分独立性（CSDI）条件，要求内容与风格的无穷小变化在数据流形上诱导正交方向；通过雅可比子空间的块状正交约束实现该条件；设计基于数值雅可比近似的随机正则化器以支持高维生成模型的可扩展训练。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验验证了可识别性分析，并在反事实生成和域迁移任务中展示了实际效益。

Conclusion: CSDI提供了一种更宽松的结构性条件，即使在内容与风格依赖且雅可比稠密的情况下也能实现可识别性，并能在高分辨率图像生成等场景中有效应用。

Abstract: Generative analysis often models multi-domain observations as nonlinear mixtures of domain-invariant content variables and domain-specific style variables. Identifying both factors from unpaired domains enables tasks such as domain transfer and counterfactual data generation. Prior work establishes identifiability under (block-wise) statistical independence between content and style, or via sparse Jacobian assumptions on the nonlinear mixing function, but such conditions can be restrictive in practice. In this work, we introduce content-style differential independence (CSDI), an alternative structural condition requiring that infinitesimal variations in content and style induce orthogonal directions on the data manifold, thereby enabling identifiability even when content and style are dependent and the Jacobian is dense. We operationalize this condition through a blockwise orthogonality constraint on the Jacobian subspaces associated with content and style. To support high-dimensional generative models, we design a stochastic regularizer based on numerical Jacobian approximation, enabling scalable training in settings such as high-resolution image generation. Experiments across multiple datasets corroborate the identifiability analysis and demonstrate practical benefits on counterfactual generation and domain translation.

</details>


### [147] [Agentic Cost-Aware Query Planning with Knowledge Distillation for Big Data Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17831)
*Mahdi Naser-Moghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种智能体查询规划系统，融合规则教师规划器、UCB1 bandit探索、成本感知预测和知识蒸馏，在资源受限环境中实现轻量级学生规划器，相比默认规划器延迟降低23%，约束满足率达94%，学生规划器推理速度快15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据分析中的查询优化在资源受限环境下计算开销大，传统优化器无法满足内存和延迟约束。

Method: 结合规则教师规划器（6种优化策略）、UCB1 bandit搜索、随机森林成本模型预测延迟，以及知识蒸馏将教师-bandit决策迁移至轻量级学生规划器（逻辑回归或梯度提升）。

Result: 在NYC Taxi和IMDB数据集上，相比默认规划器延迟降低23%，约束满足率94%；学生规划器复现最优计划准确率89%，推理速度快15倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的智能体查询规划系统能有效在资源受限环境下实现高效查询优化，单文件实现具有良好的可复现性。

Abstract: Query optimization in big data analytics remains computationally expensive, particularly for resource-constrained environments where traditional optimizers fail to satisfy memory and latency constraints. We present an agentic query planning system that combines a rule-based teacher planner, UCB1 bandit exploration, cost-aware prediction, and knowledge distillation to a lightweight student planner. Our teacher planner generates SQL plans using six key optimization strategies, while UCB1 bandit search efficiently explores the plan space under explicit resource constraints. A Random Forest cost model predicts query latency from plan features, enabling cost-aware decisions. A distilled student planner (Logistic Regression or Gradient Boosting) learns to mimic teacher-bandit decisions for fast inference. Evaluation on NYC Taxi and IMDB datasets demonstrates 23% latency reduction compared to default planners while maintaining 94% constraint satisfaction. The student planner achieves 89% accuracy in replicating optimal plans with 15x faster inference time. Our single-file implementation enables reproducible big-data analytics on resource-limited machines and is publicly available at https://github.com/mahdinaser/agentic-kd-planner.

</details>


### [148] [SNLP: Layer-Parallel Inference via Structured Newton Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17842)
*Ligong Han,Kai Xu,Hao Wang,Akash Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化牛顿层并行（SNLP）框架，通过将Transformer层间依赖视为非线性残差方程，使用架构诱导的廉价牛顿更新（残差Transformer中的IDN和mHC架构中的HCN）实现层并行训练与推理，结合SNLP正则化，在0.5B nanochat模型上实现2.3倍加速并提升困惑度6.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型逐层执行Transformer层造成延迟瓶颈，传统张量或流水线并行无法消除这一依赖。本文研究能否通过将层间隐藏状态轨迹视为非线性残差方程的解，并用并行牛顿式更新来放宽层间依赖。

Method: 提出SNLP框架：1) 用架构诱导的廉价 surrogate 动力学替代精确的层雅可比矩阵（残差Transformer中为Identity Newton即前缀和更新，mHC架构中为HC Newton利用残差混合矩阵）；2) 引入SNLP感知正则化，训练模型使少量结构化牛顿迭代能准确逼近顺序前向传播。

Result: 在nanochat规模Transformer上：SNLP正则化将基线困惑度降低4.7%-23.4%；在0.5B Nanochat模型上推理时结合层融合和分块分解实现2.3倍加速，同时困惑度提升6.1%。

Conclusion: 层并行推理不仅是顺序执行的数值近似，还可作为有益的求解器诱导推理偏置。局限性：现成预训练模型适应性较差，精确收敛会恢复顺序计算而非提供单调推理时间缩放。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models execute Transformer layers sequentially, creating a latency bottleneck that is not removed by conventional tensor or pipeline parallelism. We study whether this layerwise dependency can be relaxed by treating the hidden-state trace across layers as the solution of a nonlinear residual equation and solving it with parallel Newton-style updates. While this view is principled, exact Newton corrections require expensive Jacobian-vector products and naive fixed-point iterations are unstable on trained Transformers. We introduce Structured Newton Layer Parallelism (SNLP), a training and inference framework that replaces exact layer Jacobians with cheap architecture-induced surrogate dynamics. In residual Transformers, this yields Identity Newton (IDN), where the correction reduces to a prefix-sum-like update; in mHC-style architectures, HC Newton (HCN) uses the model's residual mixing matrix. We further introduce SNLP-aware regularization, which trains models to make one or a few structured Newton iterations accurately approximate the sequential forward. Experiments on nanochat-scale Transformers show that SNLP regularization improves layer-parallel compatibility and can also improve standard sequential perplexity, reducing baseline PPL by 4.7%-23.4%. At inference time, SNLP combined with layer fusion and chunkwise decomposition achieves practical wall-clock speedups: on a 0.5B Nanochat model, it reaches 2.3x speedup while still improving PPL by 6.1%. These results suggest that layer-parallel inference is not merely a numerical approximation to sequential execution, but can act as a useful solver-induced inference bias. We also characterize limitations: off-the-shelf pretrained models are less amenable to this procedure, and exact convergence recovers the sequential computation rather than providing monotonic inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [149] [$\boldsymbol{f}$-OPD: Stabilizing Long-Horizon On-Policy Distillation with Freshness-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17862)
*Xianwei Chen,Shimin Zhang,Jibin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出f-OPD框架，通过新鲜度评分自适应调节异步蒸馏中过期样本的影响，在保持异步执行吞吐优势的同时，实现了与同步优化相当的策略性能，首次建立了策略蒸馏中性能-效率权衡的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型的在线策略蒸馏面临异步执行与在线策略目标之间的根本矛盾：异步执行对系统效率至关重要，但会导致目标偏差。现有方法缺乏对这种偏差的理论分析和系统性的缓解方案。

Method: 首先将异步策略蒸馏的目标偏差理论分解为rollout漂移（学生rollout的过时性）和监督漂移（教师上下文的过时性）。引入样本级新鲜度评分量化缓冲样本相对于在线策略目标的可靠性，并基于此提出f-OPD框架，自适应调节过期样本的影响并约束异步训练下的策略累积漂移。

Result: 在推理、工具使用和编程智能体等交互长度递增的任务上，f-OPD实现了与同步优化相当的任务性能，同时大幅保留了异步执行的吞吐优势。

Conclusion: 首次建立了在线策略蒸馏中性能-效率权衡的可行方案，为长交互智能体的后训练规模化铺平了道路。

Abstract: Scaling on-policy distillation (OPD) for large language models (LLMs) confronts a fundamental tension: asynchronous execution is necessary for system efficiency, but structurally deviates from the ideal on-policy objective. To address this challenge, we theoretically decompose the objective discrepancy into rollout drift and supervision drift, capturing staleness in student rollout and teacher context, respectively. Building on this, we introduce a sample-level freshness score that quantifies the reliability of a buffered sample with respect to the on-policy objective. Guided by this signal, we further propose f-OPD, a novel framework that adaptively regulates stale-sample influence and constrains policy drift accumulated under asynchronous training. Across reasoning, tool-use, and coding-agent tasks of increasing interaction horizon, f-OPD consistently achieves task performance comparable to synchronous optimization while largely retaining the throughput advantages of asynchronous execution. Our results establish the first recipe for achieving a performance-efficiency trade-off in OPD, paving the way for long-horizon agentic post-training at scale.

</details>


### [150] [DAD4TS: Data-Augmentation-Oriented Diffusion Model for Time-Series Forecasting with Small-Scale Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17866)
*Masahiro Suzuki,Bohui Xia,Hiroto Yamamoto,Masanori Miyahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAD4TS，一种基于扩散模型和强化学习的数据增强方法，用于小样本时间序列预测。通过几何空间投影训练扩散模型，结合强化学习控制生成器提升预测精度，在多个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中小样本数据是关键问题，现有数据增强方法在生成有意义数据方面存在局限性。

Method: 提出DAD4TS框架：1）数据生成器与时间序列模型联合训练；2）强化学习模型控制生成器以生成提升预测精度的样本；3）利用数学方法将时间序列投影到几何空间训练扩散模型，替代传统VAE方法以支持小样本数据。

Result: 在6个真实数据集、8个时间序列模型上，与7种对比方法进行定性和定量实验，在5个数据集上验证了DAD4TS的有效性。

Conclusion: DAD4TS能有效解决小样本时间序列预测中的数据增强问题，扩散模型结合强化学习的方案优于传统方法。

Abstract: Small-scale data is a critical problem in time-series forecasting tasks. Data augmentation is an effective strategy for this task, but it has a limitation in generating meaningful data. To address this limitation, we propose DAD4TS, a diffusion-model-based data augmentation method with reinforcement learning, designed for time-series forecasting with small-scale data. In DAD4TS, a data generator is simultaneously trained with a time-series model and controlled by a reinforcement learning model to efficiently generate samples that improve the forecast accuracy of the time-series model. To support small-scale data, we use mathematical methods instead of conventional VAE methods to train the diffusion model by projecting the time-series data into the geometric space. We validated the effectiveness of DAD4TS with seven comparative methods through qualitative and quantitative experiments on six real-world datasets and eight time-series models. As a result, DAD4TS was validated on five datasets.

</details>


### [151] [HINT-SD: Targeted Hindsight Self-Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17873)
*Woongyeng Yeo,Yumin Choi,Taekyung Ki,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HINT-SD框架，通过全轨迹 hindsight 选择失败相关动作，仅在目标动作片段上应用反馈条件蒸馏，在BFCL v3和AppWorld上相比密集逐轮反馈基线提升最高18.80%，同时训练速度提升2.26倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体强化学习中，稀疏结果奖励无法揭示中间动作的正确性，现有方法在每个回合生成反馈效率低下，且固定或错位的回合反馈无法监督导致失败的关键动作。

Method: HINT-SD：利用完整轨迹的后见之明（hindsight）筛选与失败相关的动作片段，仅在这些目标片段上应用反馈条件蒸馏，而非对所有回合进行密集反馈。

Result: 在BFCL v3和AppWorld上，相比密集逐轮反馈基线，性能提升最高18.80%，每个训练步骤时间降低2.26倍。

Conclusion: 选择蒸馏位置是长程智能体训练中实现有效性和效率双提升的关键因素。

Abstract: Training long-horizon LLM agents with reinforcement learning is challenging because sparse outcome rewards reveal whether a task succeeds, but not which intermediate actions caused the outcome or how they should be corrected. Recent methods alleviate this issue by generating rewards or textual hints from turn-level action-output signals, or by using feedback-conditioned self-distillation. However, generating feedback at every turn is inefficient when many intermediate turns are already successful or neutral, and applying feedback at a fixed or misaligned turn often fails to supervise the actions that contributed to the failure. To bridge this gap, we propose HINT-SD, a targeted self-distillation framework that uses full-trajectory hindsight to select failure-relevant actions and applies feedback-conditioned distillation only on targeted action spans. Experiments on BFCL v3 and AppWorld show that our method improves over the dense per-turn feedback baseline by up to 18.80 percent while achieving 2.26$\times$ lower time per training step, suggesting that selecting where to distill is a key factor for both effective and efficient long-horizon agent training.

</details>


### [152] [CoX-MoE: Coalesced Expert Execution for High-Throughput MoE Inference with AMX-Enabled CPU-GPU Co-Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17889)
*Mu-Young Son,Yi Chen,Seungjae Yoo,Soongyu Choi Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoX-MoE，一种利用AMX指令集的CPU-GPU协同系统，通过合并专家执行和策略性工作负载编排，显著提升MoE推理吞吐量，相比FlexGen和MoE-Lightning分别实现最高7.1倍和2.4倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽通过稀疏专家激活提升计算效率，但推理时面临严重的GPU显存压力（大量参数和中间数据）。现有方法（专家卸载+微批处理、CPU卸载）存在操作强度降低导致内存受限、PCIe传输慢、注意力计算受限等问题，无法有效利用系统资源，严重制约端到端吞吐量。

Method: CoX-MoE提出两种核心机制：(i) 合并感知编排策略——采用普通批处理而非微批处理进行专家计算，并选择性卸载注意力计算，联合优化资源分配；(ii) 静态专家感知分层方案——预先将频繁激活的专家分配给GPU，减少PCIe传输开销并平衡CPU与GPU的工作负载。系统利用AMX指令集加速CPU端计算。

Result: 相比FlexGen吞吐量提升最高7.1倍，相比MoE-Lightning吞吐量提升最高2.4倍。

Conclusion: CoX-MoE通过CPU-GPU协同和智能工作负载编排，有效解决了MoE推理中的显存压力和系统利用率低的问题，显著提升了端到端吞吐量。

Abstract: The Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture improves computational efficiency via sparse expert activation, but throughput-oriented inference faces substantial GPU memory pressure due to a significant parameter size and intermediate data. Prior works attempt to mitigate this using expert offloading with micro-batching or by offloading computation to the CPU. However, the fragmented workload resulting from micro-batching degrades operational intensity, causing expert execution to become memory-bound. Meanwhile, CPU offloading is constrained by slow PCIe transfers and its limited applicability to attention computation in the decode stage. Consequently, these inefficiencies prevent effective system utilization, severely restricting the end-to-end throughput of MoE inference.
  To address these challenges, this paper proposes CoX-MoE, an Advanced Matrix Extensions (AMX)-enabled CPU-GPU collaborative system that comprehensively optimizes MoE inference by combining coalesced expert execution with strategic workload orchestration for higher throughput.
