CoX-MoE introduces (i) a coalescing-aware orchestration policy to jointly optimize resource allocation by adopting ordinary batch, instead of micro-batch, for expert computation and selective attention offloading, and (ii) a static expert-aware stratification scheme that pre-assigns frequently activated experts to the GPU, mitigating PCIe transfer overhead and balancing workload for the CPU and GPU during inference. Compared to state-of-the-art frameworks, CoX-MoE delivers significant gains, achieving up to 7.1x and 2.4x higher throughput than FlexGen and MoE-Lightning, respectively.

</details>


### [153] [Lightweight Gaussian Process Inference in C++ on Metal and CUDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17898)
*Yu-Hsueh Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LightGP 是一个无外部依赖的 C++17 高斯过程回归库，提供 Python 绑定，支持 CPU（Apple Accelerate/OpenBLAS）以及 GPU（Apple Metal/NVIDIA CUDA）加速，在精确和稀疏 GP 推理上相比 GPyTorch 实现 1.5–8.7× 的加速，可处理 N=100 到 N=500,000 的问题，并通过 pip install lightgp 安装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Python GP 库（如 GPyTorch、GPflow）基于深度学习框架构建，存在调度开销大、依赖沉重的问题，需要开发轻量、高效、无外部依赖的 GP 推理库。

Method: 使用 C++17 开发无依赖的静态库，提供四种推理路径：精确 Cholesky、无矩阵共轭梯度、稀疏变分自由能、以及基于 FFT 的结构化核插值（SKI）。支持 Apple Metal 和 NVIDIA CUDA 后端，以及 CPU 端通过 Apple Accelerate 和 OpenBLAS 优化。

Result: 在 Apple M4 上，CPU 端精确 GP 比 GPyTorch 快 2.6–8.7×，稀疏 GP 快约 1.5×；在 NVIDIA RTX 3060 上，CUDA 端精确 GP 直到 N=2,048 快 2.3–6.7×；Metal 上融合矩阵-free 核-向量积在 N=20,000 时加速 32× 且内存 O(N)；FFT 加速的 SKI matvec 在 N=200,000 时亚毫秒完成。

Conclusion: LightGP 通过零外部依赖的纯 C++ 实现和多种后端加速，显著提升了 GP 推理性能，且安装简便（pip install lightgp），为大规模 GP 应用提供了高效轻量的替代方案。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) inference in Python is dominated by libraries such as GPyTorch and GPflow, which are built on deep-learning frameworks and inherit their dispatch overhead and dependency footprint. We present LightGP, a dependency-free C++17 library for GP regression with Python bindings, supporting Apple Metal and NVIDIA CUDA backends alongside tuned CPU paths via Apple Accelerate and OpenBLAS. LightGP provides four inference paths -- exact Cholesky, matrix-free conjugate gradients, sparse variational free energy, and structured kernel interpolation with FFT -- covering problems from $N{=}100$ to $N{=}500{,}000$. On an Apple M4, LightGP CPU is 2.6--8.7$\times$ faster than GPyTorch CPU for exact GP and ${\sim}1.5\times$ faster for sparse GP at every scale tested. On an NVIDIA RTX~3060, LightGP CUDA is 2.3--6.7$\times$ faster than GPyTorch CUDA for exact GP up to $N{=}2{,}048$, with GPyTorch closing the gap at $N{=}4{,}096$. A fused matrix-free kernel-vector product on Metal achieves 32$\times$ over the explicit path at $N{=}20{,}000$ with $O(N)$ memory, and an FFT-accelerated SKI matvec via Accelerate vDSP runs in sub-millisecond time at $N{=}200{,}000$. LightGP compiles as a single static library with zero external dependencies and is installable via \texttt{pip install lightgp

</details>


### [154] [DCFold: Efficient Protein Structure Generation with Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17899)
*Zhe Zhang,Yuanning Feng,Yuxuan Song,Keyue Qiu,Hao Zhou,Wei-Ying Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DCFold是一种单步生成模型，通过双一致性训练框架和时序测地线匹配调度器，在保持AlphaFold3级精度的同时实现15倍推理加速，适用于结构预测和结合剂设计任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlphaFold3虽在蛋白质结构预测中达到全原子精度，但其迭代设计导致推理时间过长，限制了在虚拟筛选和蛋白质设计等下游场景中的实际部署。

Method: 提出DCFold单步生成模型，采用双一致性训练框架并引入新颖的时序测地线匹配（TGM）调度器。

Result: 在保持AlphaFold3级预测精度的前提下，实现了15倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: DCFold在结构预测和结合剂设计基准上均验证了有效性，为蛋白质生成与设计任务提供了高效的基础模型方案。

Abstract: AlphaFold3 introduces a diffusion-based architecture that elevates protein structure prediction to all-atom resolution with improved accuracy. This state-of-the-art performance has established AlphaFold3 as a foundation model for diverse generation and design tasks. However, its iterative design substantially increases inference time, limiting practical deployment in downstream settings such as virtual screening and protein design. We propose DCFold, a single-step generative model that attains AlphaFold3-level accuracy. Our Dual Consistency training framework, which incorporates a novel Temporal Geodesic Matching (TGM) scheduler, enables DCFold to achieve a 15x acceleration in inference while maintaining predictive fidelity. We validate its effectiveness across both structure prediction and binder design benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [Domain Transfer Becomes Identifiable via a Single Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17918)
*Sagar Shrestha,Subash Timilsina,Hoang-Son Nguyen,Xiao Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在雅可比矩阵支持模式的稀疏性条件下，通过分布匹配和单个配对锚样本即可实现域传递的唯一可识别性，并提出了基于随机掩蔽有限差分的雅可比稀疏正则化方法以实现高效的高维学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域传递（DT）存在根本性的不适定性问题：推前映射通常不可识别，因为保测自同构（MPA）在保持边缘分布的同时会改变跨域对应关系，导致内容不对齐的翻译。现有方法需要多个对应的条件分布标签来消除MPA，但实际中这些监督信号往往不可得。

Method: 提出在雅可比矩阵支持模式的结构稀疏性条件下，仅需分布匹配和一个配对锚样本即可识别真实传递映射；并设计了基于随机掩蔽有限差分的雅可比稀疏正则化器，避免显式计算雅可比矩阵，实现高维场景下的可扩展学习。

Result: 在合成数据和真实域传递任务上的实验结果验证了所提理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结构稀疏性假设，大幅降低了域传递可识别性所需的监督信号量（从多个条件分布降为单个配对样本），为无监督/弱监督域对齐提供了理论保障和实用算法。

Abstract: Domain transfer (DT) maps source to target distributions and supports tasks such as unsupervised image-to-image translation, single-cell analysis, and cross-platform medical imaging. However, DT is fundamentally ill-posed: push-forward mappings are generally non-identifiable, as measure-preserving automorphisms (MPAs) preserve marginals while altering cross-domain correspondences, leading to content-misaligned translation. Recent work shows that MPAs can be eliminated by jointly transferring multiple corresponding source/target conditional distributions, but supervision signals labeling such conditionals are not always available in practice. We develop an alternative route to DT identifiability. Under a structural sparsity condition on the Jacobian support pattern, we show that distribution matching together with a single paired anchor sample suffices to identify the ground-truth transfer -- requiring substantially less supervision than prior approaches. To enable practical high-dimensional learning, we further propose an efficient Jacobian sparsity regularizer based on randomized masked finite differences, yielding a scalable surrogate without explicit Jacobian evaluation. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world DT tasks validate the theory.

</details>


### [156] [Training data attribution in diffusion models via mirrored unlearning and noise-consistent skew](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17938)
*Joan Serrà,Dipam Goswami,Fabio Morreale,Wei-Hsiang Liao,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MUCS方法，通过镜像反学习和噪声一致性偏斜实现扩散模型的训练数据归属，在三个数据集上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练数据归属方法缺乏可靠性和鲁棒性，无法在实际场景中应用，亟需更稳定的TDA方法。

Method: 采用有界镜像梯度上升微调第二个模型，并使用一致噪声样本测量该模型相对于原始模型的归一化偏斜，实现训练数据归属。

Result: 在三个不同数据集上系统性地大幅优于现有方法，并分析了核心设计选择对性能的影响。

Conclusion: MUCS方法概念简单且通用，对更一般的反学习设置和需要比较扩散损失的任务具有更广泛的意义。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) should enable generative model interpretability and foster a variety of related downstream tasks. Nonetheless, current TDA approaches lack reliability and robustness, preventing their adoption in real-world setups. In this paper, we take a decisive step towards more reliable and robust TDA for diffusion models. We propose to perform TDA with mirrored unlearning and noise-consistent skew (MUCS). The idea is to fine-tune a second model with bounded mirrored gradient ascent, and to measure the normalized skew of this model with respect to the original one using consistent noise samples. We show that, while being conceptually simple and generic, MUCS systematically outperforms existing methods on three different datasets by a large margin. We additionally study the effect that core design choices have on final performance, and analyze novel aspects regarding the overlap of influential instances across generated items and the potential of ensembling TDA approaches. We believe that our findings may have broader implications for more general unlearning setups, as well as for tasks requiring the comparison of diffusion losses.

</details>


### [157] [Enhancing the Code Reasoning Capabilities of LLMs via Consistency-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17958)
*Zhanyue Qin,Jia Feng,Yibo Lyu,Yun Peng,Dianbo Sui,Cuiyun Gao,Qing Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeThinker，一个基于一致性驱动的强化学习框架用于代码推理任务。该框架通过步骤推理感知模型训练、动态波束采样策略和一致性奖励机制三个核心组件，解决了现有方法中奖励稀疏和奖励破解的问题。在多个基准测试上取得了最优性能，并在下游任务中表现出显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的代码推理方法仅依赖最终输出或粗粒度信号设计奖励，忽视了任务中步骤推理过程的内在一致性，导致奖励稀疏或奖励破解问题，限制了强化学习能力的充分发挥。

Method: CodeThinker包含三个关键组件：(1) 步骤推理感知模型训练模块，利用一致性追踪范式作为模板合成能捕捉步骤推理过程的训练数据；(2) 动态波束采样策略，在固定采样预算下提高采样输出的质量；(3) 一致性奖励机制，有效缓解奖励破解问题。

Result: 在三个流行基准上，CodeThinker在多个LLM上实现了最先进性能。在Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct上比最强基线高出4.3%的准确率。在下游任务中，无需额外训练，在数学推理和涵盖17种编程语言的代码推理任务上分别获得了5.33和3.11个百分点的平均准确率提升。

Conclusion: CodeThinker通过一致性驱动的强化学习框架有效提升了代码推理性能，验证了步骤推理一致性在强化学习奖励设计中的重要性，并能泛化到多种下游任务中。

Abstract: Code reasoning refers to the task of predicting the output of a program given its source code and specific inputs. It can measure the reasoning capability of large language models (LLMs) and also benefit downstream tasks such as code generation and mathematical reasoning. Existing work has verified the effectiveness of reinforcement learning on the task. However, these methods design rewards solely based on final outputs or coarse-grained signals, and neglect the inherent consistency of the stepwise reasoning process in the task. Therefore, these methods often result in sparse reward or reward hacking, which limits the full play of enhanced learning capabilities. To alleviate these issues, we propose CodeThinker, a consistency-driven reinforcement learning framework for code reasoning. Specifically, CodeThinker has three key components: (1) a stepwise reasoning-aware model training module, which utilizes a consistency tracing paradigm as a template to synthesize training data that captures the stepwise reasoning process; (2) a dynamic beam sampling strategy, which aims to improve the quality of sampled outputs under a fixed sampling budget; and (3) a consistency reward mechanism that can effectively alleviate reward hacking. Experiments on three popular benchmarks show that CodeThinker achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple LLMs. For instance, it outperforms the strongest baseline by 4.3% in accuracy when deployed on Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct. We also validate the effectiveness of CodeThinker on downstream tasks. Results show that, without additional training, CodeThinker obtains average accuracy gains of 5.33 and 3.11 percentage points on mathematical reasoning and code reasoning tasks covering 17 programming languages, respectively.

</details>


### [158] [Function graph transformers universally approximate operators between function spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17968)
*Takashi Furuya,David Mis,Ivan Dokmanić,Maarten V. de Hoop,Matti Lassas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出函数图Transformer（Function Graph Transformer），通过将函数提升为图上的测度，利用测度论视角的Transformer来逼近非线性算子。该框架支持测度收敛视角下的离散化细化，并保证输出保持单值函数结构，同时具备通用逼近能力，适用于负阶Sobolev正则化输入和不同输出域上的查询点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer用于算子学习缺乏统一的连续数学框架，尤其在处理离散化不变性、负阶Sobolev正则化输入以及不同输出域查询点时面临挑战。作者希望通过测度论视角为Transformer-based算子学习提供坚实的理论基础。

Method: 将函数提升为其图上的测度，利用有限token（图上的点集）作为经验近似；引入函数图Transformer作为图保持的测度论Transformer子类，确保输出仍为单值函数；证明该类映射可由标准softmax自注意力层和逐点MLP的有限组合逼近。

Result: 证明了图保持映射可以通过标准softmax自注意力层和逐点MLP的有限组合进行逼近，从而实现了对广泛非线性算子的通用逼近结果。该框架自然地处理了离散化细化、负阶Sobolev正则化输入以及不同输出域上的查询点。

Conclusion: 函数图Transformer为基于Transformer的算子学习提供了连续视角和数学工具，阐明了位置编码、图结构、正则化在确保跨离散化一致性中的作用，是一种统一的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the approximation of nonlinear operators between function spaces by transformers. Our approach is to lift functions to measures supported on their graphs and leverage a recently introduced measure-theoretic view of transformers. A function $h$ is represented by its graph measure $γ_h$, with finite tokens $\{(x_j,h(x_j))\}_{j=1}^N$ being its empirical approximations. We show that this framework elegantly models discretization refinement via convergence of measures and provides a natural setting for operator learning. Within this framework, we introduce function graph transformers, a graph-preserving subclass of measure-theoretic transformers that maps graph measures to graph measures, which is to say that outputs remain single-valued functions. Crucially, this additional structure does not reduce generality: we prove that the resulting graph-preserving maps can be approximated by finite compositions of standard softmax self-attention layers and pointwise MLPs, yielding universal approximation results for broad classes of nonlinear operators. Unlike existing theoretical approaches to operator learning with transformers, the measure-theoretic framework also accommodates regularized negative-order Sobolev inputs for which discretization invariance is particularly challenging, as well as query points on different output domains. Overall, function graph transformers provide a continuum viewpoint and mathematical toolkit for transformer-based operator learning, clarifying the roles of positional encodings, graph structure, regularization, and ensuring consistency across discretizations.

</details>


### [159] [SAFE-SVD: Sensitivity-Aware Fidelity-Enforcing SVD for Physics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17985)
*Chengjie Hong,Feixiang He,Yiheng Zeng,Lulu Kang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向物理基础模型（PFMs）的压缩框架，通过显式建模输出函数空间中的损失感知层灵敏度，在保持物理保真度的前提下实现高压缩比，实验效果显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理基础模型（PFMs）在科学AI中日益重要，但现有模型压缩方法忽略了物理数据的函数特性（偏导数编码时空动力学且对压缩高度敏感），导致物理保真度严重下降甚至失效，亟需专用压缩方法。

Method: 提出灵敏度感知与保真度强制的压缩框架，在输出函数空间中显式建模损失感知的层灵敏度，指导压缩过程以保留物理关键信息。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上，该方法相比现有方法实现显著更高的压缩比，同时保持精度；部分情况下性能提升达数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作为科学基础模型的高效、可部署、可持续化开辟了新方向，有望形成AI for Science中模型压缩的新子领域。

Abstract: We propose a new method for compressing physics foundation models (PFMs) which is a new trend in AI for Science. While model compression is essential for reducing memory use and accelerating inference in large foundation models, it remains under-explored for PFMs, where preserving physical fidelity is crucial. The challenge lies in the functional nature of physics data, where partial derivatives encode spatiotemporal dynamics and exhibit high sensitivity to compression. Conventional compression methods ignore this structure, often causing severe performance degradation or failure. To address this, we introduce a sensitivity-aware fidelity-enforcing compression framework that explicitly models loss-aware layer sensitivity in the output function space during compression. This provides a new route to compressing scientific foundation models while preserving accuracy and physical fidelity. Experiments show substantial gains over existing methods across multiple models and datasets, achieving significantly higher compression ratios while maintaining accuracy, in some cases by orders of magnitude. More broadly, the work potentially leads to a new subfield of efficient, deployable, and sustainable scientific foundation models in AI for Science.

</details>


### [160] [MARR: Module-Adaptive Residual Reconstruction for Low-Bit Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17997)
*Le Su,Xing Luo,Zhi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模块自适应残差重建方法MARR，通过PID自适应更新策略为每个模块分配特定的缩放系数，以平衡残差重建中的累积误差修正和海森近似偏差，在低比特量化（≤4-bit）下，LLM性能提升高达20.2%，ViT性能提升高达4.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差重建量化方法虽通过跨层残差减少累积误差，但残差项会引入海森近似假设带来的额外偏差，导致量化性能次优。

Method: 提出MARR方法：1) 对残差项乘以模块特定的缩放系数，以权衡累积误差修正与HA偏差；2) 采用PID自适应更新策略，以重建误差为反馈渐进优化系数，避免逐模块搜索的高成本。

Result: 在低比特量化（≤4-bit）下，LLM上相比SOTA残差重建方法性能提升高达20.2%，ViT上相对提升高达4.6%。

Conclusion: MARR通过模块自适应的残差缩放有效平衡了误差修正与偏差控制，显著提升低比特量化性能，且PID策略保证了系数估计的稳定性和高效性。

Abstract: Recently, residual reconstruction-based model quantization methods have achieved promising performance in low-bit post-training quantization (PTQ) by introducing cross-layer residuals to reduce error accumulated from previous layers.However, these residuals may also introduce additional bias arising from the Hessian-approximation (HA) assumption underlying reconstruction-based PTQ, leading to suboptimal quantization performance.In this work, we analyze that multiplying the residual term by a scaling coefficient provides a direct way to mitigate the HA bias associated with residual strength, while preserving accumulated-error correction. More importantly, we observe that this trade-off is module-dependent, making a single global residual strength insufficient to balance effective correction and residual-related bias across modules.Based on these observations, we propose Module-Adaptive Residual Reconstruction (MARR), which assigns a module-specific scaling coefficient to adaptively balance accumulated-error correction and residual-related HA bias for each module.To avoid expensive per-module coefficient search and obtain a stable coefficient estimate, we design a Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID)-based adaptive update strategy that uses reconstruction error as feedback to progressively refine this coefficient. Experiments on several typical large language models (LLMs) and vision transformers (ViTs) demonstrate the effectiveness of MARR under low-bit quantization (less than or equal to 4-bit), achieving up to 20.2% performance gains on LLMs and up to 4.6% relative gains on ViTs over the residual reconstruction state-of-the-art methods.Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [161] [RL4RLA: Teaching ML to Discover Randomized Linear Algebra Algorithms Through Curriculum Design and Graph-Based Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18004)
*Jinglong Xiong,Xiaotian Liu,Ruoxin Wang,Zihang Liu,Yefan Zhou,Yujun Yan,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RL4RLA框架，利用强化学习自动发现可解释、符号化的随机线性代数（RLA）算法，通过数值课程和蒙特卡洛图搜索等技术，成功重现有代表性的最先进RLA算法，并能针对精度、速度和稳定性等特定权衡进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLA算法的发现目前主要依赖人工手动过程，需要深厚的专家知识和灵感启发，缺乏自动化发现方法。现有的强化学习方法面对RLA算法搜索时存在奖励稀疏和搜索空间巨大的问题。

Method: 提出RL4RLA通用强化学习框架，从基本线性代数原语构建显式算法；设计数值课程（numerical curriculum）逐步递增问题难度以编码RLA领域归纳偏置；引入蒙特卡洛图搜索（Monte Carlo Graph Search）通过识别和合并等价部分算法来优化探索效率。

Result: 成功重新发现了多种最先进方法，包括sketch-and-precondition求解器、Randomized Kaczmarz和Newton Sketch；能够针对精度、速度和稳定性之间的特定权衡生成优化算法。

Conclusion: RL4RLA证明了强化学习可以自动化发现可验证、可实现的符号化RLA算法，为RLA算法设计提供了一种新的自动化范式。

Abstract: Randomized linear algebra (RLA) algorithms are a modern class of numerical linear algebra techniques that play an essential role in scientific computing and machine learning, with broad and growing adoption. However, their discovery remains mostly a manual process that requires deep expert knowledge and inspiration. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers a pathway to automation, standard approaches struggle with sparse reward landscapes and vast search spaces inherent to high-performing RLA algorithms. In this paper, we present RL4RLA, a general RL framework that automates the discovery of interpretable, symbolic RLA algorithms. Unlike black-box approaches, our method builds explicit algorithms from basic linear algebra primitives, ensuring verifiable and implementable representations. To enable efficient discovery, we introduce: (1) a numerical curriculum that progressively increments problem difficulty to encode inductive bias specific to the RLA domain; (2) Monte Carlo Graph Search, which optimizes exploration by identifying and merging equivalent partial algorithms. We demonstrate that RL4RLA rediscovers state-of-the-art methods, including sketch-and-precondition solvers, Randomized Kaczmarz, and Newton Sketch, and can be targeted to produce algorithms optimized for specific trade-offs between accuracy, speed, and stability. Code is available at https://github.com/Tim-Xiong/RL4RLA.

</details>


### [162] [Scalable Decision-Focused Learning through Cost-Sensitive Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18005)
*Noah Schutte,Senne Berden,Tias Guns,Krzysztof Postek,Neil Yorke-Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"预测-优化"型组合优化问题重构为代价敏感的多输出回归问题，提出无需在训练中反复求解组合优化问题的损失函数组件（代价不敏感归一化、决策感知非对称惩罚、基于实例的代价），在保持与现有方法相当的端到端任务质量前提下，显著提升计算效率，可扩展至此前DFL方法无法处理的大规模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策聚焦学习（DFL）方法虽然能最小化下游任务代价，但训练过程中需要反复求解底层的组合优化问题，计算成本高昂，难以扩展到大规模问题。

Method: 将学习问题重构为代价敏感的多输出回归问题，提出三种损失函数组件：1）代价不敏感归一化；2）决策感知的非对称过/欠预测惩罚；3）基于实例的代价模拟局部真实任务损失。这些组件每个训练数据实例仅需零次或一次求解，训练过程中无需额外求解。

Result: 实验表明该组合损失函数在下游任务质量上与现有最先进方法相当，但计算效率显著更高，能够扩展到此前DFL方法无法处理的大规模问题。

Conclusion: 通过将预测-优化问题重构为代价敏感多输出回归，避免了训练中重复求解组合优化问题，在保证任务质量的同时大幅提升可扩展性，为大规模不确定性组合优化问题提供了高效可行的新范式。

Abstract: Many real-world combinatorial problems involve uncertain parameters, which can be predicted given contextual features and historical data. These `predict-then-optimize' or `contextual optimization' problems have gained significant attention: end-to-end training methods can now minimize the downstream task cost rather than the predictive error. However, despite their effectiveness, these decision-focused learning (DFL) approaches often rely on repeated solving of the underlying combinatorial optimization problem during training, making them computationally expensive and difficult to scale. We reframe the learning problem as a cost-sensitive multi-output regression problem: multi-output due to the combinatorial problem having multiple uncertain parameters, and cost-sensitive due to the downstream task cost being the real target. Our technical contribution is the formalization of multiple loss function components that follow from this reframing: cost-insensitive normalization, decision-aware asymmetric penalization of over- and underpredictions, and instance-based costs that mimic the true downstream task-based loss locally. These components require zero or one solve per training data instance, while requiring no further solves during training. Experiments show that the combination of loss components achieves comparable downstream task quality to the state of the art, while being significantly more efficient, enabling scaling to problem sizes that have not been tackled before with DFL.

</details>


### [163] [Uncertainty Reliability Under Domain Shift: An Investigation for Data-Driven Blood Pressure Estimation in Photoplethysmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18008)
*Mohammad Moulaeifard,Ciaran Bench,Philip J. Aston,Nils Strodthoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了基于PPG信号的深度学习血压估计方法在分布内和分布外场景下的预测性能与不确定性量化可靠性，发现深度集成在域偏移下鲁棒性优于MC Dropout，基于GNLL的方法校准后不确定性最佳，而基于MSE的方法必须经过重校准才能实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性量化对医疗等安全关键领域至关重要，但现有研究很少在真实的分布外条件下评估不确定性量化的可靠性，因此需要系统评估不同方法在域偏移场景下的表现。

Method: 使用XResNet1D-50模型在PulseDB上训练，在四个外部数据集上测试；比较深度集成和MC Dropout两种不确定性方法，结合GNLL和MSE两种损失函数，并可选地采用保形预测、温度缩放和等渗回归进行事后重校准。

Result: (1) 深度集成在域偏移下预测鲁棒性优于MC Dropout；(2) 基于GNLL的重校准方法校准效果最佳；(3) 保形预测和温度缩放提供最一致的增益。

Conclusion: 深度集成方法在域偏移下预测性能最鲁棒，GNLL在原生不确定性量化上最强，重校准对基于MSE的不确定性实用化至关重要，需在外部数据上联合评估预测准确性和校准度以实现可信的无袖带血压估计。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is critical for safety-critical domains like healthcare, yet it is rarely evaluated under realistic out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions. Here, we assessed predictive performance and uncertainty reliability for deep learning-based blood pressure (BP) estimation from photoplethysmography (PPG) signals under both in-distribution (ID) and OOD settings. Using an XResNet1D-50 trained on PulseDB and tested on four external datasets, we compared deep ensembles (DE) and Monte Carlo dropout (MCD) with Gaussian negative log-likelihood (GNLL) and mean squared error (MSE) losses, optionally followed by post-hoc recalibration via conformal prediction (CP), temperature scaling (TS), and isotonic regression (IR).
  The key findings of our study are as follows: (1) DE provides stronger predictive robustness under domain shift than MCD, an advantage that becomes clear primarily under external shift. (2) Recalibrated GNLL-based methods yield the best uncertainty calibration (e.g., GNLL+DE+CP for systolic blood pressure (SBP), GNLL+DE+TS for diastolic blood pressure (DBP)), while MSE-based uncertainty requires recalibration to become practically useful. (3) Across settings, CP and TS offer the most consistent gains, with IR remaining competitive in several cases.
  Overall, our results identify DE-based methods as most robust for predictive performance under domain shift, GNLL as strongest for native UQ, and recalibration as essential for making MSE-based uncertainty practical. These findings highlight the need to jointly assess predictive accuracy and calibration on external data for trustworthy cuffless BP estimation

</details>


### [164] [LogRouter: Adaptive Two-Level LLM Routing for Log Question Answering in Big Data Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18015)
*Mert Coskuner,Merve Zeybel,Melik Mert Dolan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LogRouter是一个部署在TUBITAK BILGEM国家大数据平台上的端到端日志问答系统，采用两层级成本感知路由器，在四种执行路径（直接响应、Druid关键词搜索、模板查询+SQL生成、pgvector语义检索）间智能调度查询。系统结合PySpark Drain3日志解析、GPU加速嵌入、Apache Druid和PostgreSQL双索引存储，并针对语义路径使用14B或32B生成模型。在四个LogHub数据集上，路由器平均准确率达88.4%，端到端延迟18.6秒，相比固定32B基线延迟降低55%，同时答案正确性仅损失5.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自托管、资源受限的环境中，生产日志分析需要以自然语言访问海量日志流，同时避免将每次查询都路由到大语言模型所带来的高昂成本。现有方法要么完全依赖LLM导致延迟和成本过高，要么缺乏对异构日志数据的灵活查询能力。

Method: LogRouter包含：(1) PySpark驱动的Drain3日志解析管道进行日志模板提取；(2) GPU加速的嵌入生成；(3) Apache Druid和PostgreSQL+pgvector双索引存储；(4) 两级成本感知路由器：L1路由器选择四种执行路径（直接响应、Druid关键词搜索、模板查找+SQL生成、pgvector语义检索），L2路由器为语义路径选择14B或32B生成模型；(5) 专用编码器LLM处理Text-to-SQL生成。

Result: 在LogHub的Linux、Apache、Windows、Mac四个数据集（共70个问题）上，路由器达到88.4%的平均准确率（Linux上94.7%）。完整管道ROUGE-1为0.373，BERTScore为0.879，RAGAS Faithfulness为0.779，端到端延迟18.6秒。离线对比中，路由系统相比Fixed-32B基线延迟降低55%（46.3秒 vs 102.1秒），答案正确性仅损失5.8分，且在所有数据集上RAGAS Faithfulness超过Fixed-14B基线。

Conclusion: 成本感知的调度是生产日志QA的实用机制：路由系统在不到一半的延迟下恢复了始终使用32B模型的大部分质量，且L1关键词词汇表无需学习分类器即可高精度做出路由决策，验证了该方案在资源受限生产环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Production log analytics in self-hosted, resource-constrained environments requires natural-language access to massive log streams without the cost of routing every query through a large language model. We present LogRouter, an end-to-end log question-answering system deployed on TUBITAK BILGEM's national big data platform that combines a PySpark-based Drain3 ingestion pipeline, GPU-accelerated embeddings, and dual-index storage in Apache Druid and PostgreSQL with pgvector. A two-level cost-aware router dispatches each query along one of four execution paths: direct response, Druid keyword search, template lookup with SQL generation, and pgvector semantic retrieval, while a Level-2 router selects either a 14B-class or 32B-class generator for the semantic path. A dedicated coder LLM handles text-to-SQL generation. We evaluate the system on four LogHub datasets (Linux, Apache, Windows, and Mac; 70 questions in total) under both an online full-pipeline configuration and an offline configuration that isolates the generator. The router reaches 88.4% mean accuracy across datasets and 94.7% on Linux, while the full pipeline attains a mean ROUGE-1 of 0.373, BERTScore of 0.879, RAGAS Faithfulness of 0.779, and an end-to-end latency of 18.6 s. In an apples-to-apples offline comparison, the routed system reduces mean latency by 55% versus a Fixed-32B baseline (46.3 s vs. 102.1 s) while preserving Answer Correctness within 5.8 points and exceeding a Fixed-14B baseline on RAGAS Faithfulness across every dataset. Cost-aware dispatching is therefore a practical mechanism for production log QA: routing recovers most of the quality of an always-32B configuration at less than half the latency, and the L1 keyword vocabulary makes that routing decision with high precision without a learned classifier.

</details>


### [165] [Interaction-Breaking Adversarial Learning Framework for Robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18024)
*Sunwoo Lee,Mingu Kang,Yonghyeon Jo,Seungyul Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出交互破坏对抗学习框架（IBAL），通过信息论方法构建攻击以扰乱智能体观察和动作来破坏协调，并训练智能体在此类干扰下可靠运行，在多种攻击设置和智能体缺失场景下优于现有鲁棒MARL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒多智能体强化学习方法主要考虑价值导向攻击，当交互结构本身被破坏时（如智能体间观察和动作受到扰动）缺乏鲁棒性，需要研究在此类扰动下保持协调能力的方法。

Method: 提出交互破坏对抗学习框架（IBAL），从信息论视角构建攻击，通过扰动智能体的观察和行动来阻碍协调，并训练智能体在此类破坏下可靠执行任务。

Result: 在多种攻击设置下优于现有鲁棒MARL基线方法，在智能体缺失场景下也展现出更强的性能。

Conclusion: IBAL框架有效提升了多智能体系统在交互结构被破坏时的鲁棒性，填补了现有鲁棒MARL方法在处理交互结构攻击方面的空白。

Abstract: Cooperation is central to multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), yet learned coordination can be fragile when external perturbations disrupt inter-agent interactions. Prior robust MARL methods have primarily considered value-oriented attacks, leaving a gap in robustness when interaction structures themselves are corrupted. In this paper, we propose an interaction-breaking adversarial learning (IBAL) framework that takes an information-theoretic view to construct attacks that impede coordination by perturbing agents' observations and actions, and trains agents to perform reliably under such disruptions. Empirically, our approach improves robustness over existing robust MARL baselines across diverse attack settings and yields stronger performance even under agent-missing scenarios.

</details>


### [166] [FedSDR: Federated Self-Distillation with Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18028)
*Ziheng Ren,Zhanming Shen,Hao Wang,Ning Liu,You Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦自蒸馏(FedSD)作为联邦微调大语言模型的基础策略，并针对其存在的"重写悖论"问题进一步提出FedSDR框架，通过双流LoRA机制（平滑分支+矫正分支）实现全局对齐与事实正确性，实验验证其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调LLM面临严重的统计异质性，现有模型级防御大多忽视了根本原因——内在数据分布不匹配。

Method: 首先建立FedSD作为基础策略，通过将客户端表示投影到平滑的"模型理解空间"来提升性能；然后提出FedSDR框架，包含局部LoRA-S（平滑分支）通过蒸馏数据隐式吸收异质性，以及并行全局LoRA-R（矫正分支）锚定原始数据强制事实正确性，仅聚合LoRA-R。

Result: FedSD作为通用增强器优于传统算法；FedSDR解决了自蒸馏带来的幻觉和冗余问题，实验验证其优越性能。

Conclusion: FedSD是联邦微调中处理统计异质性的有效基础策略，FedSDR通过双流矫正机制进一步优化了自蒸馏范式，实现了全局对齐且事实准确的联邦模型。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of Large Language Models faces severe statistical heterogeneity. However, existing model-level defenses often overlook the root cause: intrinsic data distribution mismatches. In this work, we first establish Federated Self-Distillation (FedSD) as a fundamental and potent strategy. By projecting client representations into a smoothed ``model-understanding space,'' FedSD alone serves as a universal booster, demonstrating superior performance over conventional algorithms. Despite its success, we identify a subtle trade-off termed the Rewrite Paradox -- unconstrained self-distillation can inadvertently increase hallucinations and redundancy. To refine this paradigm, we further propose FedSDR (Federated Self-Distillation with Rectification), the ultimate reinforced framework. It augments FedSD with a dual-stream mechanism: a local LoRA-S (Smoothing) branch to implicitly absorb heterogeneity via distilled data, and a parallel global LoRA-R (Rectification) branch anchored to raw data to enforce factual correctness. By selectively aggregating only LoRA-R, FedSDR yields a globally aligned and faithful model. Extensive experiments verify its superior performance.

</details>


### [167] [Protection Is (Nearly) All You Need: Structural Protection Dominates Scoring in Globally Capped KV Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18053)
*Gabriel Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在全局受限的KV缓存驱逐场景下，七种主流策略（LRU、H2O、SnapKV、StreamingLLM、Ada-KV、QUEST、Random）都存在提示边界脆弱性：若不加以结构保护，在纯Transformer模型上质量几乎崩溃（F1≤0.064）。在每个提示边界保留10%缓存可恢复69-90%的参考质量。注意力分析表明，位置0的sink token占据约75%的prefix注意力质量，而其他边界token仅约均匀期望的0.41倍，导致评分策略虽保留sink但丢弃了其他结构关键token。保护机制主导效果，评分策略差异次要；忠实实现相比简化变体有额外0.03-0.04 F1提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存驱逐策略未考虑提示边界（prompt boundary）的结构保护，在全局受限的解码时缓存共享场景下性能急剧下降。本文旨在揭示这一共同脆弱性，并提出简单的边界保留机制以恢复性能。

Method: 在全局共享缓存容量限制下，对七种驱逐策略（LRU、H2O、SnapKV、StreamingLLM、Ada-KV、QUEST、Random）进行统一评估，引入提示边界保留机制（每边界保留10%缓存）。通过注意力质量分布分析（Qwen2.5-3B, N=30）解释脆弱性根源。采用TOST等价性检验比较简化评分变体与LRU，并在Mistral-7B、Phi-3.5等模型上进行忠实实现对比。在NIAH-32K长上下文场景进行解码与预填充的迁移验证。

Result: 无保护时七种策略在六种纯Transformer模型上F1≤0.064。边界保留10%缓存后，在7个LongBench模型上恢复69-90%参考质量（C=256，13%保留率），10模型面板覆盖68-98%。位置0的sink token占约75%前缀注意力质量，其他边界token仅~0.41倍均匀期望。保护后简化评分变体在K=32时与LRU TOST等价（Δ=0.02），K=8时注意力策略彼此收敛但比LRU高0.011-0.021 F1。忠实Ada-KV/QUEST在Mistral-7B和Phi-3.5上额外提升~0.03-0.04 F1。NIAH-32K迁移显示保护提升比率为0.99-1.00；64K场景下保护有帮助但恢复有限。

Conclusion: KV缓存驱逐中，提示边界结构保护是主导因素，评分策略的差异在边界受保护后成为次要问题。简单的位置边界保留机制即可大幅恢复性能，忠实实现的逐头评分分配可提供额外适度增益。

Abstract: We study KV cache eviction under a shared globally capped decode-time harness. Seven policies (LRU, H2O, SnapKV, StreamingLLM, Ada-KV, QUEST, Random) share a prompt-boundary vulnerability: without structural protection, they collapse to near-zero quality on six pure-transformer models (F1$\leq$0.064). Reserving 10\% of cache at each boundary recovers 69--90\% of the $C{=}2{,}048$ reference-ceiling quality on seven LongBench models at $C{=}256$ (13\% retention); a ten-model panel spans 68--98\%. An attention-mass pilot (Qwen2.5-3B, $N{=}30$) suggests why: the position-0 sink holds ${\sim}75\%$ of prefix mass, while other boundary tokens sit near ${\sim}0.41{\times}$ uniform expectation, so attention scorers retain the sink but still drop structurally critical tokens. With protection, simplified score-isolation variants are TOST-equivalent to LRU at $K{=}32$ ($Δ{=}0.02$); at $K{=}8$, attention policies pairwise converge yet beat LRU by 0.011--0.021 F1 across $C{=}256$ and $C{=}512$. Faithful Ada-KV/QUEST add ${\sim}0.03$--$0.04$ F1 on Mistral-7B and Phi-3.5 beyond simplified variants. A NIAH-32K regime-transfer pilot on Qwen3-4B (decode vs.\ prefill, $C{\in}\{512,2048\}$) shows near-identical protection lifts (ratio 0.99--1.00). At 64K, protection helps but recovery is modest; faithful per-head scoring matches full-cache ceiling on Gemma-3-4B at 6.3\% retention only when the model already supports strong 64K retrieval without eviction. Overall: protection dominates; scoring differences are secondary once boundaries are guarded; per-head allocation gives a further modest gain.

</details>


### [168] [FLAG: Foundation model representation with Latent diffusion Alignment via Graph for spatial gene expression prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18055)
*Qi Si,Penglei Wang,Yushuai Wu,Yifeng Jiao,Xuyang Liu,Xin Guo,Yuan Qi,Yuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLAG是一个基于扩散的框架，用于从H&E染色图像预测空间基因表达，通过结构化分布建模解决基因维数诅咒，并提出了新的结构评估指标GSC和SSC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型将空间基因表达预测视为孤立的逐点任务，忽略了基因协同调控和空间分布等关键生物学结构。

Method: 提出FLAG扩散框架，整合空间图编码器保持拓扑一致性，并利用基因基础模型对齐保证基因-基因保真度，从而解决高维空间中联合建模基因表达与空间交互的"基因维数诅咒"。

Result: 在传统精度指标（PCC/MSE）上具有竞争力，同时在捕获基因-基因和基因-空间关系方面显著增强了结构保真度。

Conclusion: FLAG通过结构化分布建模有效保留了基因协同与空间分布关系，为大规模分子谱分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Predicting spatial gene expression from routine H\&E enables large-scale molecular profiling, yet current models treat this as isolated pointwise tasks, thereby overlooking essential biological structures like gene coordination and spatial distribution. To preserve these relationships, we introduce \textbf{FLAG}, a diffusion-based framework that redefines this task as structured distribution modeling. At the same time, we identify the critical \textbf{Gene Dimension Curse}, where joint modeling gene expression and their spatial interactions fail in high-dimensional spaces, and FLAG solves this challenge by integrating a spatial graph encoder for topological consistency and utilizing Gene Foundation Model (GFM) alignment for gene-gene fidelity in the generation process. To rigorously assess model performance, we propose a set of novel structural evaluation metrics, including Gene Structural Correlation (\textbf{GSC}) and Spatial Structural Correlation (\textbf{SSC}). Our experiments demonstrate that FLAG is highly competitive in traditional accuracy (PCC/MSE) while achieving significantly enhanced structural fidelity in capturing both gene-gene and gene-spatial relationships. The code is available at https://github.com/darkflash03/FLAG.

</details>


### [169] [Equilibrium Selection in Multi-Agent Policy Gradients via Opponent-Aware Basin Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18078)
*Yevhen Shcherbinin,Arina Redina,Maxim Kalpin,Vlad Kochetov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体策略梯度方法在局部收敛到稳定纳什均衡时的均衡选择问题，提出通过“盆域进入概率”来刻画不同均衡的到达可能性。对于有限展开的Meta-MAPG算法，作者发现更新分解为普通策略梯度、自学习和同伴学习修正项，其中同伴学习修正是主要的均衡选择机制。在局部对齐条件下，该修正能提高进入目标稳定纳什集认证吸引域的概率。通过在进入盆域后退火修正项，可恢复普通策略梯度的局部收敛保证。实验在Stag Hunt、迭代囚徒困境及神经策略协调环境中验证了该视角的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体策略梯度方法已被证明可局部收敛到稳定纳什均衡，但局部收敛性无法确定最终到达哪一个均衡。作者希望理解不同均衡被选择的内在机制，即均衡选择问题。

Method: 对有限展开的Meta-MAPG进行分解，识别出普通策略梯度、自学习修正和同伴学习修正三项。通过盆域进入概率分析同伴学习修正对均衡选择的影响，并设计退火策略在进入盆域后恢复原始动力学。

Result: 同伴学习修正在局部对齐条件下显著提高进入目标稳定纳什集认证吸引域的概率；退火修正项后可恢复普通策略梯度的局部收敛保障；实验表明同伴感知更新增加了进入合作盆域的概率。

Conclusion: 同伴学习修正是多智能体策略梯度中均衡选择的关键机制，通过盆域进入概率框架可以系统分析不同稳定纳什均衡的被选择概率，为设计导向特定均衡（如帕累托最优）的多智能体学习算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent policy-gradient methods have been shown to converge locally near stable Nash equilibria. Local convergence, however, does not determine which equilibrium is reached. We study this question through basin-entry probability with respect to a target set of equilibria selected by an external criterion, such as payoff dominance. For finite-unroll Meta-MAPG, we show that the update decomposes into ordinary policy gradient plus own-learning and peer-learning corrections, with controlled sampling noise and finite-unroll bias. We identify the peer-learning correction as the main equilibrium-selection mechanism: under a local alignment condition, the probability of entering the certified attraction region of the target stable-Nash set increases, relative to ordinary policy gradient. Because persistent correction may shift zero-update points of the original game, annealing the correction after entering the basin recovers ordinary policy-gradient dynamics and inherits local stable-Nash convergence guarantees. Experiments in Stag Hunt, iterated Prisoner's Dilemma, and preliminary neural-policy coordination environments support this basin-entry view, showing increased entry into cooperative basins under peer-aware updates.

</details>


### [170] [The Expressive Power of Low Precision Softmax Transformers with (Summarized) Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18079)
*Moritz Brösamle,Stephan Eckstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了标准Transformer解码器（使用softmax注意力和舍入操作）的表达能力，证明其在深度和宽度随上下文长度对数增长时，可以通过思维链模拟图灵机。相比以往依赖hardmax注意力和高精度的结果，本文的构造更贴近实际模型，并在数独任务上取得了更好的可学习性对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer表达性结果通常依赖hardmax注意力、高精度和架构修改，与实际使用的模型脱节。本文旨在弥合这一差距，分析标准softmax Transformer解码器的表达能力。

Method: 首先构造具有三元激活和良好分离注意力分数的hardmax Transformer，通过思维链模拟图灵机；然后将该构造转换为等效的softmax Transformer，避免先前的参数量级或激活精度要求。同时将此技术应用于分析总结思维链范式，证明其更高效地模拟图灵机。

Result: 标准softmax Transformer解码器在深度和宽度随上下文长度对数增长时，具备模拟图灵机的能力；总结思维链范式能以与空间界而非时间界成对数比例的模型规模模拟图灵机；在数独推理任务上的实验与理论预测一致，且可学习性优于先前高精度结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了更贴近实际使用的Transformer表达能力理论，表明标准softmax Transformer在适当缩放下具备通用计算能力，且总结思维链在效率上优于标准思维链。

Abstract: Existing expressivity results for transformers typically rely on hardmax attention, high precision, and other architectural modifications that disconnect them from the models used in practice. We bridge this gap by analyzing standard transformer decoders with softmax attention and rounding of activations and attention weights, while allowing depth and width to grow logarithmically with the context length. As an intermediate step, we construct hardmax transformers with ternary activations and well-separated attention scores that simulate Turing machines using Chain-of-Thought (CoT). This lets us convert the constructions to equivalent softmax transformers without the unrealistic parameter magnitudes or activation precision that prior approaches would require. Using the same technique, we analyze a recently proposed summarized CoT paradigm and show that it simulates Turing machines more efficiently, with model size scaling logarithmically in a space bound rather than a time bound. We empirically test predictions made by our results on a Sudoku reasoning task and find better alignment with learnability than for prior high-precision results. Our code is available at https://github.com/moritzbroe/transformer-expressivity.

</details>


### [171] [pyforce-1.0.0: Python Framework for data-driven model Order Reduction of multi-physiCs problEms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18082)
*Stefano Riva,Yantao Luo,Carolina Introini,Antonio Cammi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pyforce 1.0.0 是一个基于 Python 的数据驱动降阶建模（ROM）包，专为核工程领域的多物理问题设计。相比之前依赖 dolfinx 的旧版本，新版本改用 pyvista 作为后端进行网格导入、积分计算和可视化，并将函数存储为 numpy 数组，提高了易用性和与 VTK 格式求解器的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决核工程多物理模型中计算复杂度过高的问题，通过数据驱动降阶建模技术降低计算成本，同时支持最优传感器位置搜索和实测数据同化以提升对物理系统的认知。

Method: 采用 pyvista 作为后端替代 dolfinx 进行网格导入、积分计算和可视化；将函数存储为 numpy 数组以简化操作；支持任意能将结果导出为 VTK 格式的求解器。

Result: pyforce 1.0.0 版本实现了后端框架的完全重构，摆脱了对特定有限元求解器（dolfinx）的依赖，提升了通用性和易用性。

Conclusion: pyforce 1.0.0 通过架构重构（pyvista 后端 + numpy 数组存储）增强了包的可移植性和可扩展性，使其能适配更广泛的工程求解器，为核工程多物理降阶建模提供了更灵活的 Python 工具。

Abstract: pyforce is a Python package implementing Data-Driven Reduced Order Modelling techniques for applications to multi-physics problems, mainly set in the Nuclear Engineering world. The package is part of the ROSE (Reduced Order modelling with data-driven techniques for multi-phySics problEms): mathematical algorithms aimed at reducing the complexity of multi-physics models (for nuclear reactors applications), at searching for optimal sensor positions and at integrating real measures to improve the knowledge on the physical systems. With respect to the previous original implementation based on dolfinx package (v0.6.0), version 1.0.0 of pyforce has been completely re-written using pyvista as backend for mesh importing, computing integrals, and visualisation of results; in addition, functions are stored as numpy arrays, improving the ease of use of the package. This choice allows to use pyforce with any software solver able to export results in VTK format.

</details>


### [172] [Elastic-dLLM: Position Preserving Context Compression and Augmentation of Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18165)
*Junyi Wu,Tianchen Zhao,Shaoqiu Zhang,Linfeng Zhang,Guohao Dai,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散式语言模型（dLLM）中大量[MASK]标记带来的计算冗余问题，本文提出位置保持的[MASK]标记压缩和终端感知增强方法，通过压缩冗余的[MASK]计算来加速解码，并支持长上下文扩展，同时提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式语言模型（dLLM）虽然支持并行解码，但每步需处理大量[MASK]标记，导致计算成本高昂；且大量计算耗费在重复处理前序上下文和特征表示冗余的[MASK]标记上，存在明显的计算冗余。

Method: 通过系统性分析验证[MASK]标记的冗余性及其在提供结构信息方面的关键作用；提出位置保持的[MASK]标记压缩（压缩冗余[MASK]计算）和终端感知增强（为块级dLLM增加受保护终端[MASK]标记以提升生成质量）。

Result: 方法加速了解码过程；对全序列dLLM（如LLaDA-8B-Instruct、LLaDA-1.5）实现了类似上下文折叠的长上下文扩展；对块级dLLM（如LLaDA2.0-mini）以极小开销增强了生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过压缩冗余[MASK]计算能有效加速dLLM解码并扩展上下文长度；终端感知增强能以低代价提升生成质量。

Abstract: Unlike autoregressive models, which generate one token at a time, dLLMs denoise a chunk of [MASK] tokens jointly and sample one or more tokens per step; despite enabling parallel decoding, this process incurs substantial computational cost due to the large chunk size of masked tokens. We observe that much of this cost is spent on repeatedly processing the preceding context and many [MASK] tokens with the same feature representations, indicating considerable computational redundancy. In this work, we revisit dLLM's redundancy from the perspective of [MASK] tokens. Through systematic analysis, we verify the redundancy of [MASK] tokens while revealing their critical role in providing structural information. Guided by these findings, we propose position-preserving [MASK] token compression and terminal-aware augmentation. By compressing redundant [MASK] computation, this approach accelerates decoding and further provides a natural extension toward context-folding-like long-context scaling under limited input-length constraints for full-sequence dLLMs such as LLaDA-8B-Instruct and LLaDA-1.5. Moreover, for block dLLMs such as LLaDA2.0-mini, it augments the context with a protected terminal [MASK] token to enhance generation quality with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [173] [UTOPYA: A Multimodal Deep Learning Framework for Physics-Informed Anomaly Detection and Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18188)
*Robson W. S. Pessoa,Julien Amblard,Alessandra Russo,Idelfonso B. R. Nogueira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UTOPYA是一个1520万参数的多模态框架，通过FiLM条件跨模态注意力和门控融合8种数据模态，结合物理信息正则化（时间平滑性+热力学单调性）和课程学习，在批处理蒸馏异常检测中取得0.874 AUROC，优于基线方法；消融实验表明FiLM静态上下文是关键，而多种常用正则化技术在此数据稀缺场景下会降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批处理过程中的异常检测面临瞬态动力学、故障标签稀缺以及仅依赖单模态传感器数据的局限性，现有方法难以有效融合多模态信息并利用物理约束。

Method: 提出UTOPYA框架，包含：(1) 通过FiLM条件跨模态注意力与门控融合整合8种数据模态；(2) 物理信息正则化（时间平滑性和热力学单调性约束）；(3) 按物理难度排序的课程学习策略。

Result: 在119实验的多模态批处理蒸馏数据集上，窗口级AUROC达0.832，实验级AUROC达0.874，比最佳基线（LSTM自编码器）提升0.147；FiLM静态上下文提升实验级AUROC达0.145；多种常用正则化技术（实例归一化、Mixup、集成、测试时增强、随机权重平均）在此场景下无效或降低泛化性能。

Conclusion: UTOPYA有效融合多模态数据与物理先验以提升异常检测性能；负结果揭示了平滑正则化与异常检测之间的根本矛盾，为多模态过程监控的实际部署提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in batch processes is hindered by transient dynamics, scarce fault labels, and reliance on single-modality sensor data. This work introduces UTOPYA (Unified Temporal Observation for Physics-Informed Anomaly Detection and Time-Series Prediction), a 15.2M-parameter multimodal framework that jointly addresses anomaly detection, time-series prediction, and phase classification in batch distillation by fusing eight data modalities through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) conditioned cross-modal attention and gated fusion. A physics-informed regularisation scheme introduced in this work enforces temporal smoothness and thermodynamic monotonicity, while curriculum learning introduces training samples in order of physical difficulty. On the 119-experiment multimodal batch distillation dataset of Arweiler et al. (2026), UTOPYA achieves a window-level test AUROC of 0.832 and 0.874 under multi-signal experiment-level scoring, substantially outperforming four external baselines (PCA, autoencoder, Isolation Forest, and LSTM autoencoder) evaluated under identical conditions (+0.147 window-level AUROC over the best baseline). A multimodal ablation over 15~architectural configurations shows that static context via FiLM conditioning is the key enabler, lifting experiment-level multi-signal AUROC by +0.145 over the unimodal baseline (0.729 to 0.874). Separately, a training ablation across 14 design choices reveals that several widely-adopted techniques, including instance normalisation, Mixup, ensembling, test-time augmentation, and stochastic weight averaging, fail to improve or actively degrade generalisation in this data-scarce setting. These negative results expose a fundamental tension between smoothing-based regularisation and anomaly detection, providing practical guidance for multimodal process monitoring deployment.

</details>


### [174] [Concise and Logically Consistent Conformal Sets for Neuro-Symbolic Concept-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18202)
*Samuele Bortolotti,Emanuele Marconato,Andrea Pugnana,Andrea Passerini,Stefano Teso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COCOCO框架，将共形预测集成到神经符号概念模型中，通过联合共形化概念和标签并在单一演绎-溯因修正步骤中协调它们，满足一致性、覆盖率和简洁性三个目标，提供严格的分布自由覆盖保证，并在8个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号概念模型在标签和概念预测上可能过于自信，使得利益相关者难以判断何时可以信任模型的决策，亟需一种能够提供严格不确定性量化保证的方法。

Method: 提出COCOCO框架，一种事后(post-hoc)方法，联合共形化概念和标签预测，并通过单一的演绎-溯因修正步骤来协调二者，同时保持分布自由的覆盖保证，支持用户指定的集合大小预算。

Result: 在8个数据集上的实验表明，COCOCO在性能和集合大小方面优于竞争方法和自然基线，满足所有三个期望目标（一致性、覆盖率、简洁性）。

Conclusion: COCOCO能够有效地将共形预测集成到神经符号概念模型中，提供可靠的不确定性量化，且对不完美的知识具有鲁棒性，为高可靠性应用场景提供保障。

Abstract: Neuro-Symbolic Concept-based Models (NeSy-CBMs) are a family of architectures that integrate neural networks with symbolic reasoning for enhanced reliability in high-stakes applications. They work by first extracting high-level concepts from the input and then inferring a task label from these compatibly with given logical constraints. Yet, their label and concept predictions can be overconfident, making it difficult for stakeholders to gauge when the model's decisions can be trusted. We address this issue by integrating ideas from Conformal Prediction (CP), a framework providing rigorous, distribution-free coverage guarantees. We formalize three desiderata -- consistency, coverage, and conciseness -- that any conformal method for NeSy-CBMs should satisfy, and show that existing approaches fall short of at least one. We then introduce COCOCO, a post-hoc framework that conformalizes concepts and labels jointly and reconciles them via a single deduction-abduction revision step. COCOCO satisfies all three desiderata, retains distribution-free coverage, is robust to imperfect knowledge and supports user-specified size budgets. Our experiments on 8 data sets highlight how COCOCO compares favorably against competitors and natural baselines in terms of performance and set size.

</details>


### [175] [A Simplex Witness Certificate for Constant Collapse in Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18224)
*Zegu Zhang,Jianhua Peng,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究变分自编码器中 encoder 均值与输入无关的精确常数坍缩问题，通过引入 simplex witness head 和 teacher-student 对齐损失，将这一失败模式转化为可预先设计、训练中可监控、训练后可验证的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE 训练中常出现 encoder 输出与输入无关的常数坍缩（posterior collapse 的一种极端形式），现有方法多为事后诊断或启发式缓解，缺乏可预设计、可监控、可验证的理论框架。本文旨在将这一病理现象转化为可设计的工程问题。

Method: 保持先验为标准高斯分布；给定固定的 teacher posterior，在隐层均值上附加一个固定的 simplex witness head；定义 teacher-student 对齐损失，其精确常数预测基线等于 teacher 信息量；利用 simplex witness 的封闭形式逆将任意满支撑的 teacher posterior 通过中心化对数几率嵌入隐空间，显式给出隐能量代价；引入可计算的 view gap 处理 teacher 目标来自不同视图的情况。

Result: 如果对齐损失低于 teacher 信息量基线，则隐层均值不可能与输入无关地常数坍缩；simplex witness 具有封闭逆，可对任意满支撑分布进行显式嵌入；view gap 可量化不同视图带来的差异。

Conclusion: 将 VAE 中的精确常数坍缩从训练后的病理诊断问题转化为可设计、可监控、可验证的工程问题。

Abstract: This note studies exact constant collapse in variational autoencoders, where the encoder mean becomes independent of the input. The goal is to make this specific failure mode pre-designable, monitorable during training, and certifiable after training. The prior is kept as the standard Gaussian. Given a fixed teacher posterior, we attach to the latent mean a fixed simplex witness head. The resulting teacher-student alignment loss has an exact constant-predictor baseline equal to the teacher information. If the alignment loss is below this baseline, the latent mean cannot be input-independent constant collapsed.
  The simplex witness also has a closed-form inverse. Any full-support teacher posterior can be represented by embedding its centered log-odds into the latent space. This gives an explicit latent energy cost and explains when the alignment loss can be made small. A computable view gap handles the case where teacher targets are computed from a different view. Thus exact constant collapse is converted from an after-the-fact training pathology into a design-and-certificate problem.

</details>


### [176] [Are Sparse Autoencoder Benchmarks Reliable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18229)
*David Chanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计了SAEBench这一SAE标准评估套件中的质量指标，发现Targeted Probe Perturbation (TPP)和Spurious Correlation Removal (SCR)在标准设置下存在多个问题，不应继续使用；其余指标的信噪比和区分度也低于预期；sae-probes变体是目前最可靠的指标，但仍难以区分同一架构的不同变体。研究表明需要更好的SAE基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）是大语言模型可解释性的核心工具，而SAE架构的进步依赖于能够可靠区分优劣SAE的基准测试。现有SAE评估套件SAEBench中的质量指标缺乏系统性审计，其可靠性未知。

Method: 通过三个互补视角进行审计：1）固定SAE上的重采样噪声分析；2）合成SAE上的真实相关性验证；3）训练轨迹上的区分度评估。

Result: TPP和SCR在标准设置下未能通过多个审计视角；其余指标的重采样噪声高于预期、区分度低于预期；sae-probes是最可靠的指标但仍存在局限。

Conclusion: 当前SAE基准测试存在显著缺陷，需要设计更好的评估体系来推动SAE架构的进步。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are a core interpretability tool for large language models, and progress on SAE architectures depends on benchmarks that reliably distinguish better SAEs from worse ones. We audit the SAE quality metrics in SAEBench, the de-facto standard SAE evaluation suite, through three complementary lenses: reseed noise on a fixed SAE, ground-truth correlation on synthetic SAEs, and discriminability across training trajectories. We find that two of these metrics, Targeted Probe Perturbation (TPP) and Spurious Correlation Removal (SCR), fail multiple lenses at their canonical settings and should not be used to evaluate SAEs. The other metrics show higher reseed noise and lower discriminability than the field assumes. The sae-probes variant of $k$-sparse probing is the most reliable metric we tested, but even sae-probes struggles to separate variants of the same SAE architecture. Our results show the field needs better SAE benchmarks.

</details>


### [177] [Attacking the First-Principle: A Black-Box, Query-Free Targeted Mimicry Attack on Binary Function Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18231)
*Gabriel Sauger,Jean-Yves Marion,Sazzadur Rahaman,Victor Matrat,Vincent Tourneur,Muaz Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kelpie框架，能够在黑盒、零查询条件下对二值函数分类器执行拟态攻击（一种更强的定向逃逸攻击），通过保持恶意载荷功能的代码变换使其被误分类为目标类别，成功攻击了六种最先进的分类器，并实际演示了恶意软件伪装成良性函数的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的二值函数分类器容易受到对抗性攻击，但既往方法多依赖查询目标分类器来改进非定向逃逸攻击；需要更强的定向逃逸攻击方法，且无需与目标模型交互，以揭示现有安全机制的脆弱性。

Method: 提出Kelpie框架，采用保持功能的代码变换对恶意载荷进行变形，使其被目标二值函数分类器错误分类为指定的良性类别；整个过程在黑盒、零查询条件下完成，无需访问或查询目标模型。

Result: Kelpie成功对六种代表不同架构的最先进二值函数分类器执行了拟态攻击；实际演示中将键盘记录器和擦除器隐藏在应用程序的良性函数中，验证了攻击的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究首次在黑盒、零查询环境下实现拟态攻击，表明现有基于机器学习的二值函数分类器存在严重安全隐患，对软件系统的安全性和可靠性提出了重要质疑。

Abstract: Binary function classifiers play a crucial role in maintaining the security and integrity of software systems by detecting malicious code and unauthorized modifications. However, machine learning-based classifiers are vulnerable to adversarial attacks that can evade detection. In this study, we present Kelpie, a novel framework for executing mimicry attacks, a stronger type of targeted evasion attacks, on binary function classifiers in a black-box, zero-query setting. Unlike previous approaches that rely on querying the target classifier to refine untargeted evasion attacks, Kelpie leverages code transformations that preserve the functionality of malicious payloads while causing them to be misclassified as we want. Through extensive experimentation, we demonstrate that Kelpie can successfully execute mimicry attacks against six state-of-the-art binary function classifiers representing different model architectures without requiring direct interaction with them. We further validate our approach with a practical demonstration, involving a keylogger and a wiper concealed within benign-looking functions embedded in an application. This work, to our best knowledge, is the first to demonstrate such a mimicry attack in a black-box, zero-query context, raising important questions about the reliability and security of existing machine learning-based binary function classifiers.

</details>


### [178] [Privacy Preserving Reinforcement Learning with One-Sided Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18246)
*Lin William Cong,Guangyan Gan,Hanzhang Qin,Zhenzhen Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出POOL算法，在多维连续状态动作空间和单侧反馈的强化学习场景中实现隐私保护，其样本复杂度与非私有RL的已知下界相匹配，证明了强隐私保证与高学习效率可以兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维连续状态动作空间中的单侧反馈RL面临学习效率与隐私保护的双重挑战，现有方法难以兼顾二者。

Method: 提出POOL（Privacy-preserving RL algorithm），一种新颖的隐私保护强化学习算法，在单侧反馈场景下结合差分隐私机制进行策略优化。

Result: 理论推导出POOL的样本复杂度界与非私有RL的已知下界相匹配，其中E_rho为隐私参数，H为时间步长，alpha为最优性间隙参数。

Conclusion: 在单侧反馈的多维环境中，可以在施加强隐私保证的同时保持高学习效率，推动了实用化隐私感知RL的发展。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) in multi-dimensional continuous state and action spaces with one-sided feedback, where the agent receives partial observations of the state and obtains reward information for only a subset of the state-action space at each time step. This setting introduces substantial challenges in both learning efficiency and privacy preservation. To address these challenges, we propose POOL, a novel privacy-preserving RL algorithm. We conduct a comprehensive theoretical analysis of POOL, deriving a sample complexity bound that matches the known lower bounds for non-private RL. Here, E_rho denotes the privacy parameter, H is the time horizon, and alpha is the optimality-gap parameter. Our findings show that it is possible to enforce strong privacy guarantees while maintaining high learning efficiency, marking a significant step toward practical, privacy-aware RL in multi-dimensional environments with one-sided feedback.

</details>


### [179] [Temporal Task Diversity: Inductive Biases Under Non-Stationarity in Synthetic Sequence Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18281)
*Afiq Abdillah Effiezal Aswadi,Oliver Britton,Ross Baker,Matthew Farrugia-Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究训练过程中数据分布变化（非平稳性）对深度学习归纳偏好的影响，发现在上下文线性回归序列建模中，训练任务分布在时间上的多样化会增强模型泛化而非记忆的偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习通常假设数据分布固定，但许多实际问题涉及分布变化。作者希望探究这种非平稳性如何影响深度学习对不同结构、泛化和安全性属性的归纳偏好。

Method: 以上下文线性回归序列建模作为测试平台，研究训练任务分布在时间维度上的多样化对小型Transformer模型归纳偏好的影响。

Result: 训练任务分布的时间多样性会导致模型更倾向于泛化而非记忆。

Conclusion: 数据分布的非平稳性（尤其是任务分布在训练时间上的多样化）能够增强深度学习模型的泛化偏好，这为理解实际动态学习场景中的归纳偏置提供了新视角。

Abstract: Modern deep learning science often assumes that neural networks learn from a fixed data distribution. However, many practically important learning problems involve data distributions that change throughout training. How does such non-stationarity impact the inductive biases of deep learning towards models with different structural, generalisation, and safety properties? A fruitful testbed for studying inductive bias is in-context linear regression sequence modelling, where small transformers display strikingly different generalisation patterns depending on the diversity of the (fixed) training task distribution. In this paper, we explore the effect of diversifying the task distribution across training time, finding that such temporal diversity leads to an increased bias towards generalisation over memorisation.

</details>


### [180] [PH-Dreamer: A Physics-Driven World Model via Port-Hamiltonian Generative Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18303)
*Xueyu Luan,Chenwei Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Port-Hamiltonian框架的统一世界模型，通过嵌入物理先验、构建能量世界模型以及能量引导的Actor-Critic机制，在视觉控制任务中实现了更高的渐进回报、更优的内部模拟器保真度，并显著降低了潜在相空间体积（4.18-8.41%）、能量消耗（最高7.80%）和平均平方加加速度（最高9.38%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于循环状态空间架构的世界模型虽然支持高效的潜在想象，但缺乏物理结构约束，产生的动力学违反守恒和耗散原理，导致物理不一致性。

Method: 提出三方面协同机制：(1) 将隐式物理先验嵌入循环转换，通过流和耗散控制能量路由，约束潜在演化；(2) 开发运动学感知能量世界模型，从本体感受观测中估计哈密顿量和功率平衡；(3) 构建能量引导的Actor-Critic，利用拉格朗日乘子正则化策略优化以降低能量和平滑控制。

Result: 在视觉控制基准上，该方法不仅实现了更优的渐进回报，还提升了内部模拟器保真度（想象奖励与真实奖励之间更紧致、更低方差的对齐），同时潜在相空间体积减少4.18-8.41%，能量消耗降低高达7.80%，平均平方加加速度降低高达9.38%。

Conclusion: Port-Hamiltonian框架能够有效为世界模型注入物理归纳偏置，在保持潜在想象效率的同时，显著提升动力学的物理一致性和控制性能。

Abstract: World models built on recurrent state space architectures enable efficient latent imagination, yet remain physically unstructured, producing dynamics that violate conservation and dissipative principles. We introduce a unified Port-Hamiltonian framework that remedies this through three synergistic mechanisms. First, we embed implicit physical priors into recurrent transitions by modeling projected latent evolution as action controlled energy routing governed by flow and dissipation, biasing the projected PH phase space toward a more compact and physically structured representation. Second, we develop a kinematics aware energy world model that estimates the Hamiltonian and power balance from proprioceptive observations, providing an explicit physical signal for thermodynamic reasoning. Third, leveraging these energy gradients, we establish an energy guided Actor-Critic that uses Lagrangian multipliers to regularize policy optimization toward lower energy and smoother control. Across visual control benchmarks, this paradigm not only attains superior asymptotic returns but also elevates internal simulator fidelity by establishing a tighter, lower variance alignment between imagined and real rewards, all while reducing latent phase space volume by 4.18-8.41%, energy consumption by up to 7.80%, and mean squared jerk by up to 9.38%.

</details>


### [181] [Alignment Dynamics in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18309)
*Yuhan Huang,Huanran Chen,Yinpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的理论框架，通过将参数空间的学习动力学与函数空间的对齐行为联系起来，解释了LLM在微调过程中对齐被破坏（Rebound Force）和重新激活（Driving Force）的机制，并预测了"排练启动效应"（Rehearsal Priming Effect），即在重新暴露于对齐数据时对齐恢复更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释要么归因于梯度几何，要么归因于输出分布偏移，缺乏一个统一的理论框架来桥接参数空间学习动力学与函数空间对齐行为在微调过程中的关系。

Method: 引入一个可计算的对齐分数，推导其在微调期间的闭式更新公式，将对齐更新分解为两个竞争分量：由当前对齐状态和模型分布狭窄性共同决定的"回弹力"（Rebound Force），以及由训练分布与对齐/非对齐完成的结果条件后验之间的一致性决定的"驱动力"（Driving Force）。

Result: 在安全对齐、突发性错位和情感对齐等多个场景中验证了预测，实验证明对齐反转和重新暴露下的加速重新对齐一致存在，并证实了回弹强度对后验狭窄性的依赖。

Conclusion: 提供了一个关于LLM微调中对齐如何被破坏和重新激活的统一动力学视角，解释了为什么先前的对齐可以在后续微调中被逆转，以及为什么更狭窄的后验结构会加强这种逆转。

Abstract: Although Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong alignment through supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning from human feedback, the alignment is often fragile under subsequent fine-tuning. Existing explanations either attribute alignment fragility to gradient geometry or characterize it as a distributional shift in model outputs, yet few provide a unified account that bridges parameter-space learning dynamics with function-space alignment behavior during fine-tuning. In this work, we introduce a tractable alignment score and derive its closed-form update during fine-tuning, yielding a unified framework for alignment dynamics. Our analysis decomposes alignment updates into two competing components: a \textbf{\color{red!60!black} Rebound Force}, governed jointly by the current alignment state and the narrowness of model distribution, and a \textbf{\color{green!60!black} Driving Force}, determined by how the training distribution aligns with outcome-conditioned posteriors over aligned and non-aligned completions. This decomposition explains why prior alignment can be reversed by later fine-tuning and why narrower posterior structure strengthens such reversal. Moreover, our framework predicts a \textbf{Rehearsal Priming Effect}: prior alignment leaves a latent posterior imprint that amplifies the effective Driving Force upon re-exposure, leading to faster re-alignment. We validate these predictions across safety alignment, emergent misalignment, and sentiment settings, demonstrating consistent alignment reversal and accelerated re-alignment under re-exposure. In addition, controlled experiments in safety alignment confirm the predicted dependence of rebound strength on posterior narrowness. Together, these results provide a unified dynamical perspective on how alignment is disrupted and reactivated during LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [182] [Dynamic Elliptical Graph Factor Models via Riemannian Optimization with Geodesic Temporal Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18316)
*Chuansen Peng,Xiaojing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Degfm算法，通过Grassmann流形上的黎曼测地线惩罚结合低秩加对角结构，解决高维时序数据中动态图结构的估计问题，在保持时间一致性和尊重流形几何的同时实现高效估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从高维节点观测数据中推断时变图结构面临两个核心挑战：保持潜在图在连续观测窗口间的时间一致性，以及尊重精度矩阵所在对称正定流形的内在黎曼几何结构（其测地线结构与欧氏空间根本不同）。现有方法未能同时有效处理这两个问题。

Method: 将时变精度矩阵序列建模为低秩加对角（LRaD）结构，由潜在椭圆图因子模型驱动，大幅降低有效参数数量；在Grassmann流形上施加黎曼测地线惩罚来强制执行时间一致性；设计基于Grassmann流形的黎曼梯度下降算法，保证每次迭代都尊重流形结构。

Result: 在合成基准和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，Degfm在所有评估指标上始终优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种能同时解决时变图估计中时间一致性和黎曼几何结构两大挑战的算法，并严格证明了其收敛到稳定点，验证了框架的实际有效性。

Abstract: Inferring time-varying graph structures from high-dimensional nodal observations is a fundamental problem arising in neuroscience, finance, climatology, and beyond. Two intrinsic challenges govern this problem: maintaining the \emph{temporal coherence} of the latent graph across successive observation windows, and respecting the \emph{intrinsic Riemannian geometry} of the symmetric positive definite manifold on which precision matrices naturally reside, a curved space whose geodesic structure departs fundamentally from that of the ambient Euclidean space. In this paper we propose dynamic estimation on the Grassmann manifold with a factor model (\textsc{Degfm}), a novel algorithm that jointly addresses both challenges. We model the time-varying precision matrix sequence as a low-rank-plus-diagonal structure governed by a latent elliptical graph factor model, which drastically reduces the effective parameter count and enables reliable estimation in the challenging small-sample regime. Temporal coherence is enforced through a Riemannian geodesic penalty defined on the Grassmann manifold, ensuring that the estimated graph trajectory is smooth with respect to the intrinsic geometry rather than the ambient Euclidean space. To solve the resulting non-convex optimization problem over Grassmann-manifold-valued sequences subject to the LRaD constraint, we derive an efficient Riemannian gradient descent algorithm that respects the manifold structure at every iterate and rigorously establish its convergence to a stationary point. Extensive experiments on both synthetic benchmarks and real-world datasets demonstrate that \textsc{Degfm} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all evaluation metrics, confirming the practical effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [183] [ISEP: Implicit Support Expansion for Offline Reinforcement Learning via Stochastic Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18320)
*Yifei Chen,Shaoqin Zhu,Xiaoqiang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ISEP框架，通过隐式支持扩展和随机策略优化解决离线强化学习中过于保守的问题，并用条件流匹配实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线强化学习方法施加严格约束以保证安全，但阻碍了在行为策略直接支持范围外发现最优行为的能力。

Method: 通过插值价值函数隐式扩展可行动作支持，结合随机动作选择策略在保守克隆和乐观扩展间交替优化，并使用条件流匹配与无分类器引导实现ISEP-FM。

Result: ISEP技术实现了高奖励区域的"致密化"，为策略改进创建了可导航路径，同时理论上保证了有界价值误差。

Conclusion: ISEP框架能在保证安全的同时有效探索行为策略支持范围外的潜在最优行为，解决了保守性与探索性之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning methods typically enforce strict constraints to ensure safety; yet this rigidity often prevents the discovery of optimal behaviors outside the immediate support of the behavior policy. To address this, we propose Implicit Support Expansion via stochastic Policy optimization (ISEP), which leverages a value function interpolated between in-distribution data and policy samples to implicitly expand the feasible action support. This mechanism "densifies" high-reward regions, creating a navigable path for policy improvement while theoretically guaranteeing bounded value error. However, optimizing against this expanded support creates a multimodal landscape where standard deterministic averaging leads to mode collapse and invalid actions. ISEP mitigates this via a stochastic action selection strategy, optimizing the policy by stochastically alternating between conservative cloning and optimistic expansion signals. We instantiate this framework as ISEP-FM using Conditional Flow Matching utilizing classifier-free guidance to effectively capture the interpolated value signal.

</details>


### [184] [Prune, Update and Trim: Robust Structured Pruning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18331)
*Diego Coello de Portugal Mecke,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidth-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Putri是一种针对大语言模型的后训练剪枝方法，通过更新FFN层未剪枝权重、顺序剪枝FFN层以及逐头移除注意力机制，在保持简单性的同时实现了SOTA性能，尤其在极端稀疏比下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理成本高昂，尤其在长上下文推理或资源受限设备上，现有后训练剪枝方法主要移除FFN层中信息量较少的隐藏节点和最不重要的注意力层，但效果有限。

Method: Putri提出三项改进：1) 更新FFN中未剪枝权重以补偿剪枝误差；2) 顺序剪枝FFN层，并考虑前一层更新；3) 移除单个注意力头而非完整注意力层，并扩展到分组查询注意力(GQA)。

Result: 在多种模型、不同稀疏度范围和数据集上的剪枝实验验证了Putri的泛化能力，尤其能在极端稀疏比下成功剪枝，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Putri是一种结构剪枝方法，保持简单性的同时实现SOTA性能，能够处理极端稀疏比场景，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have experienced significant growth and development in recent years. However, performing inference on LLMs remains costly, especially for long-context inference or in resource-constrained devices. This motivates the development of new post-training pruning (PTP) methods. These methods reduce LLMs' requirements by removing a substantial part of the model's parameters. The discarded weights are selected depending on their impact on the models performance. Current PTP methods prune the models by removing the less informative hidden nodes from the FFN layers, and the least important attention layers. We propose Putri, a PTP method that introduces three changes to the State- of-the-art. First, we update the un-pruned weights of the FFN to compensate for the introduced pruning error. Second, the FFN layers are pruned sequentially, taking into account the updates done to the previous layers. Third, instead of removing full attention layers, we remove individual attention-heads. We extend this method such that it can also address Grouped-Query Attention. In summary, Putri is a structure pruning method which remains simple while showing SOTA performance. Pruning experiments on multiple models with a wide variety of sparsity ranges and on different datasets, validate the generality of Putri. Notably, we demonstrate that, unlike previous methods, Putri can prune LLMs on extreme sparsity ratios. The code is available at: https://github.com/Coello-dev/Putri.

</details>


### [185] [Decoupled Conformal Optimisation: Efficient Prediction Sets via Independent Tuning and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18354)
*Fanyi Wu,Lihua Niu,Samuel Kaski,Michele Caprio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出解耦共形优化（DCO），将效率导向的结构选择与最终的共形分位数校准分离，在保证有限样本边际覆盖的同时，相比PAC风格方法减少了预测集大小或区间宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯共形优化方法通常将同一份留置数据同时用于搜索高效预测集和认证覆盖/风险，这种耦合对高概率风险控制保证是自然的，但对于标准有限样本边际共形覆盖而言并非必要。因此希望解耦这两个过程，以获得更优的效率。

Method: 提出DCO的"训练-调优-校准"三阶段设计原则：使用独立的调优分割进行效率导向的结构选择，使用全新的校准分割计算最终的共形分位数。条件于调优后的结构，标准分割共形可交换性对任何候选类都能保证有限样本边际覆盖，无需置信参数或多重检验校正。

Result: 在ImageNet-A上平均集大小从26.52降至25.26，95百分位集大小从58.95降至53.73；Diabetes上平均区间宽度从2.098降至1.914。DCO在多个基准上紧密追踪名义覆盖水平，同时相比PAC风格校准降低了平均预测集大小或区间宽度。

Conclusion: DCO追求与PAC风格方法不同的有限样本保证（边际共形覆盖而非高概率风险控制），但在风险边界的一致性假设下，两种方法趋近于相同的总体阈值。

Abstract: Bayesian conformal optimisation methods often use the same held-out data both to search for efficient prediction sets and to certify coverage or risk. This coupling is natural for high-probability risk-control guarantees, but it is not necessary when the target is standard finite-sample marginal conformal coverage. We propose Decoupled Conformal Optimisation (DCO), a train-tune-calibrate design principle that uses an independent tuning split for efficiency-oriented structural selection and a fresh calibration split for the final conformal quantile. Conditional on the tuned structure, standard split-conformal exchangeability yields finite-sample marginal coverage for any candidate class, without a confidence parameter or multiple-testing correction. DCO therefore targets a different finite-sample guarantee from PAC-style methods: marginal conformal coverage rather than high-probability risk control. Under consistency assumptions on the coupled risk bound, the two approaches nevertheless converge to the same population threshold. Across classification and regression benchmarks, including ImageNet-A, CIFAR-100, Diabetes, California Housing, and Concrete, DCO tracks the nominal coverage level closely while often reducing average prediction-set size or interval width relative to PAC-style calibration. On ImageNet-A, for example, the average set size decreases from $26.52$ to $25.26$ and the 95th-percentile set size from $58.95$ to $53.73$; on Diabetes, the average interval width decreases from $2.098$ to $1.914$.

</details>


### [186] [Proximal basin hopping: global optimization with guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18364)
*Guillaume Lauga,Cesare Molinari,Samuel Vaiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Proximal Basin Hopping (PBH)框架，结合近端优化与局部最小化，构建的算法在有限样本下以高概率收敛到全局最小值，在标准合成困难函数、深度学习缩放律拟合等实际问题上优于现有理论支撑算法，且维度越高性能优势越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局优化问题中，许多算法虽有经验成功但缺乏理论支撑，本文旨在建立兼具理论保证与实践性能的全局优化框架。

Method: 提出Proximal Basin Hopping (PBH)理论框架，巧妙结合近端优化（proximal optimization）和局部最小化（local minimization），构建实用算法。

Result: 在有限样本下以高概率收敛到全局最小值；在标准合成困难函数和深度学习缩放律拟合等实际问题上优于有理论保证的知名算法；维度越高性能差距越大。

Conclusion: PBH框架成功弥合了全局优化算法理论与实践之间的差距，在高维场景下尤其有效。

Abstract: Global optimization is a challenging problem, with plenty of algorithms displaying empirical success, but scarce theoretical backing. In this work, we propose a new theoretical framework called Proximal Basin Hopping (PBH), carefully tailored to combine proximal optimization and local minimization. We use it to construct a practical algorithm that converges to the global minimizer with high probability, when using a finite amount of samples. Proximal Basin Hopping outperforms well known algorithms with theoretical backing on standard synthetic hard functions, and real problems such as fitting scaling laws for deep learning. Furthermore, the higher the dimension, the better the performance gap.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Square Roots: Explicit Memory-Efficient Factorization for Multi-Epoch Private Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18379)
*Nikita P. Kalinin,Aki Rehn,Joel Daniel Andersson,Antti Honkela,Christoph H. Lampert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出γ-BIFR，一种统一了DP-λCGD和BISR两种相关噪声分解的通用方法，在低内存、低带宽场景下显著提升了差分隐私模型训练的效用，并给出了多轮训练中多方参与误差的更紧理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关噪声机制能提升差分隐私模型训练的效用，但现有分解方法在效用与内存成本之间存在权衡：DP-λCGD内存效率高但效用提升有限，BISR在大带宽时渐近最优但在低带宽时表现差。需要一种能在低带宽下兼顾效用和内存效率的统一方法。

Method: 提出γ-BIFR，通过引入可调参数γ将DP-λCGD和BISR两种分解统一为广义形式，在相关矩阵的带状逆分解框架下实现灵活调节带宽与效用的权衡。

Result: 在低内存、低带宽场景下，γ-BIFR显著降低了RMSE、amplified RMSE，并提升了私有训练性能，同时提供了多轮训练中多方参与误差的更紧理论保证。

Conclusion: γ-BIFR作为统一框架有效弥补了现有分解方法在低带宽场景下的不足，为差分隐私模型训练中相关噪声机制的实际部署提供了更优的内存-效用权衡方案。

Abstract: Correlated-noise mechanisms are among the most promising approaches for improving the utility of differentially private model training, but rigorous guarantees require explicit, analyzable factorizations, and practical deployment requires memory efficiency. Recent works have developed banded inverse factorizations, which address both requirements by exploiting a banded structure in the correlation matrix. The bandwidth controls the size of the noise buffer used to correlate noise across iterations, and thus governs the tradeoff between utility and memory cost. Existing factorizations highlight this tradeoff: DP-$λ$CGD achieves high memory efficiency by using only a one-step noise buffer, but this limits its utility gains, while the banded inverse square root (BISR) factorization exploits larger correlation windows and is asymptotically optimal for large bandwidths but performs poorly at low bandwidths. We propose $γ$-BIFR, a unified generalization of both factorizations. In the low-memory, low-bandwidth regime, $γ$-BIFR significantly improves RMSE, amplified RMSE, and private training performance, while yielding tighter theoretical guarantees for multi-participation error in multi-epoch training.

</details>


### [188] [Generating Physically Consistent Molecules with Energy-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18381)
*Christoph Griesbacher,Lea Bogensperger,Andreas Habring,Thomas Pock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EBMol是首个在3D分子生成任务上达到SOTA的基于能量的模型，通过原子加性标量势和流启发的Restoring Field Matching目标学习能量景观，利用Mirror-Langevin算法和并行回火进行采样，并支持可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流匹配模型通过时间条件得分或传输场在噪声和数据之间学习，虽然训练目标更易处理，但失去了能量归纳偏置。作者希望恢复能量景观这一物理上有依据的建模目标，同时避免显式模拟训练。

Method: 1) 学习原子加性标量势(atom-additive scalar potential)作为能量函数；2) 采用流启发的Restoring Field Matching目标近似能量景观，无需显式模拟训练；3) 使用Mirror-Langevin算法进行采样，实现原子位置和类型的统一更新；4) 引入并行回火(parallel tempering)进行推理时计算扩展。

Result: 在QM9和GEOM-Drugs基准上达到3D分子生成的SOTA性能；学习到的能量景观可作为原则性质量指标用于排序和过滤构象；支持形状引导采样和零样本连接子设计等可控生成。

Conclusion: EBMol首次证明了EBM在3D分子生成任务上可以达到甚至超越扩散/流匹配模型，且能量景观带来了可控生成和质量评估等额外优势。

Abstract: Molecules in equilibrium follow a Boltzmann distribution, making the underlying energy landscape a physically grounded modeling objective. However, such landscapes are difficult to learn from data and, once learned, hard to sample from. Diffusion and flow-matching models sidestep these difficulties by learning a time-conditional score or transport field between noise and data, losing the energy inductive bias in exchange for a more tractable training objective. We introduce EBMol, an energy-based model (EBM) that restores this inductive bias by learning an atom-additive scalar potential without explicit simulation during training. Our method employs a flow-inspired Restoring Field Matching objective to approximate the energy landscape. We adopt the Mirror-Langevin algorithm for sampling, enabling unified updates of atomic positions and types, and incorporate parallel tempering for inference-time compute scaling. EBMol is the first EBM for 3D molecular generation to achieve state-of-the-art performance on QM9 and GEOM-Drugs. Moreover, we show that the learned energy landscape serves as a principled quality metric for ranking and filtering configurations, and demonstrate controllable generation without retraining through shape-steered sampling via potential composition and zero-shot linker design.

</details>


### [189] [TabH2O: A Unified Foundation Model for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18383)
*Pascal Pfeiffer,Dmitry Gordeev,Mathias Müller,Laura Fink,Joan Salvà Soler,Mark Landry,Branden Murray,Marcos V. Conde,Sri Satish Ambati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabH2O是一个用于表格数据的基座模型，通过上下文学习在单次前向传播中完成分类和回归任务。该模型基于TabICL架构，主要改进包括：统一训练（双头架构同时处理分类和回归）、单阶段预训练（通过训练稳定性改进消除多阶段课程学习）、噪声感知预训练（显式噪声维度增强鲁棒性）。在TALENT基准的300个数据集上，29.2M参数的TabH2O v1平均排名2.55，优于CatBoost、H2O AutoML、LightGBM，与TabPFN v2.6相当，弱于TabICL v2，并在81%测试数据集上进入前三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格数据基座模型（如TabICL）存在以下问题：需要为分类和回归分别训练单独模型，多阶段课程学习导致预训练复杂且耗时，以及模型对无关特征缺乏鲁棒性。研究者希望开发一个统一的、训练更高效的、对噪声特征更鲁棒的表格数据基座模型。

Method: (1) 统一训练：采用双头架构（classification head + regression head），单个模型同时处理分类和回归任务，降低预训练成本；(2) 单阶段预训练：引入有界可扩展softmax、阶段间归一化、可学习残差缩放、logit软上限等技术改善训练稳定性，无需多阶段课程学习即可从开始就用完整长度序列训练；(3) 噪声感知预训练：在合成数据集中显式加入噪声维度，使模型学会忽略无关特征。

Result: 在TALENT基准（300个数据集）上，TabH2O v1（29.2M参数）平均排名2.55/6，优于CatBoost（4.07）、H2O AutoML（4.18）、LightGBM（5.08），与TabPFN v2.6（2.74）竞争力相当，落后于TabICL v2（2.12），在81%的测试数据集上进入前三。

Conclusion: TabH2O证明了大模型方法在表格数据任务上的有效性，通过统一训练、单阶段预训练和噪声感知机制，在保持竞争力的同时大幅简化了训练流程，为表格数据的基座模型提供了更高效、更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: We present TabH2O, a foundation model for tabular data that performs classification and regression in a single forward pass via in-context learning. TabH2O builds on the TabICL architecture with several key modifications: (1) unified training, a single model handles both classification and regression via a dual-head architecture, eliminating the need for separate models and reducing total pretraining cost; (2) single-stage pretraining, training stability improvements (bounded scalable softmax, inter-stage normalization, learnable residual scaling, logit soft-capping) eliminate the need for multi-stage curriculum learning, enabling training with full-length sequences from the start; and (3) noise-aware pretraining, synthetic datasets include explicit noise dimensions to teach the model robustness to irrelevant features. We evaluate TabH2O v1 (29.2M parameters) on the TALENT benchmark (300 datasets), where it achieves an average rank of 2.55 out of 6 evaluated methods, outperforming tuned CatBoost (4.07), H2O AutoML (4.18), and LightGBM (5.08), competitive with TabPFN v2.6 (2.74), and behind TabICL v2 (2.12), while placing in the top-3 on 81% of the testing datasets across classification and regression tasks.

</details>


### [190] [Graph Hierarchical Recurrence for Long-Range Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18387)
