*Stefano Carotti,Marco Pacini,Alessio Gravina,Davide Bacciu,Bruno Lepri,Sebastiano Bontorin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图层次递归（GHR）框架，通过在输入图和通过池化获得的层次抽象上联合操作，有效解决了GNN和Graph Transformer在长距离依赖和域外泛化上的局限性。GHR仅用当前SOTA模型1%的参数即可在长距离基准上持续取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN和Graph Transformer虽有效，但在需捕捉图中远距离节点间关联的任务中仍存在根本性局限，且在域外泛化（测试涉及比训练更长的距离交互）时问题更为突出。

Method: 提出Graph Hierarchical Recurrence (GHR)框架，在原始图和通过池化获得的层次抽象上联合进行递归操作，通过层次化结构增强信息传播能力。

Result: 在广泛的长距离基准上，GHR一致优于现有图模型，且仅使用当前SOTA模型1%的参数即可达到更优性能。

Conclusion: 提升模型容量本身可能不足以实现良好泛化，GHR为构建图基础模型提供了与当前规模扩展方向互补的新思路。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and Graph Transformers (GTs) are now a fundamental paradigm for graph learning, combining the representation-learning capabilities of deep models with the sample efficiency induced by their inductive biases. Despite their effectiveness, a large body of work has shown that these models still face fundamental limitations in tasks that require capturing correlations between distant regions of a graph. To address this issue, we introduce Graph Hierarchical Recurrence (GHR), a novel framework that operates jointly on the input graph and on a hierarchical abstraction obtained through pooling. We also show that the limitations of existing models are even more pronounced in out-of-range generalization, where test instances involve interactions over distances longer than those observed during training. By contrast, despite its simple design, GHR provides three key advantages: strong performance on long-range dependencies, improved out-of-range generalization, and high parameter efficiency. To corroborate these claims, we show that across a broad set of long-range benchmarks, GHR consistently outperforms existing graph models while using as little as 1% of the parameters of current state-of-the-art models. These results suggest a complementary direction to the current trend of scaling architectures to obtain graph foundation models, indicating that increased model capacity alone may not be sufficient for generalization.

</details>


### [191] [Spherical Harmonic Optimal Transport: Application to Climate Models Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18389)
*Pierre Houédry,Iskander Legheraba,Léo Buecher,Nicolas Courty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明热核代价在时间趋于零时收敛于最优传输代价（平衡与不平衡情形），并在球面上推导出快速Sinkhorn算法（O(n)内存，O(n^{3/2})时间/迭代），应用于全球气候模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输计算成本高昂，阻碍其实际应用；流形上基于热核的卷积算法虽有提出但理论性质尚不明确，亟需理论保证与高效算法。

Method: 理论分析证明热核代价收敛于最优传输代价；利用球面调和结构推导快速Sinkhorn算法（O(n)内存，O(n^{3/2})时间/迭代），支持全密集GPU操作。

Result: 在S^2上确保Sinkhorn散度保留经典最优传输的几何与分析性质；合成数据验证计算效率；在气候模型评估中提供空间与季节维度的性能洞察。

Conclusion: 热核方法在流形上为最优传输提供了理论保证的高效替代方案，球面快速算法有望成为气候模型评估等实际应用的实用工具。

Abstract: Optimal transport provides a powerful framework for comparing measures while respecting the geometry of their support, but comes with an expensive computational cost, hindering its potential application to real world use cases. On manifolds, convolutional algorithms based on the heat kernel have been proposed to alleviate this cost, but their theoretical properties remain largely unexplored. We establish that the heat kernel cost converges to the optimal transport cost as time vanishes in the balanced and unbalanced cases. In the specific case of the 2-sphere $\mathbb{S}^2$, we ensure that the associated Sinkhorn divergences retains the desirable geometric and analytic properties of classical optimal transport discrepancies. Moreover, we leverage the harmonic structure of the sphere to derive a fast Sinkhorn algorithm, requiring only $\mathcal{O}(n)$ memory and $\mathcal{O}(n^{3/2})$ time per iteration, with fully dense GPU-friendly operations. We validate its computational efficiency on synthetic data, and discuss its potential use in the evaluation of global climate models, providing both spatial and seasonal insights into models performances.

</details>


### [192] [Text2CAD-Bench: A Benchmark for LLM-based Text-to-Parametric CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18430)
*Liang Wang,Heng Meng,Zekai Xiang,Jin Liu,Pingyi Zhou,Litao Chen,Yongqiang Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个系统评估文本到CAD生成的基准Text2CAD-Bench，包含600个人工标注样本、4个复杂度级别（L1-L4）和双风格提示，发现当前模型在基础几何上表现尚可，但在复杂拓扑和高级特征上性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅关注基本图元和简单草图-拉伸序列，缺乏高级特征且仅覆盖传统机械零件，不足以评估真实场景下的文本到CAD生成能力。

Method: 构建包含600个人工标注示例的基准，按几何复杂度分为L1-L4四个级别（L1-L2基础几何，L3复杂拓扑与自由曲面，L4现实领域扩展），每个示例配有双风格提示（几何描述与程序化序列）。

Result: 主流通用大语言模型和领域专用模型在基础几何上表现合理，但在复杂拓扑和高级特征上性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: 当前文本到CAD生成模型在处理真实世界复杂几何方面仍有显著不足，需要进一步研究突破。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD generation aims to create parametric CAD models from natural language, enabling rapid prototyping and intuitive design workflows. However, existing benchmarks focus on basic primitives and simple sketch-extrude sequences, lacking advanced features essential for real-world applications and covering only traditional mechanical parts. We introduce Text2CAD-Bench, the first benchmark systematically evaluating text-to-CAD across geometric complexity and application diversity. Our benchmark comprises 600 human-curated examples spanning four levels: L1-L2 cover fundamental geometry with standard features, L3 introduces complex topology and freeform surfaces, and L4 extends to real-world domains beyond mechanical parts. Each example pairs dual-style prompts -- geometric descriptions mimicking non-expert users, and procedural sequences aligned with expert-level conventions. Evaluating mainstream general LLMs and domain-specific models, we find that current models perform reasonably on basic geometry but degrade substantially on complex topology and advanced features. We release our benchmark to drive progress in text-to-CAD research.

</details>


### [193] [Modelling Customer Trajectories with Reinforcement Learning for Practical Retail Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18449)
*Ken Ming Lee,Paul Barde,Maxime C. Cohen,Derek Nowrouzezahrai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于最大熵强化学习的智能体建模框架，用于预测零售空间内顾客的移动轨迹。相比传统的TSP和PNN启发式方法，RL生成的轨迹更接近真实顾客行为，能更准确估计冲动购买率和货架流量密度，并且基于RL预测的冲动产品 repositioning 决策与真实轨迹数据得出的决策一致，可实现相当的利润提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实顾客轨迹数据采集成本高昂，许多零售商难以获取；而现有的TSP和PNN等启发式近似方法虽然成本低，但与实际顾客轨迹偏差较大（平均偏离最短路径28%），存在准确性与实用性之间的权衡。

Method: 提出基于最大熵强化学习的智能体建模框架，将顾客轨迹预测建模为最大熵强化学习问题，在奖励最大化和随机性之间取得平衡，以更好地刻画有限理性的顾客行为。

Result: 使用便利店真实轨迹数据验证，RL生成的轨迹比TSP和PNN更接近真实顾客行为；RL能更准确估计冲动购买率和货架流量密度；只有RL的预测能够产生与真实轨迹数据一致的冲动产品 repositioning 决策，并获得相当的估计利润增益。

Conclusion: RL提供了一种实用、基于行为理论的替代方案，弥合了过度简化的启发式方法与数据密集型方法之间的差距，使准确的布局优化更加可及。源代码已在GitHub上公开。

Abstract: Understanding customer movement within retail spaces is essential for optimizing store layouts. Real-world trajectory data can provide highly accurate insights, but collecting it is costly and often infeasible for many retailers. Heuristics such as Travelling Salesman Problem (TSP) and Probabilistic Nearest Neighbours (PNN) are commonly used as inexpensive approximations, but actual customer trajectories deviate by an average of 28% from shortest paths, highlighting a tradeoff between accuracy and practicality. We propose an agent-based modelling framework that casts customer trajectory prediction as a maximum entropy reinforcement learning (RL) problem, balancing reward maximization with stochasticity to better reflect customers with bounded rationality. Using real-world trajectory data from a convenience store, we show that RL-generated trajectories align more closely with customer behaviour than TSP and PNN, providing more accurate estimates of impulse purchase rates and shelf traffic densities. Furthermore, only RL-based predictions yield repositioning decisions for impulse products that align with those derived from actual trajectory data, resulting in comparable estimated profit gains. Our work demonstrates that RL provides a practical, behaviourally grounded alternative that bridges the gap between oversimplified heuristics and data-intensive approaches, making accurate layout optimization more accessible. To encourage further research, the source code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [194] [Adaptive Experimentation for Censored Survival Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18459)
*Yuxin Wang,Dennis Frauen,Jonas Schweisthal,Maresa Schröder,Emil Javurek,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向右删失生存数据的自适应实验框架（ASE），通过推导半参数效率界和闭式效率最优分配策略，实现了因果效应的序贯估计，并在理论上保证了渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应实验方法无法处理生存数据中的删失问题（如癌症试验中因脱落导致的右删失），导致因果效应估计效率低下。

Method: 首先推导平均生存效应曲线的半参数效率界，获得闭式效率最优分配策略（推广了Neyman分配至生存场景）；然后提出Adaptive Survival Estimator (ASE)，序贯学习分配策略并估计效应，可兼容任意机器学习模型进行 nuisance 估计，利用鞅中心极限定理提供理论保证。

Result: 数值实验表明，ASE相比均匀随机化和忽略删失的基线方法，在效率上取得了一致的提升。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了删存数据自适应实验的空白，兼具理论严谨性（渐近正态性）和实践灵活性（任意ML模型），为生存数据因果推断提供了高效工具。

Abstract: Adaptive experimentation enables efficient estimation of causal effects, but existing methods are not designed for survival data with censoring, where event times are only partially observed (e.g., overall survival in cancer trials but with dropout). In this paper, we develop a novel framework for adaptive experimentation to estimate causal effects under right censoring. For this, we derive the semiparametric efficiency bound for the average survival effect curve as a function of the treatment allocation policy and thereby obtain a closed-form efficiency-optimal allocation policy. The policy generalizes classical Neyman allocation to survival settings by prioritizing patient strata where both event and censoring dynamics induce high uncertainty. Building on this, we propose the Adaptive Survival Estimator (ASE), an adaptive framework that learns the allocation policy and estimates the average survival effect curve sequentially. Our framework has three main benefits: (i) it accommodates arbitrary machine learning models for nuisance estimation; (ii) it is guided by a closed-form efficiency-optimal allocation policy; and (iii) it admits strong theoretical guarantees, including asymptotic normality via a martingale central limit theorem. We demonstrate our framework across various numerical experiments to show consistent efficiency gains over uniform randomization and censoring-agnostic baselines.

</details>


### [195] [Modality vs. Morphology: A Framework for Time Series Classification for Biological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18483)
*Jordan Tschida,Matthew Yohe,Edward Kane,Gavin Jager,Emma J. Reid,Tony G. Allen,Mark Story,Leanne Thompson,Joe Hoskins,Brandon Schreiber,Stan Seiferth,Scott Dolvin,David Cornett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的形态-模态框架，用于分析生物信号时间序列分类。通过分析EEG、EMG、ECG、PPG及眼动等多种模态，发现波形形态（而非模型类别）对模型性能和可解释性起决定性作用。未来工作包括形态学数据增强和评估指标改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物信号时间序列分类从手工特征方法发展到深度架构，但缺乏统一框架来理解波形形态如何影响模型设计。不同模态（EEG、EMG、ECG等）的信号形态各异，需要系统性的方法论指导。

Method: 提出形态-模态统一框架，将波形结构（尖峰、爆发、振荡、慢漂移、层次节律）与方法设计相关联。通过分析六种生物信号模态，展示形态如何决定预处理和建模策略，并解释深度模型在其归纳偏差与波形动力学对齐时表现优异的原因。

Result: 形态而非模型类别最强烈地决定模型性能和可解释性。深度模型的成功依赖于其归纳偏差与底层波形动力学的一致性。

Conclusion: 形态感知建模可作为开发通用、可解释且具有生理意义的时间序列分类模型的统一原则。未来需探索形态学数据增强和评估指标以提升泛化能力。

Abstract: Time series classification (TSC) of biological signals has progressed from handcrafted, modality-specific approaches to deep architectures capable of representing the diverse waveform structures of underlying physiological processes (i.e., morphology). This review introduces a unified morphology--modality framework that connects waveform structure to a methodological design, revealing how spikes, bursts, oscillations, slow drift, and hierarchical rhythms inform model design. By analyzing electroencephalography, electromyography, electrocardiography, photoplethysmography, and ocular modalities (electrooculography, pupillometry, eye-tracking), the review demonstrates how morphology determines preprocessing and modeling strategies. Integrating evidence across these biological signals, the framework reveals that morphology, not model class, most strongly determines performance and interpretability. This provides insight into why deep models succeed when their inductive biases align with underlying waveform dynamics. This review also identifies future work including morphological data augmentation and evaluation metrics to improve generalization. Together, these insights position morphology-aware modeling as a unifying principle for developing generalizable, interpretable, and physiologically meaningful TSC models across biological signals.

</details>


### [196] [DBES: A Systematic Benchmark and Metric Suite for Evaluating Expert Specialization in Large-Scale MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18498)
*Jing Wang,Hongxuan Lu,Jazze Young,Shu Wang,Zhimin Xin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DBES诊断框架，包含多领域基准和五个理论化指标，用于评估MoE模型的专家专业化程度。研究发现不同模型存在不同的专业化范式（Qwen系列模块化专业化，DeepSeek和GLM分布式协作）。通过DBES识别高专业化专家路径进行领域特定后训练，仅用15%的原始资源即可实现66%-94.48%的性能提升，证明这些诊断指标可转化为具体的优化操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型中的专家专业化机制尚未被充分理解，传统的评估方法混淆了架构层面的负载均衡与功能层面的专业化，缺乏独立于准确率指标的专家专业化系统性评估方法。

Method: 提出了DBES综合诊断框架，包含多领域基准和五种理论化指标：路由专业化（Routing Specialization）、归一化有效秩（Normalized Effective Rank）、领域隔离度（Domain Isolation）、路由刚度评分（Routing Stiffness Score）和N-gram专家度（N-gram Expertise）。

Result: Qwen系列模型表现出高领域隔离度的模块化专业化范式，DeepSeek和GLM采用分布式协作范式。通过DBES识别高专业化专家路径进行领域特定后训练，仅使用15%的原始训练资源，在专业领域实现了66%-94.48%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 专业化是MoE模型的一个诊断维度，对于下游性能是必要但不充分的条件。DBES框架提供了首个独立于准确率指标的专家专业化系统评估方法，可转化为具体的优化操作，为下一代MoE系统的设计和后训练优化提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Expert specialization in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models remains poorly understood, with traditional evaluations conflating architectural load-balancing with functional specialization. We introduce DBES, a comprehensive diagnostic framework combining a multi-domain benchmark with five theoretically grounded metrics: Routing Specialization, Normalized Effective Rank, Domain Isolation, Routing Stiffness Score, and N-gram Expertise measures.
  Critical findings demonstrate distinct specialization paradigms across models: Qwen-series exhibit modular specialization with high domain isolation, while DeepSeek and GLM employ distributed collaboration. However, we emphasize that specialization is a diagnostic dimension, necessary but not sufficient for downstream performance. Most crucially, interventional evidence validates the actionability of these metrics: by using DBES to identify high-specialization expert paths during domain-specific post-training, we achieved 66% to 94.48% improvement in specialized domains with only 15% of original training resources, demonstrating that these diagnostic tools can be converted into concrete optimization operators. This work provides the first systematic methodology for evaluating expert specialization independently of accuracy metrics, offering crucial insights for the design and post-training optimization of next-generation MoE systems.

</details>


### [197] [DiPRL: Learning Discrete Programmatic Policies via Architecture Entropy Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18508)
*Chengpeng Hu,Yingqian Zhang,Hendrik Baier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对程序化强化学习中连续松弛转离散时性能骤降的问题，提出DiPRL方法，通过架构熵正则化实现训练过程中近离散化，避免后处理离散化带来的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的PRL方法在将连续松弛的程序策略转换为离散程序时存在显著性能下降，后处理离散化会丢弃优化后的分支和参数，导致策略表达能力崩溃和任务性能降低，需要额外的微调步骤。

Method: 提出DiPRL方法，引入程序化架构熵正则化，使模型在可微训练过程中平滑地收敛到近离散的程序策略，避免单独的后处理离散化阶段。

Result: 在多个离散和连续RL任务上的实验表明，DiPRL能够通过可解释的程序化策略实现强性能。

Conclusion: DiPRL在保持梯度优化效率的同时，有效缓解了后处理离散化的风险，为程序化RL提供了更优的学习范式。

Abstract: Programmatic reinforcement learning (PRL) offers an interpretable alternative to deep reinforcement learning by representing policies as human-readable and -editable programs. While gradient-based methods have been developed to optimize continuous relaxations of programs, they face a significant performance drop when converting the continuous relaxations back into discrete programs. Post-hoc discretization can discard optimized branches and parameters in a program, which results in a collapse of policy expressivity and lowered task performance, leading in turn to a need for additional fine-tuning. To overcome these limitations, we propose Differentiable Discrete Programmatic Reinforcement Learning (DiPRL), a method that learns programmatic policies that become nearly discrete during training, avoiding a separate post-hoc fine-tuning stage. We first analyze the inherent risks of performance drop introduced by post-hoc discretization of gradient-based methods. Then, we introduce programmatic architecture entropy regularization, which enables smooth, differentiable training that encourages convergence toward a discrete program. DiPRL maintains the efficiency of gradient-based optimization while mitigating the risks of post-hoc discretization. Our experiments across multiple discrete and continuous RL tasks demonstrate that DiPRL can achieve strong performance via interpretable programmatic policies.

</details>


### [198] [Offline Contextual Bandits in the Presence of New Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18509)
*Ren Kishimoto,Tatsuhiro Shimizu,Kazuki Kawamura,Takanori Muroi,Yusuke Narita,Yuki Sasamoto,Kei Tateno,Takuma Udagawa,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PONA的离线策略学习方法，通过结合LCPI和DR估计器，解决了现有方法无法选择新动作（数据收集后引入的动作）的问题，在保持整体策略性能的同时有效选择新动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线策略学习方法虽然能有效从现有动作集中选择最优动作，但无法处理数据收集后新引入的动作（如新闻文章或视频内容），因为缺乏新动作的相关日志数据。

Method: 1) 提出局部组合伪逆（LCPI）估计器，用于策略梯度估计，泛化了最初为带状离线评估提出的伪逆估计器，控制奖励建模条件与数据收集条件之间的权衡；2) 提出PONA算法，将擅长新动作选择的LCPI与擅长现有动作学习的DR估计器进行加权组合，通过权重参数调节新动作选择比例。

Result: 实验表明，PONA在有效选择新动作的同时保持了整体策略性能，而大多数现有方法无法选择新动作。

Conclusion: PONA通过结合LCPI和DR估计器，成功解决了离线策略学习中面临动态演化动作空间时无法选择新动作的问题，实现了对新旧动作的联合优化。

Abstract: Automated decision-making algorithms drive applications such as recommendation systems and search engines. These algorithms often rely on off-policy contextual bandits or off-policy learning (OPL). Conventionally, OPL selects actions that maximize the expected reward from an existing action set. However, in many real-world scenarios, actions, such as news articles or video content, change continuously, and the action space evolves over time after data collection. We define actions introduced after deploying the logging policy as new actions and focus on OPL with new actions. Existing OPL methods identify optimal actions from the existing set effectively but cannot learn and select new actions because no relevant data are logged. To address this limitation, we propose a new OPL method that leverages action features. We first introduce the Local Combination PseudoInverse (LCPI) estimator for the policy gradient, generalizing the PseudoInverse estimator initially proposed for off-policy evaluation of slate bandits. LCPI controls the trade-off between reward-modeling condition and the condition for data collection regarding the action features, capturing the interaction effects among different dimensions of action features. Furthermore, we propose a generalized algorithm called Policy Optimization for Effective New Actions (PONA), which integrates LCPI, a component specialized for new action selection, with Doubly Robust (DR), which excels at learning within existing actions. We define PONA as a weighted sum of the LCPI and DR estimators, optimizing both the selection of existing and new actions, and allowing the proportion of new action selections to be adjusted by the weight parameter. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that PONA efficiently selects new actions while maintaining the overall policy performance as opposed to most existing methods that cannot select new actions.

</details>


### [199] [XCTFormer: Leveraging Cross-Channel and Cross-Time Dependencies for Enhanced Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18534)
*Israel Zexer,Omri Azencot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出XCTFormer，一种基于Transformer的通道依赖模型，通过增强的注意力机制显式捕获跨时间和跨通道的依赖关系，在时序预测、插补和异常检测任务中表现出色，尤其在插补任务上以MSE降低20.8%、MAE降低15.3%达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通道独立模型常优于通道依赖模型，表明后者未能充分利用变量间依赖关系；而现有通道依赖方法多采用间接建模策略，可能遗漏有意义的依赖关系。

Method: 提出XCTFormer，包含：(i)数据预处理模块，(ii)跨关系注意力块(CRAB)以增强容量和表达能力，(iii)可选的依赖压缩插件(DeCoP)提升可扩展性。模型以token-to-token方式显式建模时间和通道维度上每对token间的成对依赖关系。

Result: 在三个时序基准数据集上，XCTFormer达到强基线水平；在插补任务上取得SOTA，相比第二名方法，MSE降低20.8%，MAE降低15.3%。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨时间与跨通道依赖关系的Transformer架构能有效提升通道依赖模型的性能，尤其在插补任务上表现突出。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series analysis involves extracting informative representations from sequences of multiple interdependent variables, supporting tasks such as forecasting, imputation, and anomaly detection. In real-world scenarios, these variables are typically collected from a shared context or underlying phenomenon, suggesting the presence of latent dependencies across time and channels that can be leveraged to improve performance. However, recent findings show that channel-independent (CI) models, which assume no inter-variable dependencies, often outperform channel-dependent (CD) models that explicitly model such relationships. This surprising result indicates that current CD models may not fully exploit their potential due to limitations in how dependencies are captured. Recent studies have revisited channel dependence modeling with various approaches; however, these methods often employ indirect modeling strategies, which can lead to meaningful dependencies being overlooked. To address this issue, we introduce XCTFormer, a transformer-based channel-dependent (CD) model that explicitly captures cross-temporal and cross-channel dependencies via an enhanced attention mechanism. The model operates in a token-to-token fashion, modeling pairwise dependencies between every pair of tokens across time and channels. The architecture comprises (i) a data processing module, (ii) a novel Cross-Relational Attention Block (CRAB) that increases capacity and expressiveness, and (iii) an optional Dependency Compression Plugin (DeCoP) that improves scalability. Through extensive experiments on three time-series benchmarks, we show that XCTFormer achieves strong results compared to widely recognized baselines; in particular, it attains state-of-the-art performance on the imputation task, outperforming the second-best method by an average of 20.8% in MSE and 15.3% in MAE.

</details>


### [200] [Beyond Scaling: Agents Are Heading to the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18535)
*Chunlin Tian,Dongqi Cai,Wanru Zhao,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证个人智能体架构必须从云端迁移到边缘端，因为智能体任务的核心特性——与高保真本地上下文的耦合性以及零延迟执行需求——与云端设计不兼容，并提出了三个结构性转变来支持这一论点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体智能的瓶颈已从将世界知识压缩到单一模型转变为执行协调系统，而云端架构无法满足智能体任务对高保真本地上下文和零延迟执行的核心需求。

Method: 通过三个结构性转变来论证：1) 前额叶转向——能力杠杆已从预训练规模转向框架级执行控制，需靠近行动环境以保持认知对齐；2) 数据地理悖论——智能体关键数据（本地文件、实时传感器流、瞬态OS状态）在云传输过程中会退化或失去意义；3) 交互对齐循环——高保真隐式偏好信号（智能体精炼的唯一可持续数据源）只能通过实时本地交互产生。

Result: 提出了个人智能体下一部署周期的可验证预测。

Conclusion: 个人智能体架构必须向边缘端迁移，云端设计在认知对齐、数据真实性和可持续精炼方面存在根本性缺陷。

Abstract: The bottleneck of useful agentic intelligence has shifted from compressing world knowledge into a single model to executing a coordinated system. This position paper argues that personal-agent architecture must move to the edge because the core properties of agentic intelligence tasks, particularly their structural coupling with high-fidelity local context and the need for zero-latency execution loops, do not sit well with cloud-centric designs. We develop this claim through three structural shifts. First, the Prefrontal Turn: the main marginal lever of capability has moved from pre-training scale to framework-level executive control. Such control must remain physically close to the environment of action if the agent is to preserve cognitive alignment. Second, the Data-Geography Paradox, the ``dark matter'' of agentic data (local file hierarchies, real-time sensor streams, and transient OS states) degrades, disappears, or loses meaning once prepared for cloud transmission, thereby cutting the agent off from ground-truth context. Third, the interaction-alignment loop, the only economically and ecologically sustainable source of agentic refinement data is the high-fidelity implicit preference signal produced through real-time local interaction. Third, the interaction-alignment loop, the only economically and ecologically sustainable source of agentic refinement data is the high-fidelity implicit preference signal produced through real-time local interaction. We conclude with falsifiable predictions for the next deployment cycle of personal agents.

</details>


### [201] [Protein Fold Classification at Scale: Benchmarking and Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18552)
*Dexiong Chen,Andrei Manolache,Mathias Niepert,Karsten Borgwardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TEDBench——一个大规模、非冗余的蛋白质折叠分类基准，并提出了MiAE（掩码不变自编码器），一种基于高掩码率（90%）和SE(3)不变编码器的自监督蛋白质结构表示学习方法，在TEDBench上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质折叠分类缺乏大规模、无重复的基准数据集，且现有模型扩展性差。

Method: 构建TEDBench基准（来自TED数据库和Foldseek聚类后的AlphaFold结构）；提出MiAE框架，采用高达90%的掩码率、SE(3)不变编码器和轻量级解码器，从潜在表示和掩码令牌重建主链坐标。

Result: MiAE在TEDBench上优于有监督方法和现有最先进基线，且具有良好的扩展性；在CATH v4.4实验结构数据集上也验证了其迁移能力。

Conclusion: MiAE为蛋白质折叠分类提供了有效的自监督学习方案，TEDBench可作为未来研究的标准化评测基准。

Abstract: Classifying protein topology is essential for deciphering biological function, but progress is held back by the lack of large-scale benchmarks that avoid duplicates and by models that do not scale well. We introduce TEDBench, a large-scale, non-redundant benchmark for protein fold classification constructed from the Encyclopedia of Domains (TED) and Foldseek-clustered AlphaFold structures. We show that on TEDBench, current protein representation learning methods either require very large models or fail to deliver strong performance. To address this challenge, we propose Masked Invariant Autoencoders (MiAE), a self-supervised framework for protein structure representation learning. MiAE uses an extremely high masking ratio of up to 90% with an $\mathrm{SE(3)}$-invariant encoder and a lightweight decoder that reconstructs backbone coordinates from the latent representation and mask tokens. MiAE scales well and outperforms supervised counterparts and state-of-the-art baselines on TEDBench, establishing a strong recipe for protein fold classification. To test transfer beyond AlphaFold structures, we further benchmark on a curated dataset from experimental structures of CATH v4.4. TEDBench is available at https://github.com/BorgwardtLab/TEDBench.

</details>


### [202] [scHelix: Asymmetric Dual-Stream Integration via Explicit Gene-Level Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18576)
*Xichen Yan,Zelin Zang,Changxi Chi,Jingbo Zhou,Chang Yu,Jinlin Wu,Shenghui Cheng,Fuji Yang,Jiebo Luo,Zhen Lei,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: scHelix是一个数据集自适应框架，通过将基因显式分为域不变Anchor和域敏感Variant，利用双流稀疏扩散编码器和非对称对齐-精炼-融合协议，在消除批次效应的同时保持生物学保真度，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scRNA-seq整合中批次效应在不同基因上表现异质，现有方法均匀处理整个转录组，导致过度校正和生物信号丢失。

Method: 将基因分为Anchor和Variant两类，采用双流稀疏扩散编码器配合停止梯度图缓存学习多尺度结构表示，核心是非对称对齐-精炼-融合协议：先对齐不稳定Variant流到稳定的Anchor流拓扑，再通过边界残差门控让Anchor流吸收去噪细节。

Result: 在广泛基准测试中优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: 分治架构防止快捷学习，确保稳健的批次去除而不损害生物簇的完整性。

Abstract: A critical challenge in single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) integration is resolving the tension between eliminating batch effects and maintaining biological fidelity. While recent evidence indicates that batch effects manifest heterogeneously across genes, most existing methods process the transcriptome uniformly, frequently resulting in over-correction and loss of subtle biological signals. To address this, we present scHelix, a dataset-adaptive framework that fundamentally changes how features are processed by explicitly partitioning genes into domain-invariant Anchors and domain-sensitive Variants at the input level. scHelix utilizes a dual-stream sparse diffusion encoder equipped with stop-gradient graph caching to efficiently learn multi-scale structural representations. The core of our approach is a novel asymmetric Align-Refine-Fuse protocol: the unstable Variant stream is first aligned to the robust topology of the Anchor stream, followed by a conservative refinement phase where the Anchor stream absorbs denoised details via bounded residual gating. This divide-and-conquer architecture prevents shortcut learning and ensures robust batch removal without compromising the integrity of biological clusters. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that scHelix outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [203] [S2Aligner: Pair-Efficient and Transferable Pre-Training for Sparse Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18579)
*Yuhan Wang,Haopeng Zhang,Yibo Ding,Jiaqi Yu,Xinyu Zhao,Yuhang Liu,Ziwei Zhang,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S2Aligner框架，通过解耦语义对齐与结构建模、引入结构导向重建和稀疏感知跨域风险平衡，解决了稀疏文本属性图上图-文预训练中结构-语义对应不可靠和跨域迁移偏差问题，实验表明该方法在多种稀疏场景下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-Aligner方法假设节点文本提供充分可靠的监督信号，但在真实稀疏TAGs中，文本锚点缺失、含噪或跨域分布不均，导致图结构只能与弱语义证据对齐，产生不可靠的结构-语义对应关系和稀疏诱导的迁移偏差。

Method: S2Aligner将图-文表示解耦为语义和结构分量，通过结构导向重建与一致性控制将可靠拓扑信号注入文本表示，并在文本稀疏时抑制不一致结构信号；同时引入稀疏感知跨域风险平衡，利用全局域密度比校准域风险，并通过图可靠性估计降低不可靠稀疏样本的权重。

Result: 在多种图域、稀疏程度和下游任务上的大量实验表明，S2Aligner一致优于现有基线方法。理论分析证明其目标函数通过控制域风险差异来减少跨域泛化差距。

Conclusion: S2Aligner通过解耦语义与结构、抑制稀疏噪声并平衡跨域风险，有效提升了稀疏TAGs上图-文预训练的鲁棒性和迁移能力。

Abstract: Pre-training on text-attributed graphs (TAGs) is central to building transferable graph foundation models, where LLM-as-Aligner methods align graph and text representations through the semantic knowledge of large language models. However, these methods usually assume that node texts provide sufficient and reliable supervision, an assumption often violated in real-world sparse TAGs. When textual anchors are missing, noisy, or uneven across domains, graph structures must be aligned with weak semantic evidence, leading to unreliable structure-semantics correspondence and sparsity-induced transfer bias. This paper presents S2Aligner, a sparsity-aware and structure-enhanced LLM-as-Aligner framework for graph-text pre-training on sparse TAGs. The key idea is to decouple semantic alignment from structural modeling, allowing topology-aware signals to enhance alignment without contaminating the shared semantic space. Specifically, S2Aligner decomposes graph-text representations into semantic and structural components, uses structure-oriented reconstruction with consistency control to inject reliable topology cues into text representations, and suppresses inconsistent structural signals under textual sparsity. Moreover, S2Aligner introduces sparsity-aware cross-domain risk balancing, which calibrates domain risks through a global-domain density ratio and downweights unreliable sparse samples via graph reliability estimation. Theoretical analysis shows that this objective reduces cross-domain generalization gaps by controlling domain risk discrepancy. Extensive experiments across diverse graph domains, sparsity levels, and downstream tasks demonstrate that S2Aligner consistently outperforms existing baselines.

</details>


### [204] [Randomized Advantage Transformation (RAT): Computing Natural Policy Gradients via Direct Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18591)
*Mingfei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机优势变换（RAT），通过随机块Kaczmarz迭代高效计算Tikhonov正则化的自然策略梯度，无需显式构建Fisher矩阵或使用共轭梯度求解器，在连续和视觉控制基准测试中达到或超越现有自然梯度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然策略梯度通过考虑分布空间的几何结构改进了优化效果，但估计和求逆Fisher矩阵的高计算成本限制了其实际应用。

Method: 利用Woodbury公式将正则化自然策略梯度重写为带变换优势的vanilla策略梯度，并通过随机块Kaczmarz迭代在on-policy小批量上高效计算该变换，避免了显式构造Fisher矩阵、共轭梯度求解器和特定架构近似。

Result: 在连续控制和视觉控制基准测试中，RAT匹配或超越了已有自然梯度方法，同时实现简单且兼容多种架构。

Conclusion: RAT提供了一种高效、易实现且具有收敛保证的自然策略梯度估计方法，克服了传统自然策略梯度的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Natural policy gradients improve optimization by accounting for the geometry of distribution space, but their practical use is limited by the cost of estimating and inverting the Fisher matrix. We present Randomized Advantage Transformation (RAT), a method for estimating Tikhonov-regularized natural policy gradients via direct backpropagation. By applying the Woodbury formula, we reformulate the regularized natural policy gradients as vanilla policy gradients with a transformed advantage. RAT computes this transformation efficiently via randomized block Kaczmarz iterations on on-policy mini-batches, avoiding explicit Fisher construction, conjugate-gradient solvers, and architecture-specific approximations. We provide convergence guarantees for RAT and demonstrate empirically that it matches or exceeds established natural-gradient methods across continuous and visual control benchmarks, while remaining simple to implement and compatible with various architectures.

</details>


### [205] [AMARIS: A Memory-Augmented Rubric Improvement System for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18592)
*Peilin Wu,Xinlu Zhang,Kun Wan,Wentian Zhao,Gang Wu,Xinya Du,Zhiyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMARIS是一种基于持久化评估记忆的自适应奖励塑形方法，通过长期训练历史来修改评估标准（rubrics），在强化学习微调LLMs中持续积累和复用评估知识，相比基线方法在封闭和开放领域均取得更优表现，且异步执行仅增加约5%时间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于rubric的奖励塑形方法仅利用局部信号（当前步的rollouts或成对比较）即时调整评估标准，丢弃了评估过程中产生的诊断信息，无法长期积累和策略性复用评估知识，限制了系统检测重复出现的次优行为的能力，也丧失了训练历史自然支撑的课程式进展。

Method: AMARIS在每个训练步中分析个体rollouts，将发现聚合为步级摘要，通过静态（最近步骤）和动态（语义匹配）两种检索方式从持久化评估记忆中检索相关历史上下文，基于这些累积分析更新rubrics。该过程异步运行于常规RL循环之外。

Result: 在封闭和开放领域实验中，AMARIS持续优于基线方法。消融实验表明：静态和动态记忆检索均有贡献，二者组合效果最强；适度的检索预算即可获得大部分收益；异步执行仅增加约5%时间开销。

Conclusion: 持久化评估记忆能够将基于rubric的奖励塑形从一个无状态的、每步启发式方法转变为基于证据的闭环RL训练流程。

Abstract: Rubric-based reward shaping is an effective method for fine-tuning LLMs via RL, where structured rubrics decompose standard outcome rewards into multiple dimensions to provide richer reward signals. Recent works make the rubrics adaptive based on local signals such as the rollouts from the current step or pairwise comparisons. However, these methods discard the diagnostics produced during evaluation after immediate use and prevent the long-term accumulation and strategic reuse of evaluation knowledge. This forces the system to re-derive evaluation principles from scratch, limits its ability to detect recurring suboptimal behaviors, and forfeits the curriculum-like progression that a persistent training history would naturally support. To address these limitations, we introduce AMARIS, which grounds rubric modifications in long-term training history. At each training step, AMARIS analyzes individual rollouts, aggregates findings into step-level summaries, retrieves relevant historical context from a persistent evaluation memory through both static (recent steps) and dynamic (semantically matched) retrieval, and updates rubrics based on these accumulated analyses. This procedure runs asynchronously alongside the normal RL loop with minimal overhead. Experiments across both closed and open-ended domains show that AMARIS consistently outperforms the baselines. Ablation studies show that static and dynamic memory retrieval contributes to the performance gain and their combination provides the strongest results with moderate retrieval budgets sufficient to provide most of the gain, and that the entire pipeline adds only ~5\% time overhead through asynchronous execution. These results show that persistent evaluation memory can transform rubric-based reward shaping from a stateless, per-step heuristic into an evidence-driven loop for RL training.

</details>


### [206] [Physics-Aligned Canonical Equivariant Fourier Neural Operator under Symmetry-Induced Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18606)
*Jiaxiao Xu,Changhong Mou,Yeyu Zhang,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE-FNO，通过Lie代数坐标估计器将输入场对齐到参考坐标系，利用FNO进行预测，再恢复回目标坐标系，从而分离坐标对齐与物理演化，在周期性域上的Burgers、浅水和Navier-Stokes方程中，相比标准FNO+增强，OOD相对误差最高降低12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子逼近PDE解映射时，不保证尊重控制方程的对称性，在OOD场景下，标准神经算子需在同一映射中同时学习坐标对齐和物理演化，导致泛化能力下降。

Method: 基于周期性域上已知的连续对称性，设计Lie代数坐标估计器估计输入帧，将场映射到参考帧，应用标准FNO预测，再恢复回目标帧；使用有界对称扰动联合训练对齐与算子预测；可选的低维推理时精化步骤更新估计帧。

Result: 在1D/2D Burgers、浅水和Navier-Stokes方程上，PACE-FNO的ID精度与标准神经算子相当，OOD相对误差在平移和Galilean变换下比FNO+Aug降低最高12倍，旋转-平移耦合变换下增益较小；消融实验表明输入对齐和输出恢复贡献了大部分OOD增益。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离坐标对齐与物理演化，PACE-FNO在保持ID精度的同时显著提升OOD泛化能力，且无需修改FNO架构本身。

Abstract: Neural operators approximate PDE solution maps, but they need not respect the symmetries of the governing equation. In out-of-distribution (OOD) regimes, a standard neural operator must often learn coordinate alignment and physical evolution within a single map, which can hurt generalization. We use known continuous symmetries of evolution equations on periodic domains to separate these two roles. We propose the Physics-Aligned Canonical Equivariant Fourier Neural Operator (PACE-FNO), which estimates the input frame with a Lie-algebra coordinate estimator, maps the field to a reference frame, applies a standard Fourier Neural Operator (FNO), and restores the prediction to the target frame. We train alignment and operator prediction jointly using bounded symmetry perturbations, with an optional low-dimensional refinement step that updates the estimated frame at inference. Equivariance is enforced by the input and output transformations, while the FNO architecture remains unchanged. Across 1-D and 2-D Burgers, shallow-water, and Navier-Stokes equations on periodic domains, PACE-FNO matches the in-distribution (ID) accuracy of standard neural operators and reduces out-of-distribution (OOD) relative error by up to 12x over FNO with symmetry augmentation (FNO+Aug) under translations and Galilean shifts, with smaller gains for coupled rotation-translation shifts. Ablations show that aligning the input and restoring the output frame account for most OOD gains; inference-time refinement provides a smaller correction.

</details>


### [207] [Perfect Parallelization in Mini-Batch SGD with Classical Momentum Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18609)
*Sachin Garg,Michał Dereziński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在插值区间（二次型优化问题）上建立了随机动量加速（包括Polyak重球和Nesterov动量）的通用理论，证明了动量加速效果与梯度小批量大小成正比（直到自然饱和点），从而实现了小批量计算的完美并行化，并为动量参数提供了简单有效的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典动量方法（如Polyak重球）在结合大规模小批量计算时效果显著，但其在随机小批量优化中的理论理解一直不足，现有工作需要强噪声假设和极大批量，缺乏通用理论。

Method: 在二次型优化问题的插值区间内建立通用理论框架，涵盖重球和Nesterov动量，允许任意小批量大小，且对随机噪声假设极弱。

Result: 动量加速效果与梯度小批量大小成正比（至饱和点），可实现小批量计算的完美并行化；理论给出了动量参数的简单选择，且实验验证有效。

Conclusion: 该理论填补了随机动量加速的理论空白，为大规模深度学习训练中的动量方法选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Accelerating stochastic gradient methods with classical momentum schemes, such as Polyak's heavy ball, has proven highly successful in training large-scale machine learning models, particularly when combined with the hardware acceleration of large mini-batch computations. Yet, the effect of classical momentum on stochastic mini-batch optimization has been poorly understood theoretically, with prior works requiring strong noise assumptions and extremely large mini-batches. In this work, we develop a general theory of stochastic momentum acceleration for optimizing over quadratics in the interpolation regime, a popular abstraction for studying deep learning dynamics which also includes classical methods such as randomized Kaczmarz and coordinate descent. Our framework encompasses both heavy ball and Nesterov-style momentum, allows for arbitrary mini-batch sizes, and makes minimal assumptions on the stochastic noise. In particular, we show that acceleration from classical momentum is directly proportional to the gradient mini-batch size (up to a natural saturation point), thereby enabling perfect parallelization of mini-batch computations. Our theory also provides a simple choice for the momentum parameter, which is shown to be effective empirically.

</details>


### [208] [Aligned Training: A Parameter-Free Method to Improve Feature Quality and Stability of Sparse Autoencoders (SAE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18629)
*Michał Brzozowski,Neo Christopher Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对齐训练（aligned training），一种无需额外参数的重参数化方法，通过强制编码器与解码器方向内积为1的几何约束，同时消除稀疏自编码器（SAE）中的死亡特征、提升重建质量与训练稳定性，并在多个架构和字典规模上实现帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE存在大量特征从未激活（死亡特征）且训练不稳定的问题，尽管有改进变体，但需要额外数据、重采样或训练成本。作者观察到编码器与解码器方向间的内积（对齐分数）呈双峰分布这一被忽视的现象，并以此为动机提出对齐训练。

Method: 提出对齐训练（aligned training），一种无参数的重参数化方法。核心思想是强制每个特征的编码器与解码器方向之间的内积等于1，从而施加几何约束，消除SAE训练中的退化来源，且不引入任何超参数。

Result: 在多个模型、字典规模和稀疏度下，对齐训练在SAEBench基准上实现帕累托改进：同时改善了重建质量、消除了死亡特征、显著提升了跨训练种子的稳定性。该方法还兼容Top/BatchTop-K架构和p-Annealing等技术。

Conclusion: 对齐训练在无需额外计算复杂度或成本的前提下，显著提升SAE的特征质量和训练稳定性，是一种实用且高效的改进方案。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are one of the main methods to interpret the inner workings of deep neural networks (DNNs), decomposing activations into higher-dimensional features. However, they exhibit critical shortcomings where a large fraction of features are never activated and are unstable. Despite variants of SAEs that attempt to mitigate these issues, they require additional data, resampling, or training. We propose the \textbf{aligned training}, a parameter-free reparameterization of SAEs that simultaneously improves reconstruction quality, eliminates dead features, and significantly enhances stability across training seeds. Our approach is motivated by an overlooked observation that SAE feature quality, measured by the inner product between encoder and decoder directions (which we call the \textbf{alignment score}), follows a bimodal distribution across all modern architectures. The proposed aligned training enforces a geometric constraint between the encoder and decoder such that their inner product equals one for every feature, which removes a source of degeneracy in the SAE training without adding any hyperparameters. Across multiple models, dictionary sizes, and sparsity levels, the aligned training shows Pareto improvements on the SAEBench benchmarks. Beyond improving dead features, stability and reconstruction, our method readily integrates with techniques in mechanical interpretability such as Top/BatchTop-K architectures and p-Annealing. Overall, the aligned training substantially improves feature quality and stability of SAE without computational complexity or cost.

</details>


### [209] [Data Presentation Over Architecture: Resampling Strategies for Credit Risk Prediction with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18635)
*Aditya Tanna,Mitul Solanki,Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Pratinav Seth,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 信用违约预测中，上下文构建策略（平衡/混合采样）比模型架构选择对表格基础模型（TFM）性能影响更大，在类不平衡数据上可提升3-4个AUC点，且5K-10K平衡上下文下TFM能达到全数据训练的传统模型效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用违约预测面临类别严重不平衡、特征异质性和低延迟要求，表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文学习处理该问题，但其预测对上下文窗口构建方式高度敏感，因此需要系统研究上下文构建策略对TFM性能的影响。

Method: 在Home Credit和Lending Club数据集上，对四种经典模型和五种TFM进行基准测试，变化上下文构建策略（七种选项）和上下文大小（1K至50K），通过AUC-ROC等指标评估不同策略组合的效果。

Result: 上下文策略的选择解释的AUC-ROC方差大于TFM家族的选择；平衡和混合采样比均匀采样高出3-4个AUC点；5K-10K平衡上下文中最强TFM达到全数据训练的传统基线模型AUC水平，同时恢复有意义的违约类召回率。

Conclusion: 在不平衡信用风险场景中，上下文构建而非架构选择是TFM的主要部署杠杆。

Abstract: Credit default prediction is a tabular learning problem with severe class imbalance, heterogeneous features, and tight latency budgets. Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) approach this problem through in-context learning, which makes their predictions sensitive to how the context window is built. We benchmark four classical models and five TFMs on the Home Credit and Lending Club datasets, varying the context-construction strategy (seven options) and the context size (1K to 50K). On both datasets, the choice of context strategy explains more variance in AUC-ROC than the choice of TFM family: balanced and hybrid sampling add 3 to 4 AUC points over uniform sampling, and the gap exceeds the spread between TFMs. With a balanced context of 5K to 10K examples, the strongest TFMs reach the AUC of classical baselines trained on the full data, while also recovering meaningful default-class recall that default-threshold GBDTs do not. We frame this as evidence that context construction, rather than architecture choice, is the primary deployment lever for TFMs in imbalanced credit-risk settings.

</details>


### [210] [Post-Trained MoE Can Skip Half Experts via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18643)
*Xingtai Lv,Li Sheng,Kaiyan Zhang,Yichen You,Siyan Gao,Xueheng Luo,Yuxin Zuo,Yuchen Fan,Junlin Yang,Ganqu Cui,Bingning Wang,Fan Yang,Youbang Sun,Ning Ding,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ZEDA框架，通过注入零输出专家和两阶段自蒸馏，将预训练的静态MoE模型高效转换为动态MoE，在Qwen3-30B-A3B和GLM-4.7-Flash上消除50%以上的专家计算量，仅损失微小精度，并实现约1.20倍端到端推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态MoE方法通常需要从头预训练或任务特定适配，缺乏将已充分训练的静态MoE模型转换为动态模型的实际方案。实现这种转换可以直接降低推理成本，让简单token跳过不必要的专家计算。

Method: ZEDA框架包含：(1) 在每个MoE层注入无参数零输出专家；(2) 通过两阶段自蒸馏适配增强后的模型，使用原始MoE作为冻结教师；(3) 应用组级平衡损失来稳定架构转换。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B和GLM-4.7-Flash两个模型上，覆盖数学、代码和指令遵循等11个基准测试，ZEDA消除了超过50%的专家FLOPs且精度损失微小，比最强动态MoE基线分别高出6.1和4.0个点，并实现约1.20倍端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: ZEDA是一种低成本、高效的框架，能够将后训练的静态MoE模型转换为动态MoE，显著降低推理计算量，在大幅压缩专家计算的同时保持模型性能，为实际部署MoE模型提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales language models efficiently through sparse expert activation, and its dynamic variant further reduces computation by adjusting the activated experts in an input-dependent manner. Existing dynamic MoE methods usually rely on pre-training from scratch or task-specific adaptation, leaving the practical conversion of fully trained MoE underexplored. Enabling such adaptation would directly alleviate the inference costs by allowing easy tokens to bypass unnecessary expert during serving. This paper introduces Zero-Expert Self-Distillation Adaptation (ZEDA), a low-cost framework that transforms post-trained static MoE models into efficient dynamic ones. To stabilize this architectural conversion, ZEDA injects parameter-free zero-output experts into each MoE layer and adapts the augmented model through two-stage self-distillation, utilizing the original MoE as a frozen teacher and applying a group-level balancing loss. On Qwen3-30B-A3B and GLM-4.7-Flash across 11 benchmarks spanning math, code, and instruction following, ZEDA eliminates over 50% of expert FLOPs at marginal accuracy loss. It outperforms the strongest dynamic MoE baseline by 6.1 and 4.0 points on the two models, and delivers ~1.20$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup.

</details>


### [211] [An Assessment of Human vs. Model Uncertainty in Soft-Label Learning and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18648)
*Maja Pavlovic,Silviu Paun,Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验，解耦了人类软标签与标签模态偏移的影响，发现人类软标签的主要价值不在于提升准确率，而在于作为正则化器改善模型校准性和训练稳定性，并使模型的不确定性模式与人类对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将人类软标签的益处与误标签数据修正（模态偏移）混为一谈，无法区分人类软标签的真实效果。本文旨在通过受控实验，将软标签监督与底层标签模态偏移解耦，独立评估人类软标签对模型校准、收敛稳定性和不确定性对齐的影响。

Method: 在MNIST及其合成变体上进行受控审计，重新标注子集以提取人类不确定性。通过解耦软标签监督与标签模态偏移，对比人类软标签与合成标签的训练效果，并利用数据集制图（Dataset Cartography）分析模型不确定性模式。

Result: 人类软标签确实带来准确率提升，但其更大价值在于：1) 作为正则化器改善困难样本上的模型校准性；2) 促进跨训练轮次的稳定收敛。数据集制图显示，基于人类软标签训练的模型能够反映人类不确定性，而基于合成标签训练的模型则无法与人类对齐。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个用于诊断人-AI不确定性对齐的测试框架，表明人类软标签的核心优势在于不确定性对齐和正则化效果，而非简单的准确率提升。

Abstract: Central to human-aligned AI is understanding the benefits of human-elicited labels over synthetic alternatives. While human soft-labels improve calibration by capturing uncertainty, prior studies conflate these benefits with the implicit correction of mislabeled data (mode shifts), obscuring true effects of soft-labels. We present a controlled audit of soft-label learning across MNIST and a synthetic variant, re-annotating subsets to extract human uncertainty. By decoupling soft-label supervision from underlying label mode shifts, we show that while human soft-labels do provide accuracy gains, their larger value lies in acting as a regularizer that improves model calibration on difficult samples and promotes stable convergence across training runs. Dataset cartography reveals models trained on human soft-labels mirror human uncertainty, whereas those trained on synthetic labels fail to align with humans. Broadly, this work provides a diagnostic testbed for human-AI uncertainty alignment.

</details>


### [212] [Pocket Foundation Models: Distilling TFMs into CPU-Ready Gradient-Boosted Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18654)
*Aditya Tanna,Nassim Bouarour,Mohamed Bouadi,Vinay kumar Sankarapu,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将TabICLv2等表格基础模型（TFM）蒸馏为XGBoost/CatBoost学生模型，通过分层OOF教师标注解决ICL教师标签泄露问题，在153个数据集上实现0.882 AUC（教师96.5%）且推理仅需1.9ms（CPU），相比GPU教师加速38-860倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）虽性能优异但推理速度极慢（151-1275ms on GPU），无法满足欺诈评分等亚毫秒级实时应用需求。本文旨在通过知识蒸馏将TFM压缩为可运行在CPU上的轻量级树模型，同时解决ICL教师特有的标签泄露导致软目标退化的问题。

Method: 1) 采用分层OOF（out-of-fold）教师标注策略替代传统自标注，避免ICL教师泄露训练集标签导致软目标退化为近独热向量；2) 将TabICLv2蒸馏为XGBoost/CatBoost学生模型，运行于CPU；3) 在153个分类数据集上系统评估，涵盖TALENT、OpenML-CC18、TabZilla、TabArena。

Result: 蒸馏XGBoost达到0.882 macro-mean AUC（教师96.5%），推理仅1.9ms on CPU，相比GPU教师加速38-860倍；Wilcoxon检验p=0.0008显著优于调优CatBoost基线（胜率51%）。四项发现：教师排名完整传递给学生；增益集中在低维数据（<21特征+0.011 vs >21特征+0.001）；多教师平均对MLP学生有效（+0.006，p=0.003）但对树学生无效；高维任务上教师本身不如CatBoost时蒸馏反而有害。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏是弥合表格基础模型与轻量级树模型之间推理速度差距的有效手段，关键在于解决ICL教师的标签泄露问题。该方法已在TabTune库中开源。

Abstract: A fraud scorer needs to answer in under 2 ms. The best tabular foundation models (TFMs) take 151-1,275 ms on GPU. We close this gap by distilling the TFM offline into an XGBoost or CatBoost student that runs natively on CPU. The central obstacle is specific to in-context learning (ICL) teachers: they leak labels when scoring their own training set, so the soft targets collapse to near-one-hot vectors with no inter-class structure left to distill. Stratified out-of-fold (OOF) teacher labeling prevents this. Across 153 classification datasets drawn from TALENT, OpenML-CC18, TabZilla, and TabArena, distilling TabICLv2 into XGBoost gives 0.882 macro-mean AUC (96.5% of teacher AUC) at 1.9 ms on CPU, a 38x to 860x speedup across teacher-student pairs with a statistically significant edge over a tuned CatBoost baseline (Wilcoxon p = 0.0008; 51% win rate). Four further findings: teacher rank transfers exactly to student rank; gains concentrate on low-dimensional data (< 21 features: +0.011 over CatBoost vs. >21 features: +0.001); multi-teacher averaging helps MLP students (+0.006, p = 0.003) but adds less than 0.001 for tree students; and on high-dimensional tasks where the teacher itself trails CatBoost, distillation makes things worse rather than better. The full pipeline is open-sourced as part of the TabTune library.

</details>


### [213] [Efficient and Noise-Tolerant PAC Learning of Multiclass Linear Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18662)
*Rita Adhikari,Shiwei Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算高效的PAC学习算法，能够在恶意噪声（nasty noise）下，以O(k²·(d log d + log k))的样本复杂度学习多类线性分类器，即使在常数级噪声率下依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管二分类情况下已有多种计算高效的噪声容忍PAC学习算法，但在多类学习（k≥3）且数据被恶意破坏的情况下，是否存在计算高效的PAC学习算法仍是未知的。

Method: 算法包含两个核心组件：基于聚类的剪枝方案（cluster-based pruning scheme）和标准的多类铰链损失最小化程序（multiclass hinge loss minimization program）。

Result: 在边缘分布为有界方差分布的混合且数据集满足边际条件的情况下，算法仅需O(k²·(d log d + log k))个样本即可实现PAC学习，即使存在常数率的nasty噪声。在二分类特例（k=2）下，该结果严格优于所有先前工作。

Conclusion: 本文首次证明了在多类线性分类器恶意噪声场景下存在计算高效的PAC学习算法，且样本复杂度优于以往结果。

Abstract: Noise-tolerant PAC learning of linear models has been of central interests in machine learning community since the last century. In recent years, many computationally-efficient algorithms have been proposed for the problem of learning linear threshold functions under multiple noise models. Yet, when the problem is considered under multiclass learning settings, i.e. when the number of classes $k$ is at least $3$, it is unknown whether there exist computationally-efficient PAC learning algorithms when the data sets are maliciously corrupted. In this paper, we consider that the marginal distribution is a mixture of bounded variance distributions and the data sets satisfy a margin condition at the same time. We show that there exists a computationally-efficient algorithm that PAC learns multiclass linear classifiers $\{h_w:x\mapsto \arg\max_{y\in[k]}w_y\cdot x, x\in \mathbb{R}^d, w\in\mathbb{R}^{kd}\}$ using at most $O(k^2\cdot (d\log d+\log k))$ samples even under a constant rate of nasty noise. Our algorithm consists of two main ingredients: a cluster-based pruning scheme and a standard multiclass hinge loss minimization program. Even in the special case of binary setting, i.e. $k=2$, our result is strictly stronger than all prior works.

</details>


### [214] [COOPO: Cyclic Offline-Online Policy Optimization Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18675)
*Qisai Liu,Zhanhong Jiang,Joshua Russell Waite,Aditya Balu,Cody Fleming,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COOPO提出了一种循环离线-在线策略优化框架，通过交替进行KL正则化的离线训练和在线微调，解决了离线转在线强化学习中的分布漂移和灾难性遗忘问题，在减少在线交互的同时提升了最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RL受限于静态数据集导致分布偏移和性能受限，在线RL需要大量环境交互。现有离线转在线混合方法存在过渡阶段的分布漂移和灾难性遗忘问题，亟需一种既能有效利用离线数据又能稳定进行在线学习的统一框架。

Method: COOPO采用循环机制：(1) 离线阶段：通过KL正则化的优势加权更新锚定策略到数据集，最小化分布偏移；(2) 在线阶段：使用任意策略优化方法进行稳定探索；(3) 周期性切换回离线训练以消除遗忘和漂移，最大化数据集复用。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试上，COOPO相比最先进的混合方法减少了在线交互次数，同时提高了最终回报；对不同离线算法和在线优化器均保持鲁棒性；在标准覆盖假设下实现了单调性能改进。

Conclusion: COOPO通过离线-在线循环协同机制，为自适应RL设立了新的效率和性能标准，理论上证明了超越纯在线RL的样本效率，实践上验证了其通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning struggles with distributional shift and constrained performance due to static dataset limitations, while online RL demands prohibitive environment interactions. The recent advent of hybrid offline-to-online methods bridges these domains but suffers from distribution drift during transitions and catastrophic forgetting of offline knowledge. We introduce COOPO (Cyclic Offline-Online Policy Optimization), a generalized framework that repeatedly cycles between constrained offline training and online fine-tuning. Each cycle first anchors the policy to the dataset via KL-regularized advantage-weighted offline updates to minimize distributional shift and then fine-tunes it online using any policy optimization for stable exploration. Crucially, periodically returning to offline training eliminates forgetting and drift while maximizing dataset reuse. The cyclic behavior also helps reduce the online environment interactions. Theoretically, COOPO achieves better online sample efficiency, surpassing pure online RL, with guaranteed monotonic improvement under standard coverage assumptions. Extensive D4RL benchmarks demonstrate COOPO reduces online interactions versus state-of-the-art hybrids while improving final returns, maintaining robustness across diverse offline algorithms and online optimizers. This looped synergy sets new efficiency and performance standards for adaptive RL.

</details>


### [215] [Ensembling Tabular Foundation Models - A Diversity Ceiling And A Calibration Trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18696)
*Aditya Tanna,Yash Desai,Pratinav Seth,Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 六种现代表格基础模型(TFM)集成的效果远低于预期：它们几乎冗余（平均Q统计量0.961），最佳集成策略（两级级联堆叠）仅比最强单模型提升+0.18%准确率，但计算成本增加253倍。统计检验表明多个集成与最优单模型无显著差异，而逻辑回归堆叠虽准确率尚可但校准极差。推荐贪心选择作为实用默认方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型(TFM)在不同数据集上表现各异，没有单一模型在所有任务上最优。集成是通用的补救方法，但其在TFM上的实际效果尚未被系统评估。

Method: 计算六种现代TFM之间的成对Q统计量（平均0.961，接近1）。在153个OpenML分类任务上，对六种TFM评估六种集成策略（包括级联堆叠、逻辑回归堆叠等），并进行Friedman和Nemenyi统计检验。

Result: 最佳集成（两级级联堆叠）仅比最强单TFM提升+0.18%准确率，但计算成本增加253倍。统计检验显示三种集成与最佳单TFM属于同一等价组，另三种集成显著更差。逻辑回归堆叠的准确率和ROC-AUC尚可，但log-loss在所有集成中最差。

Conclusion: 由于TFM之间高度冗余，集成带来的收益极为有限且成本高昂。逻辑回归堆叠通过锐化类别边界提升准确率但破坏校准。推荐贪心选择作为实际部署的默认方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) now match or beat tuned gradient-boosted trees on a growing fraction of tabular tasks, but no single TFM wins on every dataset. Ensembling is the go to fix here, and it works less well than expected. Six modern TFMs form a near-redundant pool: their mean pairwise Q-statistic is $0.961$, close enough to $1$ that any convex combination is bounded above. We benchmark six ensemble strategies over six TFMs on 153 OpenML classification tasks. The best ensemble, two-level cascade stacking, buys $+0.18\%$ accuracy over the strongest single TFM at $253\times$ the compute. A Friedman and Nemenyi analysis places three ensembles and the best base TFM in a single equivalence group; three other ensembles are significantly \emph{worse} than the best base. Stacking with a logistic-regression meta-learner is the most striking case: competitive accuracy and ROC-AUC, the worst log-loss rank among the ensembles. The meta-learner improves accuracy by sharpening class boundaries, which destroys calibration. We recommend greedy selection as the practical default.

</details>


### [216] [Learning Normal Representations for Blood Biomarkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18701)
*Aashna P. Shah,Michelle M. Li,Yash Lal,Seffi Cohen,Liat F. Antwarg,Morgan Sanchez,James A. Diao,Chirag J. Patel,Ben Y. Reis,Ran D. Balicer,Noa Dagan,Arjun K. Manrai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用近20亿条纵向实验室检测数据，发现纯个性化参考区间会导致过度拟合（高达68%的测量值被标记为异常），并提出NORMA——一种基于条件Transformer的框架，通过结合患者个体历史与人群先验信息生成参考区间，其在预测死亡率、急性肾损伤和慢性疾病等临床结局方面优于纯个性化或纯人群方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于固定人群参考区间的血液生物标志物解读忽略了患者个体内稳定变异，可能掩盖偏离基线的有意义变化，导致疾病检测延迟。现有的个性化方法存在对稀疏数据过拟合、假阳性率高以及可能纳入未识别或亚临床疾病的问题。

Method: 利用来自北美、中东和东亚超过160万人的近20亿条纵向实验室测量数据，提出NORMA——一种条件Transformer框架，通过同时条件化于患者的检测历史和人群水平的"正常"变异信息来生成参考区间。

Result: 纯个性化参考区间将高达68%的测量值分类为异常，但这些异常与不良临床结局缺乏关联。NORMA生成的区间在预测死亡率、急性肾损伤和慢性疾病等结局方面实现了更高的精度。

Conclusion: 研究警示实验室医学中过度个性化的风险，并证明将个体轨迹锚定在人群先验信息上优于单独使用任何一种方法。研究公开了模型、代码和交互式用户界面以促进可及性。

Abstract: Blood-based biomarkers underpin clinical diagnosis and management, yet their interpretation relies largely on fixed population reference intervals that ignore stable, intra-patient variability. As such, population-based interpretation can mask meaningful deviation from an individual's baseline, risking delayed disease detection. To remedy this, there have been increasing efforts to personalize blood biomarker interpretation using individual testing histories. However, these methods may overfit to sparse data, inflating false-positive rates and unnecessary follow-up, and can also unwittingly include unrecognized or subclinical disease. Here, we leverage nearly 2 billion longitudinal laboratory measurements from over 1.6 million individuals across North America, the Middle East, and East Asia, to show that while laboratory values are highly individual, purely personalized intervals routinely overfit, classifying up to 68% of measurements as abnormal, without corresponding associations with adverse clinical outcomes. We then introduce NORMA, a conditional transformer-based framework that generates reference intervals by conditioning on both a patient's history and population-level data about "normal" variation. NORMA-derived intervals achieve higher precision for predicting outcomes, including mortality, acute kidney injury, and chronic disease. These findings caution against over-personalization in laboratory medicine and demonstrate that anchoring individual trajectories to population-level priors outperforms either approach alone. To promote transparency, we publicly release the model, code, and an interactive user interface for accessible, individualized laboratory interpretation.

</details>


### [217] [Distilling Tabular Foundation Models for Structured Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18702)
*Aditya Tanna,Nassim Bouarour,Mohamed Bouadi,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何通过知识蒸馏将表格基础模型（TFM）的预测能力迁移到轻量级模型上，提出了一种考虑上下文泄漏的分层折外教师标注方法。在19个医疗数据集上实验表明，蒸馏后的学生模型在保留至少90%教师AUC的同时，CPU推理速度提升26倍以上，且保持了良好的校准性和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）在医疗数据集上表现优异，但其推理成本和基础设施要求限制了实际应用。作者希望将其预测能力迁移到轻量级模型中，以在资源受限的医疗场景中实现高效部署。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏技术，提出分层折外（stratified out-of-fold）教师标注方法，以解决上下文TFM在训练集上条件推断时可能导致的上下文泄漏问题。使用6种TFM教师模型、4种学生模型族以及多种多教师集成方法进行实验。

Result: 蒸馏学生模型保留至少90%的教师AUC，部分情况下甚至超越教师；CPU推理速度提升至少26倍；模型校准性和公平性得以保持。多教师平均策略并不总能优于最佳单教师。

Conclusion: 泄漏感知蒸馏是将TFM级别预测能力部署到推理受限医疗场景的可行路径。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) achieve strong performance on health datasets, but their inference cost and infrastructure requirements limit practical use. We study whether their predictive behavior can be transferred to lightweight tabular models through knowledge distillation. Since in-context TFMs condition on the training set at inference time, naive distillation can introduce context leakage; we address this with stratified out-of-fold teacher labeling. Across $19$ healthcare datasets, $6$ TFM teachers, $4$ student families, and several multi-teacher ensembles, we find that distilled students retain at least $90\%$ of teacher AUC, outperforming teachers in some cases, while running at least $26\times$ faster on CPU and preserving calibration and fairness critical for health applications. Moreover, multi-teacher averaging does not consistently improve over the best single teacher. Leakage-aware distillation is thus a viable route for bringing TFM-quality predictions into inference-constrained health settings.

</details>


### [218] [General Preference Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18721)
*Muhammad Umer,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Ahsan Bilal,Arslan Chaudhry,Andreas Haupt,Sanmi Koyejo,Emily Fox,John M. Cioffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出通用偏好强化学习（GPRL），利用广义偏好模型（GPM）的多维偏好表示替代传统标量奖励模型，通过分维度归一化、上下文相关聚合及闭环漂移监控机制，在保持开放任务对齐能力的同时抑制奖励黑客行为，在多个基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线RL（基于可验证奖励）和偏好优化（基于人类偏好）两条对齐路线各有所长但互不相通：前者依赖程序化验证器，无法处理开放任务；后者缺乏持续探索机制。标量奖励模型无法捕捉质量的多个维度，容易导致在线RL在单一维度上崩溃。

Method: 基于广义偏好模型（GPM）将响应嵌入k个斜对称子空间，实现结构化、可感知非传递性的偏好比较。GPRL在此基础上计算每维度群相对优势，进行独立尺度归一化以防止某维度主导，再通过上下文相关的特征值聚合。同时引入闭环漂移监控器，检测单轴利用并通过重新加权维度和收紧信任域进行在线修正。

Result: 从Llama-3-8B-Instruct出发，GPRL在AlpacaEval 2.0上达到56.51%的长度控制胜率，在Arena-Hard、MT-Bench和WildBench上优于SimPO和SPPO，且在长训练中有效抵抗奖励黑客攻击。

Conclusion: 多维偏好表示配合维度归一化与在线监控，能够弥合在线RL与偏好优化之间的鸿沟，实现开放任务上的稳定对齐与持续探索。

Abstract: Post-training has split large language model (LLM) alignment into two largely disconnected tracks. Online reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards drives emergent reasoning on math and code but depends on a programmatic verifier that cannot reach open-ended tasks, while preference optimization handles open-ended generation yet forgoes the continuous exploration that powers online RL. Closing this gap requires a verifier for open-ended quality, but a scalar reward model is the wrong shape for the job. Quality is multi-dimensional, and any scalar score is an incomplete proxy that lets online RL collapse onto whichever axis the score is most sensitive to. We turn instead to the General Preference Model (GPM), which embeds responses into $k$ skew-symmetric subspaces and represents preference as a structured, intransitivity-aware comparison. Building on this, we propose General Preference Reinforcement Learning (GPRL), which carries the $k$-way structure through to the policy update. GPRL computes per-dimension group-relative advantages, normalizes each on its own scale so no axis can dominate, and aggregates them with context-dependent eigenvalues. The same structure powers a closed-loop drift monitor that detects single-axis exploitation and corrects it on the fly by reweighting dimensions and tightening the trust region. Starting from $\texttt{Llama-3-8B-Instruct}$, GPRL reaches a length-controlled win rate of $56.51\%$ on AlpacaEval~2.0 while also outperforming SimPO and SPPO on Arena-Hard, MT-Bench, and WildBench by resisting reward hacking across extended training runs.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [Evolutionary Extreme Learning Machine of ab-initio Energy Landscapes for Crystal Structure Prediction using Manta Ray Optimization with Levy Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17148)
*Adrian Rubio-Solis*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Levy飞行改进的蝠鲼觅食优化算法(MRFO-LF)来训练极限学习机(ELM)，用于预测二元体系中化合物相对于纯组分基态晶体结构的松弛与未松弛形成能。该方法结合MRFO-LF选择输入权重，利用Moore-Penrose广义逆解析确定输出权重，通过Levy飞行增强种群多样性以避免早熟收敛和局部最优。实验结果表明该算法优于其他自然启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MRFO算法在训练ELM时存在早熟收敛和易陷入局部最优的问题，需要增强种群多样性以提升ELM在材料形成能预测任务上的性能。

Method: 提出EELM-MRFO-LF方法，首先使用Levy飞行改进的MRFO算法选择ELM的输入权重，然后通过Moore-Penrose广义逆解析计算输出权重。Levy飞行用于增加ELM种群多样性，防止早熟收敛和局部最优。

Result: 在二元体系化合物的松弛与未松弛形成能预测任务上，EELM-MRFO-LF的性能优于其他已知的自然启发式算法。

Conclusion: Levy飞行改进的MRFO能够有效增强ELM训练过程中的种群多样性，避免早熟收敛，提高形成能预测的准确性。

Abstract: The Manta Ray Foraging Optimization algorithm (MRFO) has proven to be a powerful heuristic strategy in the optimal solution of a large number of engineering problems. In this paper, an improvement of MRFO with Levy Flight is suggested for the training of extreme learning machines (ELMs) whose basic model is a Single Layer Feedforward Network (SLFN). The proposed methodology that we called Evolutionary EELM-MRFO-LF for short is implemented to the prediction of unrelaxed and relaxed formation energy compounds relative to ground state crystal structure of pure components in binary systems. EELM-MRFO-LF follows the learning procedure of traditional Evolutionary ELMs in which first MRFO with LF is used to select the input weights and Moore-Penrose (MP) generalized inverse is applied to analytically determine the output weights. Levy Flight trajectory is implemented for increasing the diversity of the population of ELMs against premature convergence and the ability of avoiding getting trapped in a local optima. The performance of the suggested EELM-MRFO-LF is compared with other well-known nature-inspired algorithms under similar conditions.

</details>


### [220] [Spiker-LL: An Energy-Efficient FPGA Accelerator Enabling Adaptive Local Learning in Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18003)
*Alessio Caviglia,Filippo Marostica,Alessandro Savino,Stefano Di Carlo*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出SPIKER-LL，一种基于FPGA的SNN加速器，通过支持STSF局部学习规则，在边缘设备上实现了高效的在线学习和推理，在多个数据集上达到93%准确率、亚毫秒延迟和低于0.1mJ/推理的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘部署自适应智能面临神经模型训练的高计算和能耗挑战，SNN虽具潜力，但实现在线学习需要硬件-算法协同设计。

Method: 基于开源Spiker+推理架构进行微架构扩展，加入对STSF局部学习规则的支持，实现推理与在线学习功能融合，且无需DSP单元。

Result: 在MNIST、F-MNIST和DIGITS数据集上，准确率最高达93%，延迟低于亚毫秒级，每次推理能耗低于0.1mJ。

Conclusion: SPIKER-LL在保持无DSP、高可扩展性的前提下，有效支持了边缘FPGA上的SNN在线学习，实现了高性能与低功耗的平衡。

Abstract: Deploying adaptive intelligence at the edge remains challenging due to the high computational and energy cost of training neural models. Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer a promising alternative, but enabling on-device learning requires hardware-algorithm co-design. This paper presents SPIKER-LL, an FPGA-based SNN accelerator that extends the open-source Spiker+ inference architecture with efficient support for the STSF local learning rule. Through targeted microarchitectural extensions, SPIKER-LL performs inference and online learning with minimal overhead. Across MNIST, F-MNIST, and DIGITS, it achieves up to 93% accuracy, sub-millisecond latency, and less than 0.1 mJ per inference, while remaining DSP-free and highly scalable for edge-FPGA deployments.

</details>


### [221] [Mapping the Fitness Landscape: A Structure-Guided Approach to Multi-Modal Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18351)
*Meng Xiang,Pei Yan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出混沌景观解码进化（CLDE）框架，通过混沌映射探索、k近邻图构建、持久性引导的盆地生长和自适应阈值，将多模态优化转化为闭环的解码-评价-分配-精炼过程，在单目标和多目标问题上均取得competitive效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于小生境的进化算法虽能保持种群多样性，但无法显式恢复决策空间中的峰-盆地组织结构，导致伪多模态问题——许多不同个体最终坍缩到少数几个盆地中。

Method: CLDE框架包含四个环节：1）通过逻辑斯蒂混沌映射+衰减步长进行受控全局探索；2）在解码画布上构建k近邻图；3）进行持久性引导的盆地生长，仅当峰值间存在深谷时才合并；4）自适应持久性阈值在线调整解码分辨率，避免过度分割或合并。基于解码结构执行盆地级选择和精炼。实例化为CLDE-S（单目标）和CLDE-M（多目标）。

Result: 在20个CEC2013函数上，CLDE-S在相同评估预算下达到强峰值比；在DTLZ和MMMOP套件上，CLDE-M在IGD/IGDx指标上取得competitive结果，在强多模态问题上优势明显。

Conclusion: CLDE通过显式解码决策空间的峰-盆地结构，有效解决了伪多模态问题，为多模态优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal optimization requires finding many optima rather than merely keeping a diverse population. Yet most niching-based evolutionary algorithms rely on distances or density estimators without explicitly recovering the underlying peak--basin organization in the decision space, which can lead to pseudo-multimodality: many distinct individuals ultimately collapse into only a few basins. We introduce Chaotic Landscape-Decoding Evolution (CLDE), a decision-space-centric framework that turns multimodal search into a closed loop of decode--value--allocate--refine. CLDE injects controlled global exploration via a logistic chaotic map with a decaying step size, then builds a $k$-nearest-neighbor graph on a decoding canvas and performs persistence-guided basin growing that merges peaks only when they are not separated by deep valleys. An adaptive persistence threshold continuously tunes the decoding resolution online to avoid over-fragmentation and over-merging. Guided by the decoded structure, CLDE carries out basin-wise selection and refinement to improve solution quality while preserving basin coverage. We instantiate CLDE as CLDE-S and CLDE-M for single- and multi-objective multimodal optimization. Experiments on 20 CEC2013 functions show that CLDE-S achieves strong peak ratio under the same evaluation budget, while on DTLZ and MMMOP suites CLDE-M attains competitive IGD/IGDx, with pronounced gains on strongly multimodal problems.

</details>
